#line 1 "./sharc/HashGridCommon.h"






























































              const uint HASH_GRID_KEY_BIT_NUM = 64;
              const uint HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM = 17;
              const uint HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_NUM = 9;
              const uint HASH_GRID_NORMAL_BIT_NUM = 3;


              const uint HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK = (1u << HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM) - 1;
              const uint HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_MASK = (1u << HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_NUM) - 1;
              const uint HASH_GRID_NORMAL_BIT_MASK = (1u << HASH_GRID_NORMAL_BIT_NUM) - 1;

              const uint HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_OFFSET = HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 3;
              const uint HASH_GRID_NORMAL_BIT_OFFSET = HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_OFFSET + HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_NUM;






























float HashGridLogBase(float x, float base)
{
    return log2(x) * (1.0 / (log2(base)));
}


uint HashGridHashJenkins32(uint a)
{
    a = (a + 0x7ed55d16) + (a << 12);
    a = (a ^ 0xc761c23c) ^ (a >> 19);
    a = (a + 0x165667b1) + (a << 5);
    a = (a + 0xd3a2646c) ^ (a << 9);
    a = (a + 0xfd7046c5) + (a << 3);
    a = (a ^ 0xb55a4f09) ^ (a >> 16);

    return a;
}

uint HashGridHash32(uint64_t hashKey)
{



    return HashGridHashJenkins32(uint(hashKey)) ^ HashGridHashJenkins32(uint(hashKey >> 32));

}






uint HashGridGetBaseSlot(const uint64_t hashKey, uint capacity)
{
    const uint baseSlotCount = capacity - 16 + 1;

    return HashGridHash32(hashKey) % baseSlotCount;
}

uint HashGridGetLevel(vec3 samplePosition, HashGridParameters gridParameters)
{
         vec3 cameraOffset = gridParameters.cameraPosition - samplePosition;
    float distance2 = dot(cameraOffset, cameraOffset);
    distance2 = max(distance2, 1e-10f);
    float gridLevel = 0.5f * HashGridLogBase(distance2, gridParameters.logarithmBase) + gridParameters.levelBias;

    return uint(clamp(gridLevel, 1.0f, float(HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_MASK)));
}

float HashGridGetVoxelSize(uint gridLevel, HashGridParameters gridParameters)
{
    float exponent = log2(gridParameters.logarithmBase) * (float(gridLevel) - gridParameters.levelBias);

    return exp2(exponent) * (1.0 / (gridParameters.sceneScale));
}


   ivec4 HashGridCalculatePositionLogWithVoxelSize(vec3 samplePosition, HashGridParameters gridParameters, out float voxelSize)
{
    samplePosition += vec3(1e-4f, 1e-4f, 1e-4f);

    uint gridLevel = HashGridGetLevel(samplePosition, gridParameters);
    voxelSize = HashGridGetVoxelSize(gridLevel, gridParameters);
       ivec3 gridPosition = ivec3(floor(samplePosition * (1.0 / (voxelSize))));

    return ivec4(gridPosition.xyz, gridLevel);
}

   ivec4 HashGridCalculatePositionLog(vec3 samplePosition, HashGridParameters gridParameters)
{
    float voxelSize;
    return HashGridCalculatePositionLogWithVoxelSize(samplePosition, gridParameters, voxelSize);
}

                 uint64_t HashGridComputeSpatialHashFromGridPosition(uvec4 gridPosition, vec3 sampleNormal)
{
                     uint64_t hashKey =
        ( (uint64_t(gridPosition.x) & HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK) << (HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 0)) |
        ( (uint64_t(gridPosition.y) & HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK) << (HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 1)) |
        ( (uint64_t(gridPosition.z) & HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK) << (HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 2)) |
        ( (uint64_t(gridPosition.w) & HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_MASK) << HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_OFFSET);


    uint normalBits =
        (sampleNormal.x + 1e-3f >= 0 ? 0 : 1) +
        (sampleNormal.y + 1e-3f >= 0 ? 0 : 2) +
        (sampleNormal.z + 1e-3f >= 0 ? 0 : 4);

    hashKey |= (uint64_t(normalBits) << HASH_GRID_NORMAL_BIT_OFFSET);


    return hashKey;
}

                 uint64_t HashGridComputeSpatialHashWithVoxelSize(vec3 samplePosition, vec3 sampleNormal, HashGridParameters gridParameters, out float voxelSize)
{
        uvec4 gridPosition = uvec4(HashGridCalculatePositionLogWithVoxelSize(samplePosition, gridParameters, voxelSize));
    return HashGridComputeSpatialHashFromGridPosition(gridPosition, sampleNormal);
}

                 uint64_t HashGridComputeSpatialHash(vec3 samplePosition, vec3 sampleNormal, HashGridParameters gridParameters)
{
    float voxelSize;
    return HashGridComputeSpatialHashWithVoxelSize(samplePosition, sampleNormal, gridParameters, voxelSize);
}

     vec3 HashGridGetPositionFromKey(const uint64_t hashKey, HashGridParameters gridParameters)
{
       ivec3 gridPosition;
    gridPosition.x = int( (hashKey >> (HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 0)) & HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK);
    gridPosition.y = int( (hashKey >> (HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 1)) & HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK);
    gridPosition.z = int( (hashKey >> (HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 2)) & HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK);


    gridPosition = (gridPosition << (32 - HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM)) >> (32 - HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM);

    uint gridLevel = uint( (hashKey >> HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_OFFSET) & HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_MASK);
    float voxelSize = HashGridGetVoxelSize(gridLevel, gridParameters);
         vec3 samplePosition = (gridPosition + 0.5f) * voxelSize;

    return samplePosition;
}

struct HashGridData
{
    uint capacity;

                                                              RWStructuredBuffer_uint64_t hashEntriesBuffer;




};

void HashGridAtomicCompareExchange(in HashGridData hashData, in uint dstOffset, in uint64_t compareValue, in uint64_t value, out uint64_t originalValue)
{

                                                                                                                          originalValue = atomicCompSwap(hashData.hashEntriesBuffer.data[dstOffset], compareValue, value);























}

bool HashGridInsert(in HashGridData hashData, const uint64_t hashKey, uint baseSlot, uint probeRange, out uint cacheIndex, out uint bucketOffset)
{
    cacheIndex = 0xFFFFFFFF;
    probeRange = min(probeRange, 16);

                       [[dont_unroll]]
    for (bucketOffset = 0; bucketOffset < probeRange; ++ bucketOffset)
    {
                         uint64_t prevHashKey;
                                       HashGridAtomicCompareExchange(hashData, baseSlot + bucketOffset, 0, hashKey, prevHashKey);

        if (prevHashKey == 0 || prevHashKey == hashKey)
        {
            cacheIndex = baseSlot + bucketOffset;
            return true;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

bool HashGridFind(in HashGridData hashData, const uint64_t hashKey, uint baseSlot, uint probeRange, out uint cacheIndex, out uint bucketOffset)
{
    cacheIndex = 0xFFFFFFFF;
    probeRange = min(probeRange, 16);
    uint emptyEntires = 0;

                       [[dont_unroll]]
    for (bucketOffset = 0; bucketOffset < probeRange; ++ bucketOffset)
    {
                         uint64_t storedHashKey = hashData.hashEntriesBuffer.data[baseSlot + bucketOffset];

        if (storedHashKey == 0)
        {
            if (emptyEntires > 2)
                break;

            ++ emptyEntires;
        }


        if (storedHashKey == hashKey)
        {
            cacheIndex = baseSlot + bucketOffset;
            return true;
        }
    }

    return false;
}

bool HashGridInsertEntry(in HashGridData hashData, vec3 samplePosition, vec3 sampleNormal, HashGridParameters gridParameters, out uint64_t hashKey, out uint cacheIndex)
{
    hashKey = HashGridComputeSpatialHash(samplePosition, sampleNormal, gridParameters);
    uint baseSlot = HashGridGetBaseSlot(hashKey, hashData.capacity);
    uint bucketOffset;

    return HashGridInsert(hashData, hashKey, baseSlot, 16, cacheIndex, bucketOffset);
}

            uint HashGridFindEntry(in HashGridData hashData, vec3 samplePosition, vec3 sampleNormal, HashGridParameters gridParameters, out uint64_t hashKey)
{
                uint cacheIndex = 0xFFFFFFFF;
    hashKey = HashGridComputeSpatialHash(samplePosition, sampleNormal, gridParameters);
    uint baseSlot = HashGridGetBaseSlot(hashKey, hashData.capacity);
    uint bucketOffset;
                  HashGridFind(hashData, hashKey, baseSlot, 16, cacheIndex, bucketOffset);

    return cacheIndex;
}


     vec3 HashGridGetColorFromHash32(uint hash)
{
         vec3 color;
    color.x = ( (hash >> 0) & 0x3ff) / 1023.0f;
    color.y = ( (hash >> 11) & 0x7ff) / 2047.0f;
    color.z = ( (hash >> 22) & 0x7ff) / 2047.0f;

    return color;
}


     vec3 HashGridDebugColoredHash(vec3 samplePosition, vec3 sampleNormal, HashGridParameters gridParameters)
{
                     uint64_t hashKey = HashGridComputeSpatialHash(samplePosition, sampleNormal, gridParameters);
    uint gridLevel = HashGridGetLevel(samplePosition, gridParameters);
         vec3 color = HashGridGetColorFromHash32(HashGridHash32(hashKey)) * HashGridGetColorFromHash32(HashGridHashJenkins32(gridLevel)).xyz;

    return color;
}

     vec3 HashGridDebugOccupancy(uvec2 pixelPosition, uvec2 screenSize, HashGridData hashData)
{
    const uint elementSize = 7;
    const uint borderSize = 1;
    const uint blockSize = elementSize + borderSize;

    uint rowNum = screenSize.y / blockSize;
    uint rowIndex = pixelPosition.y / blockSize;
    uint columnIndex = pixelPosition.x / blockSize;
    uint elementIndex = (columnIndex / 16) * (rowNum * 16) + rowIndex * 16 + (columnIndex % 16);

    if (elementIndex < hashData.capacity && ( (pixelPosition.x % blockSize) < elementSize && (pixelPosition.y % blockSize) < elementSize))
    {
                         uint64_t hashGridKey = hashData.hashEntriesBuffer.data[elementIndex];
        if (hashGridKey != 0)
            return(hashGridKey >> (HASH_GRID_KEY_BIT_NUM - 1)) != 0 ? vec3(1.0f, 0.75f, 0.0f) : vec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f);
    }

    return vec3(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
}

     vec3 HashGridDebugHashCollisions(vec3 samplePosition, vec3 sampleNormal, HashGridParameters gridParameters, HashGridData hashData)
{
                     uint64_t hashKey = HashGridComputeSpatialHash(samplePosition, sampleNormal, gridParameters);
                uint cacheIndex = 0xFFFFFFFF;
    uint baseSlot = HashGridGetBaseSlot(hashKey, hashData.capacity);
    uint bucketOffset;
                  HashGridFind(hashData, hashKey, baseSlot, 16, cacheIndex, bucketOffset);

         vec3 debugColor;
    if (bucketOffset == 0)
        debugColor = vec3(0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);
    else if (bucketOffset == 1)
        debugColor = vec3(0.0f, 0.5f, 0.5f);
    else if (bucketOffset == 2)
        debugColor = vec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f);
    else if (bucketOffset == 3)
        debugColor = vec3(1.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f);
    else if (bucketOffset == 4)
        debugColor = vec3(0.75f, 0.25f, 0.0f);
    else
        debugColor = vec3(1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);

    return debugColor;
}

     vec3 SharcRGBToYCoCg(vec3 color)
{
    return vec3(0.25f * (color.x + 2.0f * color.y + color.z), color.x - color.z, color.y - 0.5f * (color.x + color.z));
}

     vec3 SharcYCoCgToRGB(vec3 color)
{
    return vec3(color.x + 0.5f * (color.y - color.z), color.x + 0.5f * color.z, color.x - 0.5f * (color.y + color.z));
}

uint SharcPackFloat16(float value)
{
    return packHalf2x16(vec2(value, 0)) & 0xFFFFu;
}

float SharcUnpackFloat16(uint value)
{
    return unpackHalf2x16(value & 0xFFFFu).x;
}

uint SharcPackFloat16Pair(vec2 value)
{
    return SharcPackFloat16(value.x) | (SharcPackFloat16(value.y) << 16);
}

     vec2 SharcUnpackFloat16Pair(uint value)
{
    return vec2(SharcUnpackFloat16(value), SharcUnpackFloat16(value >> 16));
}

SharcRadianceData SharcGetAccumulatedRadianceData(SharcAccumulationData accumulatedData, float radianceScale, float sampleNum)
{
    SharcRadianceData radianceData;
    float scale = (1.0 / (radianceScale * max(sampleNum, 1e-6f)));




    radianceData.radiance = vec3(accumulatedData.data.xyz) * scale;


    return radianceData;
}

SharcPackedData SharcPackVoxelData(SharcRadianceData radianceData, float sampleNum, uint accumulatedFrameNum, uint staleFrameNum, uint sampleDataExt)
{
    const float float16Max = 65504.0f;

    SharcPackedData packedData;





    packedData.radianceData = f16vec4(min(vec4(radianceData.radiance, sampleNum), vec4(float16Max, float16Max, float16Max, float16Max)));

    packedData.sampleData = accumulatedFrameNum | (staleFrameNum << 16);
    packedData.sampleDataExt = sampleDataExt;

    return packedData;
}

SharcVoxelData SharcUnpackVoxelData(SharcPackedData packedData)
{
    SharcVoxelData voxelData;





         vec4 radianceAndSampleNum = vec4(packedData.radianceData);
    voxelData.accumulatedRadiance.radiance = radianceAndSampleNum.xyz;
    voxelData.accumulatedSampleNum = radianceAndSampleNum.w;

    voxelData.accumulatedFrameNum = (packedData.sampleData >> 0) & ( (1 << 16) - 1);
    voxelData.staleFrameNum = (packedData.sampleData >> 16) & ( (1 << 16) - 1);
    voxelData.sampleDataExt = packedData.sampleDataExt;

    return voxelData;
}

SharcVoxelData SharcGetVoxelData(RWStructuredBuffer_SharcPackedData voxelDataBuffer, uint hashGridIndex)
{
    SharcPackedData packedData = voxelDataBuffer.data[hashGridIndex];

    return SharcUnpackVoxelData(packedData);
}

float SharcLuma(vec3 color)
{
    const vec3 luma = vec3(0.213f, 0.715f, 0.072f);

    return dot(color, luma);
}

void SharcAddVoxelData(in SharcParameters sharcParameters, uint hashGridIndex, vec3 sampleValue, vec3 sampleWeight, vec3 sampleDirection, float sampleDirectionWeight, uint sampleData)
{













        uvec3 scaledRadiance = uvec3(sampleValue * sampleWeight * sharcParameters.radianceScale);

    if (scaledRadiance.x != 0) atomicAdd(sharcParameters.accumulationBuffer.data[hashGridIndex].data.x, scaledRadiance.x);
    if (scaledRadiance.y != 0) atomicAdd(sharcParameters.accumulationBuffer.data[hashGridIndex].data.y, scaledRadiance.y);
    if (scaledRadiance.z != 0) atomicAdd(sharcParameters.accumulationBuffer.data[hashGridIndex].data.z, scaledRadiance.z);
    if (sampleData != 0) atomicAdd(sharcParameters.accumulationBuffer.data[hashGridIndex].data.w, sampleData);

}

bool SharcGetCachedRadianceFromHash(in SharcParameters sharcParameters, in SharcHitData sharcHitData, uint64_t hashGridKey, out vec3 radiance, bool skipResponsiveLighting)
{
                uint hashGridIndex;
    uint baseSlot = HashGridGetBaseSlot(hashGridKey, sharcParameters.hashGridData.capacity);
    uint bucketOffset;
    if (! HashGridFind(sharcParameters.hashGridData, hashGridKey, baseSlot, 16, hashGridIndex, bucketOffset))
        return false;

    SharcVoxelData voxelData = SharcGetVoxelData(sharcParameters.resolvedBuffer, hashGridIndex);
    if (voxelData.accumulatedSampleNum > 0)
    {
             vec3 sharcRadianceDirection = SharcGetRadianceDirection(sharcHitData);
        radiance = SharcDecodeRadiance(voxelData.accumulatedRadiance, sharcRadianceDirection);



















        return true;
    }

    return false;
}

bool SharcGetCachedRadiance(in SharcParameters sharcParameters, in SharcHitData sharcHitData, out vec3 radiance, bool skipResponsiveLighting)
{
              uint64_t hashGridKey = HashGridComputeSpatialHash(sharcHitData.positionWorld, sharcHitData.normalWorld, sharcParameters.hashGridParameters);
    return SharcGetCachedRadianceFromHash(sharcParameters, sharcHitData, hashGridKey, radiance, skipResponsiveLighting);
}

int SharcGetGridDistance2(ivec3 position)
{
    return position.x * position.x + position.y * position.y + position.z * position.z;
}

          uint64_t SharcGetAdjacentLevelHashKey(uint64_t hashGridKey, HashGridParameters gridParameters, vec3 cameraPositionPrev)
{
       ivec3 gridPosition;
    gridPosition.x = int( (hashGridKey >> HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 0) & HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK);
    gridPosition.y = int( (hashGridKey >> HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 1) & HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK);
    gridPosition.z = int( (hashGridKey >> HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 2) & HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK);


    gridPosition = (gridPosition << (32 - HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM)) >> (32 - HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM);

    int level = int( (hashGridKey >> HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_OFFSET) & HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_MASK);

    float voxelSize = HashGridGetVoxelSize(uint(level), gridParameters);
    float inverseVoxelSize = (1.0 / (voxelSize));
       ivec3 cameraGridPosition = ivec3(floor(gridParameters.cameraPosition * inverseVoxelSize));
       ivec3 cameraVector = cameraGridPosition - gridPosition;
    int cameraDistance = SharcGetGridDistance2(cameraVector);

       ivec3 cameraGridPositionPrev = ivec3(floor(cameraPositionPrev * inverseVoxelSize));
       ivec3 cameraVectorPrev = cameraGridPositionPrev - gridPosition;
    int cameraDistancePrev = SharcGetGridDistance2(cameraVectorPrev);

    if (cameraDistance < cameraDistancePrev)
    {
        gridPosition = ivec3(floor(gridPosition / gridParameters.logarithmBase));
        level = min(level + 1, int(HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_MASK));
    }
    else
    {
        gridPosition = ivec3(floor(gridPosition * gridParameters.logarithmBase));
        level = max(level - 1, 1);
    }

              uint64_t modifiedHashGridKey = ( (uint64_t(gridPosition.x) & HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK) << (HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 0))
        | ( (uint64_t(gridPosition.y) & HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK) << (HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 1))
        | ( (uint64_t(gridPosition.z) & HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_MASK) << (HASH_GRID_POSITION_BIT_NUM * 2))
        | ( (uint64_t(level) & HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_MASK) << HASH_GRID_LEVEL_BIT_OFFSET);


    modifiedHashGridKey |= hashGridKey & (uint64_t(HASH_GRID_NORMAL_BIT_MASK) << HASH_GRID_NORMAL_BIT_OFFSET);


    return modifiedHashGridKey;
}

void SharcResolveEntry(uint entryIndex, SharcParameters sharcParameters, SharcResolveParameters resolveParameters)
{
    if (entryIndex >= sharcParameters.hashGridData.capacity)
        return;

              uint64_t hashGridKey = sharcParameters.hashGridData.hashEntriesBuffer.data[entryIndex];
    if (hashGridKey == 0)
        return;

    bool isResponsiveSignal = false;




    SharcAccumulationData accumulatedData = sharcParameters.accumulationBuffer.data[entryIndex];
    SharcPackedData resolvedData = sharcParameters.resolvedBuffer.data[entryIndex];
    SharcVoxelData sharcVoxelData = SharcUnpackVoxelData(resolvedData);

    float sampleNum = SharcGetAccumulatedSampleNum(accumulatedData);
    float sampleNumPrev = sharcVoxelData.accumulatedSampleNum;
    uint accumulatedFrameNum = sharcVoxelData.accumulatedFrameNum + 1;
    uint staleFrameNum = sharcVoxelData.staleFrameNum;

    staleFrameNum = (sampleNum != 0) ? 0 : staleFrameNum + 1;
    uint staleFrameNumMax = clamp(resolveParameters.staleFrameNumMax, 8, 1024);

    if (isResponsiveSignal)
        staleFrameNumMax = resolveParameters.responsiveFrameNum;

    bool isValidElement = (staleFrameNum < staleFrameNumMax) ? true : false;
    if (! isValidElement)
    {
        SharcAccumulationData zeroAccumulationData = SharcZeroAccumulationData();
        SharcPackedData zeroPackedData = SharcZeroPackedData();

                                                                                   sharcParameters.hashGridData.hashEntriesBuffer.data[entryIndex] = 0;
                                                                       sharcParameters.accumulationBuffer.data[entryIndex] = zeroAccumulationData;
                                                                   sharcParameters.resolvedBuffer.data[entryIndex] = zeroPackedData;
        return;
    }
    else if (sampleNum == 0 && ! isResponsiveSignal)
    {

                                                                   sharcParameters.resolvedBuffer.data[entryIndex].sampleData = resolvedData.sampleData + ( (1 << 0) | (1 << 16));





        return;
    }























    if (sampleNumPrev == 0)
    {
        uint searchEnd = min(entryIndex + 1 + 8, sharcParameters.hashGridData.capacity);
        for (uint i = entryIndex + 1; i < searchEnd; ++ i)
        {
                      uint64_t hashKeyOld = sharcParameters.hashGridData.hashEntriesBuffer.data[i];
            if (hashKeyOld == hashGridKey)
            {
                resolvedData = sharcParameters.resolvedBuffer.data[i];
                sharcVoxelData = SharcUnpackVoxelData(resolvedData);
                sampleNumPrev = sharcVoxelData.accumulatedSampleNum;
                accumulatedFrameNum = sharcVoxelData.accumulatedFrameNum + 1;
                staleFrameNum = 0;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    SharcRadianceData accumulatedRadiance = SharcGetAccumulatedRadianceData(accumulatedData, sharcParameters.radianceScale, sampleNum);
    SharcRadianceData accumulatedRadiancePrev = sharcVoxelData.accumulatedRadiance;
    uint accumulationFrameNum = clamp(isResponsiveSignal ? resolveParameters.responsiveFrameNum : resolveParameters.accumulationFrameNum, 1, 1024);
    if (accumulatedFrameNum > accumulationFrameNum)
    {
        float normalizationScale = float(accumulationFrameNum) / float(accumulatedFrameNum);
        accumulatedFrameNum = accumulationFrameNum;
        sampleNumPrev *= normalizationScale;
    }
















    float sampleTotalInv = (1.0 / (sampleNumPrev + sampleNum));
    accumulatedRadiance = SharcAddRadianceData(SharcScaleRadianceData(accumulatedRadiancePrev, sampleNumPrev * sampleTotalInv), SharcScaleRadianceData(accumulatedRadiance, sampleNum * sampleTotalInv));
    float accumulatedSampleNum = sampleNumPrev + sampleNum;



         vec3 cameraOffset = sharcParameters.hashGridParameters.cameraPosition.xyz - resolveParameters.cameraPositionPrev.xyz;
    if (! isResponsiveSignal && (dot(cameraOffset, cameraOffset) > 1e-6f) && (accumulatedFrameNum <= 2))
    {
                  uint64_t adjacentLevelHashKey = SharcGetAdjacentLevelHashKey(hashGridKey, sharcParameters.hashGridParameters, resolveParameters.cameraPositionPrev);

                    uint hashGridIndex;
        uint baseSlot = HashGridGetBaseSlot(adjacentLevelHashKey, sharcParameters.hashGridData.capacity);
        uint hashCollisionsNum;
        if (HashGridFind(sharcParameters.hashGridData, adjacentLevelHashKey, baseSlot, 16, hashGridIndex, hashCollisionsNum))
        {
            SharcPackedData adjacentPackedDataPrev = sharcParameters.resolvedBuffer.data[hashGridIndex];
            SharcVoxelData adjacentVoxelDataPrev = SharcUnpackVoxelData(adjacentPackedDataPrev);
            float adjacentSampleNum = adjacentVoxelDataPrev.accumulatedSampleNum;
            if (adjacentSampleNum > 0)
            {
                float blendWeight = (1.0 / (adjacentSampleNum + accumulatedSampleNum));
                accumulatedRadiance = SharcAddRadianceData(
                    SharcScaleRadianceData(adjacentVoxelDataPrev.accumulatedRadiance, adjacentSampleNum * blendWeight),
                    SharcScaleRadianceData(accumulatedRadiance, accumulatedSampleNum * blendWeight));
                accumulatedSampleNum += adjacentSampleNum;
            }
        }
    }


                                                               sharcParameters.resolvedBuffer.data[entryIndex] = SharcPackVoxelData(accumulatedRadiance, accumulatedSampleNum, accumulatedFrameNum, staleFrameNum, sharcVoxelData.sampleDataExt);



    SharcAccumulationData zeroAccumulationData = SharcZeroAccumulationData();
                                                                   sharcParameters.accumulationBuffer.data[entryIndex] = zeroAccumulationData;

}

#line 1 "./sharc_ubo.glsl"































uint64_t sharcCacheAddress(uint sharcSlot)
{
    return packUint2x32(
        uvec2(cam.sharcBuffersLo[sharcSlot], cam.sharcBuffersHi[sharcSlot]));
}








uint sharcMode()
{



    if (cam.sharcCacheParams.x == 0u)
    {
        return 0u;
    }

    for (uint sharcSlot = 0u; sharcSlot < 4u; ++ sharcSlot)
    {
        if (cam.sharcBuffersLo[sharcSlot] == 0u &&
           cam.sharcBuffersHi[sharcSlot] == 0u)
        {
            return 0u;
        }
    }

    return min(cam.sharcCacheParams.w, 4u);
}


bool sharcUpdateActive()
{
    const uint sharcCurrentMode = sharcMode();
    return sharcCurrentMode == 3u || sharcCurrentMode == 4u;
}


bool sharcQueryActive()
{
    const uint sharcCurrentMode = sharcMode();
    return sharcCurrentMode == 2u || sharcCurrentMode == 3u;
}


SharcParameters sharcBuildParameters()
{
    SharcParameters sharcParameters;




    sharcParameters.hashGridParameters.cameraPosition = cam.viewInverse[3].xyz;
    sharcParameters.hashGridParameters.logarithmBase = 2.0f;
    sharcParameters.hashGridParameters.sceneScale =
        uintBitsToFloat(cam.sharcCacheParams.x);
    sharcParameters.hashGridParameters.levelBias = float(0);



    sharcParameters.hashGridData.capacity = cam.sharcCacheParams.y;
    sharcParameters.hashGridData.hashEntriesBuffer =
        RWStructuredBuffer_uint64_t(sharcCacheAddress(0u));


    sharcParameters.radianceScale = uintBitsToFloat(cam.sharcCacheParams.z);
    sharcParameters.accumulationBuffer =
        RWStructuredBuffer_SharcAccumulationData(sharcCacheAddress(1u));
    sharcParameters.resolvedBuffer =
        RWStructuredBuffer_SharcPackedData(sharcCacheAddress(2u));

    return sharcParameters;
}











SharcResolveParameters sharcBuildResolveParameters()
{
    SharcResolveParameters resolveParameters;
    resolveParameters.cameraPositionPrev = cam.prevViewInverse[3].xyz;
    resolveParameters.accumulationFrameNum = 20u;
    resolveParameters.responsiveFrameNum = 8u;
    resolveParameters.staleFrameNumMax = 1024;
    resolveParameters.frameIndex = cam.frameCount;
    return resolveParameters;
}
#line 369 "rt_test.rgen"



























void sharcProbeWriteBack()
{



    RWStructuredBuffer_uint64_t probeHash =
        RWStructuredBuffer_uint64_t(sharcCacheAddress(0u));
    RWStructuredBuffer_SharcAccumulationData probeAccumulation =
        RWStructuredBuffer_SharcAccumulationData(sharcCacheAddress(1u));
    RWStructuredBuffer_SharcPackedData probeResolved =
        RWStructuredBuffer_SharcPackedData(sharcCacheAddress(2u));
    RWStructuredBuffer_uint probeOut =
        RWStructuredBuffer_uint(sharcCacheAddress(3u));

    const uint64_t hashKey = probeHash.data[0];



    probeOut.data[1] =
        uint(hashKey & uint64_t(0xFFFFFFFFu));
    probeOut.data[2] = uint(hashKey >> 32u);
    probeOut.data[3] = probeAccumulation.data[0].data.x;
    probeOut.data[4] = probeResolved.data[0].sampleData;
    probeOut.data[5] = probeResolved.data[0].sampleDataExt;
    probeOut.data[6] = cam.sharcCacheParams.y;
}








































bool sharcUpdatePixelSelected()
{
    const ivec2 sharcBlock = ivec2(gl_LaunchIDEXT.xy) / 5;
    const ivec2 sharcPixelInBlock =
        ivec2(gl_LaunchIDEXT.xy) - sharcBlock * 5;
    const uint sharcBlockArea =
        uint(5) * uint(5);



    uint sharcHash = uint(sharcBlock.x) * 73856093u ^
                     uint(sharcBlock.y) * 19349663u ^
                     cam.frameCount * 83492791u;
    sharcHash ^= sharcHash >> 13u;
    sharcHash *= 0x5bd1e995u;
    sharcHash ^= sharcHash >> 15u;

    return(uint(sharcPixelInBlock.y) * uint(5) +
            uint(sharcPixelInBlock.x)) ==
        (sharcHash % sharcBlockArea);
}

layout(binding = 33, set = 0) uniform sampler2DArray waterSimulationState;

float waterSundialHash(ivec2 lattice)
{
    uvec2 v = uvec2(lattice);
    v = v * 1664525u + 1013904223u;
    v.x += v.y * 1664525u;
    v.y += v.x * 1664525u;
    v ^= v >> 16u;
    v.x += v.y * 1664525u;
    v.y += v.x * 1664525u;
    v ^= v >> 16u;
    return float(v.x ^ v.y) / 4294967296.0;
}



vec3 waterSundialValueNoise(ivec2 cell, vec2 f)
{
    const float v00 = waterSundialHash(cell);
    const float v10 = waterSundialHash(cell + ivec2(1, 0));
    const float v01 = waterSundialHash(cell + ivec2(0, 1));
    const float v11 = waterSundialHash(cell + ivec2(1, 1));
    const vec2 wgt = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
    const float value = mix(mix(v00, v10, wgt.x), mix(v01, v11, wgt.x), wgt.y);
    const vec2 dw = 6.0 * f * (1.0 - f);
    return vec3(
        value,
        ( (v10 - v00) * (1.0 - wgt.y) + (v11 - v01) * wgt.y) * dw.x,
        ( (v01 - v00) * (1.0 - wgt.x) + (v11 - v10) * wgt.x) * dw.y
    );
}



vec2 waterSundialProfileDeriv(vec2 coord, float steepness)
{
    const float PI = 3.14159265;
    const float waveSmoothness = 1.0 - 0.7 * steepness;
    const float curveOffset1 = (1.0 - waveSmoothness) / PI;
    const float curveOffset2 = pow(curveOffset1, 1.0 / waveSmoothness);
    const float coordScale = PI / (pow(1.0 + curveOffset2, waveSmoothness) - curveOffset1);

    const vec2 noiseCoord = coord * (0.003 * 64.0) + 0.5;
    const ivec2 noiseCell = ivec2(floor(noiseCoord));
    const vec3 n = waterSundialValueNoise(noiseCell, noiseCoord - vec2(noiseCell));


    vec2 noiseDerv = n.yz * (0.003 * 64.0 * 6.0);
    noiseDerv.x += 1.0;

    const float x =
        fract(coord.x * (0.5 / PI) + n.x * (6.0 * 0.5 / PI)) * 2.0 - 1.0;
    const float power = pow(abs(x) + curveOffset2, waveSmoothness - 1.0);
    return sin(coordScale * power * (abs(x) + curveOffset2) - coordScale * curveOffset1)
        * (x < 0.0 ? - coordScale : coordScale)
        / PI * noiseDerv * waveSmoothness * power;
}

vec2 waterSundialWaveSlopeCapped(vec2 worldXZ, float amplitude, int octaveCap)
{
    const float lambdaMax = clamp(cam.waterWaveShapeParams.x, 1.0, 8.0);
    const int layers =
        clamp(int(cam.waterWaveShapeParams.z + 0.5), 1, min(max(octaveCap, 1), 16));
    const float steepness = clamp(cam.waterFftParams.z * (1.0 / 3.0), 0.0, 1.0);
    const float speedMul = clamp(cam.waterFftParams.w, 0.0, 4.0);
    const float waveTime = uintBitsToFloat(cam.padding) * speedMul;
    const float waveScale = lambdaMax * (1.0 / 3.6);
    const float waveHeight = 0.9 * amplitude;


    const float dirRad = radians(cam.waterFftParams2.x);
    const float dirCos = cos(dirRad);
    const float dirSin = sin(dirRad);


    const mat2 goldenRotate =
        mat2(- 0.73736888, 0.67549029, - 0.67549029, - 0.73736888);
    const mat2 layerRotation = mat2(1.4) * mat2(
        goldenRotate[0].x, goldenRotate[1].x,
        goldenRotate[0].y, goldenRotate[1].y);
    mat2 normalRotation = transpose(goldenRotate) * mat2(32.0);

    const float speed = 2.0 * waveTime;








    vec2 coord = mat2(dirCos, - dirSin, dirSin, dirCos) * (worldXZ / waveScale);

    vec2 totalNormal = vec2(0.0);
    float currentWeight = 1.0;
    for (int i = 0; i < layers; ++ i)
    {
        coord = goldenRotate * coord;
        totalNormal += normalRotation * (currentWeight *
            waterSundialProfileDeriv(coord - vec2(speed, 0.0), steepness));
        normalRotation *= layerRotation;
        currentWeight *= 0.6;
        coord *= 1.4;
    }
    const float totalWeight = (1.0 - 0.6) / (1.0 - pow(0.6, float(layers)));





    return totalNormal * (totalWeight * 0.5) *
        (0.2 * waveHeight / (32.0 * waveScale));
}































float waterFftSlopeLodFromProj(vec3 surfacePos, float rayDistance, float projScale)
{
    if (projScale <= 0.0)
    {
        return 0.0;
    }
    const float tilePeriod = max(cam.waterFftParams.x, 1.0e-3);
    const float texelWorld = tilePeriod / 256.0;
    vec3 toCam = surfacePos - cam.viewInverse[3].xyz;
    float dist = max(length(toCam), 1.0e-3);
    float noV = max(abs(toCam.y) / dist, 0.04);
    float worldPerPixel = max(rayDistance, 1.0e-3) / (projScale * noV);



    return clamp(log2(max(worldPerPixel / texelWorld, 1.0e-4)), - 8.0, 8.0);
}




vec2 waterFftSwellSlope(vec2 worldXZ, float horizonFade, float waveLod)
{


    const float tilePeriod = max(cam.waterFftParams.x, 1.0e-3);
    const vec2 uv = fract(worldXZ / tilePeriod);


    const vec2 slope = textureLod(waterFftSlope, uv, clamp(waveLod, 0.0, 8.0)).rg;





    const float detailLod = clamp(
        waveLod + log2(max(tilePeriod / 32.0, 1.0e-4)),
        0.0,
        8.0
    );
    const vec2 detailUv = fract(worldXZ / 32.0);
    const vec2 detailSlope =
        textureLod(waterFftSlope, detailUv, detailLod).ba;



    return(slope + detailSlope) * horizonFade;
}















vec2 waterNaturalWaveGradientCapped(
    vec2 worldXZ,
    out float cameraDistance,
    int octaveCap,
    float horizonFade,
    float waveLod
)
{


    cameraDistance = 0.0;



    const float amplitude = cam.waterFftParams.y;
    if (amplitude <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec2(0.0);
    }





    return waterFftSwellSlope(worldXZ, horizonFade, waveLod);





}




const int WATER_WAVE_FULL_OCTAVE_CAP = 16;

vec2 waterNaturalWaveGradient(vec2 worldXZ, out float cameraDistance, float horizonFade, float waveLod)
{
    return waterNaturalWaveGradientCapped(
        worldXZ,
        cameraDistance,
        WATER_WAVE_FULL_OCTAVE_CAP,
        horizonFade,
        waveLod
    );
}











vec2 waterBlendWaveSlopes(vec2 slope0, vec2 slope1, float mode)
{
    if (mode < 0.001)
    {
        return slope0 + slope1;
    }
    float a = sqrt(1.0 + dot(slope0, slope0));
    float b = sqrt(1.0 + dot(slope1, slope1));
    vec2 blended = slope0 * b + slope1 * a;
    return mix(slope0 + slope1, blended, clamp(mode, 0.0, 1.0));
}


float waterWaveBlendMode()
{
    return clamp(cam.waterWaveParams.z, 0.0, 1.0);
}








vec2 waterInteractionSlope(vec2 worldXZ, out float stateAndBorderMask)
{
    stateAndBorderMask = 0.0;









    float worldTexel = cam.waterSimSurfaceAndOrigin.w;
    if (cam.waterSimState.y == 0u || worldTexel <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec2(0.0);
    }

    vec2 simulationUV =
        (worldXZ - cam.waterSimSurfaceAndOrigin.yz) /
        (worldTexel * 512.0);




    vec2 simulationBorder = smoothstep(
        vec2(0.0), vec2(0.12), simulationUV
    ) * (1.0 - smoothstep(
        vec2(0.88), vec2(1.0), simulationUV
    ));
    stateAndBorderMask = simulationBorder.x * simulationBorder.y;
    if (stateAndBorderMask <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec2(0.0);
    }

    float stateLayer = float(cam.waterSimState.x);
    vec2 uvStep = vec2(1.0 / 512.0);
    float heightLeft = texture(
        waterSimulationState,
        vec3(simulationUV - vec2(uvStep.x, 0.0), stateLayer)
    ).r;
    float heightRight = texture(
        waterSimulationState,
        vec3(simulationUV + vec2(uvStep.x, 0.0), stateLayer)
    ).r;
    float heightDown = texture(
        waterSimulationState,
        vec3(simulationUV - vec2(0.0, uvStep.y), stateLayer)
    ).r;
    float heightUp = texture(
        waterSimulationState,
        vec3(simulationUV + vec2(0.0, uvStep.y), stateLayer)
    ).r;













    float calibration = clamp(cam.waterWaveParams.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    float strength = clamp(cam.waterWaveParams.y, 0.0, 2.0);
    float interactionScale = mix(0.19, 0.5, calibration) * strength;
    return vec2(
        heightRight - heightLeft,
        heightUp - heightDown
    ) * (interactionScale / max(worldTexel, 1.0e-4));

}

#line 1 "./glass_tint_common.glsl"









































const uint VPT_STAINED_GLASS_BIT = 1u << 24u;

bool vptReadSourceVoxel(ivec3 worldBlock, out uint source);















































bool glassVertexFactorIsFaceShade(vec3 factor)
{
    float grey = factor.r;
    if (abs(factor.g - grey) > (1.0 / 255.0) ||
        abs(factor.b - grey) > (1.0 / 255.0))
    {
        return false;
    }

    return abs(grey - 1.0) < 0.01 ||
        abs(grey - 0.8) < 0.01 ||
        abs(grey - 0.6) < 0.01 ||
        abs(grey - 0.5) < 0.01;
}

vec3 glassPaneTransmissionColor(vec3 spriteRgb, vec3 vertexRgb)
{
    vec3 sprite = clamp(spriteRgb, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));
    vec3 vertex = clamp(vertexRgb, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));

    if (glassVertexFactorIsFaceShade(vertex))
    {
        return sprite * vertex;
    }

    return min(sprite, vertex);
}













vec3 glassDecodeVoxelAlbedo(uint source)
{
    return vec3(
        float( (source >> 9u) & 31u),
        float( (source >> 14u) & 31u),
        float( (source >> 19u) & 31u)
    ) * (1.0 / 31.0);
}








const float STAINED_GLASS_TRANSMISSION_SATURATION = 3.0;

vec3 vptSaturateGlassTransmission(vec3 transmission)
{
    float peak = max(transmission.r, max(transmission.g, transmission.b));
    return clamp(
        vec3(peak) +
            (transmission - vec3(peak)) *
            STAINED_GLASS_TRANSMISSION_SATURATION,
        vec3(0.0),
        vec3(peak)
    );
}















const float LOCAL_GLASS_TINT_SATURATION = 1.55;
const vec3 LOCAL_GLASS_TINT_LUMA = vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722);

vec3 strengthenLocalGlassTint(
    vec3 transmittance
)
{
    vec3 t =
        clamp(transmittance, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));

    float peak =
        max(t.r, max(t.g, t.b));


    if (peak <= 1.0e-4)
    {
        return t;
    }

    vec3 chroma =
        clamp(
            mix(
                vec3(1.0),
                t / peak,
                LOCAL_GLASS_TINT_SATURATION
            ),
            vec3(0.0),
            vec3(1.0)
        );




    float targetLuminance =
        dot(t, LOCAL_GLASS_TINT_LUMA);

    float chromaLuminance =
        dot(chroma, LOCAL_GLASS_TINT_LUMA);

    return
        clamp(
            chroma *
            (targetLuminance / max(chromaLuminance, 1.0e-4)),
            vec3(0.0),
            vec3(1.0)
        );
}



















const float LOCAL_GLASS_TINT_NEUTRAL_SPREAD = 0.08;









const float LOCAL_GLASS_TINT_ABSORPTION = 0.0;

vec3 glassTintExposureFold(vec3 value)
{
    float peak = max(value.r, max(value.g, value.b));
    if (peak <= 1.0)
    {
        return value;
    }
    return value * (1.0 / peak);
}



vec3 glassStainedPoolRadiance(vec3 radiance, vec3 transmittance)
{
    vec3 paneTransit =
        clamp(transmittance, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));
    vec3 tint =
        strengthenLocalGlassTint(paneTransit);

    float tintPeak = max(tint.r, max(tint.g, tint.b));
    float tintFloor = min(tint.r, min(tint.g, tint.b));
    if (tintPeak - tintFloor <= LOCAL_GLASS_TINT_NEUTRAL_SPREAD)
    {
        return max(radiance, vec3(0.0)) * tint;
    }












    return
        max(radiance, vec3(0.0)) *
        tint *
        pow(
            max(dot(paneTransit, LOCAL_GLASS_TINT_LUMA), 0.0),
            LOCAL_GLASS_TINT_ABSORPTION
        );
}




vec3 glassApplyStainedTint(vec3 radiance, vec3 transmittance)
{
    return glassTintExposureFold(
        glassStainedPoolRadiance(radiance, transmittance)
    );
}


















































vec3 glassTraceTransmissionToEmitter(
    vec3 origin,
    vec3 target,
    float targetVoxelEntryDistance
)
{
    vec3 segment = target - origin;
    float distanceToTarget = length(segment);
    if (distanceToTarget <= 0.03)
    {
        return vec3(1.0);
    }




    float rayMaxDistance = min(
        distanceToTarget - 0.03,
        targetVoxelEntryDistance - 0.03
    );
    if (rayMaxDistance <= 0.02)
    {
        return vec3(1.0);
    }

    vec3 direction = segment / distanceToTarget;
    ivec3 cell = ivec3(floor(origin));
    ivec3 step = ivec3(sign(direction));
    vec3 invAbs = 1.0 / max(abs(direction), vec3(1.0e-6));
    vec3 boundary = vec3(cell) + vec3(max(step, ivec3(0)));
    vec3 tMax = (boundary - origin) / direction;
    if (step.x == 0) tMax.x = 1.0e30;
    if (step.y == 0) tMax.y = 1.0e30;
    if (step.z == 0) tMax.z = 1.0e30;

    int maxTraversal = min(
        96,
        int(ceil(distanceToTarget * dot(abs(direction), vec3(1.0)))) + 2
    );
    vec3 glassTransmission = vec3(1.0);

    for (int traversal = 0; traversal < maxTraversal; ++ traversal)
    {
        int axis = tMax.x < tMax.y
            ? (tMax.x < tMax.z ? 0 : 2)
            : (tMax.y < tMax.z ? 1 : 2);
        float travel = axis == 0 ? tMax.x : (axis == 1 ? tMax.y : tMax.z);
        if (travel >= rayMaxDistance)
        {
            break;
        }

        if (axis == 0)
        {
            cell.x += step.x;
            tMax.x += invAbs.x;
        }
        else if (axis == 1)
        {
            cell.y += step.y;
            tMax.y += invAbs.y;
        }
        else
        {
            cell.z += step.z;
            tMax.z += invAbs.z;
        }

        uint source;
        if (! vptReadSourceVoxel(cell, source))
        {
            continue;
        }

        if ( (source & VPT_STAINED_GLASS_BIT) != 0u)
        {
            glassTransmission *=
                max(glassDecodeVoxelAlbedo(source), vec3(1.0 / 31.0));
        }
    }

    return clamp(
        vptSaturateGlassTransmission(glassTransmission),
        vec3(0.0),
        vec3(1.0)
    );
}


#line 495 "rt_test.rgen"


#line 1 "./glass_dielectric_common.glsl"
















































































const float GLASS_IOR_INVERSE = 1.0 / 1.5;








float glassIorForProfile(int transparentProfile)
{
    return transparentProfile == 2 ? 1.309 : 1.5;
}







float glassFresnelDielectric(float cosThetaI, float eta)
{
    float c = clamp(cosThetaI, 0.0, 1.0);
    float s2 = eta * eta * (1.0 - c * c);
    if (s2 >= 1.0)
    {
        return 1.0;
    }

    float cosThetaT = sqrt(max(1.0 - s2, 0.0));
    float denomS = eta * c + cosThetaT;
    float denomP = c + eta * cosThetaT;
    float rs = denomS > 1.0e-6
        ? (eta * c - cosThetaT) / denomS
        : 0.0;
    float rp = denomP > 1.0e-6
        ? (c - eta * cosThetaT) / denomP
        : 0.0;
    return clamp(0.5 * (rs * rs + rp * rp), 0.0, 1.0);
}






bool glassRefractDirection(
    vec3 incidentDirection,
    vec3 interfaceNormal,
    float eta,
    out vec3 transmittedDirection
)
{
    transmittedDirection = refract(incidentDirection, interfaceNormal, eta);
    return dot(transmittedDirection, transmittedDirection) > 1.0e-8;
}






vec3 glassSegmentTransmittance(vec3 blockTint, float lengthInBlocks)
{
    vec3 t = clamp(blockTint, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));
    float len = max(lengthInBlocks, 0.0);
    return max(
        pow(t, vec3(len)),
        vec3(1.0e-4)
    );
}





bool glassIsThinSheet(float thicknessBlocks)
{
    return thicknessBlocks <= 0.25;
}





struct GlassPathState
{

    bool insideGlass;

    int interfaceSteps;

    vec3 accumulatedTint;

    float accumulatedFresnel;
};

GlassPathState glassPathStateInit()
{
    GlassPathState state;
    state.insideGlass = false;
    state.interfaceSteps = 0;
    state.accumulatedTint = vec3(1.0);
    state.accumulatedFresnel = 1.0;
    return state;
}









bool glassCrossInterface(
    inout GlassPathState state,
    vec3 incidentDirection,
    vec3 outwardNormal,
    vec3 blockTint,
    out vec3 reflectedDirection,
    out vec3 refractedDirection,
    out float fresnelReflectance,
    out bool totalInternalReflection
)
{
    vec3 n = normalize(outwardNormal);
    if (dot(incidentDirection, n) > 0.0)
    {

        n = - n;
    }

    float etaI = state.insideGlass ? 1.5 : 1.0;
    float etaT = state.insideGlass ? 1.0 : 1.5;
    float eta = etaI / etaT;

    float cosThetaI = clamp(dot(- incidentDirection, n), 0.0, 1.0);
    fresnelReflectance = glassFresnelDielectric(cosThetaI, eta);
    reflectedDirection = reflect(incidentDirection, n);

    totalInternalReflection = ! glassRefractDirection(
        incidentDirection, n, eta, refractedDirection
    );

    if (totalInternalReflection)
    {
        fresnelReflectance = 1.0;
        refractedDirection = reflectedDirection;
    }


    state.accumulatedTint = max(state.accumulatedTint * clamp(blockTint, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0)), vec3(0.0));
    state.accumulatedFresnel *= clamp(1.0 - fresnelReflectance, 0.0, 1.0);
    state.insideGlass = ! state.insideGlass;
    state.interfaceSteps += 1;

    return state.interfaceSteps <= 4;
}










float glassSlabThicknessFromFace(vec3 hitPosition, vec3 faceNormal)
{
    vec3 n = normalize(faceNormal);
    vec3 cellLocal = fract(hitPosition) - vec3(0.5);
    float halfThickness = abs(dot(cellLocal, n));


    return clamp(
        2.0 * halfThickness,
                               0.125,
        1.0
    );
}































vec3 glassSlabExitPoint(
    vec3 entryPoint,
    vec3 outwardNormal,
    vec3 incidentDir,
    float thickness,
    float ior
)
{
    vec3 n = normalize(outwardNormal);
    if (dot(incidentDir, n) > 0.0)
    {
        n = - n;
    }

    float eta = 1.0 / max(ior, 1.0);
    vec3 insideDirection = refract(incidentDir, n, eta);
    if (dot(insideDirection, insideDirection) < 1.0e-8)
    {
        insideDirection = incidentDir;
    }


    float cosInside = max(- dot(insideDirection, n), 1.0e-4);
    vec3 exitPoint =
        entryPoint + insideDirection * (max(thickness, 0.0) / cosInside);



    vec3 glassCellMin = floor(entryPoint + normalize(incidentDir) * 1.0e-3);
    vec3 glassCellMax = glassCellMin + vec3(1.0);

    return clamp(
        exitPoint,
        glassCellMin + vec3(0.05),
        glassCellMax - vec3(0.05)
    );
}


float glassSlabTravelAlongView(
    vec3 outwardNormal,
    vec3 viewDir,
    float thickness
)
{
    vec3 n = normalize(outwardNormal);
    float cosView = max(abs(dot(normalize(viewDir), n)), 1.0e-4);
    return max(thickness, 0.0) / cosView;
}


float glassSlabInsidePathLength(
    vec3 outwardNormal,
    vec3 viewDir,
    float thickness,
    float ior
)
{
    vec3 n = normalize(outwardNormal);
    vec3 incident = normalize(viewDir);
    if (dot(incident, n) > 0.0)
    {
        n = - n;
    }

    vec3 insideDirection = refract(incident, n, 1.0 / max(ior, 1.0));
    if (dot(insideDirection, insideDirection) < 1.0e-8)
    {
        insideDirection = incident;
    }

    float cosInside = max(- dot(insideDirection, n), 1.0e-4);
    return max(thickness, 0.0) / cosInside;
}

vec2 directionToEquirect(vec3 dir)
{
    const float TWO_PI = 6.28318530718;



    float u = fract(atan(dir.z, dir.x) / TWO_PI);
    float v = asin(clamp(dir.y, - 1.0, 1.0)) / 3.14159265359 + 0.5;
    return vec2(u, v);
}

#line 1 "./sun_shadow.glsl"

















































const int SUN_SHADOW_RESOLUTION = 1024;


const float SUN_SHADOW_TEXEL = 1.0 / 1024.0;
const float SUN_SHADOW_CENTER_QUANTUM = 1.0 / 256.0;






















const float SUN_SHADOW_EXTENT_XY = 160.0;








const float SUN_SHADOW_OUTSIDE_VISIBILITY = 0.08;
const float SUN_SHADOW_OUTSIDE_FADE_BLOCKS = 32.0;











const float SUN_SHADOW_EXTENT_Z = 160.0;








const float SUN_SHADOW_BIAS = 0.45;






const float SUN_SHADOW_BIAS_NEAR = 0.06;


const float SUN_SHADOW_ORIGIN_BIAS = 0.02;







const uint SUN_SHADOW_CULL_MASK = 0x04u;






vec3 sunShadowLightDirection()
{
    const float TWO_PI = 6.28318530718;
    float angle = fract(cam.historyParams.w) * TWO_PI;
    vec3 direction = normalize(vec3(cos(angle), 0.81915204429 * sin(angle),
        0.57357643635 * sin(angle)));
    return direction.y >= 0.0 ? direction : - direction;
}








vec3 sunShadowCenterFor(float extentXY, float texel)
{
    float texelWorld = 2.0 * extentXY * texel;
    vec3 cameraPosition = cam.viewInverse[3].xyz;
    return floor(cameraPosition / texelWorld) * texelWorld;
}

vec3 sunShadowCenter()
{


    return sunShadowCenterFor(SUN_SHADOW_EXTENT_XY, SUN_SHADOW_CENTER_QUANTUM);
}


void sunShadowBasis(vec3 sunDirection, out vec3 basisRight, out vec3 basisUp)
{
    vec3 reference = abs(sunDirection.y) > 0.99
        ? vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0)
        : vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);
    basisRight = normalize(cross(reference, sunDirection));
    basisUp = cross(sunDirection, basisRight);
}




















vec3 sunShadowRayOrigin(vec2 uv, vec3 sunDirection, vec3 center)
{
    vec3 basisRight;
    vec3 basisUp;
    sunShadowBasis(sunDirection, basisRight, basisUp);
    vec2 offset = (uv * 2.0 - 1.0) * SUN_SHADOW_EXTENT_XY;
    return center +
        basisRight * offset.x +
        basisUp * offset.y +
        sunDirection * SUN_SHADOW_EXTENT_Z;
}



vec3 sunShadowRayOriginFar(vec2 uv, vec3 sunDirection, vec3 center,
    float extentXY, float extentZ)
{
    vec3 basisRight;
    vec3 basisUp;
    sunShadowBasis(sunDirection, basisRight, basisUp);
    vec2 offset = (uv * 2.0 - 1.0) * extentXY;
    return center +
        basisRight * offset.x +
        basisUp * offset.y +
        sunDirection * extentZ;
}








































vec2 sunShadowJitter(int sampleIndex)
{
    uint hash = uint(cam.frameCount) * 747796405u +
        uint(sampleIndex) * 2246822519u + 2891336453u;
    hash ^= hash >> 16;
    hash *= 0x7feb352du;
    hash ^= hash >> 15;
    hash *= 0x846ca68bu;
    hash ^= hash >> 16;
    return vec2(float(hash & 0xffffu), float( (hash >> 16) & 0xffffu)) /
        65535.0 - 0.5;
}




















float sunShadowVisibilityIn(
    sampler2D shadowMap,
    vec3 position,
    vec3 sunDirection,
    int sampleIndex,
    float extentXY,
    float extentZ,
    float texel,
    float centerQuantum,
    float outsideVisibility,
    float bias,
    out float outsideFade01)
{




    vec3 basisRight;
    vec3 basisUp;
    sunShadowBasis(sunDirection, basisRight, basisUp);
    vec3 local = position - sunShadowCenterFor(extentXY, centerQuantum);
    vec2 uv = vec2(dot(local, basisRight), dot(local, basisUp)) /
        (2.0 * extentXY) + 0.5;

    float outsideDistance = max(
        max(- uv.x, uv.x - 1.0),
        max(- uv.y, uv.y - 1.0)) * (2.0 * extentXY);















    float depthSigned = dot(local, sunDirection);
    outsideDistance = max(outsideDistance,
        max(- depthSigned - extentZ,
            depthSigned - extentZ));

    if (outsideDistance > 0.0)
    {










        outsideFade01 = smoothstep(
            0.0, SUN_SHADOW_OUTSIDE_FADE_BLOCKS, outsideDistance);
        return mix(
            1.0,
            outsideVisibility,
            outsideFade01);
    }
    outsideFade01 = 0.0;




    float depth = extentZ - dot(local, sunDirection);
    vec2 sampleUv = uv + sunShadowJitter(sampleIndex) * texel;



























    float stored = texture(shadowMap, sampleUv).r;
    return depth < stored + bias ? 1.0 : 0.0;

}








































const float SUN_SHADOW_FAR_OUTSIDE_VISIBILITY = 1.0;






float sunShadowFarVisibility(vec3 position, vec3 sunDirection, int sampleIndex)
{
    float ignoredOutsideFade;
    return sunShadowVisibilityIn(sunShadowMapFar, position, sunDirection, sampleIndex,
        cam.sunShadowFarParams.x, cam.sunShadowFarParams.y, cam.sunShadowFarParams.z,
        cam.sunShadowFarParams.z,
        SUN_SHADOW_FAR_OUTSIDE_VISIBILITY, SUN_SHADOW_BIAS, ignoredOutsideFade);
}
