vec3 dbgHuePurity2(vec3 transmission)
{
    vec3 v = max(transmission, vec3(0.0));
    float peak = max(v.r, max(v.g, v.b));
    float floorValue = min(v.r, min(v.g, v.b));
    bool isPureWhite = all(equal(v, vec3(1.0)));
    if (peak <= 1.0e-6)
    {
        return vec3(0.0, 1.0, isPureWhite ? 1.0 : 0.0);
    }
    float second = 0.0;
    if (peak == v.r)
    {
        second = max(v.g, v.b);
    }
    else if (peak == v.g)
    {
        second = max(v.r, v.b);
    }
    else
    {
        second = max(v.r, v.g);
    }
    return vec3(
        clamp(1.0 - (peak - floorValue) / peak, 0.0, 1.0),
        clamp(1.0 - second / peak, 0.0, 1.0),
        isPureWhite ? 1.0 : 0.0
    );
}

const bool DBG_GLASS_PEAK_READOUT = false;
const float DBG_PEAK_SCALE = 64.0;

const vec3 DBG_TERM_LUMA = vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722);

float dbgTermLuma(vec3 value)
{
    return clamp(dot(max(value, vec3(0.0)), DBG_TERM_LUMA), 0.0, 1.0);
}


float dbgDyeConcentration(vec3 value)
{
    vec3 v = max(value, vec3(0.0));
    return clamp(
        max(v.r, max(v.g, v.b)) - min(v.r, min(v.g, v.b)),
        0.0,
        1.0
    );
}




const float LOCAL_LIGHT_ENERGY_SCALE = 0.35;















float localLightIntensity()
{
    float user = uintBitsToFloat(cam.historyPadding1) - 1.0;
    return(user >= 0.0 && user <= 16.0) ? user : 1.0;
}






vec3 toneKeepHueLocal(vec3 value)
{

    if (DBG_GLASS_DYE_VS_ENERGY || DBG_GLASS_DYE_VS_ENERGY_SECONDARY)
    {
        return value;
    }
    return glassTintExposureFold(value);
}

const float VPT_LOCAL_BOUNCE_MAX_DISTANCE = 12.0;


uint vliHash32(ivec3 sectionCoord)
{
    uint h = uint(sectionCoord.x) * 0x8da6b343u;
    h ^= uint(sectionCoord.y) * 0xd8163841u;
    h ^= uint(sectionCoord.z) * 0xcb1ab31fu;
    h ^= h >> 16u;
    h *= 0x7feb352du;
    h ^= h >> 15u;
    h *= 0x846ca68bu;
    h ^= h >> 16u;
    return h;
}


bool vliLookupSectionPage(ivec3 sectionCoord, out uint pageIndex)
{
    uint slot = vliHash32(sectionCoord) & (VLI_PAGE_TABLE_CAPACITY - 1u);
    for (uint probe = 0u; probe < VLI_PAGE_TABLE_CAPACITY; ++ probe)
    {
        uint base = slot * 4u;
        uint pagePlusOne = vliPageTableBuffer.words[base + 3u];
        if (pagePlusOne == 0u)
        {
            pageIndex = 0u;
            return false;
        }

        if (
            int(vliPageTableBuffer.words[base + 0u]) == sectionCoord.x &&
            int(vliPageTableBuffer.words[base + 1u]) == sectionCoord.y &&
            int(vliPageTableBuffer.words[base + 2u]) == sectionCoord.z
        )
        {
            pageIndex = pagePlusOne - 1u;
            return true;
        }

        slot = (slot + 1u) & (VLI_PAGE_TABLE_CAPACITY - 1u);
    }

    pageIndex = 0u;
    return false;
}


int vliFloorDiv(int value, int divisor)
{
    return value >= 0 ? value / divisor : - ( (- value + divisor - 1) / divisor);
}


int vliPositiveMod(int value, int divisor)
{
    int result = value % divisor;
    return result < 0 ? result + divisor : result;
}




















bool g_vptMemoPageValid = false;
ivec3 g_vptMemoSection = ivec3(0);
uint g_vptMemoPage = 0u;


uint vliReadSourceVoxel(ivec3 worldBlock)
{
    ivec3 sectionCoord = ivec3(
        vliFloorDiv(worldBlock.x, 16),
        vliFloorDiv(worldBlock.y, 16),
        vliFloorDiv(worldBlock.z, 16)
    );
    uint pageIndex;


    if (g_vptMemoPageValid && all(equal(g_vptMemoSection, sectionCoord)))
    {
        pageIndex = g_vptMemoPage;
    }
    else
    {
        if (! vliLookupSectionPage(sectionCoord, pageIndex))
        {
            return 0u;
        }
        g_vptMemoSection = sectionCoord;
        g_vptMemoPage = pageIndex;
        g_vptMemoPageValid = true;
    }







    ivec3 local = ivec3(
        vliPositiveMod(worldBlock.x, 16),
        vliPositiveMod(worldBlock.y, 16),
        vliPositiveMod(worldBlock.z, 16)
    );
    uint index = uint(local.x) + 16u * (uint(local.z) + 16u * uint(local.y));
    return vliPagesBuffer.words[pageIndex * VLI_PAGE_WORDS + index];
}


vec3 vliDecodeEmitterColor(uint typeCode)
{
    if (typeCode == 1u) return vec3(1.0, 0.55, 0.20);
    if (typeCode == 2u) return vec3(0.20, 0.75, 1.0);
    if (typeCode == 3u) return vec3(1.0, 0.12, 0.06);
    if (typeCode == 4u) return vec3(1.0, 0.27, 0.05);


    if (typeCode == 6u) return vec3(0.70, 0.28, 1.0);
    return vec3(1.0, 0.82, 0.48);
}

vec3 sampleHemisphereCosine(vec3 normal, inout uint state);
vec3 vptDominantBlockFaceNormal(vec3 normal);




vec3 applyLocalGlassTint(vec3 radiance, vec3 transmittance);

















const uint VPT_PARTIAL_TRANSMISSION_SHIFT = 25u;
const uint VPT_PARTIAL_TRANSMISSION_MAX = 127u;

float vptPartialTransmission(uint source)
{
    return float( (source >> VPT_PARTIAL_TRANSMISSION_SHIFT) &
        VPT_PARTIAL_TRANSMISSION_MAX) * (1.0 / 127.0);
}


bool vptVoxelOccupied(uint source)
{
    uint voxelClass = source & 3u;
    return voxelClass == 1u || voxelClass == 3u;
}

vec3 evaluateOpaqueSurfaceRadiance(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 viewDir,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    float hitRoughness,
    float hitF0Metal,
    vec3 hitEmission,
    float pomSelfShadow,
    vec3 sunDirection
);


































vec3 evaluateVLIEmitterRadianceAtHit(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 hitAlbedo
)
{
    vec3 blockFaceNormal = vptDominantBlockFaceNormal(hitNormal);

    {
        vec3 fracPos = fract(hitPos);
        vec3 distToFace = min(fracPos, vec3(1.0) - fracPos);
        float nearestFace = min(distToFace.x, min(distToFace.y, distToFace.z));
        if (nearestFace <= 1.0e-3)
        {


            vec3 sampleNormal = normalize(hitNormal);
            int axis = 0;
            if (distToFace.y == nearestFace)
            {
                axis = 1;
            }
            else if (distToFace.z == nearestFace)
            {
                axis = 2;
            }
            vec3 geometricFace = vec3(0.0);
            geometricFace[axis] = sampleNormal[axis] >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : - 1.0;
            blockFaceNormal = geometricFace;
        }
    }

    ivec3 sourceBlock;
    uint source = 0u;






























    sourceBlock = ivec3(floor(hitPos - blockFaceNormal * 0.02));
    source = vliReadSourceVoxel(sourceBlock);

    uint emission = (source >> 2u) & 15u;
    if (emission == 0u)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }



    vec3 safeAlbedo = max(hitAlbedo, vec3(0.0));
    vec3 emitterTerm = vliDecodeEmitterColor( (source >> 6u) & 7u) *
        3.2 *
        LOCAL_LIGHT_ENERGY_SCALE * localLightIntensity() *
        (float(emission) / 15.0) *
        safeAlbedo;

    if (DBG_KILL_VLI_EMITTER)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    if (DBG_MARK_VLI_EMITTER)
    {
        return vec3(1.0, 0.0, 1.0) * dbgTermLuma(emitterTerm) * 20.0;
    }









    if (DBG_LOCAL_TERM_MAP)
    {
        return vec3(0.0, 0.0, dbgTermLuma(emitterTerm));
    }

    if (DBG_GLASS_DYE_VS_ENERGY)
    {


        return vec3(
            dbgDyeConcentration(vliDecodeEmitterColor( (source >> 6u) & 7u)),
            dbgTermLuma(emitterTerm),
            0.0
        );
    }
    return emitterTerm;
}


vec3 vliUnpackRadiance(uint packed)
{
    return vec3(
        float(packed & 255u),
        float( (packed >> 8u) & 255u),
        float( (packed >> 16u) & 255u)
    ) * (1.0 / 32.0);
}

vec3 vptDominantBlockFaceNormal(vec3 normal)
{
    vec3 n = normalize(normal);
    vec3 a = abs(n);
    if (a.x >= a.y && a.x >= a.z)
    {
        return vec3(n.x >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : - 1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    }
    if (a.y >= a.z)
    {
        return vec3(0.0, n.y >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : - 1.0, 0.0);
    }
    return vec3(0.0, 0.0, n.z >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : - 1.0);
}












vec3 vptGeometricFaceNormalFromRay(vec3 hitPos, vec3 rayDirection)
{
    vec3 direction = normalize(rayDirection);
    ivec3 blockOutside = ivec3(floor(hitPos - direction * 1.0e-3));
    ivec3 blockInside = ivec3(floor(hitPos + direction * 1.0e-3));
    ivec3 delta = blockInside - blockOutside;
    if (delta.x != 0) return vec3(delta.x > 0 ? - 1.0 : 1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    if (delta.y != 0) return vec3(0.0, delta.y > 0 ? - 1.0 : 1.0, 0.0);
    if (delta.z != 0) return vec3(0.0, 0.0, delta.z > 0 ? - 1.0 : 1.0);
    return vec3(0.0);
}

uint vptFaceIndexFromBlockFaceNormal(vec3 normal)
{
    if (normal.x > 0.5) return 0u;
    if (normal.x < - 0.5) return 1u;
    if (normal.y > 0.5) return 2u;
    if (normal.y < - 0.5) return 3u;
    if (normal.z > 0.5) return 4u;
    return 5u;
}





bool vptBakedFaceSample(ivec3 block, uint dirIndex, out vec3 value)
{
    ivec3 sectionCoord = ivec3(
        vliFloorDiv(block.x, 16),
        vliFloorDiv(block.y, 16),
        vliFloorDiv(block.z, 16)
    );
    uint pageIndex;
    if (! vliLookupSectionPage(sectionCoord, pageIndex))
    {
        value = vec3(0.0);
        return false;
    }

    ivec3 local = ivec3(
        vliPositiveMod(block.x, 16),
        vliPositiveMod(block.y, 16),
        vliPositiveMod(block.z, 16)
    );
    uint voxelIndex = uint(local.x) + 16u *
        (uint(local.z) + 16u * uint(local.y));
    uint source = vliPagesBuffer.words[
        pageIndex * VLI_PAGE_WORDS + voxelIndex
    ];



    if (! vptVoxelOccupied(source))
    {
        value = vec3(0.0);
        return false;
    }

    value = vliUnpackRadiance(vptSurfaceFaceBuffer.words[
        pageIndex * VPT_SURFACE_FACE_WORDS +
        voxelIndex * VPT_DIRECTION_COUNT +
        dirIndex
    ]);
    return true;
}








const float VPT_EXACT_FACE_DIFFUSE_SCALE =
    0.92 / 3.14159265359;





vec3 vptExactFaceDirectReadout(vec3 value)
{
    return DBG_LOCAL_TERM_MAP
        ? vec3(dbgTermLuma(value), 0.0, 0.0)
        : value;
}











vec3 evaluateVPTExactFaceDirectLighting(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 shadingNormal,
    vec3 hitAlbedo
)
{
    vec3 blockFaceNormal = vptDominantBlockFaceNormal(shadingNormal);
    uint dirIndex = vptFaceIndexFromBlockFaceNormal(blockFaceNormal);
    ivec3 block = ivec3(floor(hitPos - blockFaceNormal * 0.02));










    vec3 axes[3] = vec3[3] (
        vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0),
        vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0),
        vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)
    );
    int faceAxis = blockFaceNormal.x != 0.0 ? 0 :
        (blockFaceNormal.y != 0.0 ? 1 : 2);
    vec3 unitU = axes[faceAxis == 0 ? 1 : 0];
    vec3 unitV = axes[faceAxis == 2 ? 1 : 2];
    ivec3 stepU = ivec3(unitU);
    ivec3 stepV = ivec3(unitV);







    float coordU = dot(hitPos, unitU) - 0.5;
    float coordV = dot(hitPos, unitV) - 0.5;
    float fracU = fract(coordU);
    float fracV = fract(coordV);
    ivec3 cellBase = ivec3(
        unitU * (floor(coordU) - dot(vec3(block), unitU))
    ) + ivec3(
        unitV * (floor(coordV) - dot(vec3(block), unitV))
    );

    vec3 sum = vec3(0.0);
    float totalWeight = 0.0;


    vec3 ownSample = vec3(0.0);
    bool hasOwnSample = vptBakedFaceSample(block, dirIndex, ownSample);
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++ i)
    {
        float weightU = (i & 1) == 0 ? (1.0 - fracU) : fracU;
        float weightV = (i & 2) == 0 ? (1.0 - fracV) : fracV;
        float weight = weightU * weightV;
        ivec3 cell = block + cellBase;
        if ( (i & 1) != 0)
        {
            cell += stepU;
        }
        if ( (i & 2) != 0)
        {
            cell += stepV;
        }

        vec3 bakedSample;
        if (vptBakedFaceSample(cell, dirIndex, bakedSample))
        {
            sum += bakedSample * weight;
            totalWeight += weight;
        }

        else if (hasOwnSample)
        {











            sum += ownSample * weight;
            totalWeight += weight;
        }

    }

    if (totalWeight <= 0.0)
    {

        vec3 own;
        if (! vptBakedFaceSample(block, dirIndex, own))
        {
            return vec3(0.0);
        }
        return vptExactFaceDirectReadout(
            own * max(hitAlbedo, vec3(0.0)) * VPT_EXACT_FACE_DIFFUSE_SCALE
        );
    }

    return vptExactFaceDirectReadout(
        (sum / totalWeight) * max(hitAlbedo, vec3(0.0)) *
        VPT_EXACT_FACE_DIFFUSE_SCALE
    );
}




bool vptProbeHasRadiance(uint packed)
{
    return(packed & 0x7f000000u) != 0u &&
           (packed & 0x80000000u) == 0u;
}



bool vptReadSourceVoxel(ivec3 worldBlock, out uint source)
{
    ivec3 sectionCoord = ivec3(
        vliFloorDiv(worldBlock.x, 16),
        vliFloorDiv(worldBlock.y, 16),
        vliFloorDiv(worldBlock.z, 16)
    );
    uint pageIndex;
    if (g_vptMemoPageValid && all(equal(g_vptMemoSection, sectionCoord)))
    {
        pageIndex = g_vptMemoPage;
    }
    else
    {
        if (! vliLookupSectionPage(sectionCoord, pageIndex)) return false;
        g_vptMemoSection = sectionCoord;
        g_vptMemoPage = pageIndex;
        g_vptMemoPageValid = true;
    }
    ivec3 local = ivec3(
        vliPositiveMod(worldBlock.x, 16),
        vliPositiveMod(worldBlock.y, 16),
        vliPositiveMod(worldBlock.z, 16)
    );
    uint index = uint(local.x) + 16u * (uint(local.z) + 16u * uint(local.y));
    source = vliPagesBuffer.words[pageIndex * VLI_PAGE_WORDS + index];
    return true;
}



















struct VptVoxelCursor
{
    ivec3 section;
    ivec3 local;
};

VptVoxelCursor vptMakeCursor(ivec3 cell)
{
    VptVoxelCursor cursor;
    cursor.section = ivec3(
        vliFloorDiv(cell.x, 16),
        vliFloorDiv(cell.y, 16),
        vliFloorDiv(cell.z, 16)
    );
    cursor.local = cell - cursor.section * 16;
    return cursor;
}


void vptCursorStep(inout VptVoxelCursor cursor, int axis, int stepSign)
{
    if (axis == 0)
    {
        cursor.local.x += stepSign;
        if (cursor.local.x >= 16)
        {
            cursor.local.x = 0;
            cursor.section.x += 1;
        }
        else if (cursor.local.x < 0)
        {
            cursor.local.x = 15;
            cursor.section.x -= 1;
        }
    }
    else if (axis == 1)
    {
        cursor.local.y += stepSign;
        if (cursor.local.y >= 16)
        {
            cursor.local.y = 0;
            cursor.section.y += 1;
        }
        else if (cursor.local.y < 0)
        {
            cursor.local.y = 15;
            cursor.section.y -= 1;
        }
    }
    else
    {
        cursor.local.z += stepSign;
        if (cursor.local.z >= 16)
        {
            cursor.local.z = 0;
            cursor.section.z += 1;
        }
        else if (cursor.local.z < 0)
        {
            cursor.local.z = 15;
            cursor.section.z -= 1;
        }
    }
}


bool vptReadSourceVoxelAt(VptVoxelCursor cursor, out uint source)
{
    uint pageIndex;
    if (g_vptMemoPageValid && all(equal(g_vptMemoSection, cursor.section)))
    {
        pageIndex = g_vptMemoPage;
    }
    else
    {
        if (! vliLookupSectionPage(cursor.section, pageIndex)) return false;
        g_vptMemoSection = cursor.section;
        g_vptMemoPage = pageIndex;
        g_vptMemoPageValid = true;
    }

    const uint index = uint(cursor.local.x) +
        16u * (uint(cursor.local.z) + 16u * uint(cursor.local.y));
    source = vliPagesBuffer.words[pageIndex * VLI_PAGE_WORDS + index];
    return true;
}





float vptEmitterVoxelEntryDistance(
    vec3 origin,
    vec3 target,
    ivec3 block
)
{
    vec3 segment = target - origin;
    float segmentLength = length(segment);
    if (segmentLength <= 1.0e-6)
    {
        return - 1.0;
    }

    vec3 direction = segment / segmentLength;


    vec3 safeDirection = mix(
        direction,
        vec3(1.0e-6),
        lessThan(abs(direction), vec3(1.0e-6))
    );

    vec3 boxMin = vec3(block);
    vec3 boxMax = boxMin + vec3(1.0);
    vec3 t0 = (boxMin - origin) / safeDirection;
    vec3 t1 = (boxMax - origin) / safeDirection;
    vec3 tNear = min(t0, t1);
    vec3 tFar = max(t0, t1);
    float tEnter = max(max(tNear.x, tNear.y), tNear.z);
    float tExit = min(min(tFar.x, tFar.y), tFar.z);
    if (tEnter > tExit || tExit < 0.0)
    {
        return - 1.0;
    }
    return max(tEnter, 0.0);
}










































































bool vptVoxelFaceNormalFromHit(
    vec3 hitPos,
    int axis,
    out vec3 faceNormal
)
{
    vec3 axisVector =
        axis == 0 ? vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0) :
        (axis == 1 ? vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0) : vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0));

    uint positiveSource = 0u;
    uint negativeSource = 0u;
    bool positiveSolid =
        vptReadSourceVoxel(ivec3(floor(hitPos + axisVector * 0.5)), positiveSource) &&
        ( (positiveSource & 3u) == 1u);
    bool negativeSolid =
        vptReadSourceVoxel(ivec3(floor(hitPos - axisVector * 0.5)), negativeSource) &&
        ( (negativeSource & 3u) == 1u);

    if (positiveSolid == negativeSolid)
    {
        faceNormal = vec3(0.0);
        return false;
    }

    faceNormal = positiveSolid ? - axisVector : axisVector;
    return true;
}




























bool vptVoxelPathBlocked(
    vec3 origin,
    vec3 target,
    ivec3 emitterBlock,
    float stopDistance
)
{

    if (perfL10DisableVoxelDda())
    {
        return false;
    }

    vec3 segment = target - origin;
    float fullLength = length(segment);
    if (fullLength <= 1.0e-4)
    {
        return false;
    }
    float limit = min(fullLength, max(stopDistance, 0.0));
    if (limit <= 1.0e-4)
    {
        return false;
    }

    vec3 direction = segment / fullLength;
    ivec3 cell = ivec3(floor(origin));
    ivec3 stepVector = ivec3(sign(direction));
    vec3 invAbs = 1.0 / max(abs(direction), vec3(1.0e-6));
    vec3 boundary = vec3(cell) + vec3(max(stepVector, ivec3(0)));
    vec3 tMax = (boundary - origin) / direction;
    if (stepVector.x == 0) tMax.x = 1.0e30;
    if (stepVector.y == 0) tMax.y = 1.0e30;
    if (stepVector.z == 0) tMax.z = 1.0e30;


    VptVoxelCursor vptCursor = vptMakeCursor(cell);

    for (int i = 0; i < 256; ++ i)
    {
        int axis = tMax.x < tMax.y
            ? (tMax.x < tMax.z ? 0 : 2)
            : (tMax.y < tMax.z ? 1 : 2);
        float t = axis == 0 ? tMax.x : (axis == 1 ? tMax.y : tMax.z);
        if (t >= limit)
        {
            return false;
        }



        float otherA = axis == 0 ? tMax.y : tMax.x;
        float otherB = axis == 2 ? tMax.y : tMax.z;
        if (min(abs(otherA - t), abs(otherB - t)) <=
                                    1.0e-3 * (1.0 + abs(t)))
        {
            return true;
        }

        if (axis == 0)
        {
            cell.x += stepVector.x;
            vptCursorStep(vptCursor, 0, stepVector.x);
            tMax.x += invAbs.x;
        }
        else if (axis == 1)
        {
            cell.y += stepVector.y;
            vptCursorStep(vptCursor, 1, stepVector.y);
            tMax.y += invAbs.y;
        }
        else
        {
            cell.z += stepVector.z;
            vptCursorStep(vptCursor, 2, stepVector.z);
            tMax.z += invAbs.z;
        }

        if (all(equal(cell, emitterBlock)))
        {
            return false;
        }

        uint source;
        if (vptReadSourceVoxelAt(vptCursor, source) && ( (source & 3u) == 1u))
        {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

float vptLocalFaceSetCosineBound(
    bool primaryKnown,
    vec3 primaryFaceNormal,
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 lightDirection
)
{
    float bound = primaryKnown
        ? max(dot(primaryFaceNormal, lightDirection), 0.0)
        : 1.0;


    vec3 fracPos = fract(hitPos);
    vec3 distToFace = min(fracPos, vec3(1.0) - fracPos);
    for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++ c)
    {
        float faceDistance =
            c == 0 ? distToFace.x : (c == 1 ? distToFace.y : distToFace.z);
        if (faceDistance > 1.0e-3)
        {
            continue;
        }
        vec3 faceNormal;
        if (vptVoxelFaceNormalFromHit(hitPos, c, faceNormal))
        {
            bound = min(
                bound,
                max(dot(faceNormal, lightDirection), 0.0)
            );
        }
    }


    return bound;
}















bool vptLoadedPathToCandidateEmitter(
    vec3 origin,
    vec3 target,
    ivec3 targetBlock,
    out float targetVoxelEntryDistance
)
{
    vec3 segment = target - origin;
    float maxDistance = length(segment);
    targetVoxelEntryDistance = maxDistance;
    if (maxDistance <= 1.0e-4)
    {
        return true;
    }

    vec3 direction = segment / maxDistance;


    int maxTraversal = min(
        96,
        int(ceil(maxDistance * dot(abs(direction), vec3(1.0)))) + 2
    );
    ivec3 cell = ivec3(floor(origin));
    ivec3 step = ivec3(sign(direction));
    vec3 invAbs = 1.0 / max(abs(direction), vec3(1.0e-6));
    vec3 boundary = vec3(cell) + vec3(max(step, ivec3(0)));
    vec3 tMax = (boundary - origin) / direction;
    if (step.x == 0) tMax.x = 1.0e30;
    if (step.y == 0) tMax.y = 1.0e30;
    if (step.z == 0) tMax.z = 1.0e30;

    for (int traversal = 0; traversal < maxTraversal; ++ traversal)
    {
        int axis = tMax.x < tMax.y
            ? (tMax.x < tMax.z ? 0 : 2)
            : (tMax.y < tMax.z ? 1 : 2);
        float travel = axis == 0 ? tMax.x : (axis == 1 ? tMax.y : tMax.z);
        if (travel >= maxDistance)
        {
            return true;
        }

        if (axis == 0)
        {
            cell.x += step.x;
            tMax.x += invAbs.x;
        }
        else if (axis == 1)
        {
            cell.y += step.y;
            tMax.y += invAbs.y;
        }
        else
        {
            cell.z += step.z;
            tMax.z += invAbs.z;
        }

        if (all(equal(cell, targetBlock)))
        {
            targetVoxelEntryDistance = travel;
            return true;
        }

        uint source;
        if (! vptReadSourceVoxel(cell, source))
        {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return false;
}





vec3 vptTraceGlassTransmissionToEmitter(
    vec3 origin,
    vec3 target,
    float targetVoxelEntryDistance
);











const bool VPT_LOCAL_NEE_VISIBILITY_RAY =
    true;



vec3 traceVptLocalLightVisibility(
    vec3 origin,
    vec3 target,
    float targetVoxelEntryDistance,
    uint cullMask
);

float vptLocalLightRange(float emissionLevel)
{


    float userLocalMax = cam.rainWallParams1.w;
    float localMaxDistance =
        userLocalMax > 0.5
            ? min(VPT_LOCAL_MAX_DISTANCE, userLocalMax)
            : VPT_LOCAL_MAX_DISTANCE;
    return min(
        localMaxDistance,
        max(emissionLevel * VPT_LOCAL_RANGE_PER_EMISSION, 2.0)
    );
}

float vptLocalLightRangeFade(float distanceToLight, float emissionLevel)
{
    float range = vptLocalLightRange(emissionLevel);
    return 1.0 - smoothstep(
        range * VPT_LOCAL_FADE_START_FRACTION,
        range,
        distanceToLight
    );
}

float vptLocalLightDistanceAttenuation(float distanceToLight)
{
    return 0.55 / (1.0 + 0.18 * distanceToLight +
        0.025 * distanceToLight * distanceToLight);
}

float rand(inout uint state);


float vptRadicalInverseBase2(uint value)
{
    float result = 0.0;
    float weight = 0.5;
    for (int bit = 0; bit < 7; ++ bit)
    {
        if ( (value & 1u) != 0u)
        {
            result += weight;
        }
        value >>= 1u;
        weight *= 0.5;
    }
    return result;
}



vec2 vptAreaLightStratum(uint sampleIndex)
{
    return vec2(
        (float(sampleIndex) + 0.5) / float(VPT_AREA_LIGHT_HISTORY_SAMPLES) - 0.5,
        vptRadicalInverseBase2(sampleIndex) - 0.5
    );
}








uint pcg_hash(inout uint state);



bool vptReprojectPreviousPixel(
    vec3 worldPosition,
    ivec2 renderSize,
    out uint previousIndex
);


vec3 evaluateVPTRISDirectLightingSingle(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    vec3 geometricFaceNormal,
    inout uint rngState
)
{

    if (DBG_LADDER_C) { return vec3(1.0, 1.0, 0.0); }














    const vec3 directoryJitter = vec3(
        rand(rngState), rand(rngState), rand(rngState)) - vec3(0.5);
    ivec3 receiverSection = ivec3(floor( (hitPos + directoryJitter * 16.0) / 16.0));
    uint receiverPage;


    ivec3 directorySection = receiverSection;






    float borrowDonorWeightA = 1.0;
    bool borrowDonorBValid = false;
    ivec3 borrowDonorBSection = ivec3(0);
    uint borrowDonorBPage = 0u;
    float borrowDonorBDistanceSquared = 1.0e30;
    if (! vliLookupSectionPage(receiverSection, receiverPage))
    {











        bool borrowedDirectory = false;
        ivec3 donorASection = ivec3(0);
        uint donorAPage = 0u;
        float donorADistanceSquared = 1.0e30;
        for (uint neighbourIndex = 0u; neighbourIndex < 6u; ++ neighbourIndex)
        {
            ivec3 neighbour = receiverSection + (
                neighbourIndex == 0u ? ivec3(1, 0, 0) :
                neighbourIndex == 1u ? ivec3(- 1, 0, 0) :
                neighbourIndex == 2u ? ivec3(0, 1, 0) :
                neighbourIndex == 3u ? ivec3(0, - 1, 0) :
                neighbourIndex == 4u ? ivec3(0, 0, 1) :
                                       ivec3(0, 0, - 1)
            );
            uint neighbourPage;
            if (vliLookupSectionPage(neighbour, neighbourPage))
            {
                borrowedDirectory = true;
                const vec3 neighbourCenter =
                    vec3(neighbour) * 16.0 + vec3(8.0);
                const vec3 toNeighbour = hitPos - neighbourCenter;
                const float neighbourDistanceSquared =
                    dot(toNeighbour, toNeighbour);
                if (neighbourDistanceSquared < donorADistanceSquared)
                {
                    borrowDonorBSection = donorASection;
                    borrowDonorBPage = donorAPage;
                    borrowDonorBDistanceSquared = donorADistanceSquared;
                    donorASection = neighbour;
                    donorAPage = neighbourPage;
                    donorADistanceSquared = neighbourDistanceSquared;
                }
                else if (neighbourDistanceSquared < borrowDonorBDistanceSquared)
                {
                    borrowDonorBSection = neighbour;
                    borrowDonorBPage = neighbourPage;
                    borrowDonorBDistanceSquared = neighbourDistanceSquared;
                }
            }
        }
        if (! borrowedDirectory)
        {
            return vec3(0.0);
        }
        directorySection = donorASection;
        receiverPage = donorAPage;


        borrowDonorBValid = borrowDonorBDistanceSquared < 1.0e29;
        if (borrowDonorBValid)
        {
            const float donorADistance = sqrt(donorADistanceSquared);
            const float donorBDistance = sqrt(borrowDonorBDistanceSquared);
            borrowDonorWeightA =
                donorBDistance / max(donorADistance + donorBDistance, 1.0e-6);
        }
    }





    bool waterColumnReceiver = dot(hitNormal, hitNormal) <= 1.0e-8;
    vec3 N = waterColumnReceiver
        ? vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0)
        : normalize(hitNormal);

























































    bool geometricFaceKnown =
        dot(geometricFaceNormal, geometricFaceNormal) > 0.25;
    vec3 geometricFaceRecovered = geometricFaceNormal;

    if (! waterColumnReceiver && ! geometricFaceKnown)
    {
        vec3 fracPos = fract(hitPos);
        vec3 distToFace = min(fracPos, vec3(1.0) - fracPos);
        float nearestFace = min(distToFace.x, min(distToFace.y, distToFace.z));
        if (nearestFace <= 1.0e-3)
        {
            int faceAxis = 0;
            if (distToFace.y == nearestFace)
            {
                faceAxis = 1;
            }
            else if (distToFace.z == nearestFace)
            {
                faceAxis = 2;
            }
            vec3 axisVector = vec3(0.0);
            axisVector[faceAxis] = 1.0;

            uint positiveSource = 0u;
            uint negativeSource = 0u;


            bool positiveSolid =
                vptReadSourceVoxel(ivec3(floor(hitPos + axisVector * 0.5)), positiveSource) &&
                vptVoxelOccupied(positiveSource);
            bool negativeSolid =
                vptReadSourceVoxel(ivec3(floor(hitPos - axisVector * 0.5)), negativeSource) &&
                vptVoxelOccupied(negativeSource);

            geometricFaceRecovered = vec3(0.0);
            geometricFaceRecovered[faceAxis] =
                (positiveSolid && ! negativeSolid) ? - 1.0 : 1.0;
            geometricFaceKnown = true;
        }
    }

    vec3 localGeometricN = waterColumnReceiver
        ? vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0)
        : (geometricFaceKnown
            ? normalize(geometricFaceRecovered)
            : vptDominantBlockFaceNormal(N));


    vec3 selectedContribution = vec3(0.0);
    vec3 selectedSample = vec3(0.0);
    ivec3 selectedBlock = ivec3(0);
    float weightSum = 0.0;
    uint directoryBase = receiverPage * VPT_SOURCE_DIRECTORY_ENTRIES_PER_PAGE;
    uint sourceSectionCount = min(
        vptSourceDirectoryPagesBuffer.entries[directoryBase].pageIndex,
        VPT_SOURCE_DIRECTORY_MAX_SECTIONS
    );
    if (sourceSectionCount == 0u)
    {












        bool borrowedDirectoryEntries = false;
        for (uint neighbourIndex = 0u; neighbourIndex < 6u; ++ neighbourIndex)
        {
            ivec3 neighbour = directorySection + (
                neighbourIndex == 0u ? ivec3(1, 0, 0) :
                neighbourIndex == 1u ? ivec3(- 1, 0, 0) :
                neighbourIndex == 2u ? ivec3(0, 1, 0) :
                neighbourIndex == 3u ? ivec3(0, - 1, 0) :
                neighbourIndex == 4u ? ivec3(0, 0, 1) :
                                       ivec3(0, 0, - 1)
            );
            uint neighbourPage;
            if (! vliLookupSectionPage(neighbour, neighbourPage))
            {
                continue;
            }
            const uint neighbourBase =
                neighbourPage * VPT_SOURCE_DIRECTORY_ENTRIES_PER_PAGE;
            const uint neighbourCount = min(
                vptSourceDirectoryPagesBuffer.entries[neighbourBase].pageIndex,
                VPT_SOURCE_DIRECTORY_MAX_SECTIONS
            );
            if (neighbourCount == 0u)
            {
                continue;
            }
            directorySection = neighbour;
            receiverPage = neighbourPage;
            directoryBase = neighbourBase;
            sourceSectionCount = neighbourCount;
            borrowedDirectoryEntries = true;
            break;
        }
        if (! borrowedDirectoryEntries)
        {
            return vec3(0.0);
        }
    }

    for (uint proposalIndex = 0u;
        proposalIndex < clamp(cam.perfSecondary.x, 1u, 32u);
        ++ proposalIndex)
    {


        ivec3 proposalDirectorySection = directorySection;
        uint proposalDirectoryBase = directoryBase;
        uint proposalSectionCount = sourceSectionCount;
        float proposalDonorWeight = borrowDonorWeightA;
        if (borrowDonorBValid && rand(rngState) >= borrowDonorWeightA)
        {
            proposalDirectorySection = borrowDonorBSection;
            proposalDirectoryBase =
                borrowDonorBPage * VPT_SOURCE_DIRECTORY_ENTRIES_PER_PAGE;
            proposalSectionCount = min(
                vptSourceDirectoryPagesBuffer.entries[proposalDirectoryBase].pageIndex,
                VPT_SOURCE_DIRECTORY_MAX_SECTIONS
            );
            proposalDonorWeight = 1.0 - borrowDonorWeightA;
        }
        if (proposalSectionCount == 0u || proposalDonorWeight <= 0.0)
        {
            continue;
        }
        uint sourceDirectoryBucket = min(
            uint(rand(rngState) * float(proposalSectionCount)),
            proposalSectionCount - 1u
        );
        VPTSourceDirectoryEntry sourceDirectoryBucketEntry =
            vptSourceDirectoryPagesBuffer.entries[
            proposalDirectoryBase + 1u + sourceDirectoryBucket
        ];
        uint sourceDirectoryIndex = rand(rngState) <=
            float(sourceDirectoryBucketEntry.aliasThresholdQ16) / 65535.0
            ? sourceDirectoryBucket
            : min(sourceDirectoryBucketEntry.aliasIndex, proposalSectionCount - 1u);
        VPTSourceDirectoryEntry sourceDirectoryEntry =
            vptSourceDirectoryPagesBuffer.entries[
                proposalDirectoryBase + 1u + sourceDirectoryIndex
            ];
        uint sourcePage = sourceDirectoryEntry.pageIndex;
        uint sourceMetadata = sourceDirectoryEntry.emitterCountAndOffset;
        uint emitterCount = sourceMetadata & 0x1fffu;
        if (emitterCount == 0u)
        {
            continue;
        }

        uint emitterBucket = min(
            uint(rand(rngState) * float(emitterCount)),
            emitterCount - 1u
        );
        uint emitterAliasWord = vptEmitterAliasPagesBuffer.words[
            sourcePage * VPT_EMITTER_PAGE_WORDS +
            VPT_EMITTER_HEADER_WORDS + emitterBucket
        ];
        uint emitterIndex = rand(rngState) <=
            float(emitterAliasWord & 0xffffu) / 65535.0
            ? emitterBucket
            : min(emitterAliasWord >> 16u, emitterCount - 1u);
        uint packedEmitter = vptEmitterIndexPagesBuffer.words[
            sourcePage * VPT_EMITTER_PAGE_WORDS +
            VPT_EMITTER_HEADER_WORDS + emitterIndex
        ];
        uint emission = (packedEmitter >> 12u) & 15u;
        if (emission == 0u) continue;

        ivec3 sourceSection = proposalDirectorySection + ivec3(
            int( (sourceMetadata >> 13u) & 7u) - 2,
            int( (sourceMetadata >> 16u) & 7u) - 2,
            int( (sourceMetadata >> 19u) & 7u) - 2
        );
        ivec3 emitterBlock = sourceSection * 16 + ivec3(
            int(packedEmitter & 15u),
            int( (packedEmitter >> 4u) & 15u),
            int( (packedEmitter >> 8u) & 15u)
        );

        vec3 emitterCenter = vec3(emitterBlock) + vec3(0.5);
        vec3 toLight = emitterCenter - hitPos;
        float distanceSquared = dot(toLight, toLight);
        if (distanceSquared <= 1.0e-6)
        {
            continue;
        }
        float distanceToLight = sqrt(distanceSquared);
        float emissionLevel = float(emission);
        if (distanceToLight > vptLocalLightRange(emissionLevel))
        {
            continue;
        }

        float receiverCosine = waterColumnReceiver
            ? 1.0
            : max(dot(N, toLight / distanceToLight), 0.0);



        receiverCosine = min(
            receiverCosine,
            max(dot(localGeometricN, toLight / distanceToLight), 0.0)
        );

        if (receiverCosine <= 0.0)
        {
            continue;
        }

        uint typeCode = (packedEmitter >> 16u) & 7u;
        vec3 centreDirection = toLight / distanceToLight;
        vec3 referenceAxis = abs(centreDirection.y) < 0.95
            ? vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0)
            : vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
        vec3 tangent = normalize(cross(referenceAxis, centreDirection));
        vec3 bitangent = normalize(cross(centreDirection, tangent));

        vec2 sampleUv = vec2(rand(rngState), rand(rngState)) - vec2(0.5);
        vec3 emitterSample = emitterCenter +
            (tangent * sampleUv.x + bitangent * sampleUv.y) *
            VPT_LOCAL_LIGHT_AREA_SIZE;





        emitterSample = clamp(
            emitterSample,
            vec3(emitterBlock) + vec3(0.001),
            vec3(emitterBlock) + vec3(0.999)
        );
        vec3 sampledToLight = emitterSample - hitPos;
        float sampledDistance = length(sampledToLight);
        if (sampledDistance <= 1.0e-6) continue;
        vec3 lightDirection = sampledToLight / sampledDistance;
        float sampledCosine = max(dot(N, lightDirection), 0.0);

        sampledCosine = min(
            sampledCosine,
            vptLocalFaceSetCosineBound(
                geometricFaceKnown,
                geometricFaceRecovered,
                hitPos,
                lightDirection
            )
        );

        if (sampledCosine <= 0.0) continue;
        float scale = 3.2 * LOCAL_LIGHT_ENERGY_SCALE * localLightIntensity() *
            (emissionLevel / 15.0) *
            vptLocalLightDistanceAttenuation(sampledDistance) *
            vptLocalLightRangeFade(sampledDistance, emissionLevel);
        vec3 contribution = vliDecodeEmitterColor(typeCode) * scale * sampledCosine;




        float target = max(dot(contribution, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722)), 0.0);
        uint totalEmitterPower = vptEmitterIndexPagesBuffer.words[
            sourcePage * VPT_EMITTER_PAGE_WORDS + 2u
        ];
        float proposalPdf = max(


            proposalDonorWeight *
            sourceDirectoryEntry.selectionProbability *
            (float(emission) / float(max(totalEmitterPower, 1u))),
            1.0e-8
        );
        float weight = target / proposalPdf;
        if (weight <= 0.0) continue;
        weightSum += weight;
        if (rand(rngState) * weightSum < weight)
        {
            selectedContribution = contribution;
            selectedSample = emitterSample;
            selectedBlock = emitterBlock;
        }
    }
    if (weightSum <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }











    vec3 neeRayDirection = normalize(selectedSample - hitPos);
    vec3 receiverOrigin = hitPos + neeRayDirection * 0.001;

















    bool sampledPointInsideEmitterVoxel =
        all(greaterThanEqual(selectedSample, vec3(selectedBlock))) &&
        all(lessThanEqual(
            selectedSample,
            vec3(selectedBlock) + vec3(1.0)
        ));
    float targetVoxelEntryDistance = vptEmitterVoxelEntryDistance(
        receiverOrigin, selectedSample, selectedBlock
    );
    if (! sampledPointInsideEmitterVoxel || targetVoxelEntryDistance < 0.0)
    {
        targetVoxelEntryDistance = distance(selectedSample, receiverOrigin);
    }










    vec3 transmittance = VPT_LOCAL_NEE_VISIBILITY_RAY
        ? traceVptLocalLightVisibility(
            receiverOrigin,
            selectedSample,
            targetVoxelEntryDistance,
            0xffu
        )
        : vptTraceGlassTransmissionToEmitter(
            receiverOrigin,
            selectedSample,
            targetVoxelEntryDistance
        );
    if (max(transmittance.r, max(transmittance.g, transmittance.b)) <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }



    if (vptVoxelPathBlocked(
        receiverOrigin,
        selectedSample,
        selectedBlock,
        targetVoxelEntryDistance
    ))
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }





    float selectedWeight = max(dot(selectedContribution,
        vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722)), 1.0e-6);
    return applyLocalGlassTint(
        selectedContribution * (weightSum /
            (float(clamp(cam.perfSecondary.x, 1u, 32u)) * selectedWeight)) *
            max(hitAlbedo, vec3(0.0)) * 0.92,
        transmittance
    );
}


































const float VPT_LOCAL_KEEP_RAMP_BLOCKS = 16.0;
const float VPT_LOCAL_KEEP_TAIL_END_BLOCKS = 200.0;

float vptLocalLightKeepProbability(float cameraDistance)
{
    if (cameraDistance <= 56.0) return 1.0;
    if (cameraDistance <= 72.0)
    {
        return mix(1.0, 0.5,
            smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, (cameraDistance - 56.0) / VPT_LOCAL_KEEP_RAMP_BLOCKS));
    }
    if (cameraDistance <= 120.0) return 0.5;
    if (cameraDistance <= 136.0)
    {
        return mix(0.5, 0.25,
            smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, (cameraDistance - 120.0) / VPT_LOCAL_KEEP_RAMP_BLOCKS));
    }
    if (cameraDistance <= 152.0) return 0.25;
    if (cameraDistance <= VPT_LOCAL_KEEP_TAIL_END_BLOCKS)
    {
        return mix(0.25, 0.0,
            smoothstep(0.0, 1.0,
                (cameraDistance - 152.0) /
                (VPT_LOCAL_KEEP_TAIL_END_BLOCKS - 152.0)));
    }
    return 0.0;
}


















vec3 dynamicLightBRDF(
    vec3 N,
    vec3 V,
    vec3 L,
    vec3 albedo,
    vec3 f0,
    float metallic,
    float roughness
);














float dynamicLightIntensity()
{
    float user = uintBitsToFloat(dynamicLights.header.y) - 1.0;
    return(user >= 0.0 && user <= 16.0) ? user : 1.0;
}








const float DYNAMIC_LIGHT_VISIBILITY_SHORTEN = 0.6;

















const uint DYNAMIC_LIGHT_CULL_MASK = 0xfdu;






vec3 dynamicLightColor(uint typeCode, uint packedRgb)
{
    if (packedRgb != 0u)
    {
        return vec3(
            float( (packedRgb >> 16u) & 255u),
            float( (packedRgb >> 8u) & 255u),
            float(packedRgb & 255u)
        ) * (1.0 / 255.0);
    }
    return vliDecodeEmitterColor(typeCode);
}

vec3 dynamicLightContribution(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 N,
    vec3 V,
    vec3 albedo,
    vec3 f0,
    float metallic,
    float roughness,
    float emissionLevel,
    uint typeCode,
    uint packedRgb,
    vec3 lightPosition
)
{
    vec3 toLight = lightPosition - hitPos;
    float distanceSquared = dot(toLight, toLight);
    if (distanceSquared <= 1.0e-6)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }
    float distanceToLight = sqrt(distanceSquared);
    if (distanceToLight > vptLocalLightRange(emissionLevel))
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }
    vec3 lightDirection = toLight / distanceToLight;
    float receiverCosine = max(dot(N, lightDirection), 0.0);
    if (receiverCosine <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }


    float scale = 3.2 * LOCAL_LIGHT_ENERGY_SCALE * localLightIntensity() *
        dynamicLightIntensity() *
        (emissionLevel / 15.0) *
        vptLocalLightDistanceAttenuation(distanceToLight) *
        vptLocalLightRangeFade(distanceToLight, emissionLevel);





    vec3 brdf = dynamicLightBRDF(
        N, V, lightDirection, albedo, f0, metallic, roughness);
    return dynamicLightColor(typeCode, packedRgb) * scale * brdf;
}

vec3 evaluateDynamicLightRISDirect(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    float receiverCameraDistance,
    vec3 f0,
    float metallic,
    float roughness,
    inout uint rngState
)
{


    vec3 dynamicLightCameraPosition =
        (cam.viewInverse * vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)).xyz;
    vec3 viewDirection = normalize(dynamicLightCameraPosition - hitPos);

    uint lightCount = min(dynamicLights.header.x, 128u);
    if (lightCount == 0u)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }








    const float DYNAMIC_LIGHT_RECEIVER_GATE = 80.0;
    if (receiverCameraDistance > DYNAMIC_LIGHT_RECEIVER_GATE)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    vec3 N = normalize(hitNormal);


    uint proposals = clamp(cam.perfSecondary.x, 1u, 4u);
    vec3 selectedContribution = vec3(0.0);
    vec3 selectedPosition = vec3(0.0);
    float weightSum = 0.0;

    for (uint proposalIndex = 0u; proposalIndex < proposals; ++ proposalIndex)
    {
        uint lightIndex = min(
            uint(rand(rngState) * float(lightCount)),
            lightCount - 1u
        );
        DynamicLightRecord light = dynamicLights.lights[lightIndex];
        float emissionLevel = light.worldPosEmission.w;
        if (emissionLevel <= 0.0)
        {
            continue;
        }
        vec3 contribution = dynamicLightContribution(
            hitPos, N, viewDirection, hitAlbedo, f0, metallic, roughness,
            emissionLevel, light.meta.x, light.meta.y,
            light.worldPosEmission.xyz);
        float target = max(dot(contribution, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722)), 0.0);
        if (target <= 0.0)
        {
            continue;
        }

        float weight = target * float(lightCount);
        weightSum += weight;
        if (rand(rngState) * weightSum < weight)
        {
            selectedContribution = contribution;
            selectedPosition = light.worldPosEmission.xyz;
        }
    }

    if (weightSum <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }



    vec3 neeRayDirection = normalize(selectedPosition - hitPos);
    vec3 receiverOrigin = hitPos + neeRayDirection * 0.001;
    float targetDistance = max(
        distance(selectedPosition, receiverOrigin) - DYNAMIC_LIGHT_VISIBILITY_SHORTEN,
        0.05
    );
    vec3 transmittance = VPT_LOCAL_NEE_VISIBILITY_RAY
        ? traceVptLocalLightVisibility(
            receiverOrigin,
            selectedPosition,
            targetDistance,
            DYNAMIC_LIGHT_CULL_MASK)
        : vptTraceGlassTransmissionToEmitter(
            receiverOrigin,
            selectedPosition,
            targetDistance);
    if (max(transmittance.r, max(transmittance.g, transmittance.b)) <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    float selectedWeight = max(dot(selectedContribution,
        vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722)), 1.0e-6);


    return applyLocalGlassTint(
        selectedContribution * (weightSum /
            (float(proposals) * selectedWeight)),
        transmittance
    );
}




vec3 evaluateVPTRISDirectLighting(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    vec3 geometricFaceNormal,
    vec3 f0,
    float metallic,
    float roughness,
    inout uint rngState
)
{
    vec3 cameraPosition =
        (cam.viewInverse * vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)).xyz;
    float receiverCameraDistance = length(hitPos - cameraPosition);







    vec3 voxelDirect = evaluateVPTRISDirectLightingSingle(
        hitPos, hitNormal, hitAlbedo, geometricFaceNormal, rngState);



    return voxelDirect + evaluateDynamicLightRISDirect(
        hitPos, hitNormal, hitAlbedo, receiverCameraDistance,
        f0, metallic, roughness, rngState);
}



vec3 evaluateVPTRISDirectLighting(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    inout uint rngState
)
{

    return evaluateVPTRISDirectLighting(
        hitPos, hitNormal, hitAlbedo, vec3(0.0), vec3(- 1.0), - 1.0, 1.0, rngState);
}




vec3 evaluateVPTLocalSecondaryBounce(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    inout uint rngState
)
{

    if (perfL10DisableVoxelDda())
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    vec3 N = normalize(hitNormal);
    vec3 direction = sampleHemisphereCosine(N, rngState);
    int maxTraversal = min(
        48,
        int(ceil(VPT_LOCAL_BOUNCE_MAX_DISTANCE *
            dot(abs(direction), vec3(1.0)))) + 2
    );
    vec3 origin = hitPos + N * 0.035;
    ivec3 cell = ivec3(floor(origin));
    ivec3 step = ivec3(sign(direction));
    vec3 invAbs = 1.0 / max(abs(direction), vec3(1.0e-6));
    vec3 boundary = vec3(cell) + vec3(max(step, ivec3(0)));
    vec3 tMax = (boundary - origin) / direction;
    if (step.x == 0) tMax.x = 1.0e30;
    if (step.y == 0) tMax.y = 1.0e30;
    if (step.z == 0) tMax.z = 1.0e30;




    vec3 glassTransmission = vec3(1.0);







    {
        uint startSource;
        if (vptReadSourceVoxel(cell, startSource) && (startSource & 3u) == 1u)
        {
            return vec3(0.0);
        }
    }



    VptVoxelCursor vptCursor = vptMakeCursor(cell);

    for (int traversal = 0; traversal < maxTraversal; ++ traversal)
    {
        int axis = tMax.x < tMax.y
            ? (tMax.x < tMax.z ? 0 : 2)
            : (tMax.y < tMax.z ? 1 : 2);
        float travel = axis == 0 ? tMax.x : (axis == 1 ? tMax.y : tMax.z);
        if (travel > VPT_LOCAL_BOUNCE_MAX_DISTANCE) return vec3(0.0);

        vec3 faceNormal;
        if (axis == 0)
        {
            cell.x += step.x;
            vptCursorStep(vptCursor, 0, step.x);
            tMax.x += invAbs.x;
            faceNormal = vec3(- step.x, 0.0, 0.0);
        }
        else if (axis == 1)
        {
            cell.y += step.y;
            vptCursorStep(vptCursor, 1, step.y);
            tMax.y += invAbs.y;
            faceNormal = vec3(0.0, - step.y, 0.0);
        }
        else
        {
            cell.z += step.z;
            vptCursorStep(vptCursor, 2, step.z);
            tMax.z += invAbs.z;
            faceNormal = vec3(0.0, 0.0, - step.z);
        }

        uint source;







        if (! vptReadSourceVoxelAt(vptCursor, source)) continue;



        if ( (source & VPT_STAINED_GLASS_BIT) != 0u)
        {
            glassTransmission *=
                max(glassDecodeVoxelAlbedo(source), vec3(1.0 / 31.0));
        }
        uint voxelClass = source & 3u;
        if (voxelClass == 3u)
        {





            glassTransmission *=
                max(vec3(vptPartialTransmission(source)), vec3(1.0 / 127.0));
            continue;
        }
        if (voxelClass != 1u) continue;

        vec3 bounceAlbedo = glassDecodeVoxelAlbedo(source);
        if (max(bounceAlbedo.r, max(bounceAlbedo.g, bounceAlbedo.b)) <= 0.0)
        {
            continue;
        }

        ivec3 sectionCoord = ivec3(
            vliFloorDiv(cell.x, 16), vliFloorDiv(cell.y, 16),
            vliFloorDiv(cell.z, 16)
        );
        uint pageIndex;
        if (! vliLookupSectionPage(sectionCoord, pageIndex)) continue;
        ivec3 local = ivec3(
            vliPositiveMod(cell.x, 16), vliPositiveMod(cell.y, 16),
            vliPositiveMod(cell.z, 16)
        );
        uint voxelIndex = uint(local.x) + 16u *
            (uint(local.z) + 16u * uint(local.y));
        uint packedDirect = vptSurfaceFaceBuffer.words[
            pageIndex * VPT_SURFACE_FACE_WORDS +
            voxelIndex * VPT_DIRECTION_COUNT +
            vptFaceIndexFromBlockFaceNormal(faceNormal)
        ];
        vec3 outgoingRadiance = vliUnpackRadiance(packedDirect) *
            bounceAlbedo * (1.0 / 3.14159265359);







        vec3 secondaryTerm = outgoingRadiance * max(hitAlbedo, vec3(0.0)) *
            0.92 * vptLocalLightDistanceAttenuation(travel) *
            vptSaturateGlassTransmission(glassTransmission);


        if (DBG_LOCAL_TERM_MAP)
        {
            return vec3(0.0, dbgTermLuma(secondaryTerm), 0.0);
        }
        if (DBG_GLASS_DYE_VS_ENERGY_SECONDARY)
        {


            return vec3(
                dbgDyeConcentration(vptSaturateGlassTransmission(glassTransmission)),
                dbgTermLuma(secondaryTerm),
                0.0
            );
        }
        if (DBG_GLASS_PEAK_READOUT)
        {



            return dbgHuePurity2(vptSaturateGlassTransmission(glassTransmission));
        }
        return toneKeepHueLocal(secondaryTerm);
    }
    return vec3(0.0);
}



vec3 traceVPTEmitterDDA(vec3 origin, vec3 direction, out vec3 blockerAlbedo,
                         out vec3 blockerPos, out vec3 blockerNormal)
{
    ivec3 cell = ivec3(floor(origin));
    ivec3 step = ivec3(sign(direction));
    vec3 invAbs = 1.0 / max(abs(direction), vec3(1.0e-6));
    vec3 boundary = vec3(cell) + vec3(max(step, ivec3(0)));
    vec3 tMax = (boundary - origin) / direction;
    if (step.x == 0) tMax.x = 1.0e30;
    if (step.y == 0) tMax.y = 1.0e30;
    if (step.z == 0) tMax.z = 1.0e30;
    blockerAlbedo = vec3(0.0);
    blockerPos = origin;
    blockerNormal = vec3(0.0);



    vec3 glassTransmission = vec3(1.0);


    VptVoxelCursor vptCursor = vptMakeCursor(cell);

    for (int i = 0; i < 64; ++ i)
    {
        int axis = tMax.x < tMax.y ? (tMax.x < tMax.z ? 0 : 2)
                                    : (tMax.y < tMax.z ? 1 : 2);
        float t = axis == 0 ? tMax.x : (axis == 1 ? tMax.y : tMax.z);
        if (t > 30.0) break;
        if (axis == 0) { cell.x += step.x; vptCursorStep(vptCursor, 0, step.x); tMax.x += invAbs.x; blockerNormal = vec3(- step.x, 0, 0); }
        else if (axis == 1) { cell.y += step.y; vptCursorStep(vptCursor, 1, step.y); tMax.y += invAbs.y; blockerNormal = vec3(0, - step.y, 0); }
        else { cell.z += step.z; vptCursorStep(vptCursor, 2, step.z); tMax.z += invAbs.z; blockerNormal = vec3(0, 0, - step.z); }

        uint source;
        if (! vptReadSourceVoxelAt(vptCursor, source)) break;
        if ( (source & VPT_STAINED_GLASS_BIT) != 0u)
        {
            glassTransmission *=
                max(glassDecodeVoxelAlbedo(source), vec3(1.0 / 31.0));
        }
        uint emission = (source >> 2u) & 15u;
        if (emission > 0u)
        {
            return vliDecodeEmitterColor( (source >> 6u) & 7u) *
                (float(emission) / 15.0) * 3.2 *
                LOCAL_LIGHT_ENERGY_SCALE * localLightIntensity() *
                vptSaturateGlassTransmission(glassTransmission);
        }
        uint voxelClass = source & 3u;
        if (voxelClass == 3u)
        {



            glassTransmission *=
                max(vec3(vptPartialTransmission(source)), vec3(1.0 / 127.0));
            continue;
        }
        if (voxelClass == 1u)
        {
            blockerAlbedo = glassDecodeVoxelAlbedo(source);
            blockerPos = origin + direction * t;
            return vec3(0.0);
        }
    }
    return vec3(0.0);
}

vec3 evaluateVPTPrimaryIndirect(vec3 hitPos, vec3 hitNormal, vec3 hitAlbedo,
                                vec3 direction, inout uint rngState)
{
    vec3 blockerAlbedo, blockerPos, blockerNormal;
    vec3 first = traceVPTEmitterDDA(hitPos + hitNormal * 0.02, direction,
        blockerAlbedo, blockerPos, blockerNormal);
    if (max(first.r, max(first.g, first.b)) > 0.0)
        return hitAlbedo * first;



    if (max(blockerAlbedo.r, max(blockerAlbedo.g, blockerAlbedo.b)) > 0.0)
    {
        vec3 secondAlbedo, secondPos, secondNormal;
        vec3 second = traceVPTEmitterDDA(blockerPos + blockerNormal * 0.02,
            sampleHemisphereCosine(blockerNormal, rngState), secondAlbedo,
            secondPos, secondNormal);
        return hitAlbedo * blockerAlbedo * second * 0.55;
    }
    return vec3(0.0);
}






const float SOLAR_LATITUDE_35_SIN = 0.57357643635;
const float SOLAR_LATITUDE_35_COS = 0.81915204429;







vec3 getTimeDrivenSunDirection()
{
    const float TWO_PI = 6.28318530718;
    float dayAngle =
        fract(cam.historyParams.w) * TWO_PI;







    if (abs(cam.weatherParams.z - 3.0) < 0.25)
    {

        return vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);
    }


    return vec3(
        cos(dayAngle),
        SOLAR_LATITUDE_35_COS * sin(dayAngle),
        SOLAR_LATITUDE_35_SIN * sin(dayAngle)
    );
}







const float TIME_DRIVEN_SUN_NOON_SCALE = 1.22077458876;


const float TIME_DRIVEN_MOON_NOON_SCALE = 0.03;







const float END_ORB_SUN_LIGHT_SCALE = 0.6;




const float WEATHER_RF_SKY_BRIGHTNESS = 0.20;
const float WEATHER_RF_SUN_BRIGHTNESS = 0.10;
const float WEATHER_RF_DENSITY = 1.0;
const vec3 WEATHER_THUNDER_COLD_TINT = vec3(0.72, 0.78, 0.86);

float getTimeDrivenSunElevation()
{
    const float TWO_PI = 6.28318530718;



    if (abs(cam.weatherParams.z - 3.0) < 0.25)
    {
        return 1.0;
    }

    return SOLAR_LATITUDE_35_COS *
        sin(fract(cam.historyParams.w) * TWO_PI);
}

{

        ? clamp(cam.weatherParams.x, 0.0, 1.0)
        : 0.0;
}

{
    return abs(cam.weatherParams.z - 1.0) < 0.25
        ? clamp(cam.weatherParams.y, 0.0, 1.0)
        : 0.0;
}

const float RAIN_WALL_ALBEDO_DARKENING = 0.30;
const float RAIN_WALL_FILM_ROUGHNESS = 0.10;










const float RAIN_WALL_DRIP_ROUGHNESS = 0.045;












const float RAIN_VISIBILITY_GAIN = 2.0;



















float rainPuddleHash(vec2 p)
{
    p = fract(p * vec2(0.1031, 0.11369));
    p += dot(p, p.yx + 19.19);
    return fract( (p.x + p.y) * p.x);
}
























const float RAIN_WALL_HEARTFELT_SCALE = 0.25;
const float RAIN_WALL_HEARTFELT_STATIC_SCALE = 0.15;
const float RAIN_WALL_HEARTFELT_FLOW_SCALE = 0.4;
const float RAIN_WALL_HEARTFELT_TIME_SCALE = 0.2;
const float RAIN_WALL_HEARTFELT_NORMAL_EPSILON = 0.004;
const float RAIN_WALL_HEARTFELT_BUMP = 6.0;

vec3 heartfeltN13(float p)
{
    vec3 p3 = fract(vec3(p) * vec3(0.1031, 0.11369, 0.13787));
    p3 += dot(p3, p3.yzx + 19.19);
    return fract(vec3(
        (p3.x + p3.y) * p3.z,
        (p3.x + p3.z) * p3.y,
        (p3.y + p3.z) * p3.x
    ));
}

float heartfeltN(float t)
{
    return fract(sin(t * 12345.564) * 7658.76);
}

float heartfeltSaw(float b, float t)
{
    return smoothstep(0.0, b, t) * smoothstep(1.0, b, t);
}

float heartfeltStaticDrops(vec2 uv, float t)
{
    uv *= 40.0;

    vec2 id = floor(uv);
    uv = fract(uv) - 0.5;

    vec3 n = heartfeltN13(id.x * 107.45 + id.y * 3543.654);
    vec2 p = (n.xy - 0.5) * 0.7;
    float d = length(uv - p);

    float fade = heartfeltSaw(0.025, fract(t + n.z));
    return smoothstep(0.3, 0.0, d) * fract(n.z * 10.0) * fade;
}

vec2 heartfeltDropLayer(vec2 uv, vec2 flowUv, float t)
{
    uv.y += t * 0.75;

    vec2 a = vec2(6.0, 1.0);
    vec2 grid = a * 2.0;
    vec2 id = floor(uv * grid);

    uv.y += heartfeltN(id.x);

    id = floor(uv * grid);
    vec3 n = heartfeltN13(id.x * 35.2 + id.y * 2376.1);
    vec2 st = fract(uv * grid) - vec2(0.5, 0.0);

    float x = n.x - 0.5;
    float y = flowUv.y * 20.0;
    float wiggle = sin(y + sin(y));
    x += wiggle * (0.5 - abs(x)) * (n.z - 0.5);
    x *= 0.7;

    float ti = fract(t + n.z);
    y = (heartfeltSaw(0.85, ti) - 0.5) * 0.9 + 0.5;
    vec2 p = vec2(x, y);

    float d = length( (st - p) * a.yx);
    float mainDrop = smoothstep(0.4, 0.0, d);

    float r = sqrt(smoothstep(1.0, y, st.y));
    float cd = abs(st.x - x);
    float trail = smoothstep(0.23 * r, 0.15 * r * r, cd);
    float trailFront = smoothstep(- 0.02, 0.02, st.y - y);
    trail *= trailFront * r * r;

    y = fract(flowUv.y * 10.0) + (st.y - 0.5);
    float dd = length(st - vec2(x, y));
    float droplets = smoothstep(0.3, 0.0, dd);
    float m = mainDrop + droplets * r * trailFront;

    return vec2(m, trail);
}







vec2 heartfeltDrops(vec2 wallUV, float t, float l0, float l1, float l2, float sizeScale)
{
    float inverseSize = 1.0 / max(sizeScale, 1.0e-3);
    vec2 uv = wallUV * (RAIN_WALL_HEARTFELT_SCALE * inverseSize);
    vec2 flowUv = wallUV * (RAIN_WALL_HEARTFELT_FLOW_SCALE * inverseSize);

    float s = heartfeltStaticDrops(
        wallUV * (RAIN_WALL_HEARTFELT_STATIC_SCALE * inverseSize), t) * l0;

    vec2 m1 = heartfeltDropLayer(uv, flowUv, t) * l1;
    vec2 m2 = heartfeltDropLayer(uv * 1.85, flowUv, t) * l2;

    float c = s + m1.x + m2.x;
    return vec2(smoothstep(0.3, 1.0, c), max(m1.y * l0, m2.y * l1));
}

float evaluateRainPuddlePattern(vec2 worldXZ)
{
    vec2 p = worldXZ * 0.31;
    vec2 cell = floor(p);
    vec2 f = fract(p);
    vec2 blend = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);

    float n00 = rainPuddleHash(cell);
    float n10 = rainPuddleHash(cell + vec2(1.0, 0.0));
    float n01 = rainPuddleHash(cell + vec2(0.0, 1.0));
    float n11 = rainPuddleHash(cell + vec2(1.0, 1.0));
    float broad = mix(mix(n00, n10, blend.x), mix(n01, n11, blend.x), blend.y);
    float detail = rainPuddleHash(floor(worldXZ * 1.73));
    return smoothstep(0.56, 0.82, broad * 0.80 + detail * 0.20);
}






float evaluateContinuousRainWetnessNoise(vec2 worldXZ)
{
    vec2 p0 = worldXZ * 5.0;
    vec2 cell0 = floor(p0);
    vec2 frac0 = fract(p0);
    vec2 blend0 = frac0 * frac0 * (3.0 - 2.0 * frac0);
    float n00 = rainPuddleHash(cell0);
    float n10 = rainPuddleHash(cell0 + vec2(1.0, 0.0));
    float n01 = rainPuddleHash(cell0 + vec2(0.0, 1.0));
    float n11 = rainPuddleHash(cell0 + vec2(1.0, 1.0));
    float n0 = mix(mix(n00, n10, blend0.x), mix(n01, n11, blend0.x), blend0.y);

    vec2 p1 = p0 * 0.37;
    vec2 cell1 = floor(p1);
    vec2 frac1 = fract(p1);
    vec2 blend1 = frac1 * frac1 * (3.0 - 2.0 * frac1);
    float n20 = rainPuddleHash(cell1);
    float n30 = rainPuddleHash(cell1 + vec2(1.0, 0.0));
    float n21 = rainPuddleHash(cell1 + vec2(0.0, 1.0));
    float n31 = rainPuddleHash(cell1 + vec2(1.0, 1.0));
    float n1 = mix(mix(n20, n30, blend1.x), mix(n21, n31, blend1.x), blend1.y);

    float n = n0 + n1 * 3.0;
    return clamp(1.0 - n * 0.25, 0.0, 1.0) * 0.7 + 0.3;
}





vec2 evaluateRainPuddleRippleGradient(vec2 worldXZ)
{
    float rain = getOverworldRainStrength();
    if (rain <= 0.01)
    {
        return vec2(0.0);
    }




    const float CELL_SIZE = 0.432;
    const float RING_SPEED = 0.336;
    const float RING_FREQUENCY = 66.66667;
    float time = uintBitsToFloat(cam.padding);
    vec2 baseCell = floor(worldXZ / CELL_SIZE);
    vec2 gradient = vec2(0.0);

    for (int y = - 1; y <= 1; ++ y)
    {
        for (int x = - 1; x <= 1; ++ x)
        {
            vec2 cell = baseCell + vec2(float(x), float(y));
            float seedX = rainPuddleHash(cell + vec2(17.0, 43.0));
            float seedZ = rainPuddleHash(cell + vec2(71.0, 29.0));
            float age = fract(time * mix(0.42, 1.08, rain) +
                rainPuddleHash(cell + vec2(113.0, 7.0)));
            vec2 impact = (cell + vec2(seedX, seedZ)) * CELL_SIZE;
            vec2 delta = worldXZ - impact;
            float distanceToImpact = length(delta);
            float envelope = (1.0 - smoothstep(0.52, 1.0, age)) *
                (1.0 - smoothstep(0.12, 0.204, distanceToImpact));
            if (envelope <= 0.0 || distanceToImpact <= 1.0e-4)
            {
                continue;
            }

            float radius = age * RING_SPEED;
            float slope = cos( (distanceToImpact - radius) * RING_FREQUENCY);
            gradient += normalize(delta) * slope * envelope;
        }
    }





    return gradient * (0.060 * rain) * RAIN_VISIBILITY_GAIN;
}





const int C3_CLOUD_HQ_SAMPLES = 16;
const int C3_CLOUD_LQ_SAMPLES = 5;
const int C3_CLOUD_SUN_SAMPLES = 5;




const float C3_CLOUD_BASIC_SHADOWLIGHT = 0.10;
const float C3_CLOUD_BASIC_SKYLIGHT = 0.10;
const float C3_CLOUD_FADE_DISTANCE = 30000.0;
const float C3_CLOUD_MIN_TRANSMITTANCE = 0.20;
const float C3_CLOUD_MIN_SUN_TRANSMITTANCE = 0.10;

float c3Saturate(float value)
{
    return clamp(value, 0.0, 1.0);
}

#line 1 "./tyndall_cloud_field.glsl"













const float TYNDALL_RADIUS = 6360000.0;

















const float TYNDALL_MAX_DISTANCE = 42000.0;

vec3 tyndallRegionHash(vec3 cell)
{
    uvec3 h = uvec3(ivec3(cell));
    h = h * uvec3(1597334677u, 3812015801u, 2798796415u);
    uint seed = h.x ^ h.y ^ h.z;
    uvec3 v = seed ^ uvec3(2246822519u, 3266489917u, 668265263u);
    v ^= v >> 16u;
    v *= 2246822519u;
    v ^= v >> 13u;
    return vec3(v & 0x00ffffffu) / 16777216.0;
}

vec3 tyndallRegion(vec3 p)
{
    vec3 cell = floor(p);
    vec3 f = fract(p);
    f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
    return mix(
        mix(mix(tyndallRegionHash(cell), tyndallRegionHash(cell + vec3(1, 0, 0)), f.x),
            mix(tyndallRegionHash(cell + vec3(0, 1, 0)), tyndallRegionHash(cell + vec3(1, 1, 0)), f.x), f.y),
        mix(mix(tyndallRegionHash(cell + vec3(0, 0, 1)), tyndallRegionHash(cell + vec3(1, 0, 1)), f.x),
            mix(tyndallRegionHash(cell + vec3(0, 1, 1)), tyndallRegionHash(cell + vec3(1, 1, 1)), f.x), f.y), f.z);
}

float tyndallTime()
{
    return max(0.0, uintBitsToFloat(cam.padding));
}

vec3 tyndallWind()
{
    return vec3(0.81915204429, 0.0, 0.57357643635) *
        cam.cloudParams1.y * tyndallTime();
}







const float TYNDALL_RAIN_CHURN_SCALE = 0.12;

float tyndallEvolution()
{
    return cam.cloudParams1.z *
        mix(1.0, TYNDALL_RAIN_CHURN_SCALE, clamp(cam.weatherParams.x, 0.0, 1.0));
}

float tyndallHeight(vec3 p)
{
    vec2 horizontal = p.xz - cam.cloudParams3.xy;
    float vertical = p.y - cam.cloudParams0.x;

    float delta = dot(horizontal, horizontal) + vertical * (2.0 * TYNDALL_RADIUS + vertical);
    float altitude = delta / (sqrt(max(TYNDALL_RADIUS * TYNDALL_RADIUS + delta, 0.0)) + TYNDALL_RADIUS);
    return altitude / max(cam.cloudParams0.y, 1.0);
}







float tyndallNoiseLod(float footprint, float frequency, float texResolution)
{
    return clamp(log2(max(footprint * frequency * texResolution, 1.0)), 0.0, 16.0);
}





















struct TyndallRegions
{
    vec3 region;

    vec3 regionLarge;
};

TyndallRegions tyndallRegionsFor(vec3 p)
{
    vec3 advected = p + tyndallWind();
    float size = clamp(cam.cloudParams3.z, 0.50, 3.0);
    TyndallRegions r;

    r.region = tyndallRegion(vec3(advected.x * 0.00012 / size, 0.37, advected.z * 0.00012 / size));
    r.regionLarge = vec3(0.0);
    if (clamp(cam.cloudParams6.w, 0.0, 2.0) > 0.001)
    {
        r.regionLarge = tyndallRegion(
            vec3(advected.x * 0.000041 / size, 0.61, advected.z * 0.000041 / size));
    }
    return r;
}


float tyndallDensityFromRegions(vec3 p, bool cheap, float footprint, TyndallRegions regions)
{
    if (cam.cloudParams1.w < 0.5 || abs(cam.weatherParams.z - 1.0) >= 0.25)
        return 0.0;
    float h = tyndallHeight(p);
    if (h <= 0.0 || h >= 1.0) return 0.0;

    vec3 wind = tyndallWind();
    vec3 advected = p + wind;
    float size = clamp(cam.cloudParams3.z, 0.50, 3.0);
    float distribution = clamp(cam.cloudParams3.w, 0.0, 1.0);
    vec3 region = regions.region;










    vec2 detileWarp = vec2(0.0);
    float detileStrength = clamp(cam.cloudParams6.w, 0.0, 2.0);
    if (detileStrength > 0.001)
    {
        vec3 regionLarge = regions.regionLarge;
        detileWarp = detileStrength * (
            (region.xy - 0.5) * (3200.0 * size) +
            (regionLarge.xy - 0.5) * (8200.0 * size));
    }

    float baseNoise = textureLod(cloudShapeNoise,
        vec3( (advected.xz + detileWarp) * (0.0000525 / size), 0.37),
        tyndallNoiseLod(footprint, 0.0000525 / size, 128.0)).r;
    float localBase = baseNoise * min(0.125, 260.0 / max(cam.cloudParams0.y, 1.0));
    h = (h - localBase) / max(1.0 - localBase, 0.01);
    if (h <= 0.0 || h >= 1.0) return 0.0;






    if (cam.cloudParams6.z > 0.001)
    {
        float stretchCover = mix(cam.cloudParams0.z,
            min(cam.cloudParams0.z + 0.30, 0.98),
            clamp(cam.weatherParams.x, 0.0, 1.0));
        float s = 1.0 + cam.cloudParams6.z * 2.0 * stretchCover;
        h = h / (1.0 + (s - 1.0) * h);
    }



    vec3 q = (advected + vec3(0.0, - tyndallTime() * tyndallEvolution() * 22.0, 0.0)) * (0.000105 / size);
    q += (region - 0.5) * 0.65;
    const mat3 rotation = mat3(0.0, 0.8, 0.6, - 0.8, 0.36, - 0.48, - 0.6, - 0.48, 0.64);
    vec2 a = textureLod(cloudShapeNoise, q, tyndallNoiseLod(footprint, 0.000105 / size, 128.0)).rg;
    vec2 b = textureLod(cloudShapeNoise, rotation * q * 1.37 + 0.173,
        tyndallNoiseLod(footprint, 0.000105 * 1.37 / size, 128.0)).rg;
    float shape = mix(dot(a, vec2(0.85, 0.15)), dot(b, vec2(0.85, 0.15)), 0.25);
    shape = mix(shape, smoothstep(0.22, 0.78, shape), 0.45);































    float bottomSmoothHeight = cam.cloudShapeParams.x >= 0.0
        ? cam.cloudShapeParams.x : 0.22;
    float bottomSmoothPow = cam.cloudShapeParams.y > 0.0
        ? cam.cloudShapeParams.y : 1.0;
    float bottomBlend = 0.0;
    if (bottomSmoothHeight > 0.0)
    {
        bottomBlend = pow(clamp(1.0 - h / bottomSmoothHeight, 0.0, 1.0),
            max(bottomSmoothPow, 1.0e-3));
    }
    shape = mix(shape, 1.0, bottomBlend);
























    float profileType = clamp(cam.cloudProfileParams.x +
        (region.x - 0.5) * clamp(cam.cloudProfileParams.y, 0.0, 1.0), 0.0, 1.0);
    bool profileLowerHalf = profileType < 0.5;
    float profileTypeT = profileLowerHalf ? profileType * 2.0 : (profileType - 0.5) * 2.0;
    float profileTypeLo = profileLowerHalf
        ? mix(1.00, 1.40, profileTypeT) : mix(1.40, 0.60, profileTypeT);
    float profileTypeHi = profileLowerHalf
        ? mix(1.00, 0.80, profileTypeT) : mix(0.80, 1.23, profileTypeT);
    float profileTypeCurve = profileLowerHalf
        ? mix(1.00, 0.85, profileTypeT) : mix(0.85, 0.70, profileTypeT);
    float profile = clamp(h / (clamp(cam.cloudParams4.x, 0.03, 0.30) * profileTypeLo),
        0.0, 1.0) * clamp( (1.0 - h) / (0.75 * profileTypeHi), 0.0, 1.0);











    float profileCurve = cam.cloudShape2Params.z > 0.0
        ? cam.cloudShape2Params.z : 1.0;

    profile = pow(clamp(profile, 0.0, 1.0),
        max(profileCurve * profileTypeCurve, 1.0e-3));

    float rain = clamp(cam.weatherParams.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    float coverage = mix(cam.cloudParams0.z, min(cam.cloudParams0.z + 0.30, 0.98), rain);
    coverage = clamp(coverage + (region.x - 0.5) * distribution * 0.60, 0.02, 0.95);
    float density = shape * profile - (1.0 - coverage);
    if (density <= 0.0) return 0.0;











    density = density / max(coverage, 1.0e-3);


    if (! cheap)
    {









        float detailWeight = 1.0 - smoothstep(60.0, 320.0, footprint);



        float detail = 0.5;
        if (detailWeight > 0.01)
        {



            float detailScale = cam.cloudShape2Params.x > 0.0
                ? cam.cloudShape2Params.x : 1.0;
            float detailContrast = cam.cloudShape2Params.y > 0.0
                ? cam.cloudShape2Params.y : 1.0;
            float detailFreq = 0.000775 * detailScale;
            vec3 dq = (p + detileWarp.xyx * vec3(1.0, 0.0, 1.0) + wind * 2.5 +
                vec3(tyndallTime() * tyndallEvolution() * 35.0,
                tyndallTime() * tyndallEvolution() * 14.0, 0.0)) * detailFreq;
            vec3 curl = textureLod(cloudCurlNoise, dq * 0.08,
                tyndallNoiseLod(footprint, detailFreq * 0.08, 64.0)).xyz * 2.0 - 1.0;





            vec3 detailSample = textureLod(cloudShapeNoise, dq + curl * 0.08,
                tyndallNoiseLod(footprint, detailFreq, 128.0)).rba;
            detail = mix(0.5, detailSample.r, detailWeight);
            detail = clamp(0.5 + (detail - 0.5) * detailContrast, 0.0, 1.0);
            float wispyStrength = clamp(cam.cloudDetailParams.x, 0.0, 2.0);
            if (wispyStrength > 0.001)
            {




                float wispyEdgeWidth = cam.cloudDetailParams.y > 0.0
                    ? cam.cloudDetailParams.y : 0.35;
                float wispyReach = clamp(cam.cloudDetailParams.z, 0.0, 1.0);
                float wispyTopHeight = cam.cloudDetailParams.w > 0.0
                    ? clamp(cam.cloudDetailParams.w, 0.05, 1.0) : 0.45;
                float wispy = mix(detailSample.g, detailSample.b, clamp(h, 0.0, 1.0));
                float topWeight = smoothstep(wispyTopHeight * 0.5, wispyTopHeight, h);
                float edgeWeight = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0,
                    max(wispyEdgeWidth, 1.0e-3), density);
                float wispyErosion = wispy * (1.0 + wispyReach);
                detail = mix(detail, wispyErosion, clamp(wispyStrength *
                    mix(edgeWeight, 1.0, topWeight), 0.0, 1.0));
            }
        }




















        float erosion = clamp(detail * cam.cloudParams1.x * 1.0 *
            mix(0.75, 1.25, h) * (1.0 - bottomBlend), 0.0, 0.8);
        density = clamp( (density - erosion) / max(1.0 - erosion, 1.0e-3),
            0.0, 1.0);




    }








    {
        const float kPi = 3.14159265;
        float covClear = clamp(cam.cloudParams0.z, 0.0, 0.95);
        float covRain = clamp(cam.cloudParams0.z + 0.30, 0.0, 0.98);
        float cov = mix(covClear, covRain, clamp(cam.weatherParams.x, 0.0, 1.0));
        float hh = clamp(h, 0.0, 1.0);
        cov *= (1.0 - hh * hh);
        float cloudBase = clamp(baseNoise - (0.65 - cov), 0.0, 1.0);
        float profile = (hh * kPi) * (hh * kPi) / (kPi * kPi);



        density = cloudBase * profile * 5.0;
    }

    return max(density, 0.0) * cam.cloudParams0.w;
}



float tyndallDensity(vec3 p, bool cheap, float footprint)
{
    if (cam.cloudParams1.w < 0.5 || abs(cam.weatherParams.z - 1.0) >= 0.25)
        return 0.0;
    float h = tyndallHeight(p);
    if (h <= 0.0 || h >= 1.0) return 0.0;
    return tyndallDensityFromRegions(p, cheap, footprint, tyndallRegionsFor(p));
}

bool tyndallSphereRoots(vec3 p, vec3 direction, float radius, out vec2 roots)
{
    vec2 xz = p.xz - cam.cloudParams3.xy;
    float y = p.y - cam.cloudParams0.x + (TYNDALL_RADIUS - radius);
    float b = dot(vec3(xz.x, y + radius, xz.y), direction);
    float c = dot(xz, xz) + y * (2.0 * radius + y);
    float discriminant = b * b - c;
    if (discriminant < 0.0) return false;
    float q = - b - (b >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : - 1.0) * sqrt(discriminant);
    float other = abs(q) > 1.0e-5 ? c / q : - b;
    roots = vec2(min(q, other), max(q, other));
    return true;
}

bool tyndallInterval(vec3 origin, vec3 direction, out float entry, out float exitDistance)
{
    vec2 outer;
    if (! tyndallSphereRoots(origin, direction, TYNDALL_RADIUS + cam.cloudParams0.y, outer))
        return false;
    entry = max(outer.x, 0.0);
    exitDistance = min(outer.y, TYNDALL_MAX_DISTANCE);
    vec2 inner;
    if (tyndallSphereRoots(origin, direction, TYNDALL_RADIUS, inner) && inner.y > entry)
    {
        if (inner.x > entry) exitDistance = min(exitDistance, inner.x);
        else entry = max(entry, inner.y);
    }

    exitDistance = min(exitDistance, entry + 18000.0);
    return exitDistance > entry + 0.01;
}











bool tyndallIntervalFar(vec3 origin, vec3 direction, out float entry, out float exitDistance)
{
    vec2 outer;
    if (! tyndallSphereRoots(origin, direction, TYNDALL_RADIUS + cam.cloudParams0.y, outer))
        return false;
    entry = max(outer.x, 0.0);
    exitDistance = min(outer.y, 420000.0);
    vec2 inner;
    if (tyndallSphereRoots(origin, direction, TYNDALL_RADIUS, inner) && inner.y > entry)
    {
        if (inner.x > entry) exitDistance = min(exitDistance, inner.x);
        else entry = max(entry, inner.y);
    }
    return exitDistance > entry + 0.01;
}

































































const float TYNDALL_SHADOW_DENSITY_GATE = 0.45;

float tyndallShadowGate(float density)
{

    return max(density - TYNDALL_SHADOW_DENSITY_GATE, 0.0) /
        (1.0 - TYNDALL_SHADOW_DENSITY_GATE);



}

float c3CloudDensity(vec3 worldPos, bool simplified)
{
    return tyndallDensity(worldPos, simplified, 0.0);
}


































float c3CloudSunTransmittanceUncached(vec3 worldPos, vec3 sunDirection);

bool g_c3CloudSunCacheValid = false;
vec3 g_c3CloudSunCachePos = vec3(0.0);
vec3 g_c3CloudSunCacheSun = vec3(0.0);
float g_c3CloudSunCacheValue = 1.0;

float c3CloudSunTransmittance(vec3 worldPos, vec3 sunDirection)
{

    if (g_c3CloudSunCacheValid &&
       dot(sunDirection, g_c3CloudSunCacheSun) > 0.999999 &&
       max(abs(worldPos.x - g_c3CloudSunCachePos.x),
           max(abs(worldPos.y - g_c3CloudSunCachePos.y),
               abs(worldPos.z - g_c3CloudSunCachePos.z))) <=
                                       0.05)
    {
        return g_c3CloudSunCacheValue;
    }
    float cachedValue =
        c3CloudSunTransmittanceUncached(worldPos, sunDirection);
    g_c3CloudSunCacheValid = true;
    g_c3CloudSunCachePos = worldPos;
    g_c3CloudSunCacheSun = sunDirection;
    g_c3CloudSunCacheValue = cachedValue;
    return cachedValue;



}

float c3CloudSunTransmittanceUncached(vec3 worldPos, vec3 sunDirection)
{

    if (abs(cam.weatherParams.z - 1.0) >= 0.25 || sunDirection.y <= 1.0e-5)
    {
        return 1.0;
    }

    float entryDistance;
    float exitDistance;
    if (! tyndallInterval(worldPos, sunDirection, entryDistance, exitDistance))
        return 1.0;
    float stepLength = (exitDistance - entryDistance) / float(C3_CLOUD_SUN_SAMPLES);
    float transmittance = 1.0;
    for (int i = 0; i < C3_CLOUD_SUN_SAMPLES; ++ i)
    {
        vec3 samplePos = worldPos + sunDirection *
            (entryDistance + stepLength * (float(i) + 0.5));



        float opticalDepth = tyndallShadowGate(c3CloudDensity(samplePos, true)) *
                              cam.cloudParams2.z * stepLength;




        transmittance *= exp2(- opticalDepth * 1.44269504089);
        if (transmittance < C3_CLOUD_MIN_SUN_TRANSMITTANCE)
        {
            break;
        }
    }
    return c3Saturate( (transmittance - C3_CLOUD_MIN_SUN_TRANSMITTANCE) /
        (1.0 - C3_CLOUD_MIN_SUN_TRANSMITTANCE));



}

float getClearTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale()
{
    float elevation = getTimeDrivenSunElevation();


    float normalizedCycleElevation =
        abs(elevation) / SOLAR_LATITUDE_35_COS;
    return elevation >= 0.0
        ? normalizedCycleElevation * TIME_DRIVEN_SUN_NOON_SCALE
        : normalizedCycleElevation * TIME_DRIVEN_MOON_NOON_SCALE;
}

float getTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale()
{
    float clearScale = getClearTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale();
    float lightScale = getTimeDrivenSunElevation() >= 0.0



        ? clearScale * getWeatherSunRadianceFactor()
        : clearScale;


    if (abs(cam.weatherParams.z - 3.0) < 0.25)
    {
        lightScale *= END_ORB_SUN_LIGHT_SCALE;
    }
    return lightScale;
}

vec3 getTimeDrivenSkyLightDirection()
{


    return getTimeDrivenSunElevation() >= 0.0
        ? getTimeDrivenSunDirection()
        : - getTimeDrivenSunDirection();
}

const vec3 SKY_SUN_OPTICAL_DEPTH = vec3(0.0824, 0.1656, 0.2024);



float skySunAirMass(float elevation)
{
    float h = clamp(elevation, - 0.065, 1.0);
    return min(24.0, 1.0 / max(0.045, h + 0.135));
}



vec3 skySunPhysicalBaseColor(float elevation)
{
    float mass = skySunAirMass(elevation);
    return exp2(- 1.44269504089 * SKY_SUN_OPTICAL_DEPTH * (mass - 1.0));
}























float skySunGoldenHourWeight(float elevation)
{
    return smoothstep(- 0.035, 0.005, elevation)
        * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.06, 0.22, elevation));
}





vec3 skySunGoldenTint(vec3 baseColor, float elevation)
{
    float w = skySunGoldenHourWeight(elevation);
    if (w <= 0.0)
    {
        return baseColor;
    }
    float luma = dot(baseColor, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));





    vec3 saturated = max(mix(vec3(luma), baseColor, 1.0 + 1.0 * w), vec3(0.0));
    return saturated * mix(vec3(1.0), vec3(1.32, 0.62, 0.30), w);
}

vec3 sunDirection()
{
    const float TWO_PI = 6.28318530718;
    float angle = fract(cam.historyParams.w) * TWO_PI;
    return normalize(vec3(cos(angle), 0.81915204429 * sin(angle),
        0.57357643635 * sin(angle)));
}









const vec3 END_SKY_POLE = vec3(0.0, 0.57357643635, - 0.81915204429);






const float END_SKY_SPIN_RATE = 2.0;


const float END_EARTH_PHASE_OFFSET = 0.75;

const float END_EARTH_TILT = 0.40840704497;

vec3 endEclipticDirection(float t)
{
    const float TWO_PI = 6.28318530718;
    float a = fract(t) * TWO_PI;
    return normalize(vec3(cos(a), 0.81915204429 * sin(a),
        0.57357643635 * sin(a)));
}


vec3 endEarthDirection()
{
    return endEclipticDirection(
        cam.historyParams.w * END_SKY_SPIN_RATE + END_EARTH_PHASE_OFFSET);
}








vec3 endEarthAxis()
{
    float t = cam.historyParams.w * END_SKY_SPIN_RATE +
        END_EARTH_PHASE_OFFSET + 0.25;
    return normalize(cos(END_EARTH_TILT) * endEclipticDirection(t) +
        sin(END_EARTH_TILT) * END_SKY_POLE);
}

float endEarthEclipseFactor(vec3 sunDir, vec3 earthDir)
{
    if (abs(cam.weatherParams.z - 3.0) >= 0.25)
    {
        return 1.0;
    }
    float angle = acos(clamp(dot(sunDir, earthDir), - 1.0, 1.0));
    return smoothstep(END_EARTH_ANGULAR_RADIUS - END_SUN_ANGULAR_RADIUS,
                      END_EARTH_ANGULAR_RADIUS + END_SUN_ANGULAR_RADIUS, angle);
}


float endEarthPhase(vec3 sunDir, vec3 earthDir)
{
    return 0.5 * (1.0 - dot(sunDir, earthDir));
}


const vec3 TWILIGHT_DAWN_TINT = vec3(1.00, 0.48, 0.24);
const vec3 TWILIGHT_DUSK_TINT = vec3(1.00, 0.45, 0.08);
const vec3 TWILIGHT_PREDAWN_TINT = vec3(0.62, 0.50, 0.86);

float twilightEveningWeight()
{
    const float TWO_PI = 6.28318530718;
    return smoothstep(0.20, - 0.20, cos(fract(cam.historyParams.w) * TWO_PI));
}


float twilightPredawnWeight(float sunElevation)
{
    return(1.0 - twilightEveningWeight()) *
        smoothstep(- 0.30, - 0.10, sunElevation) *
        (1.0 - smoothstep(- 0.10, - 0.02, sunElevation));
}


vec3 twilightPalette(float sunElevation)
{
    return mix(
        mix(TWILIGHT_DAWN_TINT, TWILIGHT_DUSK_TINT, twilightEveningWeight()),
        TWILIGHT_PREDAWN_TINT,
        twilightPredawnWeight(sunElevation)
    );
}




vec3 skyModelSunColor(vec3 lightDirection, float goldenStrength)
{


    vec3 color = skySunPhysicalBaseColor(lightDirection.y);




    float rain = clamp(cam.weatherParams.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    float thunder = clamp(cam.weatherParams.y, 0.0, 1.0);
    float sunset = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.04, 0.22, lightDirection.y);
    vec3 twilightTint = twilightPalette(lightDirection.y);
    color = mix(color, twilightTint, sunset * 0.78);

    color = mix(color, skySunGoldenTint(color, lightDirection.y), goldenStrength);

    return color * mix(1.0, 0.35, rain) *
        mix(vec3(1.0), vec3(0.72, 0.78, 0.86), thunder);
}
