#line 1 "./aurora_common.glsl"









































const float AURORA_AMBIENT_STRENGTH = 0.06;

const vec3 AURORA_LIGHT_COLOR = vec3(0.35, 1.00, 0.55);


const float AURORA_EXPOSURE = 0.95;








const float AURORA_LAYER_BASE = 0.80;
const float AURORA_LAYER_GROW = 0.002;
const float AURORA_STEPS = 50.0;
const float AURORA_NOISE_SCALE = 0.06;
const float AURORA_WARP_SPEED = 0.06;
const float AURORA_DITHER = 0.006;
const float AURORA_GAIN = 1.8;



const float AURORA_TIME_PER_FRAME = 1.0 / 60.0;


const mat2 AURORA_M2 = mat2(0.80, 0.60, - 0.60, 0.80);


mat2 auroraRot(float a)
{
    float c = cos(a);
    float s = sin(a);
    return mat2(c, - s, s, c);
}



float auroraTri(float x)
{
    return clamp(abs(fract(x) - 0.5), 0.01, 0.49);
}

vec2 auroraTri2(vec2 p)
{
    return vec2(
        auroraTri(p.x) + auroraTri(p.y),
        auroraTri(p.y + auroraTri(p.x)));
}



float auroraTriNoise2d(vec2 p, float time)
{
    float z = 1.8;
    float z2 = 2.5;
    float rz = 0.0;
    p *= auroraRot(p.x * AURORA_NOISE_SCALE);
    vec2 bp = p;
    for (float i = 0.0; i < 5.0; i ++)
    {

        vec2 dg = auroraTri2(bp * 1.85) * 0.75 * auroraRot(time * AURORA_WARP_SPEED);
        p -= dg / z2;
        bp *= 1.3;
        z2 *= 0.45;
        z *= 0.42;
        p *= 1.21 + (rz - 1.0) * 0.02;
        rz += auroraTri(p.x + auroraTri(p.y)) * z;
        p *= - AURORA_M2;
    }
    return clamp(1.0 / pow(rz * 29.0, 1.3), 0.0, 0.55);
}







float auroraDither(vec3 rd)
{
    vec2 sp = vec2(
        dot(rd, vec3(127.1, 311.7, 74.7)),
        dot(rd, vec3(269.5, 183.3, 246.1))) * 512.0;
    return fract(sin(sp.x + sp.y) * 43758.5453123);
}



vec3 auroraRaymarch(vec3 rd, float strength, float time)
{
    float dither = AURORA_DITHER * auroraDither(rd);

    vec4 col = vec4(0.0);
    vec4 avgCol = vec4(0.0);
    for (float i = 0.0; i < AURORA_STEPS; i ++)
    {

        float of = dither * smoothstep(0.0, 15.0, i);


        float pt = (AURORA_LAYER_BASE + pow(i, 1.4) * AURORA_LAYER_GROW)
            / (rd.y * 2.0 + 0.4) - of;



        vec3 bpos = pt * rd;
        float rzt = auroraTriNoise2d(bpos.zx, time);
        vec4 col2 = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, rzt);


        col2.rgb = (sin(1.0 - vec3(2.15, - 0.5, 1.2) + i * 0.043) * 0.5 + 0.5) * rzt;

        avgCol = mix(avgCol, col2, 0.5);

        col += avgCol * exp2(- i * 0.065 - 2.5) * smoothstep(0.0, 5.0, i);
    }


    col *= clamp(rd.y * 15.0 + 0.4, 0.0, 1.0);
    return col.rgb * (AURORA_GAIN * strength);
}



vec3 evaluateAurora(vec3 rayDir, float sunElevation)
{
    float strength = cam.auroraParams.x * cam.auroraParams.y;
    if (strength <= 0.001)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    if (abs(cam.weatherParams.z - 1.0) >= 0.25)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    float night = smoothstep(0.06, - 0.20, sunElevation);
    float weather = 1.0 - clamp(cam.weatherParams.x, 0.0, 1.0) * 0.85;
    float gate = strength * night * weather;
    if (gate <= 0.001)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }



    if (rayDir.y <= - 0.02)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }
    float time = float(cam.frameCount) * AURORA_TIME_PER_FRAME * cam.auroraParams.z;
    return auroraRaymarch(rayDir, gate * AURORA_EXPOSURE, time);
}

vec3 getTimeDrivenSkyLightColorRaw()
{
    float elevation = getTimeDrivenSunElevation();
    if (elevation < 0.0)
    {





        float twilight = smoothstep(- 0.22, - 0.005, elevation);
        vec3 twilightTint = mix(TWILIGHT_DAWN_TINT, TWILIGHT_DUSK_TINT,
            twilightEveningWeight());
        return mix(vec3(0.46, 0.56, 1.00), twilightTint, twilight * 0.72);



    }




    vec3 base = skySunPhysicalBaseColor(elevation);








    float sunset = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.04, 0.22, elevation);
    vec3 twilightTint = mix(TWILIGHT_DAWN_TINT, TWILIGHT_DUSK_TINT,
        twilightEveningWeight());



    vec3 lightColor = mix(base, twilightTint, sunset * 0.72);
    return skySunGoldenTint(lightColor, elevation);



}







vec3 getTimeDrivenSkyLightColor()
{


    vec3 color = getTimeDrivenSkyLightColorRaw();











    float auroraNight = smoothstep(0.06, - 0.20, getTimeDrivenSunElevation());
    color += AURORA_LIGHT_COLOR *
        (AURORA_AMBIENT_STRENGTH * clamp(cam.auroraParams.x, 0.0, 1.0) *
         clamp(cam.auroraParams.y, 0.0, 3.0) * auroraNight);
    return color;
}














float getSurfaceAmbientBase()
{
    float base = clamp(cam.cameraParams.w, 0.0, 1.0);
    float noonShadowContrast = smoothstep(
        0.35,
        SOLAR_LATITUDE_35_COS,
        max(getTimeDrivenSunElevation(), 0.0)
    );
    float ambient = base * mix(1.0, 0.55, noonShadowContrast);











    return ambient;
}

#line 1 "./clear_sky_aerial.glsl"

















const vec3 ATM_RAYLEIGH_BETA = vec3(5.5e-6, 13.0e-6, 22.4e-6);
const vec3 ATM_MIE_BETA = vec3(18.0e-6);
const float ATM_RAYLEIGH_HEIGHT = 8000.0;
const float ATM_MIE_HEIGHT = 1200.0;
const float ATM_MIE_G = 0.76;




const float ATM_SKY_EXPOSURE = 5.22209428235;








const vec3 ATM_A1_OZONE_OD = vec3(0.0168, 0.0400, 0.0016);







const vec3 ATM_AP_BASE_EXTINCTION = vec3(0.000030, 0.000052, 0.000090);

float atmosphereAirMass(float elevation)
{
    float h = clamp(elevation, - 0.065, 1.0);
    return min(24.0, 1.0 / max(0.045, h + 0.135));
}

float atmosphereRayleighPhase(float cosTheta)
{
    return 0.05968310366 * (1.0 + cosTheta * cosTheta);
}

float atmosphereMiePhase(float cosTheta)
{
    float g2 = ATM_MIE_G * ATM_MIE_G;
    float denominator = max(1.0e-4, 1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * ATM_MIE_G * cosTheta);
    float invDenominator = inversesqrt(denominator);
    return(3.0 * (1.0 - g2) * (1.0 + cosTheta * cosTheta)) /
        (8.0 * 3.14159265359 * (2.0 + g2)) *
        invDenominator * invDenominator * invDenominator;
}






vec3 clearSkyInscatter(
    vec3 rayDir,
    float sunElevation,
    vec3 lightDirection,
    vec3 skyLightColor,
    float skyLightRadianceScale,
    float weatherSunRadianceFactor,
    vec3 weatherSkyTint)
{
    rayDir = normalize(rayDir);
    float cosTheta = clamp(dot(rayDir, lightDirection), - 1.0, 1.0);

    float viewMass = atmosphereAirMass(rayDir.y);
    float lightMass = atmosphereAirMass(abs(sunElevation));
    vec3 sunTransmittance = exp2(- 1.44269504089 * (
        ATM_RAYLEIGH_BETA * ATM_RAYLEIGH_HEIGHT +
        ATM_MIE_BETA * ATM_MIE_HEIGHT +
        ATM_A1_OZONE_OD
    ) * lightMass);
    vec3 rayleighScatter = vec3(1.0) - exp2(- 1.44269504089 * ATM_RAYLEIGH_BETA * ATM_RAYLEIGH_HEIGHT * viewMass);
    vec3 mieScatter = vec3(1.0) - exp2(- 1.44269504089 * ATM_MIE_BETA * ATM_MIE_HEIGHT * viewMass);

    vec3 sourceRadiance = skyLightColor *
        (3.2 * skyLightRadianceScale) *
        sunTransmittance * weatherSunRadianceFactor * weatherSkyTint;
    vec3 scatter = sourceRadiance * ATM_SKY_EXPOSURE * (
        rayleighScatter * atmosphereRayleighPhase(cosTheta) +
        mieScatter * atmosphereMiePhase(cosTheta)
    );

    float nightAmount = 1.0 - smoothstep(- 0.03, 0.12, sunElevation);
    float horizon = 1.0 - clamp(rayDir.y, 0.0, 1.0);
    horizon *= horizon * (3.0 - 2.0 * horizon);
    vec3 nightAmbient = mix(vec3(0.0010, 0.0018, 0.0050), vec3(0.004, 0.008, 0.020), horizon);
    return max(scatter + nightAmbient * nightAmount, vec3(0.0));
}



float clearSkyAerialHeightDensity(
    vec3 cameraOrigin,
    vec3 cameraDirection,
    float distance)
{
    float d = clamp(distance, 0.0, 10000.0);
    float midpointY = cameraOrigin.y + cameraDirection.y * (0.5 * d);
    float heightDensity = exp(- 0.025 * clamp(midpointY - 63.0, - 32.0, 256.0));
    return clamp(heightDensity, 0.10, 2.23);
}

vec3 samplePhysicalClearSkyForAerialPerspective(vec3 rayDir)
{


    return clearSkyInscatter(
        rayDir,
        getTimeDrivenSunElevation(),
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightDirection(),
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightColor(),
        getClearTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale(),
        getWeatherSkyRadianceFactor(),
        getWeatherSkyTint());
}


















const float AP_BASE_EXTINCTION_SCALE = 0.0;

















const int AP_OCCLUSION_STEPS = 6;

float clearSkyViewOcclusion(vec3 origin, vec3 direction, float distance)
{
    float d = clamp(distance, 1.0, 10000.0);
    vec3 lightDir = getTimeDrivenSkyLightDirection();
    float occluded = 0.0;
    for (int i = 0; i < AP_OCCLUSION_STEPS; ++ i)
    {
        float t = (float(i) + 0.5) / float(AP_OCCLUSION_STEPS) * d;
        vec3 samplePosition = origin + direction * t;
        occluded += 1.0 - sunShadowFarVisibility(samplePosition, lightDir, i);
    }
    return occluded / float(AP_OCCLUSION_STEPS);
}

vec3 applyClearSkyAerialPerspective(
    vec3 surfaceRadiance,
    vec3 cameraOrigin,
    vec3 cameraDirection,
    float cameraDistance
)
{


    float distance = clamp(cameraDistance, 0.0, 10000.0);
    float heightDensity = clearSkyAerialHeightDensity(
        cameraOrigin, cameraDirection, distance);

    float rainStrength = getOverworldRainStrength();
    float weatherFogDensity = 1.0 + WEATHER_RF_DENSITY *
        3.0 * rainStrength * rainStrength;
    float apBaseScale = AP_BASE_EXTINCTION_SCALE +
        clamp(cam.cloudAerialParams.y, 0.0, 2.0);
    vec3 extinction = ATM_AP_BASE_EXTINCTION *
        apBaseScale * heightDensity * weatherFogDensity;
    vec3 transmittance = exp(- extinction * distance);
    vec3 inscatter = samplePhysicalClearSkyForAerialPerspective(cameraDirection);


    float occlusionStrength = clamp(cam.cloudAerialParams.z, 0.0, 1.0);
    if (occlusionStrength > 0.001 && cam.sunShadowFarParams.w > 0.5)
    {
        inscatter *= mix(1.0,
            1.0 - clearSkyViewOcclusion(cameraOrigin, cameraDirection, distance),
            occlusionStrength);
    }
    vec3 clearWeatherResult = surfaceRadiance * transmittance +
        inscatter * (vec3(1.0) - transmittance);





    float rainOpticalDepth = distance * rainStrength * WEATHER_RF_DENSITY *
        heightDensity * 0.00022;
    float rainFogAmount = 1.0 - exp(- rainOpticalDepth);
    vec3 rainFogColor = mix(
        vec3(0.34, 0.40, 0.46),
        inscatter,
        0.25
    );

    return mix(clearWeatherResult, rainFogColor, rainFogAmount);
}

























const float UNDERWATER_DEEP_COLUMN = 60.0;


const float UNDERWATER_SCATTER_G = 0.4;


const float UNDERWATER_NIGHT_CRUSH = 16.0;













const vec3 UNDERWATER_COLUMN_EXTINCTION = vec3(1.0, 2.2, 2.0);
const float UNDERWATER_COLUMN_SCATTER_BOOST = 4.0;


float underwaterHGPhase(float cosine, float g)
{
    float g2 = g * g;
    float denominator = max(1.0e-4, 1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * cosine);
    return(1.0 - g2) / (4.0 * 3.14159265359 * denominator * sqrt(denominator));
}

vec3 underwaterAbsorption(float waterDistance)
{

    return exp(- waterDistance * waterAbsorptionColor() *
        UNDERWATER_COLUMN_EXTINCTION);
}



vec3 underwaterScatter(
    vec3 worldDirection,
    vec3 skyColor,
    float waterDistance,
    float skyLight
)
{




    vec3 phase;
    if (cam.waterParams.w > 0.5)
    {
        float cosSun = dot(worldDirection, getTimeDrivenSkyLightDirection());
        float rayleigh = (3.0 / (4.0 * 3.14159265359)) * (1.0 + cosSun * cosSun);
        float mie = underwaterHGPhase(cosSun, 0.6);
        vec3 phase3 = vec3(0.18, 0.42, 1.0) * (rayleigh * 0.05) + vec3(mie * 0.95);
        phase = mix(phase3, vec3(1.0), 0.35);
    }
    else
    {
        phase = vec3(underwaterHGPhase(worldDirection.y, UNDERWATER_SCATTER_G));
    }
    vec3 scatterColor = waterScatterColor() * waterScatteringAmount() * skyLight;
    vec3 absorbed = vec3(1.0) - underwaterAbsorption(waterDistance);
    vec3 nightCrush = exp(- vec3(UNDERWATER_NIGHT_CRUSH) *
        (1.0 - skyLight) * waterAbsorptionColor());


    return scatterColor * phase * skyColor * absorbed * nightCrush *
        UNDERWATER_COLUMN_SCATTER_BOOST;
}

vec3 applyUnderwaterWaterColumn(
    vec3 radiance,
    vec3 worldDirection,
    float hitDistance
)
{
    if (cam.waterSimState.w == 0u)
    {
        return radiance;
    }
    float waterDistance = hitDistance > 0.0
        ? min(hitDistance, 512.0)
        : UNDERWATER_DEEP_COLUMN;

    float skyLight = clamp(
        getClearTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale() / TIME_DRIVEN_SUN_NOON_SCALE,
        0.0, 1.0);
    vec3 skyColor = samplePhysicalClearSkyForAerialPerspective(vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0));
    return radiance * underwaterAbsorption(waterDistance) +
        underwaterScatter(worldDirection, skyColor, waterDistance, skyLight);
}


VPTDirectHistory vptReadPrimaryHistory(uint index)
{
    return cam.historyReadIndex == 0u
        ? vptDirectHistoryBuffer0.entries[index]
        : vptDirectHistoryBuffer1.entries[index];
}


void vptWritePrimaryHistory(uint index, VPTDirectHistory value)
{
    if (cam.historyReadIndex == 0u)
    {
        vptDirectHistoryBuffer1.entries[index] = value;
    }
    else
    {
        vptDirectHistoryBuffer0.entries[index] = value;
    }
}


bool vptReprojectPreviousPixel(
    vec3 worldPosition,
    ivec2 renderSize,
    out uint previousIndex
)
{
    previousIndex = 0u;
    if (cam.historyValid == 0u)
    {
        return false;
    }

    mat4 previousWorldToView = inverse(cam.prevViewInverse);
    mat4 previousViewToClip = inverse(cam.prevProjInverse);
    vec4 previousClip = previousViewToClip *
        previousWorldToView * vec4(worldPosition, 1.0);
    if (previousClip.w <= 1.0e-5)
    {
        return false;
    }

    vec2 ndc = previousClip.xy / previousClip.w;
    vec2 uv = vec2(ndc.x * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.5 - ndc.y * 0.5);
    if (any(lessThan(uv, vec2(0.0))) || any(greaterThanEqual(uv, vec2(1.0))))
    {
        return false;
    }

    ivec2 previousPixel = ivec2(floor(uv * vec2(renderSize)));
    previousPixel = clamp(previousPixel, ivec2(0), renderSize - ivec2(1));
    previousIndex = uint(previousPixel.y * renderSize.x + previousPixel.x);
    return true;
}

























vec2 getDLSSFrameJitter(uint frameIndex);

float vptJitterCompensatedPositionEpsilon(vec3 hitPosition)
{
    vec2 jitterCurrent =
        getDLSSFrameJitter(cam.frameCount);




    vec2 jitterPrevious =
        getDLSSFrameJitter(cam.frameCount + 31u);

    float jitterPixels = max(
        abs(jitterCurrent.x - jitterPrevious.x),
        abs(jitterCurrent.y - jitterPrevious.y)
    );


    float focalPixels =
        float(gl_LaunchSizeEXT.y) /
        (2.0 * max(tan(cam.cameraParams.z * 0.5), 0.05));


    vec3 cameraForward =
        normalize(- cam.viewInverse[2].xyz);

    float hitViewDepth =
        max(dot(hitPosition - cam.viewInverse[3].xyz, cameraForward), 0.0);

    return
        VPT_SCREEN_POSITION_EPSILON +
        (jitterPixels + 0.75) *
        hitViewDepth /
        max(focalPixels, 1.0);
}

bool vptPrimaryHistoryMatches(
    VPTDirectHistory history,
    vec3 hitPosition,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    float positionEpsilon
)
{
    return history.hitPositionAndValid.w > 0.5 &&
        history.radianceAndSampleCount.w > 0.0 &&
        length(history.hitPositionAndValid.xyz - hitPosition) <= positionEpsilon &&
        dot(normalize(history.normalAndPadding.xyz), hitNormal) >= VPT_SCREEN_NORMAL_COSINE;
}


vec3 evaluateVPTPrimaryTemporal(
    vec3 hitPosition,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    vec3 direction,
    inout uint rngState,
    ivec2 pixelCoord,
    ivec2 renderSize
)
{
    uint currentIndex = uint(pixelCoord.y * renderSize.x + pixelCoord.x);
    uint previousIndex = 0u;
    VPTDirectHistory previousHistory;
    bool reuseHistory = vptReprojectPreviousPixel(
        hitPosition,
        renderSize,
        previousIndex
    );
    if (reuseHistory)
    {
        previousHistory = vptReadPrimaryHistory(previousIndex);
        reuseHistory = vptPrimaryHistoryMatches(
            previousHistory,
            hitPosition,
            hitNormal,
            vptJitterCompensatedPositionEpsilon(hitPosition)
        );
    }

    uint phase = uint(pixelCoord.x & 1) | (uint(pixelCoord.y & 1) << 1u);
    bool traceCandidate = ! reuseHistory || phase == (cam.frameCount & 3u);

    vec3 filteredRadiance = reuseHistory
        ? previousHistory.radianceAndSampleCount.rgb
        : vec3(0.0);
    float sampleCount = reuseHistory
        ? previousHistory.radianceAndSampleCount.w
        : 0.0;

    if (traceCandidate)
    {
        vec3 candidateRadiance = evaluateVPTPrimaryIndirect(
            hitPosition,
            hitNormal,
            hitAlbedo,
            direction,
            rngState
        );
        float nextCount = min(sampleCount + 1.0, VPT_SCREEN_MAX_HISTORY);
        float alpha = reuseHistory ? (1.0 / nextCount) : 1.0;
        filteredRadiance = mix(filteredRadiance, candidateRadiance, alpha);
        sampleCount = nextCount;
    }

    VPTDirectHistory outputHistory;
    outputHistory.radianceAndSampleCount = vec4(filteredRadiance, sampleCount);
    outputHistory.hitPositionAndValid = vec4(hitPosition, 1.0);
    outputHistory.normalAndPadding = vec4(hitNormal, 0.0);
    vptWritePrimaryHistory(currentIndex, outputHistory);
    return filteredRadiance;
}

vec3 evaluateVPTCandidateDirectTemporal(

    vec3 historyKeyPosition,

    vec3 hitPosition,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    vec3 receiverGeometricNormal,
    ivec2 pixelCoord,
    ivec2 renderSize
)
{


    vec3 matchPosition = historyKeyPosition;















    vec3 matchNormal =
        dot(receiverGeometricNormal, receiverGeometricNormal) > 0.25
            ? normalize(receiverGeometricNormal)
            : normalize(hitNormal);










    if (dot(vptTransparentSurfaceNormal, vptTransparentSurfaceNormal) > 0.25)
    {
        matchNormal = normalize(vptTransparentSurfaceNormal);
    }


    uint currentIndex = uint(pixelCoord.y * renderSize.x + pixelCoord.x);
    uint previousIndex = 0u;





    bool reprojectOk = vptReprojectPreviousPixel(
        matchPosition, renderSize, previousIndex
    );
    bool positionOk = false;
    bool normalOk = false;
    VPTDirectHistory previousHistory;
    bool reuseHistory = reprojectOk;
    if (reuseHistory)
    {
        previousHistory = vptReadPrimaryHistory(previousIndex);



        vec3 identityPosition =

            hitPosition;



        positionOk =
            previousHistory.hitPositionAndValid.w > 0.5 &&
            previousHistory.radianceAndSampleCount.w > 0.0 &&
            length(previousHistory.hitPositionAndValid.xyz - identityPosition) <=
                vptJitterCompensatedPositionEpsilon(identityPosition);
        normalOk =
            dot(normalize(previousHistory.normalAndPadding.xyz), matchNormal) >=
            VPT_SCREEN_NORMAL_COSINE;
        reuseHistory = positionOk && normalOk;
    }

    uint temporalSeed = uint(pixelCoord.x) * 1973u +
        uint(pixelCoord.y) * 9277u ^ cam.frameCount * 26699u;




    vec3 currentRadiance = evaluateVPTRISDirectLighting(
        hitPosition, hitNormal, hitAlbedo, receiverGeometricNormal,
        vec3(- 1.0), - 1.0, 1.0,
        temporalSeed
    );




    float carriedCount = 0.0;
    if (reprojectOk && positionOk)
    {
        carriedCount = previousHistory.radianceAndSampleCount.w *
            (normalOk ? 1.0 : 0.5);
    }
    float oldCount = carriedCount;




































    float nextCount = min(oldCount + 1.0, 48.0);
    float alpha = 1.0 / nextCount;

    vec3 filteredRadiance = (oldCount > 0.0)
        ? mix(previousHistory.radianceAndSampleCount.rgb, currentRadiance, alpha)
        : currentRadiance;

    VPTDirectHistory outputHistory;
    outputHistory.radianceAndSampleCount = vec4(filteredRadiance, nextCount);






























    outputHistory.hitPositionAndValid = vec4(hitPosition, 1.0);


    outputHistory.normalAndPadding = vec4(matchNormal, 0.0);
    vptWritePrimaryHistory(currentIndex, outputHistory);
    return filteredRadiance;
}







bool vptCacheProbeVisible(vec3 origin, vec3 target)
{
    vec3 segment = target - origin;
    float maxDistance = length(segment);
    if (maxDistance <= 1.0e-4) return true;
    vec3 direction = segment / maxDistance;
    ivec3 cell = ivec3(floor(origin));
    ivec3 step = ivec3(sign(direction));
    vec3 invAbs = 1.0 / max(abs(direction), vec3(1.0e-6));
    vec3 boundary = vec3(cell) + vec3(max(step, ivec3(0)));
    vec3 tMax = (boundary - origin) / direction;
    if (step.x == 0) tMax.x = 1.0e30;
    if (step.y == 0) tMax.y = 1.0e30;
    if (step.z == 0) tMax.z = 1.0e30;

    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++ i)
    {
        int axis = tMax.x < tMax.y ? (tMax.x < tMax.z ? 0 : 2)
                                    : (tMax.y < tMax.z ? 1 : 2);
        float t = axis == 0 ? tMax.x : (axis == 1 ? tMax.y : tMax.z);
        if (t >= maxDistance) return true;













        if (axis == 0) { cell.x += step.x; tMax.x += invAbs.x; }
        else if (axis == 1) { cell.y += step.y; tMax.y += invAbs.y; }
        else { cell.z += step.z; tMax.z += invAbs.z; }
        uint source;
        if (! vptReadSourceVoxel(cell, source)) return false;
        if ( (source & 3u) == 1u) return false;
    }
    return false;
}

vec3 vptDirectionalCacheAxis(uint directionIndex)
{
    if (directionIndex == 0u) return vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    if (directionIndex == 1u) return vec3(- 1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    if (directionIndex == 2u) return vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);
    if (directionIndex == 3u) return vec3(0.0, - 1.0, 0.0);
    if (directionIndex == 4u) return vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    return vec3(0.0, 0.0, - 1.0);
}









void sampleVPTDualDirectionalIrradiance(
    vec3 worldPos,
    vec3 surfaceNormal,
    out vec3 outPrimary,
    out vec3 outSecondary
)
{
    outPrimary = vec3(0.0);
    outSecondary = vec3(0.0);

    ivec3 sectionCoord = ivec3(floor(worldPos / 16.0));










    ivec3 probeSection = sectionCoord;
    uint pageIndex;
    if (! vliLookupSectionPage(probeSection, pageIndex))
    {
        bool borrowedProbePage = false;
        for (uint neighbourIndex = 0u; neighbourIndex < 6u; ++ neighbourIndex)
        {
            ivec3 neighbour = sectionCoord + (
                neighbourIndex == 0u ? ivec3(1, 0, 0) :
                neighbourIndex == 1u ? ivec3(- 1, 0, 0) :
                neighbourIndex == 2u ? ivec3(0, 1, 0) :
                neighbourIndex == 3u ? ivec3(0, - 1, 0) :
                neighbourIndex == 4u ? ivec3(0, 0, 1) :
                                       ivec3(0, 0, - 1)
            );
            if (vliLookupSectionPage(neighbour, pageIndex))
            {
                probeSection = neighbour;
                borrowedProbePage = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (! borrowedProbePage)
        {
            return;
        }
    }



    vec3 localProbe = clamp(
        (worldPos - vec3(probeSection * 16)) * 0.5,
        vec3(0.0),
        vec3(7.999)
    );
    ivec3 base = ivec3(floor(localProbe));
    vec3 fraction = fract(localProbe);
    uint primaryPageBase = pageIndex * VLI_PAGE_WORDS + VLI_SOURCE_WORDS;
    uint secondaryPageBase = pageIndex * VPT_DIRECTIONAL_CACHE_WORDS;
    vec3 N = normalize(surfaceNormal);

    vec3 primaryAccum = vec3(0.0);
    vec3 secondaryAccum = vec3(0.0);
    float acceptedWeight = 0.0;

    for (int y = 0; y <= 1; ++ y)
    for (int z = 0; z <= 1; ++ z)
    for (int x = 0; x <= 1; ++ x)
    {
        ivec3 probe = base + ivec3(x, y, z);
        uint index = uint(probe.x) + VLI_PROBE_EDGE * (
            uint(probe.z) + VLI_PROBE_EDGE * uint(probe.y)
        );
        vec3 weight3 = mix(
            vec3(1.0) - fraction,
            fraction,
            vec3(float(x), float(y), float(z))
        );
        float weight = weight3.x * weight3.y * weight3.z;
        vec3 probeWorldPos = vec3(sectionCoord * 16 + probe * 2) + vec3(1.0);

        vec3 primaryDirIrr = vec3(0.0);
        vec3 secondaryDirIrr = vec3(0.0);

        for (uint directionIndex = 0u;
            directionIndex < VPT_DIRECTION_COUNT;
            ++ directionIndex)
        {
            float normalWeight = max(
                dot(N, vptDirectionalCacheAxis(directionIndex)),
                0.0
            );
            normalWeight *= normalWeight;


            uint packedPrimary = vliPagesBuffer.words[
                primaryPageBase + directionIndex * VLI_PROBE_WORDS + index
            ];
            primaryDirIrr += vliUnpackRadiance(packedPrimary) * normalWeight;


            uint packedSecondary = vliSecondaryPagesBuffer.words[
                secondaryPageBase + directionIndex * VLI_PROBE_WORDS + index
            ];
            secondaryDirIrr += vliUnpackRadiance(packedSecondary) * normalWeight;
        }













        {
            float visibility =
                vptCacheProbeVisible(worldPos + surfaceNormal * 0.03, probeWorldPos)
                    ? 1.0
                    : 0.0;
            primaryAccum += primaryDirIrr * weight * visibility;
            secondaryAccum += secondaryDirIrr * weight * visibility;
            acceptedWeight += weight;
        }








    }

    if (acceptedWeight > 1.0e-5)
    {
        outPrimary = primaryAccum / acceptedWeight;
        outSecondary = secondaryAccum / acceptedWeight;
    }
}


void evaluateVPTDualLighting(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    out vec3 localDirect,
    out vec3 localSecondary
)
{
    vec3 primaryIrr = vec3(0.0);
    vec3 secondaryIrr = vec3(0.0);
    sampleVPTDualDirectionalIrradiance(hitPos, hitNormal, primaryIrr, secondaryIrr);

    localDirect = primaryIrr * hitAlbedo * 0.92;
    localSecondary = secondaryIrr * hitAlbedo * 0.92;
}


const float LOCAL_LIGHT_GRID_CELL_SIZE =
    64.0;


const float LOCAL_LIGHT_MAX_RANGE =
    30.0;


uint localLightGridHash32(
    ivec3 cellCoord
)
{
    uint h =
        uint(cellCoord.x) *
        0x8da6b343u;

    h ^=
        uint(cellCoord.y) *
        0xd8163841u;

    h ^=
        uint(cellCoord.z) *
        0xcb1ab31fu;

    h ^= h >> 16u;
    h *= 0x7feb352du;
    h ^= h >> 15u;
    h *= 0x846ca68bu;
    h ^= h >> 16u;

    return
        h;
}


ivec3 localLightGridCellCoord(
    vec3 worldPos
)
{
    return
        ivec3(
            floor(
                worldPos /
                LOCAL_LIGHT_GRID_CELL_SIZE
            )
        );
}


int localLightGridNeighborMinOffset(
    float worldComponent
)
{
    float cellBase =
        floor(
            worldComponent /
            LOCAL_LIGHT_GRID_CELL_SIZE
        ) *
        LOCAL_LIGHT_GRID_CELL_SIZE;


    return
        worldComponent -
        cellBase <
        LOCAL_LIGHT_MAX_RANGE
            ? - 1
            : 0;
}


int localLightGridNeighborMaxOffset(
    float worldComponent
)
{
    float cellBase =
        floor(
            worldComponent /
            LOCAL_LIGHT_GRID_CELL_SIZE
        ) *
        LOCAL_LIGHT_GRID_CELL_SIZE;


    return
        cellBase +
        LOCAL_LIGHT_GRID_CELL_SIZE -
        worldComponent <
        LOCAL_LIGHT_MAX_RANGE
            ? 1
            : 0;
}


uint localLightGridPackedIndexBase()
{
    return
        4u +
        localLightGridBuffer.words[2] *
        8u;
}


bool localLightGridLookupCell(
    ivec3 cellCoord,
    out uint firstLightIndex,
    out uint lightCount
)
{
    uint hashCapacity =
        localLightGridBuffer.words[2];


    if (hashCapacity == 0u)
    {
        firstLightIndex =
            0u;

        lightCount =
            0u;

        return
            false;
    }


    uint hashMask =
        hashCapacity -
        1u;


    uint slotIndex =
        localLightGridHash32(
            cellCoord
        ) &
        hashMask;


    for (
        uint probe = 0u;
        probe < hashCapacity;
        ++ probe
    )
    {
        uint slotBase =
            4u +
            slotIndex *
            8u;


        if (
            localLightGridBuffer.words[
                slotBase +
                3u
            ] == 0u
        )
        {
            firstLightIndex =
                0u;

            lightCount =
                0u;

            return
                false;
        }


        if (
            int(
                localLightGridBuffer.words[
                    slotBase
                ]
            ) == cellCoord.x &&
            int(
                localLightGridBuffer.words[
                    slotBase +
                    1u
                ]
            ) == cellCoord.y &&
            int(
                localLightGridBuffer.words[
                    slotBase +
                    2u
                ]
            ) == cellCoord.z
        )
        {
            firstLightIndex =
                localLightGridBuffer.words[
                    slotBase +
                    4u
                ];

            lightCount =
                localLightGridBuffer.words[
                    slotBase +
                    5u
                ];

            return
                true;
        }


        slotIndex =
            (
                slotIndex +
                1u
            ) &
            hashMask;
    }


    firstLightIndex =
        0u;

    lightCount =
        0u;

    return
        false;
}

const uint RT_STATE_PURPOSE_MASK = 0x7u;
const uint RT_STATE_HIT_CLASS_SHIFT = 3u;
const uint RT_STATE_WATER_EVENT_SHIFT = 5u;
const uint RT_STATE_GLASS_BIT = 1u << 7u;
const uint RT_STATE_GLASS_BOTTOM_BIT = 1u << 8u;
const uint RT_STATE_PROFILE_SHIFT = 9u;




const uint RT_STATE_CLEAR_GLASS_BIT = 1u << 12u;

const uint RT_PURPOSE_FULL = 0u;
const uint RT_PURPOSE_CLASSIFY = 1u;
const uint RT_PURPOSE_DISTANCE_ONLY = 2u;
const uint RT_PURPOSE_VISIBILITY_ONLY = 3u;
const uint RT_PURPOSE_PASSTHROUGH = 4u;
const uint RT_PURPOSE_GLASS_VISIBILITY = 5u;
const uint RT_PURPOSE_WATER_MEDIUM = 6u;


const uint RT_PURPOSE_PRIMARY_SKY = 7u;

const uint RT_HIT_OPAQUE = 0u;
const uint RT_HIT_WATER = 1u;
const uint RT_HIT_TRANSLUCENT = 2u;
const uint RT_HIT_MISS = 3u;
const uint RT_WATER_NONE = 0u;
const uint RT_WATER_AIR_TO_WATER = 1u;
const uint RT_WATER_WATER_TO_AIR = 2u;

uint makeTraceState(uint purpose)
{
    return purpose & RT_STATE_PURPOSE_MASK;
}









vec3 traceVptLocalLightVisibility(
    vec3 origin,
    vec3 target,
    float targetVoxelEntryDistance,
    uint cullMask
)
{







    if (perfL10DisableLocalEmitterHitScan())
    {
        return vec3(1.0);
    }

    vec3 segment = target - origin;
    float distanceToTarget = length(segment);
    if (distanceToTarget <= 0.03)
    {
        return vec3(1.0);
    }






    float rayMaxDistance = min(
        distanceToTarget - 0.03,
        targetVoxelEntryDistance - 0.03
    );
    if (rayMaxDistance <= 0.02)
    {
        return vec3(1.0);
    }

    hit.hitDistance = 1.0;
    hit.albedo = vec3(1.0);
    hit.parallaxRayTravel = 0.0;
    hit.glassAlpha = 0.0;
    hit.state = makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_GLASS_VISIBILITY);

    traceRayEXT(
        topLevelAS,
        gl_RayFlagsTerminateOnFirstHitEXT |
            gl_RayFlagsSkipClosestHitShaderEXT,
        cullMask,
        0,
        0,
        0,
        origin,
        0.02,
        segment / distanceToTarget,
        rayMaxDistance,
        0
    );

    if (hit.hitDistance >= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    return clamp(hit.albedo, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));
}

uint getRayPurpose(uint state)
{
    return state & RT_STATE_PURPOSE_MASK;
}

uint getHitClass(uint state)
{
    return(state >> RT_STATE_HIT_CLASS_SHIFT) & 0x3u;
}

uint getWaterEvent(uint state)
{
    return(state >> RT_STATE_WATER_EVENT_SHIFT) & 0x3u;
}

uint getTransparentProfile(uint state)
{
    return(state >> RT_STATE_PROFILE_SHIFT) & 0x7u;
}

    return(state & RT_STATE_GLASS_BIT) != 0u;
}

{
    return(state & RT_STATE_GLASS_BOTTOM_BIT) != 0u;

    return(state & RT_STATE_CLEAR_GLASS_BIT) != 0u;



}











































const uint RT_NESTED_PRIORITY_AIR = 15u;
const uint RT_NESTED_PRIORITY_WATER = 14u;


uint nestedPriorityOfHit(uint state)
{
    if (getHitClass(state) == RT_HIT_WATER)
    {
        return RT_NESTED_PRIORITY_WATER;
    }



    return 1u + getTransparentProfile(state);
}







bool nestedInterfaceAccepted(uint interfacePriority, inout uint currentPriority)
{
    if (interfacePriority == currentPriority)
    {
        currentPriority = RT_NESTED_PRIORITY_AIR;
        return true;
    }

    if (currentPriority == RT_NESTED_PRIORITY_AIR)
    {
        currentPriority = interfacePriority;
        return true;
    }

    if (interfacePriority > currentPriority)
    {
        return false;
    }

    currentPriority = interfacePriority;
    return true;
}



bool nestedInsideSolidMedium(uint currentPriority)
{
    return
        currentPriority != RT_NESTED_PRIORITY_AIR &&
        currentPriority != RT_NESTED_PRIORITY_WATER;
}



float getLegacyMaterialId(uint state)
{
    uint hitClass = getHitClass(state);
    if (hitClass == RT_HIT_WATER)
    {
        return getWaterEvent(state) == RT_WATER_WATER_TO_AIR ? 2.0 : 1.0;
    }
    if (hitClass == RT_HIT_TRANSLUCENT)
    {
        return isGlassBottomHit(state) ? 4.0 : 3.0;
    }
    return 0.0;
}

uint seed;

uint pcg_hash(inout uint state) {
    uint word = ( (state >> ( (state >> 28u) + 4u)) ^ state) * 277803737u;
    state = (state * 747796405u) + 2891336453u;
    return(word >> 22u) ^ word;
}

float rand(inout uint state) {
    return float(pcg_hash(state)) / 4294967296.0;
}








float haltonValue(
    uint index,
    uint base
) {
    float f = 1.0;
    float result = 0.0;

    uint i = index;

    while (i > 0u) {
        f /= float(base);

        result +=
            f * float(i % base);

        i /= base;
    }

    return result;
}


vec2 getDLSSFrameJitter(
    uint frameIndex
) {
    const uint PHASE_COUNT = 32u;

    uint sequenceIndex =
        (frameIndex % PHASE_COUNT) + 1u;

    return vec2(
        haltonValue(sequenceIndex, 2u),
        haltonValue(sequenceIndex, 3u)
    ) - vec2(0.5);
}

vec3 sampleHemisphereCosine(vec3 normal, inout uint state) {
    float r1 = rand(state);
    float r2 = rand(state);
    float phi = 2.0 * 3.14159265 * r1;
    float r = sqrt(r2);
    float x = r * cos(phi);
    float y = r * sin(phi);
    float z = sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - x * x - y * y));

    vec3 up = abs(normal.z) < 0.999 ? vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0) : vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    vec3 tangent = normalize(cross(up, normal));
    vec3 bitangent = cross(normal, tangent);

    return tangent * x + bitangent * y + normal * z;
}

vec3 sampleGGX(vec3 normal, float roughness, inout uint state) {
    float alpha = max(roughness * roughness, 0.001);
    float alpha2 = alpha * alpha;
    float r1 = rand(state);
    float r2 = rand(state);
    float phi = 2.0 * 3.14159265 * r1;
    float cosTheta = sqrt( (1.0 - r2) / (1.0 + (alpha2 - 1.0) * r2));
    float sinTheta = sqrt(1.0 - cosTheta * cosTheta);

    vec3 H = vec3(cos(phi) * sinTheta, sin(phi) * sinTheta, cosTheta);
    vec3 up = abs(normal.z) < 0.999 ? vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0) : vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
    vec3 tangent = normalize(cross(up, normal));
    vec3 bitangent = cross(normal, tangent);

    return tangent * H.x + bitangent * H.y + normal * H.z;
}


vec3 sampleSunDirection(vec3 sunDir, inout uint state) {
    float r1 = rand(state);
    float r2 = rand(state);
    float angle = 6.2831853 * r1;


    float radius = sqrt(r2) * 0.0047;

    vec3 up = abs(sunDir.y) < 0.999 ? vec3(0, 1, 0) : vec3(1, 0, 0);
    vec3 tangent = normalize(cross(up, sunDir));
    vec3 bitangent = cross(sunDir, tangent);

    return normalize(sunDir + tangent * cos(angle) * radius + bitangent * sin(angle) * radius);
}

{
    return int(
        round(
            clamp(
                fract(materialId) * 10.0,
                0.0,
                4.0
            )
        )
    );
}

























float getTransparentProfileTransmission(int transparentProfile)
{
    if (transparentProfile == 1)
    {
        return 0.06;
    }
    if (transparentProfile == 2)
    {
        return 0.55;
    }
    if (transparentProfile == 3)
    {

    if (transparentProfile == 4)

}












































vec3 fresnelSchlick(
    float cosTheta,
    vec3 F0
);


























float fresnelSchlickScalar(
    float cosTheta,
    float F0
)
{
    float x = 1.0 - clamp(cosTheta, 0.0, 1.0);
    float x2 = x * x;
    return F0 + (1.0 - F0) * (x2 * x2) * x;
}




void glassThinSheetFactorsScalarF(
    float fresnel,
    vec3 transmittance,
    out vec3 reflection,
    out vec3 transmission
)
{
    float f = clamp(fresnel, 0.0, 1.0);
    vec3 a = clamp(transmittance, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));
    float oneMinusF = 1.0 - f;
    vec3 denominator = max(
        vec3(1.0) - (f * f) * (a * a),
        vec3(1.0e-6)
    );
    reflection =
        vec3(f) + ( (oneMinusF * oneMinusF) * f) * (a * a) / denominator;
    transmission =
        (oneMinusF * oneMinusF) * a / denominator;
}

vec3 glassSheetTransmissionScalarF(
    vec3 transmissionColor,
    float cosTheta
)
{
    float fresnel = fresnelSchlickScalar(cosTheta, 0.04);
    float oneMinusF = 1.0 - fresnel;
    vec3 a = clamp(transmissionColor, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));
    vec3 denominator = max(
        vec3(1.0) - (fresnel * fresnel) * (a * a),
        vec3(1.0e-6)
    );
    return(oneMinusF * oneMinusF) * a / denominator;
}

vec3 glassSheetTransmission(
    vec3 transmissionColor,
    vec3 faceNormal,
    vec3 directionToLight
)
{



    float cosine = clamp(
        abs(dot(faceNormal, directionToLight)),
        0.0,
        1.0
    );
    return glassSheetTransmissionScalarF(transmissionColor, cosine);
}


























vec3 computeGlassBlockTransmission(
    vec3 glassTint,
    float glassAlpha,
    int transparentProfile
)
{



    vec3 dye =
        clamp(glassTint, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));


    dye =
        pow(dye, vec3(0.5));


    return dye;
}




























vec3 glassRunDyeAfterFirstCell(vec3 entryPoint, vec3 insideDirection)
{
    vec3 direction = normalize(insideDirection);
    vec3 origin = entryPoint + direction * 1.0e-3;
    ivec3 cell = ivec3(floor(origin));
    ivec3 stepDir = ivec3(sign(direction));
    vec3 invAbs = 1.0 / max(abs(direction), vec3(1.0e-6));
    vec3 boundary = vec3(cell) + vec3(max(stepDir, ivec3(0)));
    vec3 tMax = (boundary - origin) / direction;
    if (stepDir.x == 0) { tMax.x = 1.0e30; }
    if (stepDir.y == 0) { tMax.y = 1.0e30; }
    if (stepDir.z == 0) { tMax.z = 1.0e30; }

    vec3 dyeProduct = vec3(1.0);
    float travelled = 0.0;
    for (int i = 0; i <= 8; ++ i)
    {
        float next = min(tMax.x, min(tMax.y, tMax.z));
        if (i > 0)
        {
            uint source;
            if (! vptReadSourceVoxel(cell, source) ||
               (source & VPT_STAINED_GLASS_BIT) == 0u)
            {
                break;
            }
            vec3 cellTransmission =
                computeGlassBlockTransmission(
                    glassDecodeVoxelAlbedo(source),
                    1.0,
                    0
                );
            dyeProduct *= pow(
                max(
                    cellTransmission,
                    vec3(1.0e-4)
                ),
                vec3(max(next - travelled, 0.0))
            );
        }
        travelled = next;

        if (next == tMax.x)
        {
            cell.x += stepDir.x;
            tMax.x += invAbs.x;
        }
        else if (next == tMax.y)
        {
            cell.y += stepDir.y;
            tMax.y += invAbs.y;
        }
        else
        {
            cell.z += stepDir.z;
            tMax.z += invAbs.z;
        }
    }
    return dyeProduct;
}












vec3 applyLocalGlassTint(vec3 radiance, vec3 transmittance)
{


    if (DBG_LOCAL_TERM_MAP)
    {
        return vec3(dbgTermLuma(radiance), 0.0, 0.0);
    }
    if (DBG_GLASS_DYE_VS_ENERGY)
    {


        return vec3(
            dbgDyeConcentration(strengthenLocalGlassTint(
                clamp(transmittance, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0))
            )),
            dbgTermLuma(radiance),
            0.0
        );
    }



    if (DBG_VPT_DDA_PROBE)
    {
        return vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);
    }

    if (DBG_GLASS_PEAK_READOUT)
    {

        return dbgHuePurity2(glassStainedPoolRadiance(radiance, transmittance));
    }

    return glassApplyStainedTint(radiance, transmittance);
}

vec3 traceSunTransmittanceThroughWater(
    vec3 startOrigin,
    vec3 rayDir,
    bool stableTransmission
) {

    const float WATER_IOR =
        1.33;


    const vec3 WATER_ABSORPTION =
        waterAbsorptionColor();
        const vec3 WATER_SCATTER_COLOR = waterScatterColor();

const float WATER_EXTINCTION_DISTANCE =
    16.0;

    const float WATER_F0 =
        0.0200593;

    const float RAY_BIAS =
        0.001;

    vec3 transmittance =
        vec3(1.0);

    vec3 currentOrigin =
        startOrigin;

    vec3 currentDir =
        normalize(rayDir);

    for (
        int step = 0;
        step < perfL10MarchSteps(startOrigin);
        ++ step
    )
    {
        hit.hitDistance =
            1.0;

        hit.state = makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_CLASSIFY);




















        {
            traceRayEXT(
                topLevelAS,
                gl_RayFlagsNoneEXT,
                0xff,
                0,
                0,
                0,
                currentOrigin,
                0.02,
                currentDir,
                10000.0,
                0
            );

            resolveDeferredHit(currentOrigin, currentDir);

        }


        if (
            payload.hitDistance < 0.0
        )
        {
            return transmittance;
        }

        float hitMaterialId =
            getLegacyMaterialId(payload.state);














if (hitMaterialId >= 2.5)
{





    if (hitMaterialId < 3.5)
    {
        transmittance *=
            glassSheetTransmission(
                payload.albedo,
                payload.normal,
                currentDir
            );



        if (max(transmittance.r, max(transmittance.g, transmittance.b)) < 0.01)
        {
            return vec3(0.0);
        }
    }


    vec3 glassHitPos =
        currentOrigin +
        currentDir *
        payload.hitDistance;


    currentOrigin =
        glassHitPos +
        currentDir *
        RAY_BIAS;


    continue;
}

        if (
            hitMaterialId <= 0.5
        )
        {

            return vec3(0.0);
        }









        bool exitingWater =
            hitMaterialId > 1.5;




        if (exitingWater)
        {
float waterDistance =
    stableTransmission
        ? min(payload.hitDistance, 2.0)
        : payload.hitDistance;

            vec3 extinction =
                exp(
                    - WATER_ABSORPTION *
                    waterDistance
                );

            transmittance *= extinction;

            float scatterDepth =
                1.0 -
                exp(
                    - waterDistance * 0.05
                );

            vec3 inScatter =
                WATER_SCATTER_COLOR *
                scatterDepth;

            transmittance +=
                inScatter;
        }

        vec3 hitPos =
            currentOrigin +
            currentDir *
            payload.hitDistance;


        vec3 hitNormal =
            normalize(
                payload.normal
            );

        hitNormal =
            stabilizeWaterInterfaceNormal(
                hitNormal
            );











        vec3 interfaceNormal =
            exitingWater
                ? vec3(
                    0.0,
                    hitNormal.y >= 0.0 ? 1.0 : - 1.0,
                    0.0
                )
                : hitNormal;

        float cosTheta =
            clamp(
                - dot(
                    currentDir,
                    interfaceNormal
                ),
                0.0,
                1.0
            );



        float fresnel =
            WATER_F0 +
            (
                1.0 -
                WATER_F0
            ) *
            pow(
                1.0 -
                cosTheta,
                5.0
            );

        transmittance *=
            1.0 -
            fresnel;




        if (max(transmittance.r, max(transmittance.g, transmittance.b)) < 0.01)
        {
            return vec3(0.0);
        }

float eta =
    1.0 / WATER_IOR;

        vec3 transmittedDir =
            refract(
                currentDir,
                interfaceNormal,
                eta
            );




        if (
            dot(
                transmittedDir,
                transmittedDir
            ) < 1.0e-6
        )
        {
            return vec3(0.0);
        }



        currentOrigin =
            hitPos -
            interfaceNormal *
            RAY_BIAS;

        currentDir =
            normalize(
                transmittedDir
            );
    }

    return vec3(0.0);
}
const float PI =
    3.14159265359;


int decodeF0MetalCode(
    float f0Metal
)
{
    return int(
        round(
            clamp(
                f0Metal,
                0.0,
                1.0
            ) *
            255.0
        )
    );
}


float decodeMetallicFromCode(
    int code
)
{
    return code >= 230
        ? 1.0
        : 0.0;
}


vec3 complexIorToF0(
    vec3 n,
    vec3 k
)
{
    vec3 nMinus1 =
        n - vec3(1.0);

    vec3 nPlus1 =
        n + vec3(1.0);

    return
        (
            nMinus1 * nMinus1 +
            k * k
        ) /
        (
            nPlus1 * nPlus1 +
            k * k
        );
}


vec3 decodeBaseF0(
    int code,
    vec3 albedo
)
{
    if (code <= 229)
    {
        return vec3(
            float(code) / 255.0
        );
    }

    if (code == 230)
    {

        return complexIorToF0(
            vec3(
                2.9114,
                2.9497,
                2.5845
            ),
            vec3(
                3.0893,
                2.9318,
                2.7670
            )
        );
    }

    if (code == 231)
    {

        return complexIorToF0(
            vec3(
                0.18299,
                0.42108,
                1.3734
            ),
            vec3(
                3.4242,
                2.3459,
                1.7704
            )
        );
    }

    if (code == 232)
    {

        return complexIorToF0(
            vec3(
                1.3456,
                0.96521,
                0.61722
            ),
            vec3(
                7.4746,
                6.3995,
                5.3031
            )
        );
    }

    if (code == 233)
    {

        return complexIorToF0(
            vec3(
                3.1071,
                3.1812,
                2.3230
            ),
            vec3(
                3.3314,
                3.3291,
                3.1350
            )
        );
    }

    if (code == 234)
    {

        return complexIorToF0(
            vec3(
                0.27105,
                0.67693,
                1.3164
            ),
            vec3(
                3.6092,
                2.6248,
                2.2921
            )
        );
    }

    if (code == 235)
    {

        return complexIorToF0(
            vec3(
                1.9100,
                1.8300,
                1.4400
            ),
            vec3(
                3.5100,
                3.4000,
                3.1800
            )
        );
    }

    if (code == 236)
    {

        return complexIorToF0(
            vec3(
                2.3757,
                2.0847,
                1.8453
            ),
            vec3(
                4.2655,
                3.7153,
                3.1365
            )
        );
    }

    if (code == 237)
    {

        return complexIorToF0(
            vec3(
                0.15943,
                0.14512,
                0.13547
            ),
            vec3(
                3.9291,
                3.1900,
                2.3808
            )
        );
    }



    return albedo;
}






vec3 rrEnvBRDFApprox2(
    vec3 specularColor,
    float alpha,
    float NoV
)
{
    NoV = abs(NoV);

    float Xx = 1.0;
    float Xy = NoV;
    float Xz = NoV * NoV;
    float Xw = NoV * Xz;

    float Yx = 1.0;
    float Yy = alpha;
    float Yw = alpha * alpha * alpha;

    vec2 m1x =
        vec2(
            0.99044 * Xx - 1.28514 * Xy,
            1.29678 * Xx - 0.755907 * Xy
        );

    float biasDen =
        dot(
            vec3(
                1.0 * Xx + 2.92338 * Xy + 59.4188 * Xw,
                20.3225 * Xx - 27.0302 * Xy + 222.592 * Xw,
                121.563 * Xx + 626.13 * Xy + 316.627 * Xw
            ),
            vec3(
                Yx,
                Yy,
                Yw
            )
        );

    float bias =
        dot(
            m1x,
            vec2(
                Yx,
                Yy
            )
        ) /
        max(
            biasDen,
            1.0e-6
        );

    vec2 m3x =
        vec2(
            0.0365463 * Xx + 3.32707 * Xy,
            9.0632 * Xx - 9.04756 * Xy
        );

    float scaleDen =
        dot(
            vec3(
                1.0 * Xx + 3.59685 * Xz - 1.36772 * Xw,
                9.04401 * Xx - 16.3174 * Xz + 9.22949 * Xw,
                5.56589 * Xx + 19.7886 * Xz - 20.2123 * Xw
            ),
            vec3(
                Yx,
                Yy,
                Yw
            )
        );

    float scale =
        dot(
            m3x,
            vec2(
                Yx,
                Yy
            )
        ) /
        max(
            scaleDen,
            1.0e-6
        );

    bias *=
        clamp(
            specularColor.g * 50.0,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );

    return
        specularColor *
        max(
            scale,
            0.0
        ) +
        vec3(
            max(
                bias,
                0.0
            )
        );
}


vec3 fresnelSchlick(
    float cosTheta,
    vec3 F0
)
{


    vec3 x = vec3(1.0) - vec3(cosTheta);
    vec3 x2 = x * x;
    return
        F0 +
        (
            vec3(1.0) - F0
        ) *
        ( (x2 * x2) * x);
}

















float evaluateBurleyDiffuseAngular(
    vec3 N,
    vec3 V,
    vec3 L,
    float roughness
)
{
    vec3 H =
        normalize(
            V + L
        );


    float LdotH =
        clamp(
            dot(L, H),
            0.0,
            1.0
        );


    float NdotL =
        clamp(
            dot(N, L),
            0.0,
            1.0
        );


    float NdotV =
        clamp(
            dot(N, V),
            0.0,
            1.0
        );


    float f90 =
        0.5 +
        2.0 *
        roughness *
        LdotH *
        LdotH;


    float lightScatter =
        1.0 +
        (
            f90 - 1.0
        ) *
        pow(
            1.0 - NdotL,
            5.0
        );


    float viewScatter =
        1.0 +
        (
            f90 - 1.0
        ) *
        pow(
            1.0 - NdotV,
            5.0
        );


    return
        NdotL *
        lightScatter *
        viewScatter;
}




















float evaluatePBR2ReflectionStrength(
    float encodedRoughness,
    int f0MetalCode
)
{




    float perceptualSmoothness =
        1.0 -
        sqrt(
            clamp(
                encodedRoughness,
                0.0,
                1.0
            )
        );


    float x =
        clamp(
            perceptualSmoothness * 2.5 - 0.85,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );


    float shifted =
        x - 1.0;


    float reflectionStrength =
        1.0 - shifted * shifted;


    if (f0MetalCode >= 230)
    {
        reflectionStrength =
            1.0;
    }


    return
        clamp(
            reflectionStrength,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );
}


float distributionGGX(
    vec3 N,
    vec3 H,
    float roughness
)
{
    float a =
        roughness * roughness;

    float a2 =
        a * a;

    float NdotH =
        max(
            dot(N, H),
            0.0
        );

    float NdotH2 =
        NdotH * NdotH;

    float denom =
        NdotH2 *
        (
            a2 - 1.0
        ) +
        1.0;

    return
        a2 /
        max(
            PI * denom * denom,
            1.0e-6
        );
}


float geometrySchlickGGX(
    float NdotX,
    float roughness
)
{
    float r =
        roughness + 1.0;

    float k =
        (r * r) / 8.0;

    return
        NdotX /
        (
            NdotX * (1.0 - k) + k
        );
}


float geometrySmith(
    vec3 N,
    vec3 V,
    vec3 L,
    float roughness
)
{
    float NdotV =
        max(
            dot(N, V),
            0.0
        );

    float NdotL =
        max(
            dot(N, L),
            0.0
        );

    float ggxV =
        geometrySchlickGGX(
            NdotV,
            roughness
        );

    float ggxL =
        geometrySchlickGGX(
            NdotL,
            roughness
        );

    return
        ggxV * ggxL;
}




















float ggxEavgApprox(
    float perceptualRoughness
)
{
    float r2 =
        perceptualRoughness * perceptualRoughness;

    return
        1.00068
        - 0.16407 * perceptualRoughness
        - 1.64700 * r2
        + 1.88283 * perceptualRoughness * r2
        - 0.66608 * r2 * r2;
}









vec3 multiscatterEnergyCompensation(
    vec3 f0,
    float perceptualRoughness
)
{
    return
        vec3(1.0) +
        f0 *
        (1.0 / ggxEavgApprox(perceptualRoughness) - 1.0);
}





const float WATER_SURFACE_SUN_ROUGHNESS = 0.12;

vec3 evaluatePrimaryWaterSunLighting(
    vec3 waterSurfacePosition,
    vec3 waterNormal,
    vec3 viewDirection,
    vec3 sunDirection
)
{
    const float WATER_F0 = 0.0200593;

    vec3 N = normalize(waterNormal);
    vec3 V = normalize(viewDirection);
    vec3 L = normalize(sunDirection);

    float NdotL = max(dot(N, L), 0.0);
    float NdotV = max(dot(N, V), 0.0);


    if (L.y <= 0.0 || NdotL <= 0.0 || NdotV <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }



    vec3 sunTransmittance = traceSunTransmittanceThroughWater(
        waterSurfacePosition + N * 0.035,
        L,
        true
    );

    if (max(sunTransmittance.r, max(sunTransmittance.g, sunTransmittance.b)) <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    vec3 sunRadiance =
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightColor() *
        3.2 *
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale() *
        sunTransmittance *
        c3CloudSunTransmittance(waterSurfacePosition + N * 0.035, L);

    vec3 H = normalize(V + L);
    float VdotH = max(dot(V, H), 0.0);
    float F = fresnelSchlickScalar(VdotH, WATER_F0);
    float D = distributionGGX(N, H, WATER_SURFACE_SUN_ROUGHNESS);
    float G = geometrySmith(N, V, L, WATER_SURFACE_SUN_ROUGHNESS);
    vec3 sunSpecular =
        sunRadiance *
        ( (D * G * F) / max(4.0 * NdotV * NdotL, 1.0e-4)) *
        NdotL;

    return sunSpecular;
}
























vec3 evaluateDirectSunLighting(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 viewDir,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    float hitRoughness,
    float hitF0Metal,
    float pomSelfShadow,
    vec3 sunDirection
)
{
    vec3 N =
        normalize(
            hitNormal
        );

    vec3 V =
        normalize(
            viewDir
        );

    vec3 L =
        normalize(
            sunDirection
        );

    float NdotL =
        max(
            dot(N, L),
            0.0
        );

    float NdotV =
        max(
            dot(N, V),
            0.0
        );

    if (
        NdotL <= 0.0 ||
        NdotV <= 0.0
    )
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }


    vec3 sunTransmittance =
        traceSunTransmittanceThroughWater(
            hitPos +
                L * 0.001,
            L,
            true
        );

    vec3 sunLightColor =
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightColor() *
        3.2 *
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale() *
        sunTransmittance *
        c3CloudSunTransmittance(hitPos + L * 0.001, L) *
        clamp(
            pomSelfShadow,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );


    int f0MetalCode =
        decodeF0MetalCode(
            hitF0Metal
        );

    float metallic =
        decodeMetallicFromCode(
            f0MetalCode
        );

    vec3 F0 =
        decodeBaseF0(
            f0MetalCode,
            hitAlbedo
        );


    float roughness =
        clamp(
            hitRoughness,
            0.04,
            1.0
        );


    vec3 H =
        normalize(
            V + L
        );

    float VdotH =
        max(
            dot(V, H),
            0.0
        );


    vec3 F =
        fresnelSchlick(
            VdotH,
            F0
        );

    float D =
        distributionGGX(
            N,
            H,
            roughness
        );

    float G =
        geometrySmith(
            N,
            V,
            L,
            roughness
        );


    vec3 specularBRDF =
        (
            D * G * F
        ) /
        max(
            4.0 * NdotV * NdotL,
            1.0e-4
        );


    vec3 kD =
        (
            vec3(1.0) - F
        ) *
        (1.0 - metallic);





    float burleyDiffuse =
        evaluateBurleyDiffuseAngular(
            N,
            V,
            L,
            roughness
        );


    vec3 diffuseTerm =
        kD *
        hitAlbedo *
        1.2 *
        burleyDiffuse;

    vec3 specularTerm =
        specularBRDF *
        NdotL;


    return
        sunLightColor *
        (
            diffuseTerm +
            specularTerm
        );
}


















const int MAX_PRIMARY_LOCAL_LIGHTS =
    4;









const int MAX_SECONDARY_LOCAL_LIGHTS =
    2;

vec3 decodeLocalLightColor(
    uint typeCode
)
{

    if (typeCode == 0u)
    {
        return
            vec3(
                1.00,
                0.90,
                0.75
            );
    }



    if (typeCode == 1u)
    {
        return
            vec3(
                1.00,
                0.62,
                0.28
            );
    }



    if (typeCode == 2u)
    {
        return
            vec3(
                0.25,
                0.72,
                1.00
            );
    }



    if (typeCode == 3u)
    {
        return
            vec3(
                1.00,
                0.18,
                0.08
            );
    }



    if (typeCode == 4u)
    {
        return
            vec3(
                1.00,
                0.34,
                0.08
            );
    }




    if (typeCode == 5u)
    {
        return
            vec3(
                1.00,
                0.82,
                0.52
            );
    }



    if (typeCode == 6u)
    {
        return
            vec3(
                0.70,
                0.28,
                1.00
            );
    }


    return
        vec3(
            1.00,
            0.90,
            0.75
        );
}













vec3 evaluateLocalEmitterCandidateRadiance(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 incomingRayDirection,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    LocalLightGPU candidateLight
)
{
    vec3 safeIncomingDir =
        normalize(
            incomingRayDirection
        );


    vec3 emitterProbePos =
        hitPos +
        safeIncomingDir *
        0.02;


    vec4 worldPosEmission =
        candidateLight
            .worldPosEmission;


    vec3 emitterBlockMin =
        worldPosEmission.xyz -
        vec3(0.001);


    vec3 emitterBlockMax =
        worldPosEmission.xyz +
        vec3(1.001);


    bool hitThisEmitter =
        all(
            greaterThanEqual(
                emitterProbePos,
                emitterBlockMin
            )
        ) &&
        all(
            lessThanEqual(
                emitterProbePos,
                emitterBlockMax
            )
        );


    if (! hitThisEmitter)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }


    float emitterLevel =
        clamp(
            worldPosEmission.w,
            1.0,
            15.0
        );


    float emitterLevel01 =
        emitterLevel /
        15.0;


    vec3 emitterColor =
        decodeLocalLightColor(
            candidateLight.meta.x
        );


    vec3 safeAlbedo =
        clamp(
            hitAlbedo,
            vec3(0.0),
            vec3(1.0)
        );


    float albedoRadiance =
        dot(
            safeAlbedo,
            vec3(0.333333333)
        );




    return
        emitterColor *
        3.2 *
        LOCAL_LIGHT_ENERGY_SCALE * localLightIntensity() *
        emitterLevel01 *
        albedoRadiance *
        safeAlbedo;
}


vec3 evaluateLocalEmitterRadianceAtHit(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 incomingRayDirection,
    vec3 hitAlbedo
)
{







    {
        return
            vec3(0.0);
    }


    uint lightCount =
        localLightBuffer.header.x;


    if (lightCount == 0u)
    {
        return
            vec3(0.0);
    }






    vec3 emitterProbePos =
        hitPos -
        normalize(
            incomingRayDirection
        ) *
        0.02;


    ivec3 probeCell =
        localLightGridCellCoord(
            emitterProbePos
        );


    uint packedIndexBase =
        localLightGridPackedIndexBase();


    int minCellX =
        localLightGridNeighborMinOffset(
            emitterProbePos.x
        );

    int maxCellX =
        localLightGridNeighborMaxOffset(
            emitterProbePos.x
        );

    int minCellY =
        localLightGridNeighborMinOffset(
            emitterProbePos.y
        );

    int maxCellY =
        localLightGridNeighborMaxOffset(
            emitterProbePos.y
        );

    int minCellZ =
        localLightGridNeighborMinOffset(
            emitterProbePos.z
        );

    int maxCellZ =
        localLightGridNeighborMaxOffset(
            emitterProbePos.z
        );


    vec3 emitterRadiance =
        vec3(0.0);


    for (int cellZ = minCellZ; cellZ <= maxCellZ; ++ cellZ)
    {
        for (int cellY = minCellY; cellY <= maxCellY; ++ cellY)
        {
            for (int cellX = minCellX; cellX <= maxCellX; ++ cellX)
            {
                uint firstLightIndex =
                    0u;

                uint cellLightCount =
                    0u;


                if (
                    ! localLightGridLookupCell(
                        probeCell +
                        ivec3(
                            cellX,
                            cellY,
                            cellZ
                        ),
                        firstLightIndex,
                        cellLightCount
                    )
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }


                for (
                    uint candidateOffset = 0u;
                    candidateOffset < cellLightCount;
                    ++ candidateOffset
                )
                {
                    uint lightIndex =
                        localLightGridBuffer.words[
                            packedIndexBase +
                            firstLightIndex +
                            candidateOffset
                        ];


                    if (lightIndex >= lightCount)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }


                    vec3 candidateRadiance =
                        evaluateLocalEmitterCandidateRadiance(
                            hitPos,
                            incomingRayDirection,
                            hitAlbedo,
                            localLightBuffer.lights[
                                lightIndex
                            ]
                        );


                    emitterRadiance =
                        max(
                            emitterRadiance,
                            candidateRadiance
                        );
                }
            }
        }
    }


    return
        emitterRadiance;
}
vec3 evaluateLocalEmitterSurfaceRadiance(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 incomingRayDirection,
    vec3 hitAlbedo
)
{
    uint lightCount =
        localLightBuffer.header.x;


    vec3 emitterRadiance =
        vec3(0.0);


    vec3 safeAlbedo =
        clamp(
            hitAlbedo,
            vec3(0.0),
            vec3(1.0)
        );






    float albedoRadiance =
        dot(
            safeAlbedo,
            vec3(
                0.333333333
            )
        );


    for (
        uint lightIndex = 0u;
        lightIndex < lightCount;
        ++ lightIndex
    )
    {
        LocalLightGPU candidateLight =
            localLightBuffer
                .lights[lightIndex];


        vec4 worldPosEmission =
            candidateLight
                .worldPosEmission;


        vec3 emitterProbePos =
            hitPos +
            normalize(
                incomingRayDirection
            ) *
            0.02;


        vec3 emitterBlockMin =
            worldPosEmission.xyz -
            vec3(0.001);


        vec3 emitterBlockMax =
            worldPosEmission.xyz +
            vec3(1.001);


        bool hitThisEmitter =
            all(
                greaterThanEqual(
                    emitterProbePos,
                    emitterBlockMin
                )
            ) &&
            all(
                lessThanEqual(
                    emitterProbePos,
                    emitterBlockMax
                )
            );


        if (! hitThisEmitter)
        {
            continue;
        }


        float emitterLevel =
            clamp(
                worldPosEmission.w,
                1.0,
                15.0
            );


        float emitterLevel01 =
            emitterLevel /
            15.0;


        vec3 emitterColor =
            decodeLocalLightColor(
                candidateLight.meta.x
            );


        vec3 texturedEmitterRadiance =
            emitterColor *
            3.2 *
            LOCAL_LIGHT_ENERGY_SCALE * localLightIntensity() *
            emitterLevel01 *
            albedoRadiance *
            safeAlbedo;


        emitterRadiance =
            max(
                emitterRadiance,
                texturedEmitterRadiance
            );
    }


    return
        emitterRadiance;
}
float localLightRangeFade(
    float distanceToLight,
    float emissionLevel
)
{


    float range =
        max(
            emissionLevel * 2.0,
            2.0
        );





    float fadeStart =
        range *
        0.80;


    return
        1.0 -
        smoothstep(
            fadeStart,
            range,
            distanceToLight
        );
}
















float localLightDistanceAttenuation(
    float distanceToLight
)
{
    float d =
        max(
            distanceToLight,
            0.0
        );


    return
        0.55 /
        (
            1.0 +
            0.18 * d +
            0.025 * d * d
        );
}






















const int LOCAL_LIGHT_CUBE_AREA_SAMPLES =
    2;







const float LOCAL_LIGHT_CUBE_EMITTER_SIZE =
    2.0;

const float LOCAL_LIGHT_CUBE_EMITTER_HALF_SIZE =
    LOCAL_LIGHT_CUBE_EMITTER_SIZE *
    0.5;





const float LOCAL_LIGHT_CUBE_SURFACE_INSET =
    0.03;





const float LOCAL_LIGHT_CUBE_RAY_END_EPSILON =
    0.03;











const float LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_RADIUS =
    0.5;


const float LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_PROJECTED_AREA =
    3.14159265359 *
    LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_RADIUS *
    LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_RADIUS;


bool isPointLikeLocalLightType(
    uint typeCode
)
{
    return
        typeCode >= 1u &&
        typeCode <= 3u;
}





const float LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_NEARFIELD_MIN_D2 =
    1.75;

const float LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_RADIANCE_SCALE =
    LOCAL_LIGHT_ENERGY_SCALE;

const float LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_DIFFUSE_SCALE =
    0.92;

const float LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_DIFFUSE_SOFTEN =
    0.45;

const float LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_SPECULAR_SCALE =
    0.28;

const float LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_MIN_SPECULAR_ROUGHNESS =
    0.35;
















vec3 dynamicLightBRDF(
    vec3 N,
    vec3 V,
    vec3 L,
    vec3 albedo,
    vec3 f0,
    float metallic,
    float roughness
)
{
    float NdotL = max(dot(N, L), 0.0);
    if (NdotL <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }
    if (f0.x < 0.0)
    {

        return albedo * (LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_DIFFUSE_SCALE * NdotL);
    }

    float NdotV = max(dot(N, V), 1.0e-4);
    vec3 H = normalize(V + L);
    float VdotH = max(dot(V, H), 0.0);

    vec3 F = fresnelSchlick(VdotH, f0);
    float specularRoughness =
        max(roughness, LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_MIN_SPECULAR_ROUGHNESS);
    float D = distributionGGX(N, H, specularRoughness);
    float G = geometrySmith(N, V, L, specularRoughness);
    vec3 specularBRDF =
        (D * G * F) / max(4.0 * NdotV * NdotL, 1.0e-4);


    specularBRDF *=
        multiscatterEnergyCompensation(f0, specularRoughness);

    vec3 kD = (vec3(1.0) - F) * (1.0 - metallic);
    vec3 diffuseTerm =
        kD * albedo * (NdotL * LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_DIFFUSE_SCALE);
    vec3 specularTerm =
        specularBRDF * (NdotL * LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_SPECULAR_SCALE);
    return diffuseTerm + specularTerm;
}












vec3 sampleLocalLightPointSphereSurface(
    vec3 emitterBlockPos,
    vec3 receiverPos,
    int selectedSlot,
    int sampleIndex
)
{
    vec3 emitterCenter =
        emitterBlockPos +
        vec3(0.5);


    vec3 centerToReceiver =
        receiverPos -
        emitterCenter;


    float centerToReceiverLengthSquared =
        dot(
            centerToReceiver,
            centerToReceiver
        );


    vec3 frontDirection =
        centerToReceiverLengthSquared > 1.0e-8
            ? centerToReceiver *
                inversesqrt(
                    centerToReceiverLengthSquared
                )
            : vec3(
                0.0,
                1.0,
                0.0
            );


    vec3 referenceAxis =
        abs(frontDirection.y) < 0.999
            ? vec3(
                0.0,
                1.0,
                0.0
            )
            : vec3(
                1.0,
                0.0,
                0.0
            );


    vec3 tangent =
        normalize(
            cross(
                referenceAxis,
                frontDirection
            )
        );


    vec3 bitangent =
        cross(
            frontDirection,
            tangent
        );


    uvec3 emitterBits =
        floatBitsToUint(
            emitterBlockPos
        );


    uint framePhase =
        cam.frameCount &
        15u;


    uint state =
        gl_LaunchIDEXT.x * 1877u ^
        gl_LaunchIDEXT.y * 9241u ^
        framePhase * 26711u ^
        emitterBits.x * 31847u ^
        emitterBits.y * 42131u ^
        emitterBits.z * 61169u ^
        uint(selectedSlot + 1) * 1031u ^
        uint(sampleIndex + 1) * 2017u;


    float projectedRadius =
        LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_RADIUS *
        sqrt(
            rand(state)
        );


    float projectedAngle =
        6.28318530718 *
        rand(state);


    float diskX =
        projectedRadius *
        cos(projectedAngle);


    float diskY =
        projectedRadius *
        sin(projectedAngle);


    float frontOffset =
        sqrt(
            max(
                LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_RADIUS *
                    LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_RADIUS -
                    projectedRadius *
                    projectedRadius,
                0.0
            )
        );


    return
        emitterCenter +
        tangent * diskX +
        bitangent * diskY +
        frontDirection * frontOffset;
}













vec3 sampleLocalLightCubeSurface(
    vec3 emitterBlockPos,
    vec3 receiverPos,
    int selectedSlot,
    int sampleIndex,
    out vec3 emitterSurfaceNormal,
    out float faceSelectionProbability
)
{
    vec3 emitterCenter =
        emitterBlockPos +
        vec3(0.5);


    vec3 centerToReceiver =
        receiverPos -
        emitterCenter;


    vec3 faceWeights =
        abs(
            centerToReceiver
        ) +
        vec3(1.0e-4);


    float faceWeightSum =
        faceWeights.x +
        faceWeights.y +
        faceWeights.z;


    uvec3 emitterBits =
        floatBitsToUint(
            emitterBlockPos
        );





    uint framePhase =
        cam.frameCount &
        15u;


    uint state =
        gl_LaunchIDEXT.x * 1973u +
        gl_LaunchIDEXT.y * 9277u ^
        framePhase * 26699u ^
        emitterBits.x * 31847u ^
        emitterBits.y * 42131u ^
        emitterBits.z * 61169u ^
        uint(selectedSlot + 1) * 1013u ^
        uint(sampleIndex + 1) * 1999u;


    float faceSelector =
        rand(state) *
        faceWeightSum;


    float tangentExtent =
        max(
            LOCAL_LIGHT_CUBE_EMITTER_HALF_SIZE -
                LOCAL_LIGHT_CUBE_SURFACE_INSET,
            0.01
        );


    float u =
        mix(
            - tangentExtent,
            tangentExtent,
            rand(state)
        );


    float v =
        mix(
            - tangentExtent,
            tangentExtent,
            rand(state)
        );


    vec3 samplePos =
        emitterCenter;


    if (faceSelector < faceWeights.x)
    {
        faceSelectionProbability =
            faceWeights.x /
            faceWeightSum;


        bool positiveFace =
            centerToReceiver.x >=
            0.0;


        emitterSurfaceNormal =
            vec3(
                positiveFace
                    ? 1.0
                    : - 1.0,
                0.0,
                0.0
            );


                samplePos.x =
            emitterBlockPos.x +
            (
                positiveFace
                    ? 1.0
                    : 0.0
            );

        samplePos.y =
            emitterCenter.y +
            u;

        samplePos.z =
            emitterCenter.z +
            v;
    }
    else if (
        faceSelector <
        faceWeights.x +
        faceWeights.y
    )
    {
        faceSelectionProbability =
            faceWeights.y /
            faceWeightSum;


        bool positiveFace =
            centerToReceiver.y >=
            0.0;


        emitterSurfaceNormal =
            vec3(
                0.0,
                positiveFace
                    ? 1.0
                    : - 1.0,
                0.0
            );


                samplePos.x =
            emitterCenter.x +
            u;

        samplePos.y =
            emitterBlockPos.y +
            (
                positiveFace
                    ? 1.0
                    : 0.0
            );

        samplePos.z =
            emitterCenter.z +
            v;
    }
    else
    {
        faceSelectionProbability =
            faceWeights.z /
            faceWeightSum;


        bool positiveFace =
            centerToReceiver.z >=
            0.0;


        emitterSurfaceNormal =
            vec3(
                0.0,
                0.0,
                positiveFace
                    ? 1.0
                    : - 1.0
            );


        samplePos.x =
            emitterCenter.x +
            u;

        samplePos.y =
            emitterCenter.y +
            v;

        samplePos.z =
            emitterBlockPos.z +
            (
                positiveFace
                    ? 1.0
                    : 0.0
            );
    }


    return
        samplePos;
}


float localLightApproxScore(
    vec3 surfacePos,
    vec3 N,
    vec4 worldPosEmission
)
{
    float emissionLevel =
        clamp(
            worldPosEmission.w,
            1.0,
            15.0
        );




    vec3 lightPos =
        worldPosEmission.xyz +
        vec3(0.5);


    vec3 toLight =
        lightPos -
        surfacePos;


    float distanceSquared =
        dot(
            toLight,
            toLight
        );


    if (distanceSquared <= 1.0e-6)
    {
        return
            0.0;
    }


    float distanceToLight =
        sqrt(
            distanceSquared
        );


    float rangeFade =
        localLightRangeFade(
            distanceToLight,
            emissionLevel
        );


    if (rangeFade <= 0.0)
    {
        return
            0.0;
    }


    vec3 L =
        toLight /
        distanceToLight;


    float NdotL =
        max(
            dot(
                N,
                L
            ),
            0.0
        );


    if (NdotL <= 0.0)
    {
        return
            0.0;
    }





float distanceAttenuation =
    localLightDistanceAttenuation(
        distanceToLight
    );


    float emission01 =
        emissionLevel /
        15.0;


return
    emission01 *
    distanceAttenuation *
    rangeFade *
    NdotL;
}



























const float G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_PROBABILITY =
    0.5;

const float G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON =
    1.0e-6;

const float G2B_CUBE_GUIDE_INSET =
    0.001;


bool g2bSurfaceBelongsToEmitter(
    vec3 surfacePos,
    vec3 surfaceNormal,
    vec3 emitterBlockPos
)
{
    vec3 insideProbe =
        surfacePos -
        normalize(surfaceNormal) *
        0.02;


    return
        all(
            greaterThanEqual(
                insideProbe,
                emitterBlockPos -
                    vec3(0.001)
            )
        ) &&
        all(
            lessThanEqual(
                insideProbe,
                emitterBlockPos +
                    vec3(1.001)
            )
        );
}


float g2bLocalGuideSelectionWeight(
    vec3 surfacePos,
    vec3 surfaceNormal,
    LocalLightGPU light
)
{
    if (
        g2bSurfaceBelongsToEmitter(
            surfacePos,
            surfaceNormal,
            light.worldPosEmission.xyz
        )
    )
    {
        return
            0.0;
    }


    return
        max(
            localLightApproxScore(
                surfacePos,
                surfaceNormal,
                light.worldPosEmission
            ),
            0.0
        );
}


float g2bLocalGuideWeightSum(
    vec3 surfacePos,
    vec3 surfaceNormal
)
{
    uint lightCount =
        localLightBuffer.header.x;


    float weightSum =
        0.0;


    ivec3 centerCell =
        localLightGridCellCoord(
            surfacePos
        );

    uint packedIndexBase =
        localLightGridPackedIndexBase();


    for (int cellZ = - 1; cellZ <= 1; ++ cellZ)
    {
        for (int cellY = - 1; cellY <= 1; ++ cellY)
        {
            for (int cellX = - 1; cellX <= 1; ++ cellX)
            {
                uint firstLightIndex =
                    0u;

                uint cellLightCount =
                    0u;


                if (
                    ! localLightGridLookupCell(
                        centerCell +
                        ivec3(
                            cellX,
                            cellY,
                            cellZ
                        ),
                        firstLightIndex,
                        cellLightCount
                    )
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }


                for (
                    uint candidateOffset = 0u;
                    candidateOffset < cellLightCount;
                    ++ candidateOffset
                )
                {
                    uint lightIndex =
                        localLightGridBuffer.words[
                            packedIndexBase +
                            firstLightIndex +
                            candidateOffset
                        ];


                    if (lightIndex >= lightCount)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }


                    weightSum +=
                        g2bLocalGuideSelectionWeight(
                            surfacePos,
                            surfaceNormal,
                            localLightBuffer.lights[
                                lightIndex
                            ]
                        );
                }
            }
        }
    }


    return
        weightSum;
}


bool g2bSampleSphereGuideDirection(
    vec3 surfacePos,
    vec3 emitterBlockPos,
    inout uint rngState,
    out vec3 sampledDirection,
    out float directionalPdf
)
{
    vec3 sphereCenter =
        emitterBlockPos +
        vec3(0.5);


    vec3 toCenter =
        sphereCenter -
