        surfacePos;


    float centerDistanceSquared =
        dot(
            toCenter,
            toCenter
        );


    float radiusSquared =
        LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_RADIUS *
        LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_RADIUS;


    if (
        centerDistanceSquared <=
        radiusSquared +
            G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON
    )
    {
        sampledDirection =
            vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);

        directionalPdf =
            0.0;

        return
            false;
    }


    float centerDistance =
        sqrt(
            centerDistanceSquared
        );


    vec3 coneAxis =
        toCenter /
        centerDistance;


    float cosThetaMax =
        sqrt(
            max(
                1.0 -
                    radiusSquared /
                    centerDistanceSquared,
                0.0
            )
        );


    float cosTheta =
        mix(
            1.0,
            cosThetaMax,
            rand(rngState)
        );


    float sinTheta =
        sqrt(
            max(
                1.0 -
                    cosTheta *
                    cosTheta,
                0.0
            )
        );


    float phi =
        2.0 *
        PI *
        rand(rngState);


    vec3 referenceAxis =
        abs(coneAxis.z) < 0.999
            ? vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)
            : vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);


    vec3 tangent =
        normalize(
            cross(
                referenceAxis,
                coneAxis
            )
        );


    vec3 bitangent =
        cross(
            coneAxis,
            tangent
        );


    sampledDirection =
        normalize(
            tangent *
                (
                    cos(phi) *
                    sinTheta
                ) +
            bitangent *
                (
                    sin(phi) *
                    sinTheta
                ) +
            coneAxis *
                cosTheta
        );


    directionalPdf =
        1.0 /
        max(
            2.0 *
                PI *
                (
                    1.0 -
                    cosThetaMax
                ),
            G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON
        );


    return
        true;
}


float g2bSphereGuideDirectionalPdf(
    vec3 surfacePos,
    vec3 rayDirection,
    vec3 emitterBlockPos
)
{
    vec3 sphereCenter =
        emitterBlockPos +
        vec3(0.5);


    vec3 toCenter =
        sphereCenter -
        surfacePos;


    float centerDistanceSquared =
        dot(
            toCenter,
            toCenter
        );


    float radiusSquared =
        LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_RADIUS *
        LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_RADIUS;


    if (
        centerDistanceSquared <=
        radiusSquared +
            G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON
    )
    {
        return
            0.0;
    }


    vec3 originFromCenter =
        surfacePos -
        sphereCenter;


    float b =
        dot(
            originFromCenter,
            rayDirection
        );


    float c =
        dot(
            originFromCenter,
            originFromCenter
        ) -
        radiusSquared;


    float discriminant =
        b * b -
        c;


    if (discriminant < 0.0)
    {
        return
            0.0;
    }


    float root =
        sqrt(
            discriminant
        );


    float farIntersection =
        - b +
        root;


    if (farIntersection <= 0.0)
    {
        return
            0.0;
    }


    float cosThetaMax =
        sqrt(
            max(
                1.0 -
                    radiusSquared /
                    centerDistanceSquared,
                0.0
            )
        );


    return
        1.0 /
        max(
            2.0 *
                PI *
                (
                    1.0 -
                    cosThetaMax
                ),
            G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON
        );
}


bool g2bSampleCubeGuideDirection(
    vec3 surfacePos,
    vec3 emitterBlockPos,
    inout uint rngState,
    out vec3 sampledDirection,
    out float directionalPdf
)
{
    vec3 emitterCenter =
        emitterBlockPos +
        vec3(0.5);


    vec3 receiverOffset =
        surfacePos -
        emitterCenter;


    vec3 faceWeights =
        abs(receiverOffset) +
        vec3(1.0e-4);


    float faceWeightSum =
        faceWeights.x +
        faceWeights.y +
        faceWeights.z;


    float faceSelector =
        rand(rngState) *
        faceWeightSum;


    float usableSide =
        1.0 -
        2.0 *
        G2B_CUBE_GUIDE_INSET;


    float u =
        G2B_CUBE_GUIDE_INSET +
        rand(rngState) *
        usableSide;


    float v =
        G2B_CUBE_GUIDE_INSET +
        rand(rngState) *
        usableSide;


    vec3 samplePos =
        emitterCenter;


    vec3 emitterNormal =
        vec3(0.0);


    float faceProbability =
        1.0;


    if (faceSelector < faceWeights.x)
    {
        bool positiveFace =
            receiverOffset.x >= 0.0;


        faceProbability =
            faceWeights.x /
            faceWeightSum;


        samplePos =
            vec3(
                emitterBlockPos.x +
                    (
                        positiveFace
                            ? 1.0
                            : 0.0
                    ),
                emitterBlockPos.y + u,
                emitterBlockPos.z + v
            );


        emitterNormal =
            vec3(
                positiveFace
                    ? 1.0
                    : - 1.0,
                0.0,
                0.0
            );
    }
    else if (
        faceSelector <
        faceWeights.x +
            faceWeights.y
    )
    {
        bool positiveFace =
            receiverOffset.y >= 0.0;


        faceProbability =
            faceWeights.y /
            faceWeightSum;


        samplePos =
            vec3(
                emitterBlockPos.x + u,
                emitterBlockPos.y +
                    (
                        positiveFace
                            ? 1.0
                            : 0.0
                    ),
                emitterBlockPos.z + v
            );


        emitterNormal =
            vec3(
                0.0,
                positiveFace
                    ? 1.0
                    : - 1.0,
                0.0
            );
    }
    else
    {
        bool positiveFace =
            receiverOffset.z >= 0.0;


        faceProbability =
            faceWeights.z /
            faceWeightSum;


        samplePos =
            vec3(
                emitterBlockPos.x + u,
                emitterBlockPos.y + v,
                emitterBlockPos.z +
                    (
                        positiveFace
                            ? 1.0
                            : 0.0
                    )
            );


        emitterNormal =
            vec3(
                0.0,
                0.0,
                positiveFace
                    ? 1.0
                    : - 1.0
            );
    }


    vec3 toSample =
        samplePos -
        surfacePos;


    float sampleDistanceSquared =
        dot(
            toSample,
            toSample
        );


    if (
        sampleDistanceSquared <=
        G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON
    )
    {
        sampledDirection =
            vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);

        directionalPdf =
            0.0;

        return
            false;
    }


    float sampleDistance =
        sqrt(
            sampleDistanceSquared
        );


    sampledDirection =
        toSample /
        sampleDistance;


    float emitterFacing =
        max(
            dot(
                emitterNormal,
                - sampledDirection
            ),
            0.0
        );


    if (
        emitterFacing <=
        G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON
    )
    {
        directionalPdf =
            0.0;

        return
            false;
    }


    float faceArea =
        usableSide *
        usableSide;


    directionalPdf =
        faceProbability *
        sampleDistanceSquared /
        max(
            emitterFacing *
                faceArea,
            G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON
        );


    return
        true;
}


float g2bCubeGuideDirectionalPdf(
    vec3 surfacePos,
    vec3 rayDirection,
    vec3 emitterBlockPos
)
{
    vec3 boxMin =
        emitterBlockPos;


    vec3 boxMax =
        emitterBlockPos +
        vec3(1.0);


    vec3 safeDirection =
        vec3(
            abs(rayDirection.x) > 1.0e-8
                ? rayDirection.x
                : (
                    rayDirection.x >= 0.0
                        ? 1.0e-8
                        : - 1.0e-8
                ),
            abs(rayDirection.y) > 1.0e-8
                ? rayDirection.y
                : (
                    rayDirection.y >= 0.0
                        ? 1.0e-8
                        : - 1.0e-8
                ),
            abs(rayDirection.z) > 1.0e-8
                ? rayDirection.z
                : (
                    rayDirection.z >= 0.0
                        ? 1.0e-8
                        : - 1.0e-8
                )
        );


    vec3 t0 =
        (
            boxMin -
            surfacePos
        ) /
        safeDirection;


    vec3 t1 =
        (
            boxMax -
            surfacePos
        ) /
        safeDirection;


    vec3 nearByAxis =
        min(
            t0,
            t1
        );


    vec3 farByAxis =
        max(
            t0,
            t1
        );


    float nearDistance =
        max(
            max(
                nearByAxis.x,
                nearByAxis.y
            ),
            nearByAxis.z
        );


    float farDistance =
        min(
            min(
                farByAxis.x,
                farByAxis.y
            ),
            farByAxis.z
        );


    if (
        farDistance <
            max(
                nearDistance,
                0.0
            ) ||
        nearDistance <= 0.0
    )
    {
        return
            0.0;
    }


    int hitAxis =
        0;


    if (nearByAxis.y > nearByAxis.x)
    {
        hitAxis =
            1;
    }


    if (
        nearByAxis.z >
        nearByAxis[hitAxis]
    )
    {
        hitAxis =
            2;
    }


    vec3 hitPoint =
        surfacePos +
        rayDirection *
        nearDistance;


    float inset =
        G2B_CUBE_GUIDE_INSET;


    bool insideSampledFace =
        true;


    if (hitAxis == 0)
    {
        insideSampledFace =
            hitPoint.y >= boxMin.y + inset &&
            hitPoint.y <= boxMax.y - inset &&
            hitPoint.z >= boxMin.z + inset &&
            hitPoint.z <= boxMax.z - inset;
    }
    else if (hitAxis == 1)
    {
        insideSampledFace =
            hitPoint.x >= boxMin.x + inset &&
            hitPoint.x <= boxMax.x - inset &&
            hitPoint.z >= boxMin.z + inset &&
            hitPoint.z <= boxMax.z - inset;
    }
    else
    {
        insideSampledFace =
            hitPoint.x >= boxMin.x + inset &&
            hitPoint.x <= boxMax.x - inset &&
            hitPoint.y >= boxMin.y + inset &&
            hitPoint.y <= boxMax.y - inset;
    }


    if (! insideSampledFace)
    {
        return
            0.0;
    }


    vec3 receiverOffset =
        surfacePos -
        (
            emitterBlockPos +
            vec3(0.5)
        );


    vec3 faceWeights =
        abs(receiverOffset) +
        vec3(1.0e-4);


    float faceWeightSum =
        faceWeights.x +
        faceWeights.y +
        faceWeights.z;


    float faceProbability =
        faceWeights[hitAxis] /
        faceWeightSum;


    float emitterFacing =
        abs(
            rayDirection[hitAxis]
        );


    float usableSide =
        1.0 -
        2.0 *
        inset;


    float faceArea =
        usableSide *
        usableSide;


    return
        faceProbability *
        nearDistance *
        nearDistance /
        max(
            emitterFacing *
                faceArea,
            G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON
        );
}


float g2bLocalGuideDirectionalPdf(
    vec3 surfacePos,
    vec3 surfaceNormal,
    vec3 rayDirection,
    float guideWeightSum
)
{
    if (
        guideWeightSum <=
        G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON
    )
    {
        return
            0.0;
    }


    float mixturePdf =
        0.0;


    uint lightCount =
        localLightBuffer.header.x;


    ivec3 centerCell =
        localLightGridCellCoord(
            surfacePos
        );

    uint packedIndexBase =
        localLightGridPackedIndexBase();


    for (int cellZ = - 1; cellZ <= 1; ++ cellZ)
    {
        for (int cellY = - 1; cellY <= 1; ++ cellY)
        {
            for (int cellX = - 1; cellX <= 1; ++ cellX)
            {
                uint firstLightIndex =
                    0u;

                uint cellLightCount =
                    0u;


                if (
                    ! localLightGridLookupCell(
                        centerCell +
                        ivec3(
                            cellX,
                            cellY,
                            cellZ
                        ),
                        firstLightIndex,
                        cellLightCount
                    )
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }


                for (
                    uint candidateOffset = 0u;
                    candidateOffset < cellLightCount;
                    ++ candidateOffset
                )
                {
                    uint lightIndex =
                        localLightGridBuffer.words[
                            packedIndexBase +
                            firstLightIndex +
                            candidateOffset
                        ];


                    if (lightIndex >= lightCount)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }


                    LocalLightGPU light =
                        localLightBuffer.lights[
                            lightIndex
                        ];


                    float selectionWeight =
                        g2bLocalGuideSelectionWeight(
                            surfacePos,
                            surfaceNormal,
                            light
                        );


                    if (selectionWeight <= 0.0)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }


                    float shapePdf =
                        isPointLikeLocalLightType(
                            light.meta.x
                        )
                            ? g2bSphereGuideDirectionalPdf(
                                surfacePos,
                                rayDirection,
                                light.worldPosEmission.xyz
                            )
                            : g2bCubeGuideDirectionalPdf(
                                surfacePos,
                                rayDirection,
                                light.worldPosEmission.xyz
                            );


                    mixturePdf +=
                        (
                            selectionWeight /
                            guideWeightSum
                        ) *
                        shapePdf;
                }
            }
        }
    }


    return
        mixturePdf;
}


bool g2bSampleLocalGuideDirection(
    vec3 surfacePos,
    vec3 surfaceNormal,
    float guideWeightSum,
    inout uint rngState,
    out vec3 sampledDirection,
    out float directionalPdf
)
{
    uint lightCount =
        localLightBuffer.header.x;


    if (
        lightCount == 0u ||
        guideWeightSum <=
            G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON
    )
    {
        sampledDirection =
            vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);

        directionalPdf =
            0.0;

        return
            false;
    }


    float selectionTarget =
        rand(rngState) *
        guideWeightSum;


    float accumulatedWeight =
        0.0;


    uint selectedIndex =
        0u;


    bool hasEligibleLight =
        false;


    bool selected =
        false;


    ivec3 centerCell =
        localLightGridCellCoord(
            surfacePos
        );

    uint packedIndexBase =
        localLightGridPackedIndexBase();


    for (int cellZ = - 1; cellZ <= 1; ++ cellZ)
    {
        for (int cellY = - 1; cellY <= 1; ++ cellY)
        {
            for (int cellX = - 1; cellX <= 1; ++ cellX)
            {
                uint firstLightIndex =
                    0u;

                uint cellLightCount =
                    0u;


                if (
                    ! localLightGridLookupCell(
                        centerCell +
                        ivec3(
                            cellX,
                            cellY,
                            cellZ
                        ),
                        firstLightIndex,
                        cellLightCount
                    )
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }


                for (
                    uint candidateOffset = 0u;
                    candidateOffset < cellLightCount;
                    ++ candidateOffset
                )
                {
                    uint lightIndex =
                        localLightGridBuffer.words[
                            packedIndexBase +
                            firstLightIndex +
                            candidateOffset
                        ];


                    if (lightIndex >= lightCount)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }


                    float selectionWeight =
                        g2bLocalGuideSelectionWeight(
                            surfacePos,
                            surfaceNormal,
                            localLightBuffer.lights[
                                lightIndex
                            ]
                        );


                    if (selectionWeight <= 0.0)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }


                    hasEligibleLight =
                        true;


                    selectedIndex =
                        lightIndex;


                    accumulatedWeight +=
                        selectionWeight;


                    if (selectionTarget <= accumulatedWeight)
                    {
                        selected =
                            true;

                        break;
                    }
                }


                if (selected)
                {
                    break;
                }
            }


            if (selected)
            {
                break;
            }
        }


        if (selected)
        {
            break;
        }
    }


    if (! hasEligibleLight)
    {
        sampledDirection =
            vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);

        directionalPdf =
            0.0;

        return
            false;
    }





    if (! selected)
    {
        selected =
            true;
    }


    LocalLightGPU selectedLight =
        localLightBuffer.lights[selectedIndex];


    float conditionalPdf =
        0.0;


    bool sampleValid =
        false;


    if (
        isPointLikeLocalLightType(
            selectedLight.meta.x
        )
    )
    {
        sampleValid =
            g2bSampleSphereGuideDirection(
                surfacePos,
                selectedLight.worldPosEmission.xyz,
                rngState,
                sampledDirection,
                conditionalPdf
            );
    }
    else
    {
        sampleValid =
            g2bSampleCubeGuideDirection(
                surfacePos,
                selectedLight.worldPosEmission.xyz,
                rngState,
                sampledDirection,
                conditionalPdf
            );
    }


    if (! sampleValid)
    {
        directionalPdf =
            0.0;

        return
            false;
    }


    directionalPdf =
        g2bLocalGuideDirectionalPdf(
            surfacePos,
            surfaceNormal,
            sampledDirection,
            guideWeightSum
        );


    return
        directionalPdf >
        G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON;
}


void g2bSampleDiffuseDirectionMIS(
    vec3 surfacePos,
    vec3 surfaceNormal,
    inout uint rngState,
    out vec3 sampledDirection,
    out float bsdfToMixtureWeight,
    bool useLocalGuide
)
{
    vec3 N =
        normalize(
            surfaceNormal
        );


    if (
        ! useLocalGuide ||
        ! LL_P3_ENABLE_G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE ||
        perfL10DisableG2bLocalGuide()
    )
    {
        sampledDirection =
            sampleHemisphereCosine(
                N,
                rngState
            );

        bsdfToMixtureWeight =
            1.0;

        return;
    }


    float guideWeightSum =
        g2bLocalGuideWeightSum(
            surfacePos,
            N
        );


    float guideProbability =
        guideWeightSum >
            G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON
                ? G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_PROBABILITY
                : 0.0;


    bool chooseGuide =
        guideProbability > 0.0 &&
        rand(rngState) <
            guideProbability;


    float sampledGuidePdf =
        0.0;


    if (chooseGuide)
    {
        bool guideSampleValid =
            g2bSampleLocalGuideDirection(
                surfacePos,
                N,
                guideWeightSum,
                rngState,
                sampledDirection,
                sampledGuidePdf
            );


        if (! guideSampleValid)
        {
            guideProbability =
                0.0;

            sampledDirection =
                sampleHemisphereCosine(
                    N,
                    rngState
                );
        }
    }
    else
    {
        sampledDirection =
            sampleHemisphereCosine(
                N,
                rngState
            );
    }


    float NdotDirection =
        max(
            dot(
                N,
                sampledDirection
            ),
            0.0
        );


    float bsdfPdf =
        NdotDirection /
        PI;


    float lightPdf =
        0.0;


    if (guideProbability > 0.0)
    {
        lightPdf =
            chooseGuide
                ? sampledGuidePdf
                : g2bLocalGuideDirectionalPdf(
                    surfacePos,
                    N,
                    sampledDirection,
                    guideWeightSum
                );
    }


    float mixturePdf =
        (
            1.0 -
            guideProbability
        ) *
        bsdfPdf +
        guideProbability *
        lightPdf;


    bsdfToMixtureWeight =
        mixturePdf >
            G2B_LOCAL_GUIDE_EPSILON
                ? bsdfPdf /
                    mixturePdf
                : 0.0;
}


vec3 evaluatePrimaryLocalLights(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 viewDir,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    float hitRoughness,
    int hitF0MetalCode,
    float primaryDistance,
    out vec3 emitterRadiance
)
{
    emitterRadiance =
        vec3(0.0);


    int activeLightCount =
        1;


    int areaSampleCount =
        clamp(int(cam.perfSecondary.y), 1, 2);






    if (primaryDistance >= 16.0)
    {
        areaSampleCount =
            1;
    }

    uint lightCount =
        localLightBuffer.header.x;


    if (lightCount == 0u)
    {
        return
            vec3(0.0);
    }


    vec3 N =
        normalize(
            hitNormal
        );


    vec3 V =
        normalize(
            viewDir
        );


    float NdotV =
        max(
            dot(
                N,
                V
            ),
            0.0
        );


    if (NdotV <= 0.0)
    {
        return
            vec3(0.0);
    }








    uint selectedIndex[MAX_PRIMARY_LOCAL_LIGHTS];

    float selectedScore[MAX_PRIMARY_LOCAL_LIGHTS];
    for (
    int slot = 0;
    slot < MAX_PRIMARY_LOCAL_LIGHTS;
    ++ slot
)
{
    selectedIndex[slot] =
        0u;

    selectedScore[slot] =
        - 1.0;
}


    ivec3 centerCell =
        localLightGridCellCoord(
            hitPos
        );

    int minCellX =
        localLightGridNeighborMinOffset(
            hitPos.x
        );

    int maxCellX =
        localLightGridNeighborMaxOffset(
            hitPos.x
        );

    int minCellY =
        localLightGridNeighborMinOffset(
            hitPos.y
        );

    int maxCellY =
        localLightGridNeighborMaxOffset(
            hitPos.y
        );

    int minCellZ =
        localLightGridNeighborMinOffset(
            hitPos.z
        );

    int maxCellZ =
        localLightGridNeighborMaxOffset(
            hitPos.z
        );

    uint packedIndexBase =
        localLightGridPackedIndexBase();


    for (int cellZ = minCellZ; cellZ <= maxCellZ; ++ cellZ)
    {
        for (int cellY = minCellY; cellY <= maxCellY; ++ cellY)
        {
            for (int cellX = minCellX; cellX <= maxCellX; ++ cellX)
            {
                uint firstLightIndex =
                    0u;

                uint cellLightCount =
                    0u;


                if (
                    ! localLightGridLookupCell(
                        centerCell +
                        ivec3(
                            cellX,
                            cellY,
                            cellZ
                        ),
                        firstLightIndex,
                        cellLightCount
                    )
                )
                {
                    continue;
                }


                for (
                    uint candidateOffset = 0u;
                    candidateOffset < cellLightCount;
                    ++ candidateOffset
                )
                {
                    uint lightIndex =
                        localLightGridBuffer.words[
                            packedIndexBase +
                            firstLightIndex +
                            candidateOffset
                        ];


                    if (lightIndex >= lightCount)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }


                    LocalLightGPU candidateLight =
                        localLightBuffer.lights[
                            lightIndex
                        ];

                    vec4 worldPosEmission =
                        candidateLight.worldPosEmission;


                    emitterRadiance =
                        max(
                            emitterRadiance,
                            evaluateLocalEmitterCandidateRadiance(
                                hitPos,
                                - V,
                                hitAlbedo,
                                candidateLight
                            )
                        );


                    float score =
                        localLightApproxScore(
                            hitPos,
                            N,
                            worldPosEmission
                        );


                    if (score <= 0.0)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }


                    if (score > selectedScore[0])
                    {
                        selectedScore[0] =
                            score;

                        selectedIndex[0] =
                            lightIndex;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }




    vec3 F0 =
        decodeBaseF0(
            hitF0MetalCode,
            hitAlbedo
        );


    float metallic =
        decodeMetallicFromCode(
            hitF0MetalCode
        );


    float roughness =
        clamp(
            hitRoughness,
            0.04,
            1.0
        );


    vec3 accumulatedLocalLight =
        vec3(0.0);








for (
    int slot = 0;
    slot < activeLightCount;
    ++ slot
)
{
    if (selectedScore[slot] <= 0.0)
    {
        break;
    }


LocalLightGPU selectedLight =
    localLightBuffer
        .lights[
            selectedIndex[slot]
        ];


vec4 worldPosEmission =
    selectedLight
        .worldPosEmission;


uint typeCode =
    selectedLight
        .meta.x;


float emissionLevel =
            clamp(
                worldPosEmission.w,
                1.0,
                15.0
            );


        vec3 lightCenter =
            worldPosEmission.xyz +
            vec3(0.5);


        vec3 centerToLight =
            lightCenter -
            hitPos;


        float centerDistanceSquared =
            dot(
                centerToLight,
                centerToLight
            );


        if (centerDistanceSquared <= 1.0e-6)
        {
            continue;
        }


        float centerDistance =
            sqrt(
                centerDistanceSquared
            );


        float centerRangeFade =
            localLightRangeFade(
                centerDistance,
                emissionLevel
            );


        if (centerRangeFade <= 0.0)
        {
            continue;
        }


        float emission01 =
            emissionLevel /
            15.0;


        vec3 localLightColor =
            decodeLocalLightColor(
                typeCode
            );


        bool pointLikeEmitter =
            isPointLikeLocalLightType(
                typeCode
            );


        vec3 integratedAreaLight =
            vec3(0.0);




















for (
    int areaSample = 0;
    areaSample <
        areaSampleCount;
    ++ areaSample
)
        {
            vec3 emitterSurfaceNormal =
                vec3(0.0);


            float faceSelectionProbability =
                1.0;


            vec3 sampleLightPos =
                vec3(0.0);


            if (pointLikeEmitter)
            {
                sampleLightPos =
                    sampleLocalLightPointSphereSurface(
                        worldPosEmission.xyz,
                        hitPos,
                        slot,
                        areaSample
                    );
            }
            else
            {
                sampleLightPos =
                    sampleLocalLightCubeSurface(
                        worldPosEmission.xyz,
                        hitPos,
                        slot,
                        areaSample,
                        emitterSurfaceNormal,
                        faceSelectionProbability
                    );
            }


            vec3 sampleToLight =
                sampleLightPos -
                hitPos;


            float sampleDistanceSquared =
                dot(
                    sampleToLight,
                    sampleToLight
                );


            if (sampleDistanceSquared <= 1.0e-8)
            {
                continue;
            }


            float sampleDistance =
                sqrt(
                    sampleDistanceSquared
                );


            float sampleRangeFade =
                localLightRangeFade(
                    sampleDistance,
                    emissionLevel
                );


            if (sampleRangeFade <= 0.0)
            {
                continue;
            }


            vec3 sampleDirection =
                sampleToLight /
                sampleDistance;


            float geometricNdotL =
                max(
                    dot(
                        N,
                        sampleDirection
                    ),
                    0.0
                );


            if (geometricNdotL <= 0.0)
            {
                continue;
            }


            float sourceAngularWeight =
                LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_PROJECTED_AREA;






            if (! pointLikeEmitter)
            {
                float emitterFacing =
                    max(
                        dot(
                            emitterSurfaceNormal,
                            - sampleDirection
                        ),
                        0.0
                    );


                if (emitterFacing <= 0.0)
                {
                    continue;
                }


                sourceAngularWeight =
                    emitterFacing /
                    max(
                        faceSelectionProbability,
                        1.0e-4
                    );
            }


            float shadowMaxDistance =
                sampleDistance -
                LOCAL_LIGHT_CUBE_RAY_END_EPSILON;


            bool sampleVisible =
                true;


            vec3 sampleTransmittance =
                vec3(1.0);


            if (shadowMaxDistance > 0.03)
            {
                hit.hitDistance =
                    1.0;

                hit.albedo =
                    vec3(1.0);

                hit.parallaxRayTravel =
                    0.0;

                hit.state = makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_GLASS_VISIBILITY);



















                {
                    traceRayEXT(
                        topLevelAS,

                        gl_RayFlagsTerminateOnFirstHitEXT |
                        gl_RayFlagsSkipClosestHitShaderEXT,

                        0xff,

                        0,
                        0,
                        0,

                        hitPos +
                            N * 0.035,

                        0.02,

                        sampleDirection,

                        shadowMaxDistance,

                        0
                    );
                }


                sampleVisible =
                    hit.hitDistance <
                    0.0;


                if (sampleVisible)
                {
                    sampleTransmittance =
                        clamp(
                            hit.albedo,
                            vec3(0.0),
                            vec3(1.0)
                        );
                }
            }


            if (! sampleVisible)
            {
                continue;
            }




            float softDiffuseNdotL =
                geometricNdotL;


            float localSpecularRoughness =
                max(
                    roughness,
                    LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_MIN_SPECULAR_ROUGHNESS
                );


            vec3 H =
                normalize(
                    V +
                    sampleDirection
                );


            float VdotH =
                max(
                    dot(
                        V,
                        H
                    ),
                    0.0
                );


            vec3 F =
                fresnelSchlick(
                    VdotH,
                    F0
                );


            float D =
                distributionGGX(
                    N,
                    H,
                    localSpecularRoughness
                );


            float G =
                geometrySmith(
                    N,
                    V,
                    sampleDirection,
                    localSpecularRoughness
                );


            vec3 specularBRDF =
                (
                    D *
                    G *
                    F
                ) /
                max(
                    4.0 *
                    NdotV *
                    geometricNdotL,
                    1.0e-4
                );


            specularBRDF *=
                multiscatterEnergyCompensation(
                    F0,
                    localSpecularRoughness
                );


            vec3 kD =
                (
                    vec3(1.0) -
                    F
                ) *
                (
                    1.0 -
                    metallic
                );


            vec3 diffuseTerm =
                kD *
                hitAlbedo *
                softDiffuseNdotL *
                LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_DIFFUSE_SCALE;


            vec3 specularTerm =
                specularBRDF *
                geometricNdotL *
                LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_SPECULAR_SCALE;


float distanceAttenuation =
    localLightDistanceAttenuation(
        sampleDistance
    );




            float radianceScale =
    3.2 *
    LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_RADIANCE_SCALE * localLightIntensity() *
    emission01 *
    distanceAttenuation *
    sampleRangeFade *
    sourceAngularWeight;


            vec3 sampleRadiance =
                localLightColor *
                radianceScale;


            integratedAreaLight +=
                sampleRadiance *
                sampleTransmittance *
                (
                    diffuseTerm +
                    specularTerm
                );
        }


        accumulatedLocalLight +=
integratedAreaLight /
float(
    areaSampleCount
);
    }


    return
        accumulatedLocalLight;
}













vec3 traceGlassSceneReflectionOneRay(
    vec3 glassSurfacePosition,
    vec3 glassNormal,
    vec3 viewDir,
    vec3 sunDirection
)
{
    vec3 N = normalize(glassNormal);
    vec3 V = normalize(viewDir);
    float NdotV = max(dot(N, V), 0.0);
    if (NdotV <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    vec3 reflectionDirection =
        reflect(- V, N);

    hit.hitDistance = 1.0;
    hit.state = makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_FULL);




















    {
        traceRayEXT(
            topLevelAS,
            gl_RayFlagsNoneEXT,
            0xff,
            0,
            0,
            0,
            glassSurfacePosition + N * 0.035,
            0.02,
            reflectionDirection,
            10000.0,
            0
        );

        resolveDeferredHit(glassSurfacePosition + N * 0.035, reflectionDirection);

    }






    rrGlassReflectionHitDistance =
        payload.hitDistance >= 0.0
            ? payload.hitDistance
            : 65504.0;

    vec3 reflectedSceneRadiance =
        vec3(0.0);

    if (payload.hitDistance < 0.0)
    {


        reflectedSceneRadiance =
            payload.albedo;
    }
    else if (getLegacyMaterialId(payload.state) <= 0.5)
    {
        vec3 reflectionHitPos =
            glassSurfacePosition +
            N * 0.035 +
            reflectionDirection *
            payload.hitDistance;

        vec3 reflectionHitNormal =
            payload.normal;

        vec3 reflectionHitAlbedo =
            payload.albedo;






        glassReflectionReceiverValid = true;
        glassReflectionReceiverNormal =
            normalize(reflectionHitNormal);
        glassReflectionReceiverAlbedo =
            clamp(reflectionHitAlbedo, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));
        glassReflectionReceiverRoughness =
            clamp(payload.roughness, 0.0, 1.0);


        vec3 reflectionHitEmission =
            payload.emission +




            (1 != 0
                ? evaluateVLIEmitterRadianceAtHit(
                    reflectionHitPos,
                    reflectionHitNormal,
                    reflectionHitAlbedo
                )
                : vec3(0.0));

        reflectedSceneRadiance =
            evaluateOpaqueSurfaceRadiance(
                reflectionHitPos,
                reflectionHitNormal,
                - reflectionDirection,
                reflectionHitAlbedo,
                payload.roughness,
                payload.f0Metal,
                reflectionHitEmission,
                payload.pomSelfShadow,
                sunDirection
            ) +
            (1 != 0
                ? evaluateVPTExactFaceDirectLighting(
                    reflectionHitPos,
                    reflectionHitNormal,
                    reflectionHitAlbedo
                )
                : vec3(0.0)) +
            reflectionHitAlbedo *
            clamp(cam.cameraParams.w, 0.0, 1.0);

    }



    return reflectedSceneRadiance;
}


















vec3 evaluateSecondaryDiffuseLocalLights(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 viewDir,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    float hitF0Metal
)
{
    uint lightCount =
        localLightBuffer.header.x;


    if (lightCount == 0u)
    {
        return
            vec3(0.0);
    }


    vec3 N =
        normalize(
            hitNormal
        );


    vec3 V =
        normalize(
            viewDir
        );


    float NdotV =
        max(
            dot(
                N,
                V
            ),
            0.0
        );


    if (NdotV <= 0.0)
    {
        return
            vec3(0.0);
    }







    uint selectedIndex[
        MAX_SECONDARY_LOCAL_LIGHTS
    ];


    float selectedScore[
        MAX_SECONDARY_LOCAL_LIGHTS
    ];


    for (
        int slot = 0;
        slot < MAX_SECONDARY_LOCAL_LIGHTS;
        ++ slot
    )
    {
        selectedIndex[slot] =
            0u;

        selectedScore[slot] =
            - 1.0;
    }






    vec3 emitterProbePos =
        hitPos -
        N * 0.02;


    for (
        uint lightIndex = 0u;
        lightIndex < lightCount;
        ++ lightIndex
    )
    {
        LocalLightGPU candidateLight =
            localLightBuffer
                .lights[lightIndex];


        vec4 candidateWorldPosEmission =
            candidateLight
                .worldPosEmission;


        vec3 emitterBlockMin =
            candidateWorldPosEmission.xyz -
            vec3(0.001);


        vec3 emitterBlockMax =
            candidateWorldPosEmission.xyz +
            vec3(1.001);


        bool hitThisEmitter =
            all(
                greaterThanEqual(
                    emitterProbePos,
                    emitterBlockMin
                )
            ) &&
            all(
                lessThanEqual(
                    emitterProbePos,
                    emitterBlockMax
                )
            );


        if (hitThisEmitter)
        {
            continue;
        }


        float score =
            localLightApproxScore(
                hitPos,
                N,
                candidateWorldPosEmission
            );


        if (score <= 0.0)
        {
            continue;
        }


        for (
            int slot = 0;
            slot < MAX_SECONDARY_LOCAL_LIGHTS;
            ++ slot
        )
        {
            if (score <= selectedScore[slot])
            {
                continue;
            }


            for (
                int shift =
                    MAX_SECONDARY_LOCAL_LIGHTS - 1;
                shift > slot;
                -- shift
            )
            {
                selectedScore[shift] =
                    selectedScore[
                        shift - 1
                    ];

                selectedIndex[shift] =
                    selectedIndex[
                        shift - 1
                    ];
            }


            selectedScore[slot] =
                score;

            selectedIndex[slot] =
                lightIndex;

            break;
        }
    }


    int f0MetalCode =
        decodeF0MetalCode(
            hitF0Metal
        );


    float metallic =
        decodeMetallicFromCode(
            f0MetalCode
        );




    if (metallic >= 0.999)
    {
        return
            vec3(0.0);
    }


    vec3 F0 =
        decodeBaseF0(
            f0MetalCode,
            hitAlbedo
        );


    vec3 accumulatedSecondaryLocal =
        vec3(0.0);


    for (
        int slot = 0;
        slot < MAX_SECONDARY_LOCAL_LIGHTS;
        ++ slot
    )
    {
        if (selectedScore[slot] <= 0.0)
        {
            continue;
        }


        LocalLightGPU selectedLight =
            localLightBuffer
                .lights[
                    selectedIndex[slot]
                ];


        vec4 worldPosEmission =
            selectedLight
                .worldPosEmission;


        uint typeCode =
            selectedLight.meta.x;


        float emissionLevel =
            clamp(
                worldPosEmission.w,
                1.0,
                15.0
            );


        vec3 emitterSurfaceNormal =
            vec3(0.0);


        float faceSelectionProbability =
            1.0;


        bool pointLikeEmitter =
            isPointLikeLocalLightType(
                typeCode
            );



        vec3 sampleLightPos =
            vec3(0.0);


        if (pointLikeEmitter)
        {
            sampleLightPos =
                sampleLocalLightPointSphereSurface(
                    worldPosEmission.xyz,
                    hitPos,
                    slot,
                    0
                );
        }
        else
        {
            sampleLightPos =
                sampleLocalLightCubeSurface(
                    worldPosEmission.xyz,
                    hitPos,
                    slot,
                    0,
                    emitterSurfaceNormal,
                    faceSelectionProbability
                );
        }


        vec3 sampleToLight =
            sampleLightPos -
            hitPos;


        float sampleDistanceSquared =
            dot(
                sampleToLight,
                sampleToLight
            );


        if (sampleDistanceSquared <= 1.0e-8)
        {
            continue;
        }


        float sampleDistance =
            sqrt(
                sampleDistanceSquared
            );


        float sampleRangeFade =
            localLightRangeFade(
                sampleDistance,
                emissionLevel
            );


        if (sampleRangeFade <= 0.0)
        {
            continue;
        }


        vec3 L =
            sampleToLight /
            sampleDistance;


        float geometricNdotL =
            max(
                dot(
                    N,
                    L
                ),
                0.0
            );


        if (geometricNdotL <= 0.0)
        {
            continue;
        }


        float sourceAngularWeight =
            LOCAL_LIGHT_POINT_SPHERE_PROJECTED_AREA;


        if (! pointLikeEmitter)
        {
            float emitterFacing =
                max(
                    dot(
                        emitterSurfaceNormal,
                        - L
                    ),
                    0.0
                );


            if (emitterFacing <= 0.0)
            {
                continue;
            }


            sourceAngularWeight =
                emitterFacing /
                max(
                    faceSelectionProbability,
                    1.0e-4
                );
        }






        float shadowMaxDistance =
            sampleDistance -
            LOCAL_LIGHT_CUBE_RAY_END_EPSILON;


        bool visible =
            true;


        vec3 localShadowTransmittance =
            vec3(1.0);


        if (shadowMaxDistance > 0.03)
        {
            hit.hitDistance =
                1.0;

            hit.albedo =
                vec3(1.0);

            hit.parallaxRayTravel =
                0.0;

            hit.state = makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_GLASS_VISIBILITY);


























            {
                traceRayEXT(
                    topLevelAS,

                    gl_RayFlagsTerminateOnFirstHitEXT |
                    gl_RayFlagsSkipClosestHitShaderEXT,

                    0xff,

                    0,
                    0,
                    0,

                    hitPos +
                        N * 0.035,

                    0.02,

                    L,

                    shadowMaxDistance,

                    0
                );
            }


            visible =
                hit.hitDistance <
                0.0;


            if (visible)
            {
                localShadowTransmittance =
                    clamp(
                        hit.albedo,
                        vec3(0.0),
                        vec3(1.0)
                    );
            }
        }


        if (! visible)
        {
            continue;
        }










        vec3 H =
            normalize(
                V + L
            );


        float VdotH =
            max(
                dot(
                    V,
                    H
                ),
                0.0
            );


        vec3 F =
            fresnelSchlick(
                VdotH,
                F0
            );


        vec3 kD =
            (
                vec3(1.0) -
                F
            ) *
            (
                1.0 -
                metallic
            );


        float softDiffuseNdotL =
            geometricNdotL;


        vec3 diffuseTerm =
            kD *
            hitAlbedo *
            softDiffuseNdotL *
            LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_DIFFUSE_SCALE;


        float emission01 =
            emissionLevel /
            15.0;


float distanceAttenuation =
    localLightDistanceAttenuation(
        sampleDistance
    );

float radianceScale =
    3.2 *
    LOCAL_LIGHT_L7_RADIANCE_SCALE * localLightIntensity() *
    emission01 *
    distanceAttenuation *
    sampleRangeFade *
    sourceAngularWeight;


        vec3 localRadiance =
            decodeLocalLightColor(
                typeCode
            ) *
            radianceScale;


        accumulatedSecondaryLocal +=
            localRadiance *
            localShadowTransmittance *
            diffuseTerm;
    }


    return
        accumulatedSecondaryLocal;
}














vec3 traceTransparentLowCostGI(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 hitAlbedo
)
{
    vec3 N =
        normalize(
            hitNormal
        );


    vec3 giDirection =
        sampleHemisphereCosine(
            N,
            seed
        );


    hit.hitDistance =
        1.0;

    hit.state = makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_FULL);





















    {
        traceRayEXT(
            topLevelAS,
            gl_RayFlagsNoneEXT,
            0xff,
            0,
            0,
            0,
            hitPos +
                N * 0.035,
            0.02,
            giDirection,
            10000.0,
            0
        );

        resolveDeferredHit(hitPos +
                N * 0.035, giDirection);

    }


    vec3 incomingRadiance =
        vec3(0.0);


    if (payload.hitDistance < 0.0)
    {
        incomingRadiance =
            payload.albedo;
    }
    else
    {
        float secondaryMaterialId =
            getLegacyMaterialId(payload.state);





        vec3 ambientRadiance =
            vec3(clamp(
                cam.cameraParams.w,
                0.0,
                1.0
            )) + endGalaxyAmbientLift();


        if (secondaryMaterialId <= 0.5)
        {
            incomingRadiance =
                payload.emission +
                payload.albedo *
                ambientRadiance;
        }
        else
        {



            incomingRadiance =
                payload.albedo *
                ambientRadiance;
        }
    }




    return
        hitAlbedo *
        incomingRadiance *
        1.26;
}


vec3 evaluateOpaqueSurfaceRadiance(
    vec3 hitPos,
    vec3 hitNormal,
    vec3 viewDir,
    vec3 hitAlbedo,
    float hitRoughness,
    float hitF0Metal,
    vec3 hitEmission,
    float pomSelfShadow,
    vec3 sunDirection
)
{
    return
        hitEmission +
        evaluateDirectSunLighting(
            hitPos,
            hitNormal,
            viewDir,
            hitAlbedo,
            hitRoughness,
            hitF0Metal,
            pomSelfShadow,
            sunDirection
        );
}


















struct WaterTransmissionInfo {
    float waterPathLength;
    vec3 receiverWorldPos;
    vec3 receiverNormal;
    vec3 receiverViewDirection;
    vec3 receiverAlbedo;
    float receiverRoughness;
    float receiverF0Metal;
    float reachedUnderwaterReceiver;





    float waterSunVisibility;





    vec3 receiverSelfEmissionRadiance;
};




void applyTransparentReceiverRRGuides(
    WaterTransmissionInfo receiver,
    inout vec3 diffuseAlbedo,
    inout vec3 specularAlbedo,
    inout vec4 normalRoughness,
    inout float specularHitDistance
)
{
    int f0MetalCode =
        decodeF0MetalCode(
            receiver.receiverF0Metal
        );

    float metallic =
        decodeMetallicFromCode(
            f0MetalCode
        );

    vec3 N =
        normalize(
            receiver.receiverNormal
        );

    vec3 V =
        normalize(
            receiver.receiverViewDirection
        );

    float NdotV =
        abs(
            dot(N, V)
        );

    float roughness =
        clamp(
            receiver.receiverRoughness,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );

    diffuseAlbedo =
        receiver.receiverAlbedo *
        (1.0 - metallic);

    specularAlbedo =
        rrEnvBRDFApprox2(
            decodeBaseF0(
                f0MetalCode,
                receiver.receiverAlbedo
            ),
            roughness * roughness,
            NdotV
        );

    normalRoughness =
        vec4(N, roughness);




    specularHitDistance =
        65504.0;
}























































































































































































float rrTransmittedGuideRoughness(float receiverRoughness)
{



    return clamp(
        max(
            max(0.2, 0.2),
            clamp(receiverRoughness, 0.0, 1.0)
        ) +
                                0.0,
        0.0,
        1.0
    );
}



















const float WATER_CAUSTIC_IOR = 1.33;
const float WATER_CAUSTIC_REFERENCE_DEPTH = 1.0;


const float WATER_CAUSTIC_CELL = 0.125;





const float WATER_CAUSTIC_RIPPLE_LENS = 0.35;


















const float WATER_CAUSTIC_MAX_DISTANCE = 96.0;





vec3 causticWaveNormal(vec2 sourceXZ, bool includeInteraction)
{
    float cameraDistance;

    vec3 surfacePos = vec3(sourceXZ.x, cam.waterSimSurfaceAndOrigin.x, sourceXZ.y);
    float noV = abs(normalize(surfacePos - cam.viewInverse[3].xyz).y);
    float horizonFade = 1.0 - exp(- 4.0 * noV);


    float projScale = float(gl_LaunchSizeEXT.y)
        / (2.0 * max(tan(cam.cameraParams.z * 0.5), 0.05));
    float causticRayDistance =
        max(length(surfacePos - cam.viewInverse[3].xyz), 1.0e-3);
    float waveLod =
        waterFftSlopeLodFromProj(surfacePos, causticRayDistance, projScale);
    vec2 naturalSlope = waterNaturalWaveGradient(sourceXZ, cameraDistance, horizonFade, waveLod);



    vec2 interactionSlope = vec2(0.0);
    if (includeInteraction)
    {
        float stateAndBorderMask;
        interactionSlope =
            waterInteractionSlope(sourceXZ, stateAndBorderMask) *
            stateAndBorderMask;
    }
    vec2 gradient = waterBlendWaveSlopes(
        naturalSlope, interactionSlope, waterWaveBlendMode());

    return normalize(vec3(- gradient.x, 1.0, - gradient.y));
}

vec2 projectCausticWavefront(
    vec2 sourceXZ,
    vec3 sunDirection,
    bool includeInteraction
)
{
    vec3 normal = causticWaveNormal(sourceXZ, includeInteraction);
    vec3 incoming = refract(- sunDirection, normal, 1.0 / WATER_CAUSTIC_IOR);
    if (incoming.y >= - 1.0e-4)
    {
        return sourceXZ;
    }
    return sourceXZ +
        incoming.xz * (- WATER_CAUSTIC_REFERENCE_DEPTH / incoming.y);
}





vec2 invertCausticWavefrontProjection(
    vec2 receiverPlaneXZ,
    vec3 sunDirection,
    bool includeInteraction
)
{
    vec2 sourceXZ = receiverPlaneXZ;
    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < 2; ++ iteration)
    {
        vec2 projected =
            projectCausticWavefront(sourceXZ, sunDirection, includeInteraction);
        sourceXZ -= 0.85 * (projected - receiverPlaneXZ);
    }
    return sourceXZ;
}




float evaluateCausticWavefrontDensity(
    vec2 sourceXZ,
    vec3 sunDirection,
    bool includeInteraction
)
{
    vec2 qXP = projectCausticWavefront(
        sourceXZ + vec2(WATER_CAUSTIC_CELL, 0.0),
        sunDirection,
        includeInteraction
    );
    vec2 qXM = projectCausticWavefront(
        sourceXZ - vec2(WATER_CAUSTIC_CELL, 0.0),
        sunDirection,
        includeInteraction
    );
    vec2 qZP = projectCausticWavefront(
        sourceXZ + vec2(0.0, WATER_CAUSTIC_CELL),
        sunDirection,
        includeInteraction
    );
    vec2 qZM = projectCausticWavefront(
        sourceXZ - vec2(0.0, WATER_CAUSTIC_CELL),
        sunDirection,
        includeInteraction
    );

    vec2 dqdx = (qXP - qXM) / (2.0 * WATER_CAUSTIC_CELL);
    vec2 dqdz = (qZP - qZM) / (2.0 * WATER_CAUSTIC_CELL);
    float newArea = abs(dqdx.x * dqdz.y - dqdx.y * dqdz.x);
    return clamp(1.0 / max(newArea, 0.10), 0.20, 6.0);
}






vec3 evaluateWaterCrestBacklit(
    vec3 waterSurfacePos,
    vec3 surfaceNormal,
    vec3 rayDirection,
    vec3 sunDirectionLit,
    const in WaterTransmissionInfo receiver
)
{
    float strength = clamp(cam.waterParams.x, 0.0, 2.0);
    if (strength <= 0.001 || sunDirectionLit.y <= 0.02)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    float backlitFace = clamp(dot(surfaceNormal, - sunDirectionLit), 0.0, 1.0);
    if (backlitFace <= 0.001)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }



    float thickness = receiver.reachedUnderwaterReceiver > 0.5
        ? clamp(receiver.waterPathLength, 0.05, 6.0)
        : 0.6;


    float cosViewSun = dot(rayDirection, sunDirectionLit);
    float phaseBacklit = pow(clamp(cosViewSun, 0.0, 1.0), 700.0);

    vec3 transmitted = exp(- waterAbsorptionColor() * thickness * 1.5);
    vec3 sunRadiance =
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightColor() *
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale();

    return sunRadiance * backlitFace * phaseBacklit * transmitted *
        strength * 0.35;
    }



























    vec3 evaluateWaterThinLayerSSS(
    vec3 surfaceNormal,
    vec3 rayDirection,
    vec3 sunDirectionLit,
    const in WaterTransmissionInfo receiver
    )
    {
    float strength = clamp(cam.waterParams.x, 0.0, 2.0);
    if (strength <= 0.001 || sunDirectionLit.y <= 0.02)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }


    float nDotL = clamp(dot(surfaceNormal, sunDirectionLit), 0.0, 1.0);
    float gsss = 1.0 - nDotL;


    float thickness = receiver.reachedUnderwaterReceiver > 0.5
        ? clamp(receiver.waterPathLength, 0.05, 6.0)
        : 0.6;

    const float kSssPathScale = 1.5;


    const float kSssNonlinear = clamp(cam.waterSssParams.x, 0.0, 1.0);
    const float kSssBoost = clamp(cam.waterSssParams.y, 0.0, 4.0);
    const float kSssPhaseG = clamp(cam.waterSssParams.z, 0.0, 0.95);
    const float kSssStrength = clamp(cam.waterSssParams.w, 0.0, 2.0);

    vec3 scatter = waterScatterColor();
    vec3 extinction = waterAbsorptionColor() + scatter;
    float opticalDepth =
        dot(extinction, vec3(1.0 / 3.0)) * thickness * kSssPathScale;

    float pathLinear = thickness * kSssPathScale;
    float pathNonlinear = thickness * thickness * kSssPathScale *
        (1.0 + dot(scatter, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722)));
    float sssPath = mix(pathLinear, pathNonlinear,
        clamp(opticalDepth * kSssNonlinear, 0.0, 1.0));


    float cosTheta = clamp(dot(- rayDirection, sunDirectionLit), - 1.0, 1.0);
    float phaseSss = underwaterHGPhase(cosTheta, kSssPhaseG);

    vec3 transmittance = exp(- extinction * sssPath);
    vec3 sunRadiance =
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightColor() *
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale();

    return sunRadiance * scatter * transmittance *
        (phaseSss * gsss * kSssBoost * strength * kSssStrength * 1.4);
    }




float waterCausticMapLodFromProj(
    vec3 receiverPos,
    float rayDistance,
    float projScale)
{
    if (projScale <= 0.0)
    {
        return 0.0;
    }
    const float tilePeriod = max(cam.waterFftParams.x, 1.0e-3);
    const float texelWorld = tilePeriod / 1024.0;
    const float lodBias = - 0.75;
    const float maxLod = 6.0;
    vec3 toCam = receiverPos - cam.viewInverse[3].xyz;
    float dist = max(length(toCam), 1.0e-3);
    float noV = max(abs(toCam.y) / dist, 0.04);
    float worldPerPixel = max(rayDistance, 1.0e-3) / (projScale * noV);

    return clamp(
        log2(max(worldPerPixel / texelWorld, 1.0e-4)) + lodBias,
        0.0,
        maxLod);
}

vec3 evaluatePrimaryWaterReceiverCaustics(
    vec3 waterSurfacePosition,
    WaterTransmissionInfo receiver,
    vec3 sunDirection
)
{
    const float WATER_IOR = 1.33;
    const vec3 WATER_ABSORPTION = waterAbsorptionColor();
    const vec3 WATER_GLASS_TINT = vec3(0.20, 0.75, 0.78);
    const float WATER_CAUSTIC_STRENGTH = waterStyleCausticStrength();

    if (
        receiver.reachedUnderwaterReceiver <= 0.5 ||
        receiver.waterPathLength <= 0.0 ||
        sunDirection.y <= 0.0
    )
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    vec3 receiverN = normalize(receiver.receiverNormal);
    vec3 receiverV = normalize(receiver.receiverViewDirection);
    vec3 L = normalize(sunDirection);
    float waterDepth =
        max(waterSurfacePosition.y - receiver.receiverWorldPos.y, 0.0);

    if (waterDepth < 0.08 || cam.waterSimSurfaceAndOrigin.w <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }





    vec3 incomingWaterSun = refract(- L, vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0), 1.0 / WATER_IOR);
    if (dot(incomingWaterSun, incomingWaterSun) < 1.0e-6)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    vec3 receiverToSun = normalize(- incomingWaterSun);
    float NdotL = max(dot(receiverN, receiverToSun), 0.0);
    float NdotV = max(dot(receiverN, receiverV), 0.0);
    if (NdotL <= 0.0 || NdotV <= 0.0 || receiverToSun.y <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    vec3 sunTransmittance = traceSunTransmittanceThroughWater(
        receiver.receiverWorldPos + receiverN * 0.01,
        receiverToSun,
        false
    );
    if (max(sunTransmittance.r, max(sunTransmittance.g, sunTransmittance.b)) <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    vec2 flatProjectedWaterXZ =
        receiver.receiverWorldPos.xz +
        receiverToSun.xz *
        (waterDepth / max(receiverToSun.y, 0.06));




    vec2 causticReceiverPlaneXZ =
        flatProjectedWaterXZ -
        receiverToSun.xz * (1.0 / max(receiverToSun.y, 0.06));





    float projScale = float(gl_LaunchSizeEXT.y) /
        (2.0 * max(tan(cam.cameraParams.z * 0.5), 0.05));
    float receiverRayDistance = max(
        length(receiver.receiverWorldPos - cam.viewInverse[3].xyz),
        1.0e-3);
    float causticMapLod = waterCausticMapLodFromProj(
        receiver.receiverWorldPos,
        receiverRayDistance,
        projScale);







    vec3 naturalDensity3 = vec3(1.0);
    if (cam.waterFftParams.y > 0.0 && cam.waterFftParams2.w > 0.5)
    {
        const float causticTilePeriod = max(cam.waterFftParams.x, 1.0e-3);
        naturalDensity3 = textureLod(
            waterCausticMap,
            flatProjectedWaterXZ / causticTilePeriod,
            causticMapLod).rgb;
    }




    vec2 naturalSourceXZ = invertCausticWavefrontProjection(
        causticReceiverPlaneXZ,
        sunDirection,
        false);
    float interactionElevationMatch = 1.0 - smoothstep(
        0.65,
        1.50,
        abs(waterSurfacePosition.y - cam.waterSimSurfaceAndOrigin.x)
    );
    interactionElevationMatch *= float(cam.waterSimState.y != 0u);
    if (interactionElevationMatch > 0.0)
    {
        float analyticNatural = evaluateCausticWavefrontDensity(
            naturalSourceXZ,
            sunDirection,
            false);
        float interactionDensity = evaluateCausticWavefrontDensity(
            naturalSourceXZ,
            sunDirection,
            true);
        naturalDensity3 += vec3(
            (interactionDensity - analyticNatural) *
            WATER_CAUSTIC_RIPPLE_LENS * interactionElevationMatch);
    }


















    float depthDiffusion = clamp(cam.waterParams.z, 0.0, 1.0);
    if (depthDiffusion > 0.001)
    {
        naturalDensity3 = mix(naturalDensity3, vec3(1.0),
            depthDiffusion * (1.0 - exp(- waterDepth * 0.45)));
    }





    float causticDispersion = clamp(cam.waterParams.y, 0.0, 1.0);
    if (causticDispersion < 0.999)
    {
        float densityLuminance = dot(
            naturalDensity3,
            vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
        naturalDensity3 = mix(
            vec3(densityLuminance),
            naturalDensity3,
            causticDispersion);
    }





    vec3 causticFocus = min(max(naturalDensity3 - 1.0, vec3(0.0)), vec3(4.5));
    causticFocus *= 0.88 + 0.24 * causticFocus;
    if (max(causticFocus.x, max(causticFocus.y, causticFocus.z)) <= 1.0e-5)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    int f0MetalCode = decodeF0MetalCode(receiver.receiverF0Metal);
    float metallic = decodeMetallicFromCode(f0MetalCode);
    float diffuseResponse = evaluateBurleyDiffuseAngular(
        receiverN,
        receiverV,
        receiverToSun,
        clamp(receiver.receiverRoughness, 0.04, 1.0)
    );

    float cameraWaterFade = clamp(receiver.waterPathLength / 16.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    cameraWaterFade *= cameraWaterFade;
    vec3 cameraWaterTransmittance =
        exp(- WATER_ABSORPTION * receiver.waterPathLength);
    cameraWaterTransmittance = mix(
        cameraWaterTransmittance,
        cameraWaterTransmittance * WATER_GLASS_TINT,
        cameraWaterFade
    );
    cameraWaterTransmittance *= mix(1.0, 0.15, cameraWaterFade);

    vec3 sunRadiance =
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightColor() *
        3.2 *
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale() *
        sunTransmittance *
        c3CloudSunTransmittance(waterSurfacePosition, receiverToSun);

    float shallowFade = smoothstep(0.08, 0.55, waterDepth);
    return
        sunRadiance *
        cameraWaterTransmittance *
        receiver.receiverAlbedo *
        (1.0 - metallic) *
        diffuseResponse *
        (WATER_CAUSTIC_STRENGTH * causticFocus * shallowFade);
}



float shoreFoamHash(vec2 p)
{
    p = fract(p * vec2(127.1, 311.7));
    p += dot(p, p + 34.35);
    return fract(p.x * p.y);
}

float shoreFoamNoise(vec2 p)
{
    vec2 cell = floor(p);
    vec2 f = fract(p);
    f = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
    float n00 = shoreFoamHash(cell);
    float n10 = shoreFoamHash(cell + vec2(1.0, 0.0));
    float n01 = shoreFoamHash(cell + vec2(0.0, 1.0));
    float n11 = shoreFoamHash(cell + vec2(1.0, 1.0));
    return mix(
        mix(n00, n10, f.x),
        mix(n01, n11, f.x),
        f.y
    );
}




vec3 evaluateShoreFoam(
    vec3 waterSurfacePosition,
    vec3 surfaceNormal,
    const in WaterTransmissionInfo receiver,
    vec3 sunDirection
)
{
    if (
        receiver.reachedUnderwaterReceiver <= 0.5 ||
        dot(receiver.receiverNormal, vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0)) < 0.5
    )
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    float waterDepth =
        waterSurfacePosition.y - receiver.receiverWorldPos.y;



    waterDepth *= clamp(receiver.receiverViewDirection.y, 0.55, 1.0);
    if (waterDepth > 1.80)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    float foamTime = uintBitsToFloat(cam.padding);
    vec2 worldXZ = waterSurfacePosition.xz;




    float shoreDepth =
        waterDepth +
        (shoreFoamNoise(worldXZ * 0.55 + foamTime * 0.05) - 0.5) * 1.3;

    float n1 = shoreFoamNoise(worldXZ * 1.9 + foamTime * vec2(0.30, - 0.22));
    float n2 = shoreFoamNoise(worldXZ * 4.7 - foamTime * vec2(0.18, 0.34));
    float foamField = n1 * 0.65 + n2 * 0.35;



    float shoreBand = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.08, 1.10, shoreDepth);

    float foamMask = shoreBand * smoothstep(0.24, 0.55, foamField);


    foamMask = max(
        foamMask,
        (1.0 - smoothstep(0.05, 0.30, shoreDepth)) *
            smoothstep(0.15, 0.40, foamField) *
            0.90
    );
    if (foamMask <= 0.001)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    vec3 foamRadiance =
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightColor() *
        (0.45 + 0.62 * max(sunDirection.y, 0.0));

    return foamRadiance * foamMask;
}

vec3 tracePrimaryTransparentRadiance(
    vec3 startOrigin,
    vec3 startDirection,
    bool startInsideWater,
    vec3 sunDirection,
    bool enableWaterReceiverGI,
    float waterGILodDistance,



    vec3 historyKeyPosition,
    out WaterTransmissionInfo transmissionInfo
);








vec3 tracePrimaryWaterReceiverIndirectReworked(
    vec3 receiverPos,
    vec3 receiverNormal,
    vec3 receiverAlbedo,
    float lodDistance,
    vec3 sunDirection

    , bool startInsideWater

);































































































































































































bool glassMirrorPathIsClear(
    vec3 origin,
    vec3 dir,
    float maxDistance
)
{
    hit.hitDistance = 1.0;
    hit.state = makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_CLASSIFY);
    traceRayEXT(
        topLevelAS,
        gl_RayFlagsNoneEXT,
        0xff,
        0,
        0,
        0,
        origin,
        0.02,
        dir,
        maxDistance,
        0
    );

    resolveDeferredHit(origin, dir);


    return payload.hitDistance < 0.0;
}

vec3 tracePrimaryGenericTransparentRadiance(



    vec3 glassSurfacePosition,



    vec3 continuationOrigin,



    vec3 rayDirection,
    vec3 glassNormal,
    vec3 glassTint,
    float glassAlpha,



    float glassRoughness,
    int transparentProfile,


    bool clearGlassAsAir,
    vec3 sunDirection,






    vec3 historyKeyPosition,



    float cameraToGlassDistance,

    out WaterTransmissionInfo transmissionInfo



) {





    if (DBG_LADDER_A) { return vec3(1.0, 0.5, 0.0); }

    transmissionInfo.waterPathLength = 0.0;
    transmissionInfo.receiverWorldPos = vec3(0.0);
    transmissionInfo.receiverNormal = vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);
    transmissionInfo.receiverViewDirection = vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    transmissionInfo.receiverAlbedo = vec3(0.0);
    transmissionInfo.receiverRoughness = 1.0;
    transmissionInfo.receiverF0Metal = 0.0;
    transmissionInfo.reachedUnderwaterReceiver = 0.0;
    transmissionInfo.waterSunVisibility = 1.0;
    transmissionInfo.receiverSelfEmissionRadiance = vec3(0.0);

    vec3 straightDirection =
        normalize(
            rayDirection
        );


















    if (
        transparentProfile >= 1 &&
        glassRoughness > 0.02 &&
        length(glassSurfacePosition - cam.viewInverse[3].xyz) <=
                                              32.0
    )
    {
        uint frostSeed =
            uint(gl_LaunchIDEXT.x) * 1973u ^
            uint(gl_LaunchIDEXT.y) * 9277u ^
            0x9E3779B9u;
        frostSeed = frostSeed ^ (frostSeed >> 16u);
        frostSeed = frostSeed * 0x7FEB352Du;
        frostSeed = frostSeed ^ (frostSeed >> 15u);
        float frostR1 = float(frostSeed & 0xFFFFu) * (1.0 / 65535.0);
        float frostR2 = float( (frostSeed >> 16u) & 0xFFFFu) * (1.0 / 65535.0);
        float frostAngle = sqrt(frostR1) *
            (0.35 * clamp(glassRoughness, 0.0, 1.0));
        float frostPhi = 6.28318530718 * frostR2;
        vec3 frostT = normalize(
            abs(straightDirection.y) < 0.99
                ? cross(straightDirection, vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0))
                : cross(straightDirection, vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0)));
        vec3 frostB = cross(straightDirection, frostT);
        straightDirection = normalize(
            straightDirection * cos(frostAngle) +
            (frostT * cos(frostPhi) + frostB * sin(frostPhi)) *
                sin(frostAngle));
    }




    vec3 startOrigin =
        continuationOrigin +
        straightDirection *
        0.01;


    hit.hitDistance =
        1.0;

    hit.state = makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_PASSTHROUGH);





















    {
        traceRayEXT(
            topLevelAS,
            gl_RayFlagsNoneEXT,
            0xff,
            0,
            0,
            0,
            startOrigin,
            0.01,
            straightDirection,
            10000.0,
            0
        );

        resolveDeferredHit(startOrigin, straightDirection);

    }


    vec3 behindRadiance =
        vec3(0.0);


    if (payload.hitDistance < 0.0)
    {

        behindRadiance =
            payload.albedo;
    }
    else
    {
        float hitDistance =
            payload.hitDistance;




























        float hitParallaxRayTravel =
    max(
        payload.parallaxRayTravel,
        0.0
    );







        vec3 hitNormal =
            normalize(
                payload.normal
            );


        vec3 hitAlbedo =
            payload.albedo;


        float hitMaterialId =
            getLegacyMaterialId(payload.state);


        float hitRoughness =
            payload.roughness;


        float hitF0Metal =
            payload.f0Metal;


        vec3 hitEmission =
            payload.emission;


        float hitPOMSelfShadow =
            clamp(
                payload.pomSelfShadow,
                0.0,
                1.0
            );


vec3 hitPos =
    startOrigin +
    straightDirection *
    (
        hitDistance +
        hitParallaxRayTravel
    );





























vptTransparentSurfaceNormal =
    vptGeometricFaceNormalFromRay(historyKeyPosition, straightDirection);



if (hitMaterialId <= 0.5)
{
    transmissionInfo.receiverWorldPos = hitPos;
    transmissionInfo.receiverNormal = hitNormal;
    transmissionInfo.receiverViewDirection = - straightDirection;
    transmissionInfo.receiverAlbedo = hitAlbedo;
    transmissionInfo.receiverRoughness = hitRoughness;
    transmissionInfo.receiverF0Metal = hitF0Metal;
    transmissionInfo.reachedUnderwaterReceiver = 1.0;
    transmissionInfo.waterSunVisibility = 1.0;



    vec3 unifiedHitEmission =
        hitEmission +
        evaluateVLIEmitterRadianceAtHit(
            hitPos,
            hitNormal,
            hitAlbedo
        );



    vec3 ambientRadiance =
                vec3(clamp(
                    cam.cameraParams.w,
                    0.0,
                    1.0
                )) + endGalaxyAmbientLift();


            vec3 directAndEmission =
                evaluateOpaqueSurfaceRadiance(
                    hitPos,
                    hitNormal,
                    - straightDirection,
                    hitAlbedo,
                    hitRoughness,
                    hitF0Metal,
                    unifiedHitEmission,
                    hitPOMSelfShadow,
                    sunDirection
                );













if (DBG_LADDER_B) { return vec3(0.0, 1.0, 1.0); }






















const bool DBG_KILL_BEHIND_SUN = false;















const bool DBG_KILL_BEHIND_GI = (0 != 0);


















vec3 localEntityIrradiance =
    evaluateTransparentReceiverDirectLighting(

        historyKeyPosition,


        vptGeometricFaceNormalFromRay(hitPos, straightDirection),
        hitPos,
        hitNormal,
        hitAlbedo,
        0u
    );







            vec3 ambientTerm = hitAlbedo * ambientRadiance;
            if (DBG_KILL_BEHIND_SUN) { directAndEmission = vec3(0.0); }

































            if (DBG_KILL_BEHIND_AMBIENT) { ambientTerm = vec3(0.0); }
            if (DBG_KILL_BEHIND_LOCAL) { localEntityIrradiance = vec3(0.0); }
            if (DBG_KILL_BEHIND_DIRECT) { directAndEmission = vec3(0.0); }

            behindRadiance =
                directAndEmission +
                localEntityIrradiance +
                ambientTerm;












            if (! DBG_KILL_BEHIND_GI)
            {
            behindRadiance +=
                tracePrimaryWaterReceiverIndirectReworked(
                    hitPos,
                    hitNormal,
                    hitAlbedo,
                    cameraToGlassDistance,
                    sunDirection,
                    false
                );
            }

        }
        else if (
            hitMaterialId > 0.5 &&
            hitMaterialId < 2.5
        )
        {




            bool exitingWater =
                hitMaterialId > 1.5;

            float eta =
                exitingWater
                    ? 1.33
                    : (1.0 / 1.33);

            vec3 refractedDirection =
                refract(
                    straightDirection,
                    hitNormal,
                    eta
                );

            bool totalInternalReflection =
                dot(
                    refractedDirection,
                    refractedDirection
                ) < 1.0e-6;

            if (
                totalInternalReflection
            )
            {
                refractedDirection =
                    reflect(
                        straightDirection,
                        hitNormal
                    );
            }

            WaterTransmissionInfo nestedWaterInfo;
            behindRadiance =
                tracePrimaryTransparentRadiance(
                    totalInternalReflection
                        ? hitPos +
                            hitNormal *
                            0.001
                        : hitPos -
                            hitNormal *
                        0.001,
                    normalize(
                        refractedDirection
                    ),
                    totalInternalReflection
                        ? exitingWater
                        : ! exitingWater,
                    sunDirection,
                    false,
                    0.0,


                    historyKeyPosition,
                    nestedWaterInfo
                );

            transmissionInfo = nestedWaterInfo;
        }
        else if (hitMaterialId >= 2.5)
        {





            WaterTransmissionInfo fallbackWaterInfo;
            behindRadiance =
                tracePrimaryTransparentRadiance(
                    hitPos +
                        straightDirection *
                        0.001,
                    straightDirection,
                    false,
                    sunDirection,
                    false,
                    0.0,

                    historyKeyPosition,
                    fallbackWaterInfo
                );

            transmissionInfo = fallbackWaterInfo;
        }
        else
        {
            behindRadiance =
                hitAlbedo;
        }
    }


































    if (clearGlassAsAir)
    {

        glassLobeReflectionWeight = vec3(0.0);
        glassLobeTransmissionWeight = vec3(1.0);
        glassLobeWeightsValid = true;






















        const float GLASS_TINT_LIGHT_REFERENCE = 1.0;

        const vec3 TINT_LUMA_WEIGHTS =
            vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722);







        float receiverAlbedoLuma =
            max(
                dot(
                    clamp(
                        transmissionInfo.receiverAlbedo,
                        vec3(0.0),
                        vec3(1.0)
                    ),
                    TINT_LUMA_WEIGHTS
                ),
                0.02
            );

        float receiverLightLuma =
            dot(behindRadiance, TINT_LUMA_WEIGHTS) /
            receiverAlbedoLuma;

        float litGlassTintScale =
            clamp(
                receiverLightLuma / GLASS_TINT_LIGHT_REFERENCE,
                0.0,
                1.0
            );

        return mix(
            behindRadiance,
            clamp(glassTint, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0)) *
                litGlassTintScale,
            clamp(glassAlpha, 0.0, 1.0)
        );
    }









    vec3 blockTransmission =
        computeGlassBlockTransmission(
            glassTint,
            glassAlpha,
            transparentProfile
        );





    float transmissionWeight =
        getTransparentProfileTransmission(transparentProfile);


    vec3 N =
        normalize(
            glassNormal
        );


    vec3 V =
        normalize(
            - straightDirection
        );


    float NdotV =
        clamp(
            abs(
                dot(
                    N,
                    V
                )
            ),
            0.0,
            1.0
        );


    const vec3 glassF0 = vec3(0.04);






    float viewFresnel =
        fresnelSchlickScalar(
            NdotV,
            0.04
        );


    vec3 sheetReflection;
    vec3 sheetTransmission;
    glassThinSheetFactorsScalarF(
        viewFresnel,
        blockTransmission,
        sheetReflection,
        sheetTransmission
    );






    vec3 glassReflection =
        mix(
            vec3(viewFresnel),
            sheetReflection,
            transmissionWeight
        );
    vec3 glassTransmission =
        sheetTransmission *
