        transmissionWeight;

























    const float glassDyeIor =
        glassIorForProfile(transparentProfile);

    vec3 dyeNormal = normalize(glassNormal);
    if (dot(straightDirection, dyeNormal) > 0.0)
    {
        dyeNormal = - dyeNormal;
    }





    vec3 glassInsideDirection = straightDirection;










    float glassPathBlocks =
        glassSlabThicknessFromFace(glassSurfacePosition, glassNormal) /
        max(- dot(dyeNormal, glassInsideDirection), 1.0e-4);


    float glassDyeExponent = glassPathBlocks - 1.0;



    glassTransmission *=
        glassSegmentTransmittance(
            blockTransmission,
            glassDyeExponent
        );




    if (transparentProfile == 0)
    {
        glassTransmission *=
            glassRunDyeAfterFirstCell(
                glassSurfacePosition,
                glassInsideDirection
            );
    }








    glassLobeReflectionWeight = glassReflection;
    glassLobeTransmissionWeight = glassTransmission;
    glassLobeWeightsValid = true;





    const float GLASS_MIRROR_SUN_ROUGHNESS = 0.04;
    vec3 surfaceSpecular = vec3(0.0);
    vec3 L = normalize(sunDirection);
    float NdotL = max(dot(N, L), 0.0);
    if (NdotL > 0.0 && NdotV > 0.0)
    {
        vec3 H = normalize(V + L);
        float VdotH = max(dot(V, H), 0.0);
        float F = fresnelSchlickScalar(VdotH, 0.04);
        float D = distributionGGX(N, H, GLASS_MIRROR_SUN_ROUGHNESS);
        float G = geometrySmith(
            N, V, L, GLASS_MIRROR_SUN_ROUGHNESS
        );
        float specularBRDF = (D * G * F) /
            max(4.0 * NdotV * NdotL, 1.0e-4);
        vec3 sunTransmittance = traceSunTransmittanceThroughWater(
            glassSurfacePosition + N * 0.035, L, true
        );
        vec3 sunRadiance =
            getTimeDrivenSkyLightColor() *
            3.2 *
            getTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale() *
            sunTransmittance *
            c3CloudSunTransmittance(glassSurfacePosition + N * 0.035, L);
        surfaceSpecular = sunRadiance * specularBRDF * NdotL;
    }







    if (DBG_SHOW_GLASSTINT)
    {
        return clamp(blockTransmission * 4.0, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));
    }








    if (DBG_SHOW_GLASSTINT)
    {



        vec3 tintSource =
            (DBG_GLASSTINT_STAGE == 1u) ? glassTint :
            (DBG_GLASSTINT_STAGE == 2u) ? blockTransmission :
                                          sheetTransmission;
        float peak = max(tintSource.r, max(tintSource.g, tintSource.b));
        if (peak <= 1.0e-5)
        {
            return vec3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
        }
        return clamp(tintSource / peak, vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));
    }

    vec3 transmittedRadiance =
        behindRadiance *
        glassTransmission;
















    vec3 glassMirrorDirection = reflect(- V, N);

























    float glassReflectionPeak =
        max(glassReflection.r, max(glassReflection.g, glassReflection.b));


    bool glassReflectionAnalyticCandidate =
        glassReflectionPeak < 0.09 &&
        glassMirrorDirection.y > 0.05;




    vec3 surfaceSceneReflection = vec3(0.0);



    bool glassReflectionAnalytic = false;


    if (glassReflectionAnalyticCandidate)
    {
        glassReflectionAnalytic =
            glassMirrorPathIsClear(
                glassSurfacePosition,
                glassMirrorDirection,
                                                48.0
            );
    }




    if (glassReflectionAnalytic)
    {
        surfaceSceneReflection =
            textureLod(
                skyScatteringTex,
                directionToEquirect(glassMirrorDirection),
                0.0
            ).rgb *
            glassReflection;
    }
    else
    {
        surfaceSceneReflection =
            traceGlassSceneReflectionOneRay(
                glassSurfacePosition,
                N,
                V,
                sunDirection
            ) *
            glassReflection;
    }






























    return
        transmittedRadiance +
        surfaceSceneReflection +
        surfaceSpecular;

}









vec3 tracePrimaryWaterReceiverIndirectReworked(
    vec3 receiverPos,
    vec3 receiverNormal,
    vec3 receiverAlbedo,
    float lodDistance,
    vec3 sunDirection

    , bool startInsideWater

)
{
    uint lodState =
        uint(gl_LaunchIDEXT.x) * 73856093u ^
        uint(gl_LaunchIDEXT.y) * 19349663u ^
        cam.frameCount * 83492791u ^
        0x57474931u;
    uint lodRandom = pcg_hash(lodState);

    bool runWaterGI = true;
    float lodWeight = 1.0;



    if (lodDistance >= 32.0 * perfL10TierScale() && lodDistance < 64.0 * perfL10TierScale())
    {
        runWaterGI = (lodRandom & 1u) == 0u;
        lodWeight = 2.0;
    }
    else if (lodDistance >= 64.0 * perfL10TierScale() && lodDistance < 128.0 * perfL10TierScale())
    {
        runWaterGI = (lodRandom & 1u) == 0u;
        lodWeight = 2.0;
    }
    else if (lodDistance >= 128.0 * perfL10TierScale() && lodDistance < 160.0 * perfL10TierScale())
    {
        runWaterGI = (lodRandom & 3u) == 0u;
        lodWeight = 4.0;
    }
    else if (lodDistance >= 160.0 * perfL10TierScale())
    {
        runWaterGI = false;
    }
    if (! runWaterGI) return vec3(0.0);

    uint pathState = lodRandom ^ 0x57474942u;
    vec3 currentOrigin = receiverPos + normalize(receiverNormal) * 0.035;
    vec3 currentDirection =
        sampleHemisphereCosine(normalize(receiverNormal), pathState);
    vec3 throughput = receiverAlbedo * 0.7;

    bool insideWater = startInsideWater;



    int waterInterfaceSteps = 0;

    const vec3 WATER_ABSORPTION = waterAbsorptionColor();
    const vec3 WATER_SCATTER_COLOR = waterScatterColor();

const float WATER_EXTINCTION_DISTANCE =
    16.0;
    const float WATER_IOR = 1.33;
    const float WATER_F0 = 0.0200593;



    const int MAX_WATER_INTERFACE_STEPS = 3;

    for (int pathStep = 0; pathStep <= MAX_WATER_INTERFACE_STEPS; ++ pathStep)
    {
        hit.hitDistance = 1.0;
        hit.state = makeTraceState(
            insideWater ? RT_PURPOSE_WATER_MEDIUM : RT_PURPOSE_FULL
        );



















        {
            traceRayEXT(
                topLevelAS, gl_RayFlagsNoneEXT, 0xff, 0, 0, 0,
                currentOrigin, 0.001, currentDirection, 10000.0, 0
            );

            resolveDeferredHit(currentOrigin, currentDirection);

        }

        if (payload.hitDistance < 0.0)
        {
            return throughput * payload.albedo * 1.8 * lodWeight;
        }

        float hitDistance = payload.hitDistance;
        vec3 hitPos = currentOrigin + currentDirection * hitDistance;
        float hitMaterialId = getLegacyMaterialId(payload.state);
        if (insideWater)
if (insideWater)
{
    vec3 extinction =
        exp(
            - WATER_ABSORPTION *
            hitDistance
        );

float scatterAmount = 0.0;

    throughput *= extinction;

    throughput =
        mix(
            throughput,
            WATER_SCATTER_COLOR,
            scatterAmount
        );
}

        if (hitMaterialId <= 0.5)
        {
            vec3 hitNormal = payload.normal;
            vec3 hitAlbedo = payload.albedo;
            vec3 incomingRadiance = evaluateOpaqueSurfaceRadiance(
                hitPos,
                hitNormal,
                - currentDirection,
                hitAlbedo,
                max(payload.roughness, 0.05),
                payload.f0Metal,
                payload.emission +
                    evaluateVLIEmitterRadianceAtHit(hitPos, hitNormal, hitAlbedo),
                payload.pomSelfShadow,
                sunDirection
            );
            uint localState = pathState ^ 0x5747494Cu;
            pcg_hash(localState);


            int bounceF0MetalCode =
                decodeF0MetalCode(payload.f0Metal);
            incomingRadiance +=
                evaluateVPTRISDirectLighting(
                    hitPos, hitNormal, hitAlbedo, vec3(0.0),
                    decodeBaseF0(bounceF0MetalCode, hitAlbedo),
                    decodeMetallicFromCode(bounceF0MetalCode),
                    max(payload.roughness, 0.05),
                    localState);
            return throughput * incomingRadiance * 1.8 * lodWeight;
        }

        if (hitMaterialId >= 2.5)
        {

            if (hitMaterialId < 3.5)
            {
                throughput *= glassSheetTransmission(
                    payload.albedo,
                    payload.normal,
                    currentDirection
                );
            }
            currentOrigin = hitPos + currentDirection * 0.001;
            continue;
        }

        if (++ waterInterfaceSteps > MAX_WATER_INTERFACE_STEPS)
        {
            return vec3(0.0);
        }

        bool exitingWater = hitMaterialId > 1.5;
        vec3 interfaceNormal =
            stabilizeWaterInterfaceNormal(payload.normal);
        float cosTheta = clamp(
            - dot(currentDirection, interfaceNormal), 0.0, 1.0
        );
        float fresnelX = 1.0 - cosTheta;
        float fresnelX2 = fresnelX * fresnelX;
        float fresnel = WATER_F0 +
            (1.0 - WATER_F0) * (fresnelX2 * fresnelX2) * fresnelX;
        vec3 refractedDirection = refract(
            currentDirection,
            interfaceNormal,
            exitingWater ? WATER_IOR : (1.0 / WATER_IOR)
        );

        if (dot(refractedDirection, refractedDirection) < 1.0e-6)
        {
            currentOrigin = hitPos + interfaceNormal * 0.001;
            currentDirection = reflect(currentDirection, interfaceNormal);
            continue;
        }

        throughput *= 1.0 - fresnel;
        insideWater = ! exitingWater;
        currentOrigin = hitPos - interfaceNormal * 0.001;
        currentDirection = refractedDirection;
    }

    return vec3(0.0);
}








vec3 tracePrimaryWaterReceiverIndirect(
    vec3 receiverPos,
    vec3 receiverNormal,
    vec3 receiverAlbedo,
    float lodDistance,
    vec3 sunDirection
)
{
    uint lodState =
        uint(gl_LaunchIDEXT.x) * 73856093u ^
        uint(gl_LaunchIDEXT.y) * 19349663u ^
        cam.frameCount * 83492791u ^
        0x57474931u;
    uint lodRandom = pcg_hash(lodState);

    bool runWaterGI = true;
    float lodWeight = 1.0;



    if (lodDistance >= 32.0 * perfL10TierScale() && lodDistance < 64.0 * perfL10TierScale())
    {
        runWaterGI = (lodRandom & 1u) == 0u;
        lodWeight = 2.0;
    }
    else if (lodDistance >= 64.0 * perfL10TierScale() && lodDistance < 128.0 * perfL10TierScale())
    {
        runWaterGI = (lodRandom & 1u) == 0u;
        lodWeight = 2.0;
    }
    else if (lodDistance >= 128.0 * perfL10TierScale() && lodDistance < 160.0 * perfL10TierScale())
    {
        runWaterGI = (lodRandom & 3u) == 0u;
        lodWeight = 4.0;
    }
    else if (lodDistance >= 160.0 * perfL10TierScale())
    {
        runWaterGI = false;
    }

    if (! runWaterGI)
    {
        return vec3(0.0);
    }

    uint pathState = lodRandom ^ 0x57474942u;
    vec3 N = normalize(receiverNormal);
    vec3 currentOrigin = receiverPos + N * 0.035;
    vec3 currentDirection = sampleHemisphereCosine(N, pathState);
    vec3 pathThroughput = receiverAlbedo * 0.7;
    bool insideWater = true;
    int waterInterfaceSteps = 0;

    const vec3 WATER_ABSORPTION = waterAbsorptionColor();
    const float WATER_IOR = 1.33;
    const float WATER_F0 = 0.0200593;
    const int MAX_WATER_INTERFACE_STEPS = 6;

    for (int pathStep = 0; pathStep <= MAX_WATER_INTERFACE_STEPS; ++ pathStep)
    {
        hit.hitDistance = 1.0;
        hit.state = makeTraceState(
            insideWater ? RT_PURPOSE_WATER_MEDIUM : RT_PURPOSE_FULL
        );




















        {
            traceRayEXT(
                topLevelAS,
                gl_RayFlagsNoneEXT,
                0xff,
                0,
                0,
                0,
                currentOrigin,
                0.001,
                currentDirection,
                10000.0,
                0
            );

            resolveDeferredHit(currentOrigin, currentDirection);

        }

        if (payload.hitDistance < 0.0)
        {
            return pathThroughput * payload.albedo * 1.8 * lodWeight;
        }

        float hitDistance = payload.hitDistance;
        vec3 hitPos = currentOrigin + currentDirection * hitDistance;
        vec3 hitNormal = payload.normal;
        vec3 hitAlbedo = payload.albedo;
        vec3 hitEmission = payload.emission;
        float hitMaterialId = getLegacyMaterialId(payload.state);
        float hitRoughness = max(payload.roughness, 0.05);
        float hitF0Metal = payload.f0Metal;
        float hitPOMSelfShadow = payload.pomSelfShadow;

        if (insideWater)
        {
            pathThroughput *= exp(- WATER_ABSORPTION * hitDistance);
        }

        bool isWaterInterface =
            hitMaterialId > 0.5 &&
            hitMaterialId < 2.5;

        if (! isWaterInterface)
        {


            if (hitMaterialId > 2.5)
            {
                return vec3(0.0);
            }

            vec3 unifiedEmission =
                hitEmission +
                evaluateVLIEmitterRadianceAtHit(
                    hitPos,
                    hitNormal,
                    hitAlbedo
                );

            vec3 directSun =
                evaluateDirectSunLighting(
                    hitPos,
                    hitNormal,
                    - currentDirection,
                    hitAlbedo,
                    hitRoughness,
                    hitF0Metal,
                    hitPOMSelfShadow,
                    sunDirection
                );

            uint vptState = pathState ^ 0x5747494Cu;
            pcg_hash(vptState);

            int pathF0MetalCode =
                decodeF0MetalCode(hitF0Metal);
            vec3 localDirect =
                evaluateVPTRISDirectLighting(
                    hitPos,
                    hitNormal,
                    hitAlbedo,
                    vec3(0.0),
                    decodeBaseF0(pathF0MetalCode, hitAlbedo),
                    decodeMetallicFromCode(pathF0MetalCode),
                    max(hitRoughness, 0.05),
                    vptState
                );

            return pathThroughput *
                (unifiedEmission + directSun + localDirect) *
                1.8 * lodWeight;
        }

        waterInterfaceSteps += 1;
        if (waterInterfaceSteps > MAX_WATER_INTERFACE_STEPS)
        {
            return vec3(0.0);
        }

        bool exitingWater = hitMaterialId > 1.5;
        vec3 interfaceNormal = stabilizeWaterInterfaceNormal(hitNormal);
        float cosTheta = clamp(
            - dot(currentDirection, interfaceNormal),
            0.0,
            1.0
        );
        float fresnelX = 1.0 - cosTheta;
        float fresnelX2 = fresnelX * fresnelX;
        float fresnel = WATER_F0 +
            (1.0 - WATER_F0) * (fresnelX2 * fresnelX2) * fresnelX;

        if (rand(pathState) < fresnel)
        {
            currentOrigin = hitPos + interfaceNormal * 0.001;
            currentDirection =
                reflect(currentDirection, interfaceNormal);
            continue;
        }

        float eta = exitingWater ? WATER_IOR : (1.0 / WATER_IOR);
        vec3 refractedDirection = refract(
            currentDirection,
            interfaceNormal,
            eta
        );

        if (dot(refractedDirection, refractedDirection) < 1.0e-6)
        {
            currentOrigin = hitPos + interfaceNormal * 0.001;
            currentDirection =
                reflect(currentDirection, interfaceNormal);
            continue;
        }

        insideWater = ! exitingWater;
        currentOrigin = hitPos - interfaceNormal * 0.001;
        currentDirection = refractedDirection;
    }

    return vec3(0.0);
}















const float WATER_WALKER_FULL_DISTANCE = 48.0;
const float WATER_WALKER_MID_DISTANCE = 96.0;


const int WATER_WALKER_STEPS_FULL = 3;
const int WATER_WALKER_STEPS_MID = 2;
const int WATER_WALKER_STEPS_FAR = 1;




















float perfL10WaterAdaptiveScale()
{
    const float frameMs =
        cam.rtSampleParams.w;

    if (frameMs <= 0.0)
    {
        return 1.0;
    }

    const float tightness = clamp(
        (frameMs - 12.0) / 10.0,
        0.0,
        1.0
    );

    return mix(1.0, 0.35, tightness);
}

int waterTransparentWalkerSteps(float lodDistance)
{


    const float adaptiveScale =
        perfL10WaterAdaptiveScale();

    const float scaledDistance =
        lodDistance / adaptiveScale;

    return scaledDistance < WATER_WALKER_FULL_DISTANCE
        ? WATER_WALKER_STEPS_FULL
        : (scaledDistance < WATER_WALKER_MID_DISTANCE
            ? WATER_WALKER_STEPS_MID
            : WATER_WALKER_STEPS_FAR);
}




vec3 tracePrimaryTransparentRadiance(
    vec3 startOrigin,
    vec3 startDirection,
    bool startInsideWater,
    vec3 sunDirection,
    bool enableWaterReceiverGI,
    float waterGILodDistance,

    vec3 historyKeyPosition,
    out WaterTransmissionInfo transmissionInfo
)
{



    vec3 transparentHistoryKey = historyKeyPosition;




    transmissionInfo.waterPathLength = 0.0;
    transmissionInfo.receiverWorldPos = vec3(0.0);
    transmissionInfo.receiverNormal = vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);
    transmissionInfo.receiverViewDirection = vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    transmissionInfo.receiverAlbedo = vec3(0.0);
    transmissionInfo.receiverRoughness = 1.0;
    transmissionInfo.receiverF0Metal = 0.0;
    transmissionInfo.reachedUnderwaterReceiver = 0.0;
    transmissionInfo.waterSunVisibility = 1.0;
    transmissionInfo.receiverSelfEmissionRadiance = vec3(0.0);

    const float WATER_IOR =
        1.33;


const vec3 WATER_ABSORPTION =
    waterAbsorptionColor();


const float WATER_SHALLOW_DISTANCE =
    3.0;


const float WATER_EXTINCTION_DISTANCE =
    16.0;



const float WATER_SCATTERING =
    waterScatteringAmount();

const vec3 WATER_SCATTER_COLOR = waterScatterColor();


    const float WATER_F0 =
        0.0200593;


    const float RAY_BIAS =
        0.001;


    vec3 throughput =
        vec3(1.0);
        vec3 accumulatedWaterScattering =
    vec3(0.0);


    vec3 currentOrigin =
        startOrigin;


    vec3 currentDirection =
        normalize(
            startDirection
        );





    vec3 waterEntryDirection =
        currentDirection;


    bool hasWaterEntryDirection =
        startInsideWater;





    float waterSunVisibility =
        startInsideWater ? 1.0 : 0.0;

    bool waterSunVisibilityTraced =
        false;


    bool insideWater =
        startInsideWater;


    for (
        int step = 0;
        step < waterTransparentWalkerSteps(waterGILodDistance);
        ++ step
    )
    {
        hit.hitDistance =
            1.0;


        hit.state = makeTraceState(
            insideWater ? RT_PURPOSE_WATER_MEDIUM : RT_PURPOSE_FULL
        );




















        {
            traceRayEXT(
                topLevelAS,
                gl_RayFlagsNoneEXT,
                0xff,
                0,
                0,
                0,
                currentOrigin,
                0.01,
                currentDirection,
                10000.0,
                0
            );

            resolveDeferredHit(currentOrigin, currentDirection);

        }








if (payload.hitDistance < 0.0)
{





    if (insideWater && hasWaterEntryDirection)
    {
        hit.hitDistance =
            1.0;

        hit.state = makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_WATER_MEDIUM);




















        {
            traceRayEXT(
                topLevelAS,
                gl_RayFlagsNoneEXT,
                0xff,
                0,
                0,
                0,
                currentOrigin,
                0.01,
                waterEntryDirection,
                10000.0,
                0
            );

            resolveDeferredHit(currentOrigin, waterEntryDirection);

        }

        if (
            payload.hitDistance >= 0.0 &&
            getLegacyMaterialId(payload.state) <= 0.5
        )
        {
            vec3 fallbackHitPos =
                currentOrigin +
                waterEntryDirection *
                payload.hitDistance;

            vec3 fallbackHitNormal =
                payload.normal;

            transmissionInfo.waterPathLength +=
                payload.hitDistance;
            transmissionInfo.receiverWorldPos = fallbackHitPos;
            transmissionInfo.receiverNormal = fallbackHitNormal;
            transmissionInfo.receiverViewDirection = - waterEntryDirection;
            transmissionInfo.receiverAlbedo = payload.albedo;
            transmissionInfo.receiverRoughness = payload.roughness;
            transmissionInfo.receiverF0Metal = payload.f0Metal;
            transmissionInfo.reachedUnderwaterReceiver = 1.0;

            transmissionInfo.waterSunVisibility = waterSunVisibility;
            transmissionInfo.receiverSelfEmissionRadiance = vec3(0.0);

            vec3 fallbackLocalDirect =
         evaluateTransparentReceiverDirectLighting(


                    transparentHistoryKey,
                    vptGeometricFaceNormalFromRay(
                        fallbackHitPos, waterEntryDirection),
                    fallbackHitPos,
                    fallbackHitNormal,
                    payload.albedo,
                    0x57424F54u
                    );

            vec3 fallbackRadiance =
                evaluateOpaqueSurfaceRadiance(
                    fallbackHitPos,
                    fallbackHitNormal,
                    - waterEntryDirection,
                    payload.albedo,
                    payload.roughness,
                    payload.f0Metal,
                    payload.emission +
                        evaluateVLIEmitterRadianceAtHit(
                            fallbackHitPos,
                            fallbackHitNormal,
                            payload.albedo
                        ),
                    payload.pomSelfShadow,
                    sunDirection
                ) +
                fallbackLocalDirect;

            return
                throughput *
                exp(
                    - WATER_ABSORPTION *
                    payload.hitDistance
                ) *
                fallbackRadiance;
        }
    }

return
    accumulatedWaterScattering +
    throughput *
    payload.albedo;
}








        float hitDistance =
            payload.hitDistance;


        vec3 hitNormal =
            normalize(
                payload.normal
            );


        vec3 hitAlbedo =
            payload.albedo;


        float hitMaterialId =
            getLegacyMaterialId(payload.state);


        float hitF0Metal =
            payload.f0Metal;
            float hitRoughness =
    payload.roughness;



        vec3 hitEmission =
            payload.emission;


        float hitPOMSelfShadow =
            clamp(
                payload.pomSelfShadow,
                0.0,
                1.0
            );


        vec3 hitPos =
            currentOrigin +
            currentDirection *
            hitDistance;














if (insideWater)
{
    transmissionInfo.waterPathLength +=
        hitDistance;


















    if (cam.waterSimState.w == 0u)
    {
        vec3 volumeExtinction = waterAbsorptionColor() + waterScatterColor();
        float scatterCos = dot(currentDirection, getTimeDrivenSkyLightDirection());
        vec3 volumePhase;
        if (cam.waterParams.w > 0.5)
        {
            float rayleigh = (3.0 / (4.0 * 3.14159265359)) *
                (1.0 + scatterCos * scatterCos);
            vec3 phase3 = vec3(0.18, 0.42, 1.0) * (rayleigh * 0.05) +
                vec3(underwaterHGPhase(scatterCos, 0.6) * 0.95);
            volumePhase = mix(phase3, vec3(1.0), 0.35);
        }
        else
        {
            volumePhase = vec3(underwaterHGPhase(scatterCos, 0.6));
        }
        vec3 stepInScatter = vec3(1.0) - exp(- volumeExtinction * hitDistance);





        float waterEntryCos = clamp(getTimeDrivenSkyLightDirection().y, 0.0, 1.0);
        float waterEntryFresnel = WATER_F0 +
            (1.0 - WATER_F0) * pow(1.0 - waterEntryCos, 5.0);
        accumulatedWaterScattering +=
            waterScatterColor() * waterScatteringAmount() *
            getTimeDrivenSkyLightColor() * getTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale() *
            volumePhase * stepInScatter * throughput *
            (1.0 - waterEntryFresnel) *


            waterSunVisibility *
            UNDERWATER_COLUMN_SCATTER_BOOST;
    }


    const vec3 WATER_GLASS_TINT =
        vec3(
            0.20,
            0.75,
            0.78
        );


    const float WATER_END_DISTANCE =
        16.0;


    float waterFade =
        clamp(
            transmissionInfo.waterPathLength /
            WATER_END_DISTANCE,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );



    waterFade =
        waterFade *
        waterFade;


    vec3 absorption =
        exp(
            - WATER_ABSORPTION *
            hitDistance
        );


    throughput *= absorption;



    throughput =
        mix(
            throughput,
            throughput * WATER_GLASS_TINT,
            waterFade
        );



    throughput *=
        mix(
            1.0,
            0.15,
            waterFade
        );
}













if (max(throughput.r, max(throughput.g, throughput.b)) < 0.03)
{
    return accumulatedWaterScattering + throughput * hitEmission;
}






if (hitMaterialId <= 0.5)
{
    transmissionInfo.receiverWorldPos = hitPos;
    transmissionInfo.receiverNormal = hitNormal;
    transmissionInfo.receiverViewDirection = - currentDirection;
    transmissionInfo.receiverAlbedo = hitAlbedo;
    transmissionInfo.receiverRoughness = hitRoughness;
    transmissionInfo.receiverF0Metal = hitF0Metal;
    transmissionInfo.reachedUnderwaterReceiver = 1.0;

    transmissionInfo.waterSunVisibility = waterSunVisibility;

    vec3 unifiedHitEmission =
        hitEmission +
        evaluateVLIEmitterRadianceAtHit(
            hitPos,
            hitNormal,
            hitAlbedo
        );




    transmissionInfo.receiverSelfEmissionRadiance =
        throughput * unifiedHitEmission;























const float WATER_RECEIVER_LIGHTING_MAX_PATH = 1.0e9;

bool waterReceiverLightingNear =
    length(hitPos - startOrigin) <=
    WATER_RECEIVER_LIGHTING_MAX_PATH * perfL10WaterAdaptiveScale();

vec3 surfaceRadiance =
    vec3(0.0);

if (waterReceiverLightingNear)
{
surfaceRadiance =
    evaluateOpaqueSurfaceRadiance(
        hitPos,
        hitNormal,
        - currentDirection,
        hitAlbedo,
        hitRoughness,
        hitF0Metal,
        unifiedHitEmission,
        hitPOMSelfShadow,
        sunDirection
    );







vec3 waterReceiverLocalDirect =
    evaluateTransparentReceiverDirectLighting(


        transparentHistoryKey,
        vptGeometricFaceNormalFromRay(hitPos, currentDirection),
        hitPos,
        hitNormal,
        hitAlbedo,
        0x57424F54u
    );

surfaceRadiance +=
    waterReceiverLocalDirect;

if (insideWater && enableWaterReceiverGI)
{
    surfaceRadiance +=
        tracePrimaryWaterReceiverIndirectReworked(
            hitPos,
            hitNormal,
            hitAlbedo,
            waterGILodDistance,
            sunDirection



            , insideWater

        );
}
}


return
    throughput *
    surfaceRadiance;








if (hitMaterialId >= 2.5)
{
    if (hitMaterialId < 3.5)
    {
        throughput *=
            glassSheetTransmission(
                hitAlbedo,
                payload.normal,
                currentDirection
            );
    }

    currentOrigin =
        hitPos +
        currentDirection *
        RAY_BIAS;

    continue;
}





        hitNormal =
            stabilizeWaterInterfaceNormal(
                hitNormal
            );

        bool exitingWater =
            hitMaterialId > 1.5;

        if (! exitingWater)
        {
            waterEntryDirection =
                currentDirection;

            hasWaterEntryDirection =
                true;









            if (! waterSunVisibilityTraced)
            {
                waterSunVisibilityTraced = true;
                if (sunDirection.y > 0.0)
                {
                    hit.hitDistance = 1.0;
                    hit.state =
                        makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_VISIBILITY_ONLY);
















                    {
                        traceRayEXT(
                            topLevelAS,
                            gl_RayFlagsTerminateOnFirstHitEXT |
                                gl_RayFlagsSkipClosestHitShaderEXT,
                            0xff,
                            0,
                            0,
                            0,
                            hitPos + hitNormal * 0.035,
                            0.02,
                            sunDirection,
                            10000.0,
                            0
                        );
                    }
                    waterSunVisibility =
                        hit.hitDistance < 0.0 ? 1.0 : 0.0;
                }
                transmissionInfo.waterSunVisibility =
                    waterSunVisibility;
            }
        }


        float cosTheta =
            clamp(
                - dot(
                    currentDirection,
                    hitNormal
                ),
                0.0,
                1.0
            );


        float fresnel =
            WATER_F0 +
            (
                1.0 -
                WATER_F0
            ) *
            pow(
                1.0 -
                cosTheta,
                5.0
            );


        float eta =
            exitingWater
                ? WATER_IOR
                : (1.0 / WATER_IOR);


        vec3 transmittedDirection =
            refract(
                currentDirection,
                hitNormal,
                eta
            );






        if (
            dot(
                transmittedDirection,
                transmittedDirection
            ) < 1.0e-6
        )
        {
            currentOrigin =
                hitPos +
                hitNormal *
                RAY_BIAS;


            currentDirection =
                normalize(
                    reflect(
                        currentDirection,
                        hitNormal
                    )
                );



            continue;
        }




        throughput *=
            1.0 -
            fresnel;


        insideWater =
            ! exitingWater;


        currentOrigin =
            hitPos -
            hitNormal *
            RAY_BIAS;


        currentDirection =
            normalize(
                transmittedDirection
            );
    }








    if (insideWater)
    {
        return
            accumulatedWaterScattering +
            throughput *
            WATER_SCATTER_COLOR *
            WATER_SCATTERING *
            getTimeDrivenSkyLightColor() *
            getTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale() *
            waterSunVisibility *
            UNDERWATER_COLUMN_SCATTER_BOOST;
    }

    return
        accumulatedWaterScattering +
        throughput *
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightColor() *
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale();
}














}
vec3 tracePrimaryGlossyReflectionRadiance(
    vec3 primarySurfaceOrigin,
    vec3 startOrigin,
    vec3 startDirection,
    vec3 sunDirection,
    inout uint state,
    out float firstSpecularHitDistance
)
{
    firstSpecularHitDistance =
        65504.0;
    const float WATER_IOR =
        1.33;

    const float WATER_F0 =
        0.0200593;

    const vec3 WATER_ABSORPTION =
        waterAbsorptionColor();
        const vec3 WATER_SCATTER_COLOR = waterScatterColor();

const float WATER_EXTINCTION_DISTANCE =
    16.0;

    const float RAY_BIAS =
        0.001;

    const int MAX_WATER_INTERFACE_STEPS =
    6;




const int MAX_OPAQUE_INDIRECT_CONTINUATIONS =
    1;





const int MIRROR_BOUNCE_HARD_CAP = 8;
const int MAX_PATH_TRACE_STEPS =
    MAX_WATER_INTERFACE_STEPS +
    MIRROR_BOUNCE_HARD_CAP +
    MAX_OPAQUE_INDIRECT_CONTINUATIONS +
    2;


vec3 throughput =
    vec3(1.0);


vec3 accumulatedRadiance =
    vec3(0.0);


vec3 currentOrigin =
    startOrigin;


vec3 currentDirection =
    normalize(
        startDirection
    );


bool insideWater =
    false;


int waterInterfaceStepCount =
    0;


int opaqueContinuationCount =
    0;



int mirrorBounceCount =
    0;

int mirrorBounceLimit =
    clamp(int(cam.waterFftParams2.y + 0.5), 1, MIRROR_BOUNCE_HARD_CAP);


for (
    int pathStep = 0;
    pathStep < MAX_PATH_TRACE_STEPS;
    ++ pathStep
)
    {
        hit.hitDistance =
            1.0;


        hit.state = makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_FULL);




















        {
            traceRayEXT(
                topLevelAS,
                gl_RayFlagsNoneEXT,
                0xff,
                0,
                0,
                0,
                currentOrigin,
                0.001,
                currentDirection,
                10000.0,
                0
            );

            resolveDeferredHit(currentOrigin, currentDirection);

        }


        if (pathStep == 0)
        {
            if (payload.hitDistance >= 0.0)
            {
                vec3 firstSpecularHitPos =
                    currentOrigin +
                    currentDirection *
                    payload.hitDistance;

                firstSpecularHitDistance =
                    length(
                        firstSpecularHitPos -
                        primarySurfaceOrigin
                    );
            }
            else
            {
                firstSpecularHitDistance =
                    65504.0;
            }
        }






        if (payload.hitDistance < 0.0)
{



    return
        accumulatedRadiance +
        throughput *
        payload.albedo;
}




        float hitDistance =
            payload.hitDistance;


        vec3 hitNormal =
            normalize(
                payload.normal
            );


        vec3 hitAlbedo =
            payload.albedo;


        float hitMaterialId =
            getLegacyMaterialId(payload.state);


        float hitRoughness =
            payload.roughness;


        float hitF0Metal =
            payload.f0Metal;


        vec3 hitEmission =
            payload.emission;


        float hitPOMSelfShadow =
            clamp(
                payload.pomSelfShadow,
                0.0,
                1.0
            );


        vec3 hitPos =
            currentOrigin +
            currentDirection *
            hitDistance;



        if (insideWater)
        {
throughput *=
    exp(
        - WATER_ABSORPTION *
        hitDistance
    );


        }






if (hitMaterialId <= 0.5)
{










    vec3 surfaceRadiance =
        evaluateOpaqueSurfaceRadiance(
            hitPos,
            hitNormal,
            - currentDirection,
            hitAlbedo,
            hitRoughness,
            hitF0Metal,
            hitEmission,
            hitPOMSelfShadow,
            sunDirection
        );







    const bool hitIsPureMirror =
        decodeMetallicFromCode(decodeF0MetalCode(hitF0Metal)) > 0.5 &&
        hitRoughness < 0.05;

    if (hitIsPureMirror)
    {
        if (mirrorBounceCount >= mirrorBounceLimit)
        {
            accumulatedRadiance +=
                throughput *
                surfaceRadiance;

            return
                accumulatedRadiance;
        }

        const float mirrorNdotV =
            max(
                dot(
                    hitNormal,
                    - currentDirection
                ),
                0.0
            );

        if (mirrorNdotV <= 1.0e-5)
        {
            return
                accumulatedRadiance;
        }

        const int mirrorF0Code =
            decodeF0MetalCode(
                hitF0Metal
            );

        const vec3 mirrorF0 =
            decodeBaseF0(
                mirrorF0Code,
                hitAlbedo
            );

        throughput *=
            fresnelSchlick(
                mirrorNdotV,
                mirrorF0
            );

        currentOrigin =
            hitPos +
            hitNormal *
            RAY_BIAS;

        currentDirection =
            reflect(
                currentDirection,
                hitNormal
            );

        mirrorBounceCount +=
            1;

        continue;
    }

    accumulatedRadiance +=
        throughput *
        surfaceRadiance;









    if (
        opaqueContinuationCount >=
        MAX_OPAQUE_INDIRECT_CONTINUATIONS
    )
    {
        return
            accumulatedRadiance;
    }






    vec3 bounceV =
        normalize(
            - currentDirection
        );


    float bounceNdotV =
        max(
            dot(
                hitNormal,
                bounceV
            ),
            0.0
        );


    if (bounceNdotV <= 1.0e-5)
    {
        return
            accumulatedRadiance;
    }






    int hitF0MetalCode =
        decodeF0MetalCode(
            hitF0Metal
        );


    float hitMetallic =
        decodeMetallicFromCode(
            hitF0MetalCode
        );


    vec3 bounceF0 =
        decodeBaseF0(
            hitF0MetalCode,
            hitAlbedo
        );


    float bounceRoughness =
        max(
            hitRoughness,
            0.05
        );








    vec3 bounceFForChoice =
        fresnelSchlick(
            bounceNdotV,
            bounceF0
        );


    float bounceFMax =
        max(
            max(
                bounceFForChoice.r,
                bounceFForChoice.g
            ),
            bounceFForChoice.b
        );


    float bounceSpecularProbability =
        hitMetallic > 0.5
            ? 1.0
            : clamp(
                bounceFMax,
                0.05,
                0.95
            );


    bool chooseBounceSpecular =
        rand(state) <
        bounceSpecularProbability;


    vec3 bounceDirection =
        vec3(0.0);


    vec3 bounceWeight =
        vec3(0.0);






    if (chooseBounceSpecular)
    {
        vec3 bounceH =
            normalize(
                sampleGGX(
                    hitNormal,
                    bounceRoughness,
                    state
                )
            );


        float bounceVdotH =
            max(
                dot(
                    bounceV,
                    bounceH
                ),
                0.0
            );


        float bounceNdotH =
            max(
                dot(
                    hitNormal,
                    bounceH
                ),
                0.0
            );


        bounceDirection =
            normalize(
                reflect(
                    - bounceV,
                    bounceH
                )
            );


        float bounceNdotL =
            max(
                dot(
                    hitNormal,
                    bounceDirection
                ),
                0.0
            );


        if (
            bounceVdotH <= 1.0e-5 ||
            bounceNdotH <= 1.0e-5 ||
            bounceNdotL <= 1.0e-5
        )
        {
            return
                accumulatedRadiance;
        }


        vec3 bounceF =
            fresnelSchlick(
                bounceVdotH,
                bounceF0
            );


        float bounceD =
            distributionGGX(
                hitNormal,
                bounceH,
                bounceRoughness
            );


        float bounceG =
            geometrySmith(
                hitNormal,
                bounceV,
                bounceDirection,
                bounceRoughness
            );


        vec3 bounceSpecularBRDF =
            (
                bounceD *
                bounceG *
                bounceF
            ) /
            max(
                4.0 *
                bounceNdotV *
                bounceNdotL,
                1.0e-5
            );


        bounceSpecularBRDF *=
            multiscatterEnergyCompensation(
                bounceF0,
                bounceRoughness
            );










        float bounceSpecularPdf =
            max(
                (
                    bounceD *
                    bounceNdotH
                ) /
                max(
                    4.0 *
                    bounceVdotH,
                    1.0e-5
                ),
                1.0e-5
            );


        bounceWeight =
            (
                bounceSpecularBRDF *
                bounceNdotL
            ) /
            (
                bounceSpecularPdf *
                max(
                    bounceSpecularProbability,
                    1.0e-5
                )
            );
    }






    else
    {
        float bounceDiffuseProbability =
            max(
                1.0 -
                bounceSpecularProbability,
                1.0e-5
            );


        vec3 bounceKD =
            (
                vec3(1.0) -
                bounceFForChoice
            ) *
            (
                1.0 -
                hitMetallic
            );


        bounceDirection =
            sampleHemisphereCosine(
                hitNormal,
                state
            );







        bounceWeight =
            (
                bounceKD *
                hitAlbedo
            ) /
            bounceDiffuseProbability;
    }






    throughput *=
        bounceWeight *
        0.7;


    currentOrigin =
        hitPos +
        hitNormal *
        RAY_BIAS;


    currentDirection =
        bounceDirection;


    opaqueContinuationCount +=
        1;


    continue;
}









if (
    waterInterfaceStepCount >=
    MAX_WATER_INTERFACE_STEPS
)
{
    return
        accumulatedRadiance;
}


waterInterfaceStepCount +=
    1;

        hitNormal =
            stabilizeWaterInterfaceNormal(
                hitNormal
            );

        bool exitingWater =
            hitMaterialId > 1.5;


        float cosTheta =
            clamp(
                - dot(
                    currentDirection,
                    hitNormal
                ),
                0.0,
                1.0
            );


        float fresnel =
            WATER_F0 +
            (
                1.0 -
                WATER_F0
            ) *
            pow(
                1.0 -
                cosTheta,
                5.0
            );


        float waterChoice =
            rand(
                state
            );






        if (
            waterChoice <
            fresnel
        )
        {







            insideWater =
                exitingWater;


            currentOrigin =
                hitPos +
                hitNormal *
                RAY_BIAS;


            currentDirection =
                normalize(
                    reflect(
                        currentDirection,
                        hitNormal
                    )
                );


            continue;
        }






        float eta =
            exitingWater
                ? WATER_IOR
                : (1.0 / WATER_IOR);


        vec3 refractedDirection =
            refract(
                currentDirection,
                hitNormal,
                eta
            );



        if (
            dot(
                refractedDirection,
                refractedDirection
            ) < 1.0e-6
        )
        {
            insideWater =
                exitingWater;


            currentOrigin =
                hitPos +
                hitNormal *
                RAY_BIAS;


            currentDirection =
                normalize(
                    reflect(
                        currentDirection,
                        hitNormal
                    )
                );


            continue;
        }



        insideWater =
            ! exitingWater;


        currentOrigin =
            hitPos -
            hitNormal *
            RAY_BIAS;


        currentDirection =
            normalize(
                refractedDirection
            );
    }


return
    accumulatedRadiance;
}













float traceWaterReflectedSun(
    vec3 receiverPos,
    vec3 receiverNormal,
    vec3 sunDirection
)
{
    const vec3 WATER_PLANE_NORMAL =
        vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);

    const float WATER_F0 =
        0.0200593;









    vec3 toWaterDir =
        normalize(
            reflect(
                sunDirection,
                WATER_PLANE_NORMAL
            )
        );

    float receiverNdotL =
        max(
            dot(
                receiverNormal,
                toWaterDir
            ),
            0.0
        );


    if (receiverNdotL <= 0.0)
    {
        return 0.0;
    }





    hit.hitDistance =
        1.0;

    hit.state = makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_CLASSIFY);




















    {
        traceRayEXT(
            topLevelAS,
            gl_RayFlagsNoneEXT,
            0xff,
            0,
            0,
            0,
            receiverPos +
                receiverNormal * 0.035,
            0.02,
            toWaterDir,
            10000.0,
            0
        );

        resolveDeferredHit(receiverPos +
                receiverNormal * 0.035, toWaterDir);

    }



    if (payload.hitDistance < 0.0)
    {
        return 0.0;
    }


    float waterMaterialId =
        getLegacyMaterialId(payload.state);


    if (waterMaterialId <= 0.5)
    {
        return 0.0;
    }







    if (waterMaterialId > 1.5)
    {
        return 0.0;
    }


    vec3 waterHitPos =
        receiverPos +
        receiverNormal * 0.035 +
        toWaterDir *
        payload.hitDistance;

    vec3 waterHitNormal =
        normalize(
            payload.normal
        );





    if (
        dot(
            waterHitNormal,
            WATER_PLANE_NORMAL
        ) < 0.5
    )
    {
        return 0.0;
    }








    hit.hitDistance =
        1.0;

    hit.state = makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_VISIBILITY_ONLY);


















    {
        traceRayEXT(
            topLevelAS,
            gl_RayFlagsTerminateOnFirstHitEXT |
            gl_RayFlagsSkipClosestHitShaderEXT,
            0xff,
            0,
            0,
            0,
            waterHitPos +
                WATER_PLANE_NORMAL * 0.035,
            0.02,
            sunDirection,
            10000.0,
            0
        );
    }



    if (hit.hitDistance >= 0.0)
    {
        return 0.0;
    }
















    float reflectedCausticGain = 1.0;
    if (cam.waterFftParams.y > 0.0 && cam.waterFftParams2.w > 0.5)
    {
        float projScale = float(gl_LaunchSizeEXT.y) /
            (2.0 * max(tan(cam.cameraParams.z * 0.5), 0.05));
        float causticLod = waterCausticMapLodFromProj(
            receiverPos,
            length(receiverPos - cam.viewInverse[3].xyz),
            projScale);
        float causticDensity = textureLod(
            waterReflectCausticMap,
            waterHitPos.xz / max(cam.waterFftParams.x, 1.0e-3),
            causticLod).r;
        if (causticDensity > 1.0e-4)
        {
            float causticStrength = waterStyleCausticStrength();
            reflectedCausticGain = clamp(
                1.0 + (causticDensity - 1.0) * causticStrength,
                0.0,
                6.0);
        }
    }





    float waterCosTheta =
        clamp(
            dot(
            WATER_PLANE_NORMAL,
            sunDirection
            ),
            0.0,
            1.0
        );

    float waterFresnel =
        WATER_F0 +
        (
            1.0 -
            WATER_F0
        ) *
        pow(
            1.0 -
            waterCosTheta,
            5.0
        );


    return
        receiverNdotL *
        waterFresnel *
        reflectedCausticGain;
}
























vec2 dhFarScreenUv(ivec2 pixelCoord)
{
    return(vec2(pixelCoord) + 0.5) / vec2(imageSize(image));
}


void dhFarPrimaryRay(ivec2 pixelCoord, out vec3 cameraPos, out vec3 rayDir);




















vec2 dhFarSampleUv(ivec2 pixelCoord)
{
    int modeRequested = int(cam.dhFarFieldLight.x + 0.5);

    if (modeRequested != 13 || cam.dhFarFieldMvp[0][0] == 0.0) {


        vec2 uv = dhFarScreenUv(pixelCoord);
        uv.y = 1.0 - uv.y;
        return uv;
    }

    vec3 cameraPos = vec3(0.0);
    vec3 rayDir = vec3(0.0, 0.0, - 1.0);
    dhFarPrimaryRay(pixelCoord, cameraPos, rayDir);

    vec4 clip = cam.dhFarFieldMvp * vec4(rayDir, 0.0);
    if (abs(clip.w) < 1.0e-9) {
        return vec2(- 1.0);
    }
    vec2 uv = clip.xy / clip.w * 0.5 + 0.5;
    uv.y = 1.0 - uv.y;
    return uv;
}

vec2 dhFarTextureUv(ivec2 pixelCoord)
{
    return dhFarSampleUv(pixelCoord);
}











float dhFarDepthAt(ivec2 pixelCoord)
{
    ivec2 outputSize = imageSize(image);
    if (any(lessThan(pixelCoord, ivec2(0)))
        || any(greaterThanEqual(pixelCoord, outputSize))) return 0.0;
    vec2 uv = dhFarTextureUv(pixelCoord);



    if (any(lessThan(uv, vec2(0.0))) || any(greaterThanEqual(uv, vec2(1.0)))) return 0.0;
    ivec2 depthSize = textureSize(dhFarDepth, 0);
    ivec2 depthPixel = clamp(ivec2(uv * vec2(depthSize)),
                              ivec2(0), depthSize - ivec2(1));
    return texelFetch(dhFarDepth, depthPixel, 0).r;
}










bool dhFarDepthIsGeometry(float depthValue)
{
    return depthValue > 0.0 && depthValue <= 1.0;
}






bool dhFarFinite(float value)
{
    return ! (isnan(value) || isinf(value));
}





void dhFarPrimaryRay(ivec2 pixelCoord, out vec3 cameraPos, out vec3 rayDir)
{
    cameraPos = (cam.viewInverse * vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)).xyz;

    vec2 uv = dhFarScreenUv(pixelCoord);
    vec2 ndc = vec2(uv.x * 2.0 - 1.0, 1.0 - uv.y * 2.0);

    vec4 target = cam.projInverse * vec4(ndc, 1.0, 1.0);
    vec3 viewTarget = abs(target.w) > 1.0e-6 ? target.xyz / target.w : target.xyz;
    float viewLength = length(viewTarget);

    rayDir = viewLength > 1.0e-6
        ? normalize( (cam.viewInverse * vec4(viewTarget / viewLength, 0.0)).xyz)
        : vec3(0.0, 0.0, - 1.0);
}


vec3 dhFarNdc(ivec2 pixelCoord, float depthValue, int variant)
{
    vec2 uv = dhFarScreenUv(pixelCoord);
    float ndcY = ( ( (variant & 1) == 0) ? 1.0 : - 1.0) * (1.0 - 2.0 * uv.y);
    return vec3(uv.x * 2.0 - 1.0, ndcY, depthValue);
}




vec3 dhFarWorldPoint(ivec2 pixelCoord, float depthValue, int variant)
{
    mat4 inverseMvp = ( (variant & 2) == 0)
        ? cam.dhFarFieldInvMvp
        : transpose(cam.dhFarFieldInvMvp);
    vec4 homogeneous = inverseMvp * vec4(dhFarNdc(pixelCoord, depthValue, variant), 1.0);
    return abs(homogeneous.w) > 1.0e-6
        ? homogeneous.xyz / homogeneous.w
        : homogeneous.xyz;
}






















float dhFarDistanceFor(
    ivec2 pixelCoord,
    float depthValue,
    vec3 cameraPos,
    vec3 rayDir,
    int variant,
    float nearClip,
    float farClip,
    out int source
)
{






    bool lanesUsable = nearClip > 0.0 && farClip > nearClip * 4.0 + 1.0;
    float lowLimit = lanesUsable ? nearClip * 0.5 : 0.01;
    float highLimit = lanesUsable ? farClip * 1.5 : 1.0e12;

    if (lanesUsable) {


        float analyticDistance = nearClip * farClip
            / max(nearClip + depthValue * (farClip - nearClip), 1.0e-3);

        if (dhFarFinite(analyticDistance)
            && analyticDistance > lowLimit
            && analyticDistance < highLimit) {
            source = 0;
            return analyticDistance;
        }
    }

    vec3 matrixPoint = dhFarWorldPoint(pixelCoord, depthValue, variant);
    float matrixDistance = dot(matrixPoint - cameraPos, rayDir);

    if (dhFarFinite(matrixDistance)
        && matrixDistance > lowLimit
        && matrixDistance < highLimit) {
        source = 1;
        return matrixDistance;
    }

    vec3 mirrorPoint = dhFarWorldPoint(pixelCoord, 1.0 - depthValue, variant);
    float mirrorDistance = dot(mirrorPoint - cameraPos, rayDir);

    if (dhFarFinite(mirrorDistance)
        && mirrorDistance > lowLimit
        && mirrorDistance < highLimit) {
        source = 2;
        return mirrorDistance;
    }


    source = 3;

    float fallbackLength = length(matrixPoint - cameraPos);

    if (! dhFarFinite(fallbackLength) || fallbackLength < 0.01) {
        fallbackLength = length(mirrorPoint - cameraPos);
    }

    if (! dhFarFinite(fallbackLength) || fallbackLength < 0.01) {
        fallbackLength = 1.0;
    }

    return fallbackLength;
}












bool dhFarNeighborPoint(
    ivec2 pixelCoord,
    ivec2 offset,
    float centerDepth,
    int variant,
    float nearClip,
    float farClip,
    out vec3 point
)
{
    ivec2 neighborCoord = pixelCoord + offset;
    float neighborDepth = dhFarDepthAt(neighborCoord);

    if (! dhFarDepthIsGeometry(neighborDepth)) {
        return false;
    }

    float depthScale = max(max(abs(centerDepth), abs(neighborDepth)), 1.0e-6);

    if (abs(neighborDepth - centerDepth) / depthScale > 0.12) {
        return false;
    }



    vec3 neighborCameraPos;
    vec3 neighborRayDir;
    dhFarPrimaryRay(neighborCoord, neighborCameraPos, neighborRayDir);

    int neighborSource = 2;
    float neighborDistance = dhFarDistanceFor(
        neighborCoord,
        neighborDepth,
        neighborCameraPos,
        neighborRayDir,
        variant,
        nearClip,
        farClip,
        neighborSource
    );

    point = neighborCameraPos + neighborRayDir * neighborDistance;
    return true;
}


















layout(std430, binding = 19, set = 0) readonly buffer LodRoles {
    uint roles[];
} lodRoles;

const uint LOD_ROLE_TERRAIN = 0u;
const uint LOD_ROLE_VOXY_LOD = 1u;
const uint LOD_ROLE_DH_FAR = 2u;
const uint LOD_COVERAGE_SLOT_LIMIT = (1u << 16);

uint materialLodRole(uint instanceIndex)
{
    if (instanceIndex >= LOD_COVERAGE_SLOT_LIMIT)
    {
        return LOD_ROLE_TERRAIN;
    }

    return lodRoles.roles[instanceIndex];
}

vec2 g_debugUV = vec2(- 1.0);

float materialHitT = 0.0;
vec3 materialRayOrigin = vec3(0.0);
vec3 materialRayDirection = vec3(0.0, 0.0, - 1.0);

















uint makeHitState(
    uint purpose,
    uint hitClass,
    uint waterEvent,
    bool glass,
    bool glassBottom,
    uint transparentProfile
)
{
    return
        (purpose & RT_STATE_PURPOSE_MASK) |
        ( (hitClass & 0x3u) << RT_STATE_HIT_CLASS_SHIFT) |
        ( (waterEvent & 0x3u) << RT_STATE_WATER_EVENT_SHIFT) |
        (glass ? RT_STATE_GLASS_BIT : 0u) |
        (glassBottom ? RT_STATE_GLASS_BOTTOM_BIT : 0u) |
        ( (transparentProfile & 0x7u) << RT_STATE_PROFILE_SHIFT);
}

uint getTransparentProfileFromRawMaterialId(float materialId)
{
    return uint(
        round(
            clamp(fract(materialId) * 10.0, 0.0, 4.0)
        )
    );
}

    float x, y, z;

    float pad1;
    float pad2;
};

};

};

vec3 readVertexMotion(VertexMotions motions, uint vertexIndex)
{
    const uint base = vertexIndex * 3u;

    return vec3(
        motions.motion[base + 0u],
        motions.motion[base + 1u],
        motions.motion[base + 2u]
    );
}

    vec3 normal;

float rainRippleHash(vec2 p)
{
    p = fract(p * vec2(0.1031, 0.11369));
    p += dot(p, p.yx + 19.19);
    return fract( (p.x + p.y) * p.x);
}





vec2 rainImpactRippleGradient(vec2 worldXZ, float cameraDistance)
{
    float rain = abs(cam.weatherParams.z - 1.0) < 0.25
        ? clamp(cam.weatherParams.x, 0.0, 1.0)
        : 0.0;
    if (rain <= 0.01 || getRayPurpose(payload.state) != RT_PURPOSE_PRIMARY_SKY)
    {
        return vec2(0.0);
    }

    float nearFade = 1.0 - smoothstep(28.0, 72.0, cameraDistance);
    if (nearFade <= 0.0)
    {
        return vec2(0.0);
    }



    const float CELL_SIZE = 0.432;
    const float RING_SPEED = 0.336;
    const float RING_FREQUENCY = 66.66667;


    float time = uintBitsToFloat(cam.padding);
    vec2 baseCell = floor(worldXZ / CELL_SIZE);
    vec2 gradient = vec2(0.0);

    for (int y = - 1; y <= 1; ++ y)
    {
        for (int x = - 1; x <= 1; ++ x)
        {
            vec2 cell = baseCell + vec2(float(x), float(y));
            float seedX = rainRippleHash(cell + vec2(17.0, 43.0));
            float seedZ = rainRippleHash(cell + vec2(71.0, 29.0));
            float age = fract(time * mix(0.42, 1.08, rain) +
                rainRippleHash(cell + vec2(113.0, 7.0)));
            vec2 impact = (cell + vec2(seedX, seedZ)) * CELL_SIZE;
            vec2 delta = worldXZ - impact;
            float distanceToImpact = length(delta);
            float radius = age * RING_SPEED;
            float envelope = (1.0 - smoothstep(0.52, 1.0, age)) *
                (1.0 - smoothstep(0.12, 0.204, distanceToImpact));
            if (envelope <= 0.0 || distanceToImpact <= 1.0e-4)
            {
                continue;
            }

            float slope = cos( (distanceToImpact - radius) * RING_FREQUENCY);
            gradient += normalize(delta) * slope * envelope;
        }
    }


    return gradient * (0.060 * rain * nearFade);
}

WaterWaveSurface evaluateWaterWaveSurface(vec2 worldXZ, float waterY)
{
    float cameraDistance;
    vec3 surfacePos = vec3(worldXZ.x, waterY, worldXZ.y);
    float noV = abs(normalize(surfacePos - cam.viewInverse[3].xyz).y);
    float horizonFade = 1.0 - exp(- 4.0 * noV);

    float projScale = float(gl_LaunchSizeEXT.y)
        / (2.0 * max(tan(cam.cameraParams.z * 0.5), 0.05));
    float waveLod = waterFftSlopeLodFromProj(surfacePos, materialHitT, projScale);
    vec2 naturalSlope = waterNaturalWaveGradient(worldXZ, cameraDistance, horizonFade, waveLod);





    float stateAndBorderMask;
    vec2 interactionSlope =
        waterInteractionSlope(worldXZ, stateAndBorderMask);
    float elevationMatch = 1.0 - smoothstep(
        0.65, 1.50, abs(waterY - cam.waterSimSurfaceAndOrigin.x)
    );



    vec2 gradient = waterBlendWaveSlopes(
        naturalSlope,
        interactionSlope * (stateAndBorderMask * elevationMatch),
        waterWaveBlendMode()
    );






    gradient += rainImpactRippleGradient(worldXZ, cameraDistance);

    WaterWaveSurface result;
    result.normal = normalize(vec3(- gradient.x, 1.0, - gradient.y));
    return result;
}









const int WATER_CLASSIFY_WAVE_OCTAVES = 1;

WaterWaveSurface evaluateWaterWaveSurfaceClassify(vec2 worldXZ, float waterY)
{
    float cameraDistance;
    vec3 surfacePos = vec3(worldXZ.x, waterY, worldXZ.y);
    float noV = abs(normalize(surfacePos - cam.viewInverse[3].xyz).y);
    float horizonFade = 1.0 - exp(- 4.0 * noV);
    float projScale = float(gl_LaunchSizeEXT.y)
        / (2.0 * max(tan(cam.cameraParams.z * 0.5), 0.05));
    float waveLod = waterFftSlopeLodFromProj(surfacePos, materialHitT, projScale);
    vec2 naturalSlope =
        waterNaturalWaveGradientCapped(
            worldXZ,
            cameraDistance,
            WATER_CLASSIFY_WAVE_OCTAVES,
            horizonFade,
            waveLod
        );

    float stateAndBorderMask;
    vec2 interactionSlope =
        waterInteractionSlope(worldXZ, stateAndBorderMask);
    float elevationMatch = 1.0 - smoothstep(
        0.65, 1.50, abs(waterY - cam.waterSimSurfaceAndOrigin.x)
    );
    vec2 gradient = waterBlendWaveSlopes(
        naturalSlope,
        interactionSlope * (stateAndBorderMask * elevationMatch),
        waterWaveBlendMode()
    );

    WaterWaveSurface result;
    result.normal = normalize(vec3(- gradient.x, 1.0, - gradient.y));
    return result;
}

        case 57u : return textureLod(entityAtlases[56], uv, 0.0);
        case 58u : return textureLod(entityAtlases[57], uv, 0.0);
        case 59u : return textureLod(entityAtlases[58], uv, 0.0);
        case 60u : return textureLod(entityAtlases[59], uv, 0.0);
        case 61u : return textureLod(entityAtlases[60], uv, 0.0);
        case 62u : return textureLod(entityAtlases[61], uv, 0.0);
        case 63u : return textureLod(entityAtlases[62], uv, 0.0);
        case 64u : return textureLod(entityAtlases[63], uv, 0.0);
        default : return vec4(1.0);
    }
}

        case 27u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[26], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[26], uv, 0.0); return;

        case 47u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[46], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[46], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 48u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[47], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[47], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 49u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[48], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[48], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 50u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[49], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[49], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 51u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[50], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[50], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 52u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[51], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[51], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 53u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[52], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[52], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 54u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[53], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[53], uv, 0.0); return;

        case 56u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[55], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[55], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 57u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[56], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[56], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 58u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[57], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[57], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 59u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[58], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[58], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 60u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[59], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[59], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 61u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[60], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[60], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 62u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[61], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[61], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 63u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[62], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[62], uv, 0.0); return;
        case 64u : normalTexel = textureLod(entityNormalAtlases[63], uv, 0.0); specularTexel = textureLod(entitySpecularAtlases[63], uv, 0.0); return;
        default :
            normalTexel = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
            specularTexel = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
            return;
    }
}

vec4 sampleRTAlbedo(uint textureSlot, vec2 uv)
{
    return textureSlot == 0u
        ? textureLod(blockAtlas, uv, 0.0)
        : sampleEntityAtlas(textureSlot, uv);
}

vec4 sampleParticleAtlas(uint textureSlot, vec2 uv)
{
    switch (textureSlot)
    {
        case 1u : return textureLod(particleAtlases[0], uv, 0.0);
        case 2u : return textureLod(particleAtlases[1], uv, 0.0);
        case 3u : return textureLod(particleAtlases[2], uv, 0.0);
        default : return vec4(0.0);
    }
}




vec4 sampleRainStreakAtlas(vec2 uv)
{
    return textureLod(rainStreakAtlas, uv, 0.0);
}






































const float VOXY_ATLAS_SIZE = 1024.0;
const float VOXY_TILE_PIXELS = 16.0;













float voxyLodMip(vec3 pos0, vec3 pos1, vec3 geoNormal)
{



    vec3 edge = abs(pos1 - pos0);
    float cellW = max(edge.x, max(edge.y, edge.z));
    float projScale = float(gl_LaunchSizeEXT.y)
        / (2.0 * max(tan(cam.cameraParams.z * 0.5), 0.05));
    float facing = abs(dot(geoNormal, materialRayDirection));
    float cellPx = cellW * projScale * max(facing, 0.02)
        / max(materialHitT, 1.0e-3);
    return clamp(
        log2(VOXY_TILE_PIXELS / max(cellPx, 1.0e-4)),
        0.0,
        4.0
    );
}

vec2 voxyTileSafeUV(vec2 uv, vec2 uv0, vec2 uv1, vec2 uv2, float mip)
{



    vec2 tileOrigin = min(uv0, min(uv1, uv2)) - vec2(0.5 / VOXY_ATLAS_SIZE);
    float inset = exp2(ceil(mip)) * (0.5 / VOXY_ATLAS_SIZE);
    vec2 tileSpan = vec2(VOXY_TILE_PIXELS / VOXY_ATLAS_SIZE);
    return clamp(uv, tileOrigin + inset, tileOrigin + tileSpan - inset);
}

vec3 voxyLodAlbedo(float mipShade, vec2 uv, vec2 uv0, vec2 uv1, vec2 uv2,
    float mip, vec3 tintOrFallback)
{
    if (mipShade < 0.0)
    {
        return tintOrFallback;
    }
    float shade = mipShade - floor(mipShade);
    vec2 safeUV = voxyTileSafeUV(uv, uv0, uv1, uv2, mip);
    return textureLod(voxyLodAtlas, safeUV, mip).rgb * tintOrFallback * shade;
}




















float dhBlockMip(vec3 hitPos, vec3 normal)
{
    return voxyLodMip(hitPos, hitPos + vec3(1.0), normal);
}

vec2 dhFaceUv(vec3 hitPos, float normalIndex)
{
    vec3 pos = fract(hitPos);
    if (normalIndex < 0.5) return vec2(pos.x, 1.0 - pos.z);
    if (normalIndex < 1.5) return vec2(pos.x, pos.z);
    if (normalIndex < 2.5) return vec2(1.0 - pos.x, 1.0 - pos.y);
    if (normalIndex < 3.5) return vec2(pos.x, 1.0 - pos.y);
    if (normalIndex < 4.5) return vec2(pos.z, 1.0 - pos.y);
    return vec2(1.0 - pos.z, 1.0 - pos.y);
}





















float dhNoiseRand(float co)
{
    return fract(sin(co * 91.3458) * 47453.5453);
}

float dhNoiseRand(vec2 co)
{
    return fract(sin(dot(co.xy, vec2(12.9898, 78.233))) * 43758.5453);
}

float dhNoiseRand(vec3 co)
{
    return dhNoiseRand(co.xy + dhNoiseRand(co.z));
}

vec3 dhFakeTextureNoise(vec3 albedo, vec3 hitPos, vec3 geoNormal)
{
    const float DH_NOISE_INTENSITY = 0.18;
    const float DH_NOISE_STEPS = 8.0;
    const float DH_NOISE_DROPOFF = 1024.0;

    vec3 fixedPos = hitPos + normalize(geoNormal) * 0.001;
    vec3 grid = floor(fixedPos * DH_NOISE_STEPS) / DH_NOISE_STEPS;

    float luminance = (albedo.r + albedo.g + albedo.b) * (1.0 / 3.0);

    float amplification =
        (1.0 - pow(luminance * 2.0 - 1.0, 2.0)) * DH_NOISE_INTENSITY;

    float randomValue =
        dhNoiseRand(grid) * 2.0 * amplification - amplification;
    vec3 newColor = clamp(albedo + (1.0 - albedo) * randomValue, 0.0, 1.0);


    float viewDistance = length(hitPos - cam.viewInverse[3].xyz);
    float distanceFade = min(viewDistance / DH_NOISE_DROPOFF, 1.0);
    return mix(newColor, albedo, distanceFade);
}






























const float DH_LOD_NEAR_GAIN = 0.85;
const float DH_LOD_FAR_GAIN = 0.65;
const float DH_LOD_NEAR_SAT = 1.0;
const float DH_LOD_FAR_SAT = 1.45;
const float DH_LOD_NEAR_BLOCKS = 96.0;
const float DH_LOD_FAR_BLOCKS = 768.0;



vec3 dhLodColorCompensation(vec3 color, vec3 hitPos)
{
    float viewDistance = length(hitPos - cam.viewInverse[3].xyz);
    float t = clamp(
        (viewDistance - DH_LOD_NEAR_BLOCKS) /
        max(DH_LOD_FAR_BLOCKS - DH_LOD_NEAR_BLOCKS, 1.0),
        0.0,
        1.0);
    vec3 gained = color * mix(DH_LOD_NEAR_GAIN, DH_LOD_FAR_GAIN, t);




    float luma = dot(gained, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
    return clamp(
        mix(vec3(luma), gained, mix(DH_LOD_NEAR_SAT, DH_LOD_FAR_SAT, t)),
        0.0,
        1.0);
}

vec3 dhLodAlbedo(float tileId, float normalIndex, vec3 hitPos, vec3 geoNormal,
    vec3 vertexColor)
{



    if (tileId < 0.5)
    {
        return dhLodColorCompensation(
            dhFakeTextureNoise(vertexColor, hitPos, geoNormal), hitPos);
    }
    float mip = clamp(dhBlockMip(hitPos, geoNormal), 0.0, 4.0);
    ivec2 atlasSize = textureSize(dhBlockRatioAtlas, 0);
    vec2 tileOrigin = vec2(mod(tileId, 256.0), floor(tileId / 256.0)) * 16.0;
    vec2 uv = (tileOrigin + dhFaceUv(hitPos, normalIndex) * 16.0) / vec2(atlasSize);
    vec4 tile = textureLod(dhBlockRatioAtlas, uv, mip);
    vec3 tinted = clamp(vertexColor * (tile.rgb * 2.0), 0.0, 1.0);
    return dhLodColorCompensation(
        mix(vertexColor, tinted, tile.a), hitPos);
}



















const float DH_BAKED_BLOCK_GAIN = 0.25;
const vec3 DH_BAKED_BLOCK_TINT = vec3(1.0, 0.82, 0.60);

float dhBlockLightLevel(float packedLight)
{
    float code = floor(clamp(packedLight, 0.0, 1.0) * 255.0 + 0.5);
    return mod(code, 16.0);
}

vec3 dhBakedBlockLight(vec3 albedo, float packedLight)
{
    float level = dhBlockLightLevel(packedLight) * (1.0 / 15.0);

    return albedo * DH_BAKED_BLOCK_TINT * (DH_BAKED_BLOCK_GAIN * level * level * level);
}
















layout(binding = 4, set = 0) uniform sampler2D normalAtlas;
layout(binding = 5, set = 0) uniform sampler2D specularAtlas;


























float sampleLabPBRDepth(
    vec2 uv
)
{
    float a =
        clamp(
            textureLod(
                normalAtlas,
                uv,
                0.0
            ).a,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );





    a +=
        clamp(
            1.0 - a * 1.0e20,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );


    return
        1.0 -
        a;
}











bool isPOMSpriteUVInBounds(
    vec2 uv,
    vec2 safeMin,
    vec2 safeMax
)
{
    return
        all(greaterThanEqual(uv, safeMin)) &&
        all(lessThanEqual(uv, safeMax));
}




float pomHeightAlpha(ivec2 texel, ivec2 lo, ivec2 hi)
{
    texel = clamp(texel, lo, hi);

    float a =
        clamp(
            texelFetch(normalAtlas, texel, 0).a,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );

    return
        a +
        clamp(
            1.0 - a * 1.0e20,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );
}



















bool pomSmoothSampling()
{
    return cam.pomParams.x <= 0.5;
}


float sampleLabPBRDepthInSprite(
    vec2 uv,
    vec2 safeMin,
    vec2 safeMax
)
{
    vec2 atlasSizeF =
        vec2(textureSize(normalAtlas, 0));

    vec2 texelSpace =
        uv * atlasSizeF - 0.5;

    ivec2 baseTexel =
        ivec2(floor(texelSpace));

    vec2 f =
        texelSpace - vec2(baseTexel);

    ivec2 lo =
        ivec2(floor(safeMin * atlasSizeF + 1.0e-4));

    ivec2 hi =
        ivec2(ceil(safeMax * atlasSizeF - 1.0e-4)) - ivec2(1);

    hi =
        max(hi, lo);



    if (! pomSmoothSampling())
    {
        ivec2 sharpTexel =
            ivec2(floor(uv * atlasSizeF));

        return
            1.0 -
            pomHeightAlpha(sharpTexel, lo, hi);
    }

    float a00 =
        pomHeightAlpha(baseTexel + ivec2(0, 0), lo, hi);

    float a10 =
        pomHeightAlpha(baseTexel + ivec2(1, 0), lo, hi);

    float a01 =
        pomHeightAlpha(baseTexel + ivec2(0, 1), lo, hi);

    float a11 =
        pomHeightAlpha(baseTexel + ivec2(1, 1), lo, hi);

    float a =
        mix(
            mix(a00, a10, f.x),
            mix(a01, a11, f.x),
            f.y
        );

    return
        1.0 -
        a;
}























vec2 wrapPOMSpriteUV(
    vec2 uv,
    vec2 spriteMin,
    vec2 spriteSpan
)
{
    vec2 local =
        (uv - spriteMin) /
        max(spriteSpan, vec2(1.0e-8));

    local -=
        floor(local);

    return
        spriteMin +
        local *
        spriteSpan;
}


float sampleLabPBRDepthTiled(
    vec2 uv,
    vec2 spriteMin,
    vec2 spriteSpan,
    vec2 safeMin,
    vec2 safeMax
)
{


    return
        sampleLabPBRDepthInSprite(
            min(
                wrapPOMSpriteUV(uv, spriteMin, spriteSpan),
                safeMax
            ),
            safeMin,
            safeMax
        );
}


vec2 snapPOMSpriteUVToTexelCenter(
    vec2 uv,
    vec2 safeMin,
    vec2 safeMax
)
{
    ivec2 atlasSize = textureSize(normalAtlas, 0);
    vec2 texel = floor(uv * vec2(atlasSize));
    return clamp(
        (texel + vec2(0.5)) / vec2(atlasSize),
        safeMin,
        safeMax
    );
}


float getPOMDepthScale()
{

    return
        clamp(
            cam.historyParams.z,
            0.0,
            0.20
        );
}


struct ParallaxHit
{
    vec2 uv;

    float depth01;
    float borderScale;

    float depthWorld;
    float rayTravelWorld;

    vec3 worldPosition;

    bool valid;
};


ParallaxHit traceParallax(
    vec2 baseUV,
    vec2 spriteMin,
    vec2 safeMin,
    vec2 safeMax,
    vec2 spriteSpan,
    mat3 tbn,
    vec3 worldRayDirection
)
{
    float pomDepthScale =
        getPOMDepthScale();




    if (pomDepthScale <= 1.0e-5)
    {
        ParallaxHit result;

        result.uv =
            baseUV;

        result.depth01 =
            0.0;

        result.borderScale =
            1.0;

        result.depthWorld =
            0.0;

        result.rayTravelWorld =
            0.0;

        result.worldPosition =
            materialRayOrigin +
            materialRayDirection *
            materialHitT;

        result.valid =
            false;

        return result;
    }









    vec3 viewDirTS =
        normalize(
            transpose(tbn) *
            (- worldRayDirection)
        );




    if (viewDirTS.z < 0.0)
    {
        viewDirTS =
            - viewDirTS;
    }


    float viewZ =
        clamp(
            viewDirTS.z,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );












    const float POM_GRAZING_MIN_Z =
        0.04;

    const float POM_GRAZING_FULL_Z =
        0.10;


    float grazingFade =
        smoothstep(
            POM_GRAZING_MIN_Z,
            POM_GRAZING_FULL_Z,
            viewZ
        );


    float stableViewZ =
        max(
            viewZ,
            POM_GRAZING_MIN_Z
        );


    float facing =
        viewZ;







    float layerDensity =
        mix(
            48.0,
            32.0,
            facing
        );


    layerDensity =
        clamp(
            layerDensity,
            32.0,
            48.0
        );


    float layerStep =
        1.0 /
        layerDensity;



    vec2 totalUVOffset =
        (
            viewDirTS.xy /
            stableViewZ
        ) *
        spriteSpan *
        pomDepthScale *
        grazingFade;










    vec2 totalTileOffset =
        totalUVOffset /
        max(spriteSpan, vec2(1.0e-8));

    totalTileOffset =
        sign(totalTileOffset) *
        min(
            abs(totalTileOffset),
            vec2(1.0)
        );

    totalUVOffset =
        totalTileOffset *
        spriteSpan;



    vec2 currentUV =
        baseUV;


    float currentLayerDepth =
        0.0;


    float currentMapDepth =
        sampleLabPBRDepthInSprite(
            currentUV,
            safeMin,
            safeMax
        );





    for (
        int i = 0;
        i < 48;
        ++ i
    )
    {
        if (
            currentLayerDepth >=
            currentMapDepth
        )
        {
            break;
        }


        if (currentLayerDepth >= 1.0)
        {
            break;
        }


        float nextLayerDepth =
            min(
                currentLayerDepth +
                    layerStep,
                1.0
            );


        vec2 nextUV =
            baseUV -
            totalUVOffset *
            nextLayerDepth;






        nextUV =
            wrapPOMSpriteUV(
                nextUV,
                spriteMin,
                spriteSpan
            );










currentUV =
    nextUV;

currentLayerDepth =
    nextLayerDepth;

currentMapDepth =
    sampleLabPBRDepthTiled(
        currentUV,
        spriteMin,
        spriteSpan,
        safeMin,
        safeMax
    );

    }


    ParallaxHit result;





    result.uv =
        clamp(
            currentUV,
            safeMin,
            safeMax
        );






    vec2 pomAtlasSize =
        max(
            vec2(textureSize(normalAtlas, 0)),
            vec2(1.0)
        );

    vec2 edgeDistanceTexels =
        min(
            (result.uv - safeMin) * pomAtlasSize,
            (safeMax - result.uv) * pomAtlasSize
        );

    float nearestEdgeTexels =
        max(
            min(edgeDistanceTexels.x, edgeDistanceTexels.y),
            0.0
        );

    result.borderScale =
        smoothstep(
            0.0,
            2.0,
            nearestEdgeTexels
        );





    result.depth01 =
        currentMapDepth *
        grazingFade;


    result.depthWorld =
        result.depth01 *
        pomDepthScale;


    result.rayTravelWorld =
        result.depthWorld /
        stableViewZ;


    vec3 originalWorldHit =
        materialRayOrigin +
        materialRayDirection *
        materialHitT;


    result.worldPosition =
        originalWorldHit +
        normalize(
            worldRayDirection
        ) *
        result.rayTravelWorld;


    result.valid =
        true;


    return result;
}








float tracePOMSelfShadow(
    vec2 hitUV,
    float hitDepth01,
    vec2 spriteMin,
    vec2 safeMin,
    vec2 safeMax,
    vec2 spriteSpan,
    mat3 tbn,
    vec3 worldLightDirection
)
{
    float pomDepthScale =
        getPOMDepthScale();


    if (
        hitDepth01 <= 0.01 ||
        pomDepthScale <= 1.0e-5
    )
    {
        return 1.0;
    }


    vec3 lightDirTS =
        normalize(
            transpose(tbn) *
            normalize(worldLightDirection)
        );




    if (lightDirTS.z <= 1.0e-3)
    {
        return 1.0;
    }


    float facing =
        clamp(
            lightDirTS.z,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );


    float lightZ =
        max(
            lightDirTS.z,
            0.05
        );




















    float grazingBoost =
        clamp(
            0.25 / lightZ,
            1.0,
            4.0
        );




    const int VL_POM_SELF_SHADOW_MAX_SAMPLES = 24;


    int sampleCount =
        int(
            round(
                mix(
                    8.0,
                    4.0,
                    facing
                ) *
                grazingBoost
            )
        );


    sampleCount =
        clamp(
            sampleCount,
            4,
            VL_POM_SELF_SHADOW_MAX_SAMPLES
        );


















    const float SELF_SHADOW_FULL_NORMALIZED_DEPTH =
        0.02;


    float maxBlockDepthNormalized =
        0.0;





    vec2 selfShadowTileOffset =
        (
            lightDirTS.xy /
            lightZ
        ) *
        pomDepthScale;


    selfShadowTileOffset =
        sign(selfShadowTileOffset) *
        min(
            abs(selfShadowTileOffset),
            vec2(1.0)
        );



    for (int i = 1; i <= VL_POM_SELF_SHADOW_MAX_SAMPLES; ++ i)
    {
        if (i > sampleCount)
        {
            break;
        }




        float progress =
            float(i) /
            float(sampleCount + 1);


        float depthRise =
            hitDepth01 *
            progress;


        float rayDepth =
            hitDepth01 -
            depthRise;


        vec2 sampleUV =
            hitUV +
            selfShadowTileOffset *
            spriteSpan *
            depthRise;







        float blockerDepth =
            sampleLabPBRDepthTiled(
                sampleUV,
                spriteMin,
                spriteSpan,
                safeMin,
                safeMax
            );























        float blockDepth =
            rayDepth -
            blockerDepth -
            0.004;


        float blockDepthNormalized =
            max(
                blockDepth,
                0.0
            );


        maxBlockDepthNormalized =
            max(
                maxBlockDepthNormalized,
                blockDepthNormalized
            );




        if (
            maxBlockDepthNormalized >=
            SELF_SHADOW_FULL_NORMALIZED_DEPTH
        )
        {
            return 0.0;
        }
    }


    return
        1.0 -
        smoothstep(
            0.0,
            SELF_SHADOW_FULL_NORMALIZED_DEPTH,
            maxBlockDepthNormalized
        );
}








bool voxyTileIsFullyCovered(vec2 uv0, vec2 uv1, vec2 uv2)
{
    vec2 tileOrigin = min(uv0, min(uv1, uv2)) - vec2(0.5 / VOXY_ATLAS_SIZE);
    vec2 tileCentre = tileOrigin
        + vec2( (VOXY_TILE_PIXELS * 0.5) / VOXY_ATLAS_SIZE);
    return textureLod(voxyLodAtlas, tileCentre, 4.0).a >= 0.985;
}
