#line 1 "./material_resolve.glsl"




































void resolveHitMaterial(vec3 rayOrigin, vec3 rayDirection)
{


    if (payload.hitDistance < 0.0)
    {
        return;
    }

    const uint instanceIndex =
        uint(payload.normal.x + 0.5);

    const uint primitiveIndex =
        uint(payload.normal.y + 0.5);

    const vec3 barycentrics =
        vec3(
            1.0 - payload.normal.z - payload.roughness,
            payload.normal.z,
            payload.roughness
        );

    const float hitT =
        payload.hitDistance;






    materialHitT = hitT;
    materialRayOrigin = rayOrigin;
    materialRayDirection = rayDirection;

    const uvec4 sectionAddressEntry =
        sectionVertexAddressBuffer.vertexAddresses[instanceIndex];

    uint64_t rawAddr =
        packUint2x32(sectionAddressEntry.xy);

    if (rawAddr == 0ul)
    {
        payload.hitDistance = - 1.0;
        payload.state = makeHitState(
            getRayPurpose(payload.state), RT_HIT_OPAQUE,
            RT_WATER_NONE, false, false, 0u
        );
        payload.parallaxRayTravel = 0.0;
        payload.pomSelfShadow = 1.0;
        payload.pomMicroOcclusion = 1.0;
        payload.glassAlpha = 1.0;
        payload.prevOffsetX = 0.0;
        payload.prevOffsetY = 0.0;
        payload.prevOffsetZ = 0.0;
        return;
    }

    Vertices verts =
        Vertices(rawAddr);

    uint firstVertex =
        primitiveIndex * 3u;

    Vertex v0 = verts.v[firstVertex + 0u];
    Vertex v1 = verts.v[firstVertex + 1u];
    Vertex v2 = verts.v[firstVertex + 2u];




    {
        vec3 hitWorldOffset =
            vec3(0.0);

        if (sectionAddressEntry.z != 0u)
        {
            VertexMotions sectionMotions =
                VertexMotions(
                    rawAddr + uint64_t(sectionAddressEntry.z)
                );

            hitWorldOffset =
                readVertexMotion(sectionMotions, firstVertex + 0u) * barycentrics.x +
                readVertexMotion(sectionMotions, firstVertex + 1u) * barycentrics.y +
                readVertexMotion(sectionMotions, firstVertex + 2u) * barycentrics.z;



            hitWorldOffset +=
                cam.prevViewInverse[3].xyz -
                cam.viewInverse[3].xyz;
        }

        payload.prevOffsetX = hitWorldOffset.x;
        payload.prevOffsetY = hitWorldOffset.y;
        payload.prevOffsetZ = hitWorldOffset.z;
    }

float interpU =
    v0.u * barycentrics.x +
    v1.u * barycentrics.y +
    v2.u * barycentrics.z;


float interpV =
    v0.v * barycentrics.x +
    v1.v * barycentrics.y +
    v2.v * barycentrics.z;


vec2 texCoord =
    vec2(
        interpU,
        interpV
    );


float materialId =
    v0.materialId * barycentrics.x +
    v1.materialId * barycentrics.y +
    v2.materialId * barycentrics.z;





float voxyLodMarker =
    v0.pad1 * barycentrics.x +
    v1.pad1 * barycentrics.y +
    v2.pad1 * barycentrics.z;









const uint instanceRole = materialLodRole(instanceIndex);
bool isDhLod = instanceRole == LOD_ROLE_DH_FAR;
float dhLodMaterial =
    isDhLod
        ? clamp(round(- voxyLodMarker) - 3.0, 0.0, 15.0)
        : 0.0;





























bool isVoxyLod =
                  0 != 0 && ! isDhLod && voxyLodMarker < - 0.5 && materialId > 0.5;
bool isVoxyEmissive =
                  0 != 0 && ! isDhLod && voxyLodMarker < - 1.5 && materialId > 0.5;




float rainPuddleCandidate =
    isVoxyLod ? 0.0 : clamp(
        (v0.pad1 * barycentrics.x +
         v1.pad1 * barycentrics.y +
         v2.pad1 * barycentrics.z) * 4.0,
        0.0,
        1.0
    );




float voxyMipShade =
    v0.pad2 * barycentrics.x +
    v1.pad2 * barycentrics.y +
    v2.pad2 * barycentrics.z;




float encodedTextureSlot =
    (isVoxyLod || isDhLod) ? 0.0 :
    (v0.pad2 * barycentrics.x +
     v1.pad2 * barycentrics.y +
     v2.pad2 * barycentrics.z);
uint entityTextureSlot = encodedTextureSlot > 0.5
    ? uint(round(encodedTextureSlot)) : 0u;
uint particleTextureSlot = encodedTextureSlot < - 0.5
    ? uint(round(- encodedTextureSlot)) : 0u;
bool isEntityMaterial = entityTextureSlot != 0u;
bool isParticleMaterial = particleTextureSlot != 0u;






float entityMaterialClass =
    isEntityMaterial
        ? floor(fract(encodedTextureSlot) * 32.0 + 0.5)
        : 0.0;
float particleEmissionMarker =
    v0.pad1 * barycentrics.x +
    v1.pad1 * barycentrics.y +
    v2.pad1 * barycentrics.z;
float entityEmissionMarker = particleEmissionMarker;
bool isRtWeatherRain =
    isParticleMaterial &&
    particleEmissionMarker < - 1.5;
    vec4 c0 = vec4(v0.r, v0.g, v0.b, v0.a);
    vec4 c1 = vec4(v1.r, v1.g, v1.b, v1.a);
    vec4 c2 = vec4(v2.r, v2.g, v2.b, v2.a);
    vec4 interpColor = c0 * barycentrics.x + c1 * barycentrics.y + c2 * barycentrics.z;

    vec3 pos0 = vec3(v0.x, v0.y, v0.z);
    vec3 pos1 = vec3(v1.x, v1.y, v1.z);
    vec3 pos2 = vec3(v2.x, v2.y, v2.z);

    vec3 geoNormal = normalize(cross(pos1 - pos0, pos2 - pos0));











    if (getRayPurpose(payload.state) == RT_PURPOSE_DISTANCE_ONLY)
    {
        payload.hitDistance = hitT;

        payload.state = makeHitState(
            RT_PURPOSE_DISTANCE_ONLY,
            RT_HIT_OPAQUE,
            RT_WATER_NONE,
            false,
            false,
            0u
        );

        return;
    }












    if (getRayPurpose(payload.state) == RT_PURPOSE_CLASSIFY)
    {
        bool classifyIsWater =
            materialId > 0.5 &&
            materialId < 1.5;

        bool classifyIsGlass =
            materialId > 1.5 &&
            materialId < 2.5;

        bool hitBackFace =
            dot(
                geoNormal,
                rayDirection
            ) > 0.0;

        vec3 fixedNormal =
            geoNormal;

        if (classifyIsWater && abs(geoNormal.y) > 0.5)
        {
            vec3 geometricHitPosition =
                rayOrigin +
                rayDirection *
                hitT;



            WaterWaveSurface waterWave =
                evaluateWaterWaveSurfaceClassify(
                    geometricHitPosition.xz,
                    geometricHitPosition.y
                );

            fixedNormal =
                dot(waterWave.normal, geoNormal) < 0.0
                    ? - waterWave.normal
                    : waterWave.normal;
        }

        if (dot(fixedNormal, rayDirection) > 0.0)
        {
            fixedNormal = - fixedNormal;
        }










        vec4 classifyAlbedo =
            vec4(1.0);

        if (classifyIsGlass)
        {
            classifyAlbedo =
                (isVoxyLod || isDhLod)
                    ? vec4(
                        voxyLodAlbedo(
                            voxyMipShade,
                            texCoord,
                            vec2(v0.u, v0.v),
                            vec2(v1.u, v1.v),
                            vec2(v2.u, v2.v),
                            voxyLodMip(pos0, pos1, geoNormal),
                            interpColor.rgb
                        ),
                        interpColor.a
                      )
                    : (isParticleMaterial
                        ? sampleParticleAtlas(particleTextureSlot, texCoord)
                        : sampleRTAlbedo(entityTextureSlot, texCoord)) *
                      interpColor;
        }

        payload.hitDistance =
            hitT;

        payload.parallaxRayTravel =
            0.0;

        payload.pomSelfShadow =
            1.0;
        payload.pomMicroOcclusion =
            1.0;

        payload.normal =
            normalize(fixedNormal);

        payload.albedo =
            classifyAlbedo.rgb;

        payload.glassAlpha =
            classifyIsGlass
                ? clamp(
                    classifyAlbedo.a,
                    0.0,
                    1.0
                )
                : 1.0;

        if (classifyIsGlass)
        {
            bool glassBottomFace =
                geoNormal.y < - 0.5;

            payload.state = makeHitState(
                getRayPurpose(payload.state),
                RT_HIT_TRANSLUCENT,
                RT_WATER_NONE,
                true,
                glassBottomFace,
                uint(getTransparentProfileFromRawMaterialId(materialId))
            );
        }
        else if (classifyIsWater)
        {
            payload.state = makeHitState(
                getRayPurpose(payload.state),
                RT_HIT_WATER,
                hitBackFace ? RT_WATER_WATER_TO_AIR : RT_WATER_AIR_TO_WATER,
                false,
                false,
                0u
            );
        }
        else
        {
            payload.state = makeHitState(
                getRayPurpose(payload.state),
                RT_HIT_OPAQUE,
                RT_WATER_NONE,
                false,
                false,
                0u
            );
        }

        payload.roughness =
            1.0;

        payload.f0Metal =
            0.0;

        payload.emission =
            vec3(0.0);

        return;
    }

    if (isVoxyLod)
    {





        bool isVoxyWater = materialId > 2.5 && materialId < 3.5;
        bool isVoxyGlass = materialId > 1.5 && materialId < 2.5;







        bool voxyWaterSurface = isVoxyWater && abs(geoNormal.y) > 0.5;





        bool isVoxyLeaves = materialId > 3.5 && materialId < 4.5;
        vec3 fixedNormal = dot(geoNormal, rayDirection) > 0.0
            ? - geoNormal : geoNormal;
        vec3 voxyAlbedo = voxyLodAlbedo(
            voxyMipShade,
            texCoord,
            vec2(v0.u, v0.v),
            vec2(v1.u, v1.v),
            vec2(v2.u, v2.v),
            voxyLodMip(pos0, pos1, geoNormal),
            interpColor.rgb
        );
        payload.hitDistance = hitT;
        payload.parallaxRayTravel = 0.0;
        payload.pomSelfShadow = 1.0;
        payload.pomMicroOcclusion = 1.0;
        payload.normal = normalize(fixedNormal);
        payload.albedo = voxyAlbedo;
        payload.glassAlpha = isVoxyGlass ? clamp(interpColor.a, 0.06, 0.72) : 1.0;






        payload.state = voxyWaterSurface
            ? makeHitState(getRayPurpose(payload.state), RT_HIT_WATER,
                RT_WATER_NONE,
                false, false, 0u)
            : makeHitState(getRayPurpose(payload.state),
                isVoxyGlass ? RT_HIT_TRANSLUCENT : RT_HIT_OPAQUE,
                RT_WATER_NONE, isVoxyGlass, isVoxyGlass && geoNormal.y < - 0.5, 0u);

        payload.roughness = voxyWaterSurface ? 0.08 : (isVoxyLeaves ? 0.92 : 0.78);
        payload.f0Metal = 0.0;
        payload.emission = isVoxyEmissive ? voxyAlbedo * 4.0 : vec3(0.0);
        return;
    }
















    if (isDhLod && ! isParticleMaterial && ! isEntityMaterial)
    {


        bool dhIsWater = abs(dhLodMaterial - 12.0) < 0.5;
        bool dhIsLava = abs(dhLodMaterial - 6.0) < 0.5;
        bool dhIsLit = abs(dhLodMaterial - 15.0) < 0.5;
        bool dhIsMetal = abs(dhLodMaterial - 4.0) < 0.5;
        bool dhIsLeaves = abs(dhLodMaterial - 1.0) < 0.5;
        bool dhIsSnow = abs(dhLodMaterial - 8.0) < 0.5;

        vec3 dhHitPosition =
            rayOrigin +
            rayDirection * hitT;



        vec3 dhAlbedo = dhLodAlbedo(
            floor(texCoord.x + 0.5),
            floor(texCoord.y + 0.5),
            dhHitPosition,
            geoNormal,
            interpColor.rgb
        );

        bool dhBackFace =
            dot(geoNormal, rayDirection) > 0.0;
        vec3 dhNormal = dhBackFace ? - geoNormal : geoNormal;






        if (dhIsWater && abs(geoNormal.y) > 0.5)
        {
            WaterWaveSurface dhWave =
                evaluateWaterWaveSurface(
                    dhHitPosition.xz,
                    dhHitPosition.y
                );

            dhNormal =
                dot(dhWave.normal, geoNormal) < 0.0
                    ? - dhWave.normal
                    : dhWave.normal;

            if (dot(dhNormal, rayDirection) > 0.0)
            {
                dhNormal = - dhNormal;
            }
        }

        payload.hitDistance = hitT;
        payload.parallaxRayTravel = 0.0;
        payload.pomSelfShadow = 1.0;
        payload.pomMicroOcclusion = 1.0;
        payload.normal = normalize(dhNormal);
        payload.albedo = dhAlbedo;
        payload.glassAlpha = 1.0;
        payload.state = dhIsWater
            ? makeHitState(
                getRayPurpose(payload.state),
                RT_HIT_WATER,
                dhBackFace ? RT_WATER_WATER_TO_AIR : RT_WATER_AIR_TO_WATER,
                false,
                false,
                0u
            )
            : makeHitState(
                getRayPurpose(payload.state),
                RT_HIT_OPAQUE,
                RT_WATER_NONE,
                false,
                false,
                0u
            );







        float dhRoughness = 0.78;
        float dhF0 = 0.0;
        vec3 dhEmission = vec3(0.0);
        if (dhIsWater)
        {
            dhRoughness = 0.08;
        }
        else if (dhIsLava)
        {
            dhRoughness = 0.92;
            dhEmission = dhAlbedo * 4.0;
        }
        else if (dhIsLit)
        {
            dhRoughness = 0.86;
            dhEmission = dhAlbedo * 4.0;
        }
        else if (dhIsMetal)
        {
            dhRoughness = 0.35;
            dhF0 = 230.0 / 255.0;
        }
        else if (dhIsLeaves)
        {
            dhRoughness = 0.92;
        }
        else if (dhIsSnow)
        {
            dhRoughness = 0.45;
        }

        payload.roughness = dhRoughness;
        payload.f0Metal = dhF0;



        payload.emission = dhEmission + dhBakedBlockLight(dhAlbedo, interpColor.a);
        return;
    }





    if (isEntityMaterial || isParticleMaterial)
    {
        vec3 fixedEntityNormal = geoNormal;
        if (dot(fixedEntityNormal, rayDirection) > 0.0)
        {
            fixedEntityNormal = - fixedEntityNormal;
        }

        vec4 entityAlbedo =
            (isRtWeatherRain
                ? vec4(
                    mix(
                        vec3(0.58, 0.68, 0.78),
                        sampleRainStreakAtlas(texCoord).rgb,
                        0.35
                    ) * RAIN_VISIBILITY_GAIN,
                    1.0
                )
                : isParticleMaterial
                ? sampleParticleAtlas(particleTextureSlot, texCoord)
                : sampleRTAlbedo(entityTextureSlot, texCoord)) *
            interpColor;

        payload.hitDistance = hitT;
        payload.parallaxRayTravel = 0.0;
        payload.pomSelfShadow = 1.0;
        payload.pomMicroOcclusion = 1.0;
        payload.glassAlpha = 1.0;





        vec4 entityNormalTexel;
        vec4 entitySpecularTexel;
        sampleEntityPbr(entityTextureSlot, texCoord, entityNormalTexel, entitySpecularTexel);
        bool hasEntityNormalMap =
            dot(entityNormalTexel.rgb, entityNormalTexel.rgb) >= 1.0e-4;
        bool hasEntitySpecularMap =
            any(greaterThan(entitySpecularTexel.rgb, vec3(0.0)));




        if (hasEntityNormalMap)
        {
            payload.pomMicroOcclusion =
                clamp(entityNormalTexel.b, 0.0, 1.0);
        }




        vec3 entityNormal = normalize(fixedEntityNormal);
        vec3 referenceUp = abs(entityNormal.y) < 0.9
            ? vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0)
            : vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);
        vec3 entityTangent = normalize(cross(referenceUp, entityNormal));
        vec3 entityBitangent = cross(entityNormal, entityTangent);

        if (hasEntityNormalMap)
        {


            vec2 tangentNormalXY = entityNormalTexel.rg * 2.0 - 1.0;
            float tangentNormalZ =
                sqrt(max(0.0, 1.0 - dot(tangentNormalXY, tangentNormalXY)));
            entityNormal = normalize(
                entityTangent * tangentNormalXY.x +
                entityBitangent * tangentNormalXY.y +
                entityNormal * tangentNormalZ);
        }
        else if (entityMaterialClass > 0.5 && entityMaterialClass < 6.5)
        {









            const float ENTITY_DETAIL_STEP = 1.0 / 64.0;
            const float ENTITY_DETAIL_GAIN = 3.5;
            float hCenter = dot(entityAlbedo.rgb, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
            vec3 albedoU = sampleRTAlbedo(
                entityTextureSlot, texCoord + vec2(ENTITY_DETAIL_STEP, 0.0)).rgb;
            vec3 albedoV = sampleRTAlbedo(
                entityTextureSlot, texCoord + vec2(0.0, ENTITY_DETAIL_STEP)).rgb;
            float hU = dot(albedoU, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
            float hV = dot(albedoV, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));

            vec2 gradient = clamp(
                vec2(hU - hCenter, hV - hCenter) * ENTITY_DETAIL_GAIN,
                vec2(- 0.45), vec2(0.45));
            entityNormal = normalize(
                entityNormal - (entityTangent * gradient.x +
                    entityBitangent * gradient.y));
        }
        payload.normal = entityNormal;
        payload.albedo = entityAlbedo.rgb;
        payload.state = makeHitState(
            getRayPurpose(payload.state), RT_HIT_OPAQUE,
            RT_WATER_NONE, false, false, 0u
        );





        float entityRoughness = 0.72;
        float entityF0 = 0.0;
        if (entityMaterialClass > 0.5)
        {
            if (entityMaterialClass < 1.5)
            {

                entityRoughness = 0.35;
                entityF0 = 230.0 / 255.0;
            }
            else if (entityMaterialClass < 2.5)
            {

                entityRoughness = 0.25;
                entityF0 = 230.0 / 255.0;
            }
            else if (entityMaterialClass < 3.5)
            {

                entityRoughness = 0.22;
                entityF0 = 230.0 / 255.0;
            }
            else if (entityMaterialClass < 4.5)
            {

                entityRoughness = 0.42;
                entityF0 = 230.0 / 255.0;
            }
            else if (entityMaterialClass < 5.5)
            {

                entityRoughness = 0.30;
                entityF0 = 230.0 / 255.0;
            }
            else
            {

                entityRoughness = 0.62;
                entityF0 = 10.0 / 255.0;
            }
        }





        float entityLabPbrEmissive = 0.0;
        if (hasEntitySpecularMap)
        {
            entityRoughness = pow(
                1.0 - clamp(entitySpecularTexel.r, 0.0, 1.0), 2.0);
            entityF0 = clamp(entitySpecularTexel.g, 0.0, 1.0);
            int entityEmissiveCode =
                int(round(clamp(entitySpecularTexel.a, 0.0, 1.0) * 255.0));
            entityLabPbrEmissive = entityEmissiveCode >= 255
                ? 0.0
                : clamp(float(entityEmissiveCode) / 254.0, 0.0, 1.0);
        }
        payload.roughness = entityRoughness;
        payload.f0Metal = entityF0;


        payload.emission =
            (isParticleMaterial && particleEmissionMarker > 0.5) ||
            (isEntityMaterial && entityEmissionMarker < - 0.5)
                ? entityAlbedo.rgb * 10.0
                : vec3(0.0);


        payload.emission += entityAlbedo.rgb * (entityLabPbrEmissive * 10.0);
        return;
    }











    vec2 duv1 = vec2(v1.u - v0.u, v1.v - v0.v);
    vec2 duv2 = vec2(v2.u - v0.u, v2.v - v0.v);
    vec3 dp1 = pos1 - pos0;
    vec3 dp2 = pos2 - pos0;

    float determinant =
        duv1.x * duv2.y -
        duv1.y * duv2.x;

    vec3 tangent =
        vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);

    vec3 bitangent =
        vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);


    if (abs(determinant) > 1.0e-6)
    {
        float invDet =
            1.0 /
            determinant;


        vec3 rawTangent =
            (
                dp1 * duv2.y -
                dp2 * duv1.y
            ) *
            invDet;



        rawTangent -=
            geoNormal *
            dot(
                rawTangent,
                geoNormal
            );


        if (dot(rawTangent, rawTangent) > 1.0e-12)
        {
            tangent =
                normalize(
                    rawTangent
                );


            float handedness =
                determinant < 0.0
                    ? - 1.0
                    : 1.0;


            bitangent =
                normalize(
                    cross(
                        geoNormal,
                        tangent
                    )
                ) *
                handedness;
        }
        else
        {
            vec3 c1 =
                cross(
                    geoNormal,
                    vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)
                );

            vec3 c2 =
                cross(
                    geoNormal,
                    vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0)
                );

            tangent =
                length(c1) > length(c2)
                    ? c1
                    : c2;

            tangent =
                normalize(
                    tangent
                );

            bitangent =
                normalize(
                    cross(
                        geoNormal,
                        tangent
                    )
                );
        }
    }
    else
    {
        vec3 c1 =
            cross(
                geoNormal,
                vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)
            );

        vec3 c2 =
            cross(
                geoNormal,
                vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0)
            );

        tangent =
            length(c1) > length(c2)
                ? c1
                : c2;

        tangent =
            normalize(
                tangent
            );

        bitangent =
            normalize(
                cross(
                    geoNormal,
                    tangent
                )
            );
    }


    mat3 tbn =
        mat3(
            tangent,
            bitangent,
            geoNormal
        );








vec2 uv0 =
    vec2(
        v0.u,
        v0.v
    );

vec2 uv1 =
    vec2(
        v1.u,
        v1.v

        ? 0.0

        );
payload.hitDistance = hitT;

        ? parallaxHit.rayTravelWorld
        : 0.0;

    ) > 0.0;

    worldNormal;

{
    vec3 geometricHitPosition =
        rayOrigin + rayDirection * hitT;
    WaterWaveSurface waterWave =
        evaluateWaterWaveSurface(
            geometricHitPosition.xz,
            geometricHitPosition.y
        );
    fixedNormal = dot(waterWave.normal, geoNormal) < 0.0
        ? - waterWave.normal : waterWave.normal;
}

if (
    dot(
        fixedNormal,

    ) > 0.0
)
{
    fixedNormal =
        - fixedNormal;
}

        getTimeDrivenSkyLightDirection();


    if (
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale() > 0.0 &&
        dot(

            pomSunDirection
        ) > 0.0
    )
    {
        pomSelfShadow =

                texCoord,

                spriteMin,
                safeMin,
                safeMax,
                spriteSpan,
                tbn,

            );
    }
}































payload.pomSelfShadow =

        0.5,

    ) *
    materialAO;


payload.glassAlpha =

        materialId > 1.5 &&
        materialId < 2.5
    )
        ? clamp(
            albedoColor.a,
            0.0,
            1.0
        )
        : (rainPuddleCandidate > 0.5 ? 0.25 : 1.0);

payload.normal =
    normalize(fixedNormal);

payload.albedo =
    albedoColor.rgb;














bool t12IsWater =
    materialId > 0.5 &&
    materialId < 1.5;






const bool t12IsPortal =
    materialId > 5.5 &&
    materialId < 6.5;
bool t12IsGlass =
    (materialId > 1.5 &&
     materialId < 2.5) ||
    t12IsPortal;


payload.albedo =
    albedoColor.rgb;




if (t12IsGlass)
{



















    bool glassBottomFace =
        geoNormal.y < - 0.5;


    payload.state = makeHitState(
        getRayPurpose(payload.state), RT_HIT_TRANSLUCENT,
        RT_WATER_NONE, true, glassBottomFace,
        uint(getTransparentProfileFromRawMaterialId(materialId))
    );










    if (materialId < 2.05 && glassVertexFactorIsFaceShade(interpColor.rgb))
    {
        payload.state |= RT_STATE_CLEAR_GLASS_BIT;
    }
}
else if (t12IsWater)
{
    payload.state = makeHitState(
        getRayPurpose(payload.state), RT_HIT_WATER,
        hitBackFace ? RT_WATER_WATER_TO_AIR : RT_WATER_AIR_TO_WATER,
        false, false, 0u
    );
}
else
{
    payload.state = makeHitState(
        getRayPurpose(payload.state), RT_HIT_OPAQUE,
        RT_WATER_NONE, false, false, 0u
    );
}













{
    vec3 macroHitPos =
        rayOrigin + rayDirection * hitT;
    vec2 macroP = macroHitPos.xz * (1.0 / 9.0) +
        vec2(macroHitPos.y * (1.0 / 23.0));
    vec2 macroCell = floor(macroP);
    vec2 macroF = fract(macroP);
    macroF = macroF * macroF * (3.0 - 2.0 * macroF);
    float macroHashX = macroCell.x * 127.1 + macroCell.y * 311.7;
    float macroN00 = fract(sin(macroHashX) * 43758.5453);
    float macroN10 = fract(sin(macroHashX + 127.1) * 43758.5453);
    float macroN01 = fract(sin(macroHashX + 311.7) * 43758.5453);
    float macroN11 = fract(sin(macroHashX + 438.8) * 43758.5453);
    float macroNoise = mix(
        mix(macroN00, macroN10, macroF.x),
        mix(macroN01, macroN11, macroF.x),
        macroF.y);
    albedoColor *= 1.0 + (macroNoise - 0.5) * 0.12;
    roughness = clamp(roughness + (macroNoise - 0.5) * 0.16, 0.0, 1.0);
}


payload.roughness =
    roughness;

payload.f0Metal =
    f0Metal;


payload.emission =
    t12IsPortal
        ? albedoColor.rgb * 4.0
        : (t12IsGlass
            ? vec3(0.0)
            : albedoColor.rgb * emission * 10.0);
}

void resolveHitMaterialGlass(vec3 rayOrigin, vec3 rayDirection, vec3 barycentrics)
{
    if (payload.hitDistance < 0.0)
    {
        return;
    }

    const uint instanceIndex =
        uint(payload.normal.x + 0.5);

    const uint primitiveIndex =
        uint(payload.normal.y + 0.5);

    const float hitT =
        payload.hitDistance;


    materialHitT = hitT;
    materialRayOrigin = rayOrigin;
    materialRayDirection = rayDirection;

    const uvec4 sectionAddressEntry =
        sectionVertexAddressBuffer.vertexAddresses[instanceIndex];

    uint64_t rawAddr =
        packUint2x32(sectionAddressEntry.xy);

    if (rawAddr == 0ul)
    {
        payload.hitDistance = - 1.0;
        payload.state = makeHitState(
            getRayPurpose(payload.state), RT_HIT_OPAQUE,
            RT_WATER_NONE, false, false, 0u
        );
        payload.parallaxRayTravel = 0.0;
        payload.pomSelfShadow = 1.0;
        payload.pomMicroOcclusion = 1.0;
        payload.glassAlpha = 1.0;
        payload.prevOffsetX = 0.0;
        payload.prevOffsetY = 0.0;
        payload.prevOffsetZ = 0.0;
        return;
    }

    Vertices verts = Vertices(rawAddr);

    uint firstVertex =
        primitiveIndex * 3u;

    Vertex v0 = verts.v[firstVertex + 0u];
    Vertex v1 = verts.v[firstVertex + 1u];
    Vertex v2 = verts.v[firstVertex + 2u];

    {
        vec3 hitWorldOffset = vec3(0.0);

        if (sectionAddressEntry.z != 0u)
        {
            VertexMotions sectionMotions =
                VertexMotions(rawAddr + uint64_t(sectionAddressEntry.z));

            hitWorldOffset =
                readVertexMotion(sectionMotions, firstVertex + 0u) * barycentrics.x +
                readVertexMotion(sectionMotions, firstVertex + 1u) * barycentrics.y +
                readVertexMotion(sectionMotions, firstVertex + 2u) * barycentrics.z;

            hitWorldOffset +=
                cam.prevViewInverse[3].xyz -
                cam.viewInverse[3].xyz;
        }

        payload.prevOffsetX = hitWorldOffset.x;
        payload.prevOffsetY = hitWorldOffset.y;
        payload.prevOffsetZ = hitWorldOffset.z;
    }

float interpU =
    v0.u * barycentrics.x +
    v1.u * barycentrics.y +
    v2.u * barycentrics.z;


float interpV =
    v0.v * barycentrics.x +
    v1.v * barycentrics.y +
    v2.v * barycentrics.z;


vec2 texCoord =
    vec2(
        interpU,
        interpV
    );


float materialId =
    v0.materialId * barycentrics.x +
    v1.materialId * barycentrics.y +
    v2.materialId * barycentrics.z;
float voxyLodMarker =
    v0.pad1 * barycentrics.x +
    v1.pad1 * barycentrics.y +
    v2.pad1 * barycentrics.z;






const uint instanceRole = materialLodRole(instanceIndex);
bool isDhLod = instanceRole == LOD_ROLE_DH_FAR;
float dhLodMaterial =
    isDhLod
        ? clamp(round(- voxyLodMarker) - 3.0, 0.0, 15.0)
        : 0.0;
bool isVoxyLod = ! isDhLod && voxyLodMarker < - 0.5;
bool isVoxyEmissive = ! isDhLod && voxyLodMarker < - 1.5;
    vec4 c0 = vec4(v0.r, v0.g, v0.b, v0.a);
    vec4 c1 = vec4(v1.r, v1.g, v1.b, v1.a);
    vec4 c2 = vec4(v2.r, v2.g, v2.b, v2.a);
    vec4 interpColor = c0 * barycentrics.x + c1 * barycentrics.y + c2 * barycentrics.z;

    vec3 pos0 = vec3(v0.x, v0.y, v0.z);
    vec3 pos1 = vec3(v1.x, v1.y, v1.z);
    vec3 pos2 = vec3(v2.x, v2.y, v2.z);

    vec3 geoNormal = normalize(cross(pos1 - pos0, pos2 - pos0));








    if (getRayPurpose(payload.state) == RT_PURPOSE_DISTANCE_ONLY)
    {
        payload.hitDistance = hitT;

        payload.state = makeHitState(
            RT_PURPOSE_DISTANCE_ONLY,
            RT_HIT_OPAQUE,
            RT_WATER_NONE,
            false,
            false,
            0u
        );

        return;
    }





    if (isVoxyLod)
    {





        bool isVoxyWater = materialId > 2.5 && materialId < 3.5;









        bool voxyWaterSurface = isVoxyWater && abs(geoNormal.y) > 0.5;
        float voxyMipShade =
            v0.pad2 * barycentrics.x +
            v1.pad2 * barycentrics.y +
            v2.pad2 * barycentrics.z;








        bool isVoxyGlass =
            ! isVoxyEmissive &&
            materialId > 1.5 && materialId < 2.5 &&
            (voxyMipShade < 0.0 ||
                voxyTileIsFullyCovered(
                    vec2(v0.u, v0.v),
                    vec2(v1.u, v1.v),
                    vec2(v2.u, v2.v)));








        bool isVoxyLeaves = materialId > 3.5 && materialId < 4.5;

        vec3 fixedNormal = dot(geoNormal, rayDirection) > 0.0
            ? - geoNormal : geoNormal;
        vec3 voxyAlbedo = voxyLodAlbedo(
            voxyMipShade,
            texCoord,
            vec2(v0.u, v0.v),
            vec2(v1.u, v1.v),
            vec2(v2.u, v2.v),
            voxyLodMip(pos0, pos1, geoNormal),
            interpColor.rgb
        );
        payload.hitDistance = hitT;
        payload.parallaxRayTravel = 0.0;
        payload.pomSelfShadow = 1.0;
        payload.pomMicroOcclusion = 1.0;
        payload.normal = normalize(fixedNormal);
        payload.albedo = voxyAlbedo;
        payload.glassAlpha = isVoxyGlass ? clamp(interpColor.a, 0.06, 0.72) : 1.0;






        payload.state = voxyWaterSurface
            ? makeHitState(getRayPurpose(payload.state), RT_HIT_WATER,
                RT_WATER_NONE,
                false, false, 0u)
            : makeHitState(getRayPurpose(payload.state),
                isVoxyGlass ? RT_HIT_TRANSLUCENT : RT_HIT_OPAQUE,
                RT_WATER_NONE, isVoxyGlass, isVoxyGlass && geoNormal.y < - 0.5, 0u);

        payload.roughness = voxyWaterSurface ? 0.08 : (isVoxyLeaves ? 0.92 : 0.78);
        payload.f0Metal = 0.0;
        payload.emission = isVoxyEmissive ? voxyAlbedo * 4.0 : vec3(0.0);
        return;
    }


















    if (isDhLod)
    {


        bool dhIsWater = abs(dhLodMaterial - 12.0) < 0.5;
        bool dhIsLava = abs(dhLodMaterial - 6.0) < 0.5;
        bool dhIsLit = abs(dhLodMaterial - 15.0) < 0.5;
        bool dhIsMetal = abs(dhLodMaterial - 4.0) < 0.5;
        bool dhIsLeaves = abs(dhLodMaterial - 1.0) < 0.5;
        bool dhIsSnow = abs(dhLodMaterial - 8.0) < 0.5;

        vec3 dhHitPosition =
            rayOrigin +
            rayDirection * hitT;

        float dhTileId =
            v0.u * barycentrics.x +
            v1.u * barycentrics.y +
            v2.u * barycentrics.z;
        float dhNormalIndex =
            v0.v * barycentrics.x +
            v1.v * barycentrics.y +
            v2.v * barycentrics.z;
        vec3 dhAlbedo = dhLodAlbedo(
            floor(dhTileId + 0.5),
            floor(dhNormalIndex + 0.5),
            dhHitPosition,
            geoNormal,
            interpColor.rgb
        );

        bool dhBackFace =
            dot(geoNormal, rayDirection) > 0.0;
        vec3 dhNormal = dhBackFace ? - geoNormal : geoNormal;




        if (dhIsWater && abs(geoNormal.y) > 0.5)
        {
            WaterWaveSurface dhWave =
                evaluateWaterWaveSurface(
                    dhHitPosition.xz,
                    dhHitPosition.y
                );

            dhNormal =
                dot(dhWave.normal, geoNormal) < 0.0
                    ? - dhWave.normal
                    : dhWave.normal;

            if (dot(dhNormal, rayDirection) > 0.0)
            {
                dhNormal = - dhNormal;
            }
        }

        payload.hitDistance = hitT;
        payload.parallaxRayTravel = 0.0;
        payload.pomSelfShadow = 1.0;
        payload.pomMicroOcclusion = 1.0;
        payload.normal = normalize(dhNormal);
        payload.albedo = dhAlbedo;


        payload.glassAlpha = 1.0;
        payload.state = dhIsWater
            ? makeHitState(
                getRayPurpose(payload.state),
                RT_HIT_WATER,
                dhBackFace ? RT_WATER_WATER_TO_AIR : RT_WATER_AIR_TO_WATER,
                false,
                false,
                0u
            )
            : makeHitState(
                getRayPurpose(payload.state),
                RT_HIT_OPAQUE,
                RT_WATER_NONE,
                false,
                false,
                0u
            );

        float dhRoughness = 0.78;
        float dhF0 = 0.0;
        vec3 dhEmission = vec3(0.0);
        if (dhIsWater)
        {
            dhRoughness = 0.08;
        }
        else if (dhIsLava)
        {
            dhRoughness = 0.92;
            dhEmission = dhAlbedo * 4.0;
        }
        else if (dhIsLit)
        {
            dhRoughness = 0.86;
            dhEmission = dhAlbedo * 4.0;
        }
        else if (dhIsMetal)
        {
            dhRoughness = 0.35;
            dhF0 = 230.0 / 255.0;
        }
        else if (dhIsLeaves)
        {
            dhRoughness = 0.92;
        }
        else if (dhIsSnow)
        {
            dhRoughness = 0.45;
        }

        payload.roughness = dhRoughness;
        payload.f0Metal = dhF0;



        payload.emission = dhEmission + dhBakedBlockLight(dhAlbedo, interpColor.a);
        return;
    }



























































    vec2 duv1 = vec2(v1.u - v0.u, v1.v - v0.v);
    vec2 duv2 = vec2(v2.u - v0.u, v2.v - v0.v);
    vec3 dp1 = pos1 - pos0;
    vec3 dp2 = pos2 - pos0;

    float determinant =
        duv1.x * duv2.y -
        duv1.y * duv2.x;

    vec3 tangent =
        vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0);

    vec3 bitangent =
        vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);


    if (abs(determinant) > 1.0e-6)
    {
        float invDet =
            1.0 /
            determinant;


        vec3 rawTangent =
            (
                dp1 * duv2.y -
                dp2 * duv1.y
            ) *
            invDet;



        rawTangent -=
            geoNormal *
            dot(
                rawTangent,
                geoNormal
            );


        if (dot(rawTangent, rawTangent) > 1.0e-12)
        {
            tangent =
                normalize(
                    rawTangent
                );


            float handedness =
                determinant < 0.0
                    ? - 1.0
                    : 1.0;


            bitangent =
                normalize(
                    cross(
                        geoNormal,
                        tangent
                    )
                ) *
                handedness;
        }
        else
        {
            vec3 c1 =
                cross(
                    geoNormal,
                    vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)
                );

            vec3 c2 =
                cross(
                    geoNormal,
                    vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0)
                );

            tangent =
                length(c1) > length(c2)
                    ? c1
                    : c2;

            tangent =
                normalize(
                    tangent
                );

            bitangent =
                normalize(
                    cross(
                        geoNormal,
                        tangent
                    )
                );
        }
    }
    else
    {
        vec3 c1 =
            cross(
                geoNormal,
                vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0)
            );

        vec3 c2 =
            cross(
                geoNormal,
                vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0)
            );

        tangent =
            length(c1) > length(c2)
                ? c1
                : c2;

        tangent =
            normalize(
                tangent
            );

        bitangent =
            normalize(
                cross(
                    geoNormal,
                    tangent
                )
            );
    }


    mat3 tbn =
        mat3(
            tangent,
            bitangent,
            geoNormal
        );








vec2 uv0 =
    vec2(
        v0.u,
        v0.v
    );

vec2 uv1 =
    vec2(
        v1.u,
        v1.v
    );

    vec2(
        v2.u,
        v2.v

    max(

        vec2(1.0)

    spriteMin +
    atlasTexel * 0.5;


vec2 safeMax =
    spriteMax -
    atlasTexel * 0.5;

        spriteMin +

    ) * 0.5;


safeMin =
    min(
        safeMin,
        spriteCenter
    );


safeMax =
    max(
        safeMax,
        spriteCenter
    );
















vec2 basePbrTexCoord =
    clamp(
        texCoord,
        safeMin,
        safeMax
    );






const bool isWaterMaterial = false;





const bool isWaterMediumTransport = false;




bool isTransparentReceiverTransport =
    getRayPurpose(payload.state) == RT_PURPOSE_PASSTHROUGH;


vec4 baseNormalSample =
    vec4(0.0);


if (! isWaterMaterial)
{
    baseNormalSample =
        textureLod(
            normalAtlas,
            basePbrTexCoord,
            0.0
        );
}
bool hasPBRNormalData =
    ! isWaterMaterial &&
    ! isWaterMediumTransport &&
    length(baseNormalSample.rgb) >= 0.01;

ParallaxHit parallaxHit;


parallaxHit.uv =
    texCoord;

parallaxHit.depth01 =
    0.0;

parallaxHit.borderScale =
    1.0;

    0.0;

parallaxHit.rayTravelWorld =
    0.0;

parallaxHit.worldPosition =
    rayOrigin +
    rayDirection *
    hitT;

parallaxHit.valid =
    false;





bool isGlassSurface =
    materialId >= 1.5 &&
    materialId < 2.5;
















int glassVertexFlags =
    (isVoxyLod || isDhLod)
        ? 0
        : int(
              v0.pad2 * barycentrics.x +
              v1.pad2 * barycentrics.y +
              v2.pad2 * barycentrics.z +
              0.5);

const bool isFlatGlassWindow =
    isGlassSurface &&
    (glassVertexFlags & 16) != 0;

bool isPOMEligibleMaterial =
    isGlassSurface &&
    ! isFlatGlassWindow;

    isPOMEligibleMaterial &&
    hasPBRNormalData;


if (
    isPOMSurface
)
{
    parallaxHit =
        traceParallax(
            basePbrTexCoord,
            spriteMin,
            safeMin,
            safeMax,
            spriteSpan,
            tbn,
            rayDirection
        );

    texCoord =
        parallaxHit.uv;
}





















if (
    isGlassSurface &&
    ! isVoxyLod &&
    ! isDhLod
)
{
    int glassConnectMask =
        glassVertexFlags &
        15;

    if (glassConnectMask != 0)
    {
        vec2 glassSpriteTexels =
            spriteSpan *
            atlasSize;

        vec2 glassOneTexel =
            1.0 /
            max(glassSpriteTexels, vec2(1.0));

        vec2 glassLocalUv =
            (texCoord - spriteMin) /
            spriteSpan;

        if ( (glassConnectMask & 1) != 0 && glassLocalUv.x < glassOneTexel.x)
        {
            glassLocalUv.x += glassOneTexel.x;
        }

        if ( (glassConnectMask & 2) != 0 && glassLocalUv.x > 1.0 - glassOneTexel.x)
        {
            glassLocalUv.x -= glassOneTexel.x;
        }

        if ( (glassConnectMask & 4) != 0 && glassLocalUv.y < glassOneTexel.y)
        {
            glassLocalUv.y += glassOneTexel.y;
        }

        if ( (glassConnectMask & 8) != 0 && glassLocalUv.y > 1.0 - glassOneTexel.y)
        {
            glassLocalUv.y -= glassOneTexel.y;
        }

        texCoord =
            spriteMin +
            glassLocalUv *
            spriteSpan;
    }
}

    vec4(0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 1.0);

    normalSample =
        textureLod(
            normalAtlas,

bool hasNormalData =
    hasPBRNormalData &&
    ! isFlatGlassWindow;

    geoNormal;




if (hasNormalData)
{
    vec2 normalXY =
        normalSample.rg * 2.0 - 1.0;

    if (
        isPOMEligibleMaterial
    )
    {
        float viewFacing =
            clamp(
                abs(
                    dot(
                        geoNormal,
                        - rayDirection
                    )
                ),
                0.0,
                1.0
            );

        float grazingNormalStrength =
            mix(
                0.125,
                1.0,
                smoothstep(
                    0.0,
                    1.0,
                    viewFacing
                )
            );

        normalXY *=
            grazingNormalStrength;
    }


    float normalZ =
        sqrt(
            max(
                1.0 -
                dot(
                    normalXY,
                    normalXY
                ),
                0.0
            )
        );


    vec3 tangentNormal =
        normalize(
            vec3(
                normalXY,
                normalZ
            )
        );


    worldNormal =
        normalize(
            tbn *
            tangentNormal
        );
}




float materialAO =
    hasNormalData
        ? clamp(normalSample.b, 0.0, 1.0)
        : 1.0;





float heightDepth =
    hasNormalData
        ? (1.0 - clamp(normalSample.a, 0.0, 1.0)) * 0.25
        : 0.0;

    specularSample =

        ? 0.0

payload.hitDistance = hitT;

        ? parallaxHit.rayTravelWorld
        : 0.0;

        geoNormal,

    ) > 0.0;

    worldNormal;

if (isWaterMaterial && abs(geoNormal.y) > 0.5)
{

        rayOrigin + rayDirection * hitT;
    WaterWaveSurface waterWave =
        evaluateWaterWaveSurface(

        ? - waterWave.normal : waterWave.normal;































































}

if (
    dot(

    ) > 0.0
)
{
    fixedNormal =
        - fixedNormal;
}

    1.0;

    parallaxHit.valid &&

)

        getTimeDrivenSkyLightDirection();


    if (
        getTimeDrivenSkyLightRadianceScale() > 0.0 &&

        materialId > 1.5 &&

    payload.albedo =

    {

        false, false, 0u
    );

else

    );
}

    roughness;

    f0Metal;

    t12IsPortal
        ? albedoColor.rgb * 4.0
        : (t12IsGlass
            ? vec3(0.0)
            : albedoColor.rgb * emission * 10.0);

}

void resolveDeferredHit(vec3 rayOrigin, vec3 rayDirection)
{
    if (hit.hitDistance < 0.0)
    {

        payload.hitDistance = - 1.0;
        payload.albedo = hit.albedo;
        payload.state = hit.state;
        payload.normal = vec3(0.0);
        payload.roughness = 1.0;
        payload.f0Metal = 0.0;
        payload.emission = vec3(0.0);
        payload.parallaxRayTravel = 0.0;
        payload.pomSelfShadow = 1.0;
        payload.prevOffsetX = 0.0;
        payload.prevOffsetY = 0.0;
        payload.prevOffsetZ = 0.0;
        return;
    }


    payload.hitDistance = hit.hitDistance;
    payload.state = hit.state & 0x7u;
    payload.normal = vec3(float(hit.instanceIndex), float(hit.primitiveIndex), hit.baryX);
    payload.roughness = hit.baryY;




    payload.pomSelfShadow = 1.0;
    payload.pomMicroOcclusion = 1.0;
    payload.glassAlpha = hit.glassAlpha;
    payload.parallaxRayTravel = hit.parallaxRayTravel;































    if ( (hit.state & (1u << 13u)) != 0u)
    {
        resolveHitMaterialGlass(
            rayOrigin,
            rayDirection,
            vec3(
                1.0 - payload.normal.z - payload.roughness,
                payload.normal.z,
                payload.roughness
            )
        );
    }
    else
    {
        resolveHitMaterial(rayOrigin, rayDirection);
    }
}

primaryViewDirectionForWater = worldDir.xyz;














float rtLodDistance =
    max(
        primaryHitDistance,
        0.0
    );


uint rtLodState =
    uint(pixelCoord.x) * 73856093u ^
    uint(pixelCoord.y) * 19349663u ^
    cam.frameCount * 83492791u;


uint rtLodRandom =
    pcg_hash(
        rtLodState
    );


bool rtLodHalfSample =
    (rtLodRandom & 1u) == 0u;


bool rtLodQuarterSample =
    (rtLodRandom & 3u) == 0u;


bool rtLodGlossyHalfSample =
    ( (rtLodRandom >> 3u) & 1u) == 0u;


bool rtLodGlossyQuarterSample =
    ( (rtLodRandom >> 3u) & 3u) == 0u;



bool rtLodEighthSample =
    ( (rtLodRandom >> 5u) & 1u) == 0u;

bool rtLodSixteenthSample =
    ( (rtLodRandom >> 6u) & 1u) == 0u;

bool rtLodThirtySecondSample =
    ( (rtLodRandom >> 7u) & 1u) == 0u;





























bool rtLodDiffuseGiEnabled = false;

float rtLodDiffuseGiWeight = 1.0;

if (rtLodDistance < 64.0 * perfL10TierScale())
{
    rtLodDiffuseGiEnabled = true;
}
else if (rtLodDistance < 128.0 * perfL10TierScale())
{
    rtLodDiffuseGiEnabled = rtLodHalfSample;
    rtLodDiffuseGiWeight = 2.0;
}
else if (rtLodDistance < 160.0 * perfL10TierScale())
{
    rtLodDiffuseGiEnabled = rtLodQuarterSample;
    rtLodDiffuseGiWeight = 4.0;
}
else if (rtLodDistance < 256.0 * perfL10TierScale())
{
    rtLodDiffuseGiEnabled = rtLodEighthSample;
    rtLodDiffuseGiWeight = 8.0;
}
else if (rtLodDistance < 384.0 * perfL10TierScale())
{
    rtLodDiffuseGiEnabled = rtLodSixteenthSample;
    rtLodDiffuseGiWeight = 16.0;
}
else
{
    rtLodDiffuseGiEnabled = rtLodThirtySecondSample;
    rtLodDiffuseGiWeight = 32.0;
}



if (perfL10DisableDiffuseGi())
{
    rtLodDiffuseGiEnabled = false;
}


float primaryPOMSelfShadow =
    clamp(
        payload.pomSelfShadow,
        0.0,
        1.0
    );



float primaryPOMMicroOcclusion =
    clamp(
        payload.pomMicroOcclusion,
        0.0,
        1.0
    );


float primaryGlassAlpha =
    clamp(
        payload.glassAlpha,
        0.0,
        1.0
    );


float primaryRoughness =
    clamp(
        payload.roughness,
        0.0,
        1.0
    );


float primaryF0Metal =
    payload.f0Metal;


float primaryParallaxRayTravel =
    max(
        payload.parallaxRayTravel,
        0.0
    );





















vec3 primaryGlassEntryPoint =
    origin.xyz +
    worldDir.xyz *
    primaryHitDistance;

float primaryGlassSlabThickness =
    0.0;

bool primaryGlassUsesDielectric =
    false;


primaryGlassUsesDielectric =
    getLegacyMaterialId(payload.state) >= 2.5;

if (primaryGlassUsesDielectric)
{
    primaryGlassSlabThickness =
        glassSlabThicknessFromFace(
            primaryGlassEntryPoint,
            payload.normal
        );


    primaryParallaxRayTravel =
        glassSlabTravelAlongView(
            payload.normal,
            worldDir.xyz,
            primaryGlassSlabThickness
        );
}



















if (
    s == 0 &&
    primaryHitDistance >= 0.0 &&
    getLegacyMaterialId(payload.state) <= 0.5
)
{
    vec3 guideAlbedo =
        payload.albedo;

    vec3 guideNormal =
        normalize(
            payload.normal
        );

    float guideRoughness =
        clamp(
            payload.roughness,
            0.0,
            1.0
        );

    int guideF0MetalCode =
        decodeF0MetalCode(
            payload.f0Metal
        );

    float guideMetallic =
        decodeMetallicFromCode(
            guideF0MetalCode
        );

    vec3 guideF0 =
        decodeBaseF0(
            guideF0MetalCode,
            guideAlbedo
        );

    vec3 guideV =
        normalize(
            - worldDir.xyz
        );

    float guideNdotV =
        abs(
            dot(
                guideNormal,
                guideV
            )
        );

    rrDiffuseAlbedo =
        guideAlbedo *
        (
            1.0 -
            guideMetallic
        );

    rrSpecularAlbedo =
        rrEnvBRDFApprox2(
            guideF0,
            guideRoughness *
                guideRoughness,
            guideNdotV
        );

    rrNormalRoughness =
        vec4(
            guideNormal,
            guideRoughness
        );
}














if (s == 0)
{


    float primaryDepth =
        0.0;



    vec2 primaryMotionPixels =
        vec2(0.0);


    if (primaryHitDistance >= 0.0)
    {
        float primarySurfaceTravel =
            getHitClass(payload.state) == RT_HIT_TRANSLUCENT
                ? primaryParallaxRayTravel
                : 0.0;

        vec3 primaryHitPos =
    origin.xyz +
    worldDir.xyz *
    (primaryHitDistance + primarySurfaceTravel);























        {







            const vec3 prevWorldPos =
                primaryHitPos + primaryHitWorldOffset;

            const mat4 prevViewProj =
                inverse(cam.prevViewInverse * cam.prevProjInverse);

            const vec4 prevClipCur =
                prevViewProj * vec4(primaryHitPos, 1.0);

            const vec4 prevClipPrev =
                prevViewProj * vec4(prevWorldPos, 1.0);

            if (abs(prevClipCur.w) > 1.0e-6 &&
               abs(prevClipPrev.w) > 1.0e-6)
            {
                const vec2 prevUvCur =
                    prevClipCur.xy / prevClipCur.w;

                const vec2 prevUvPrev =
                    prevClipPrev.xy / prevClipPrev.w;


                const vec2 deltaNdc =
                    prevUvPrev - prevUvCur;




                primaryMotionPixels =
                    deltaNdc * 0.5 * vec2(imageSize(dlssMotionImage));

            }
        }


        vec4 viewPos =
            viewMatrix *
            vec4(
                primaryHitPos,
                1.0
            );


        vec4 clipPos =
            projectionMatrix *
            viewPos;


        if (abs(clipPos.w) > 1.0e-6)
        {
            primaryDepth =
                clamp(
                    clipPos.z /
                    clipPos.w,
                    0.0,
                    1.0
                );
        }
    }


    imageStore(
        depthImage,
        pixelCoord,
        vec4(
            primaryDepth,
            0.0,
            0.0,
            0.0
        )
    );














    const vec2 motionOutput =
        cam.perfAblationStage.z != 0u
            ? primaryMotionPixels
            : vec2(0.0);

    imageStore(
        dlssMotionImage,
        pixelCoord,
        vec4(
            motionOutput,
            0.0,
            1.0
        )
    );


    rtDebugPrimaryMotion = primaryMotionPixels;
}


vec3 sampleColor =
    vec3(0.0);




if (primaryHitDistance < 0.0) {


    sampleColor =
        payload.albedo;

} else {
    vec3 hitPos =
        origin.xyz +
        worldDir.xyz *
        primaryHitDistance;
            vec3 albedo = payload.albedo;
            vec3 normal = payload.normal;

vec3 primaryGeometricNormal =
    payload.normal;







bool rainSolidSurfaceCandidate =

bool rainWallSurfaceCandidate =

    abs(primaryGeometricNormal.y) < 0.5;





if (matId > 0.5 && matId < 2.5) {
                vec3 viewDir = - worldDir.xyz;
                float cosTheta = clamp(dot(normal, viewDir), 0.0, 1.0);
                float R0 = 0.02;
                float fresnelX = 1.0 - cosTheta;
                float fresnelX2 = fresnelX * fresnelX;
                float fresnel = R0 + (1.0 - R0) * (fresnelX2 * fresnelX2) * fresnelX;




                if (s == 0)
                {
                    const float waterGuideRoughness =
                        WATER_SURFACE_SUN_ROUGHNESS;

                    rrDiffuseAlbedo =
                        vec3(0.0);

                    rrSpecularAlbedo =
                        rrEnvBRDFApprox2(
                            vec3(R0),
                            waterGuideRoughness *
                                waterGuideRoughness,
                            cosTheta
                        );

                    rrNormalRoughness =
                        vec4(
                            normalize(normal),
                            waterGuideRoughness
                        );

                        normalize(
                            reflect(
                                worldDir.xyz,
                                normal
                            )
                        );

                        hitPos +

                            10000.0,
                            0

                            rrWaterReflectionOrigin +

                                rrWaterReflectionHitPos -

                        rrSpecularHitDistance =
                            65504.0;
                    }
                }







                WaterTransmissionInfo reflectedTransmissionInfo;

                        hitPos +
                            normal *
                            0.01,
                        normalize(
                            reflect(
                                worldDir.xyz,
                                normal
                            )
                        ),
                        false,
                        sunDir,
                        false,
                        primaryHitDistance,

                        hitPos,
                        reflectedTransmissionInfo
                    );

                    refract(

                        normal,
                        1.0 / 1.33
                    );

                if (
                    dot(
                        refrDir,
                        refrDir
                    ) < 1.0e-6
                )
                {
                    sampleColor =
                        reflectedRadiance;
                }
                else
                {
                    WaterTransmissionInfo transmittedTransmissionInfo;
                    vec3 transmittedRadiance =
                        tracePrimaryTransparentRadiance(
                            hitPos -
                                normal *
                                0.01,
                            refrDir,
                            true,
                            sunDir,
                            true,
                            primaryHitDistance,

                            transmittedTransmissionInfo
                            );





                    if (
                        s == 0 &&
                        transmittedTransmissionInfo.reachedUnderwaterReceiver > 0.5 &&
                        fresnel < 0.5

                            transmittedTransmissionInfo,
                            rrDiffuseAlbedo,
                            rrSpecularAlbedo,
                            rrNormalRoughness,
                            rrSpecularHitDistance
                        );

                        rrTransparentReceiverWorldPos =
                            transmittedTransmissionInfo.receiverWorldPos;

                        rrTransparentReceiverGuideValid =
                            true;
                    }

                    sampleColor =
                        mix(
                            transmittedRadiance,
                            reflectedRadiance,
                            fresnel
                        );




                    if (primaryHitDistance <
                       WATER_CAUSTIC_MAX_DISTANCE * perfL10WaterAdaptiveScale())
                    {
                        sampleColor +=
                            evaluatePrimaryWaterReceiverCaustics(
                                hitPos,
                                transmittedTransmissionInfo,
                                sunDir
                            );
                    }









                    float waterSssSunVisibility = 0.0;
                    if (cam.waterParams.x > 0.001 && sunDir.y > 0.02)
                    {
                        hit.hitDistance = 1.0;
                        hit.state =
                            makeTraceState(RT_PURPOSE_VISIBILITY_ONLY);
















                        {
                            traceRayEXT(
                                topLevelAS,
                                gl_RayFlagsTerminateOnFirstHitEXT |
                                    gl_RayFlagsSkipClosestHitShaderEXT,
                                0xff,
                                0,
                                0,
                                0,
                                hitPos + normal * 0.035,
                                0.02,
                                sunDir,
                                10000.0,
                                0
                            );
                        }
                        waterSssSunVisibility =
                            hit.hitDistance < 0.0 ? 1.0 : 0.0;
                    }



                    sampleColor +=
                        evaluateWaterCrestBacklit(
                            hitPos,
                            normal,
                            worldDir.xyz,
                            sunDir,
                            transmittedTransmissionInfo
                        ) * (1.0 - fresnel) *
                        waterSssSunVisibility;




                    sampleColor +=
                        evaluateWaterThinLayerSSS(
                            normal,
                            worldDir.xyz,
                            sunDir,
                            transmittedTransmissionInfo
                        ) * (1.0 - fresnel) *





                        waterSssSunVisibility;











                    if (transmittedTransmissionInfo.reachedUnderwaterReceiver > 0.5)
                    {
                        const float kWaterMultiScatterScale = 2.0;
                        vec3 waterMultiExtinction =
                            waterAbsorptionColor() + waterScatterColor();
                        float waterMultiPath = clamp(
                            transmittedTransmissionInfo.waterPathLength, 0.05, 60.0);
                        vec3 waterMultiShare = vec3(1.0) -
                            exp(- waterMultiExtinction * waterMultiPath);






                        vec3 waterMultiSource = max(
                            transmittedRadiance -
                                transmittedTransmissionInfo.receiverSelfEmissionRadiance,
                            vec3(0.0));
                        sampleColor += waterMultiSource *
                            waterScatterColor() * waterScatteringAmount() *
                            waterMultiShare *
                            (kWaterMultiScatterScale * (1.0 - fresnel));
                    }




















                }




                sampleColor +=
                    evaluatePrimaryWaterSunLighting(
                        hitPos,
                        normal,
                        viewDir,
                        sunDir
                    );

            }










else if (
    matId >= 2.5 &&
    matId < 4.5
)

                GlassSurfaceInfo glassSurface;

                if (s == 0)

                    );

                        if (

            else {
vec3 emission =
    payload.emission;


float roughness =
    max(
        payload.roughness,
        0.05
    );













int f0MetalCode =
    int(
        round(
            clamp(
                payload.f0Metal,
                0.0,

            ) * 255.0
        )
    );


bool isMetal =
    f0MetalCode >= 230;


float metallic =
    isMetal
        ? 1.0
        : 0.0;
